### [1] [GAP3D: Generative Alignment of VLM Latents to Patch-Level Embeddings for 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28995)
*Polytimi Anna Gkotsi, Andrii Zadaianchuk, Mohammad Mahdi Derakhshani*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (57.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GAP3D 提出一种模块化的扩散对齐方法，将 VLM 生成的潜在表示直接对齐到预训练图像编码器的完整 patch 级特征空间，使冻结的下游生成模型（如 3D 生成模型）能够利用 VLM 作为提示编码器，同时保持空间结构化的条件信号。该方法主要使用通用域图像-文本对训练，无需大规模 3D 数据，并展现出对多模态提示的零样本能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将 VLM 作为生成模型提示编码器的方法要么需要昂贵的端到端训练，要么将特征映射到压缩表示中，丢失了 3D 资产生成等几何感知任务所需的密集空间结构信息。因此需要一种模块化方法，在不进行端到端训练的前提下，将 VLM 的语义理解能力与图像编码器的空间结构信息结合起来。

Method: GAP3D 采用扩散模型将 VLM 生成的潜在表示（如 CLIP 文本嵌入）对齐到预训练图像编码器（如 DINOv2）的完整 patch 级特征空间。具体地，训练一个条件扩散模型，以 VLM 输出为条件，生成与图像编码器 patch 特征分布一致的表示。下游 3D 生成模型（如 Point-E）保持冻结，直接使用对齐后的 patch 级特征作为条件信号。训练主要在通用域图像-文本对上进行，无需 3D 数据。

Result: 在 3D 资产生成任务上，GAP3D 无需大规模 3D 训练数据即可实现有效的条件生成。该方法展现出对多模态提示（如图像+文本）的零样本能力，尽管仅使用文本输入进行训练。实验表明 VLM 与图像编码器特征空间之间的表示差距可以通过扩散对齐部分弥合。

Conclusion: GAP3D 展示了通过扩散对齐将 VLM 与图像编码器特征空间进行模块化集成的可行性，为 3D 生成提供了一种无需端到端训练或压缩表示的条件信号方案。当前方法优先关注高层语义而非细粒度细节，未来工作可进一步弥合表示差距，实现更精细的生成控制。

Classification reason: 论文提出 GAP3D，一种基于扩散对齐的方法，将 VLM 生成的潜在表示与预训练图像编码器的 patch 级特征空间对齐，用于 3D 资产生成。核心任务是 3D 生成，因此映射到 multimodal_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" (three_d_generation)。同时涉及 VLM 作为提示编码器，与 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向相关。

Importance reason: Novel modular alignment for 3D generation, but 3D generation is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong boost keywords. Signals: 3D generation, diffusion-based alignment, VLM as prompt encoder, zero-shot multimodal, no large-scale 3D data needed

Abstract: Recent approaches integrating vision-language models (VLMs) as prompt encoders for generative model conditioning typically rely on expensive end-to-end training or map features to compressed representations, discarding the dense spatial structure required for geometry-aware tasks like 3D asset generation. To address this, we propose GAP3D, a modular, diffusion-based approach that aligns VLM-generated latents directly to the complete, patch-level feature space of a pre-trained image encoder, enabling a frozen downstream generative model to utilize a VLM as prompt encoder while maintaining a spatially structured conditioning signal. Evaluated on 3D asset generation, our method bypasses the need for large-scale 3D data by training mainly on general-domain image-text pairs. It also exhibits emergent zero-shot capabilities for multimodal prompts, despite being trained exclusively on text input. Finally, while currently prioritizing high-level semantics over fine-grained detail, GAP3D demonstrates that the representation gap between VLM and image-encoder feature spaces can be partially bridged through diffusion-based alignment, taking the first steps towards a modular integration of foundation models through generative alignment to dense embedding spaces.

</details>


### [2] [Auditing Training-Free 3D Shape Retrieval with Diffused Geodesic Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29004)
*Zhicheng Du, Changyue Liu, Wenji Xi, Zhaotian Xie, Zhuo Deng, Ziheng Zhang, Yang Liu, Lan Ma*

Main category: cs.GR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (50.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Diffused Geodesic Moments (DGM)，一种基于种子条件的无训练3D形状描述子，通过稀疏隐式热响应和低阶矩聚合实现形状检索。论文将描述子评估重新定义为"协议审计"，揭示了输入场和聚合协议对检索结果的主导作用，并提供了可复现的协议级联分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练形状描述子的检索分数混杂了局部信号设计、归一化、聚合、码本拟合和度量选择等多种因素，难以独立评估各组件贡献。作者希望将描述子评估重新框架化为协议审计，以隔离和量化各协议环节的影响。

Method: 提出 Diffused Geodesic Moments (DGM)：计算稀疏种子点的隐式热响应，将其转换为距离场，并通过低阶矩（均值、方差等）在多个种子和尺度上汇总每个顶点的特征。同时构建了基于热核签名（HKS）的几何矩形状描述子（GMSD-HKS）作为基线。在FAUST-Reg和TOSCA数据集上进行聚合匹配实验，系统分析协议级联中各环节的影响。

Result: GMSD-HKS基线在FAUST-Reg上获得0.621 mAP/0.820 top-1，在TOSCA上获得0.865 mAP/0.963 top-1，为当前实现中最高分。WKS仍是强经典信号。DGM主要在稀疏求解、非频谱部署或对称信息种子帧优先的场景中有用。核心发现：输入场和聚合协议对检索结果的影响远大于矩公式本身。

Conclusion: 论文贡献了可复现的协议级联分析、跨形状对齐诊断工具，以及设计和报告无训练形状描述子的具体建议。强调未来研究应更关注协议设计而非仅关注描述子公式本身。

Classification reason: 论文研究无训练3D形状检索的描述子（Diffused Geodesic Moments），属于3D形状分析与检索，与 canonical subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）中的3D物体/场景生成与重建主题高度相关，且该 subtopic 涵盖3D形状表示与检索方法。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D shape retrieval, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key directions. Signals: 3D shape retrieval, training-free descriptor, geodesic moments, protocol audit, non-spectral baseline

Abstract: Reported retrieval scores for training-free shape descriptors conflate local signal design, normalization, aggregation, codebook fitting, and metric choices, making isolated component evaluation difficult. This paper reframes descriptor evaluation as a {\em protocol audit}. We introduce Diffused Geodesic Moments (DGM), a seed-conditioned descriptor that computes sparse implicit heat responses, converts them to distance-like fields, and summarizes each vertex by low-order moments across seeds and scales. DGM is used both as a practical non-spectral baseline and as an instrument for isolating protocol effects. On the registered FAUST benchmark split (FAUST-Reg) and the TOSCA shape collection, aggregation-matched experiments show that an independent Geometric Moment Shape Descriptor baseline built on Heat Kernel Signature features (GMSD-HKS) obtains the highest scores in this implementation ($0.621/0.820$ and $0.865/0.963$ mean average precision (mAP)/top-1), Wave Kernel Signature (WKS) remains a strong classical signal, and DGM is useful mainly when sparse solves, non-spectral deployment, or symmetry-informative seed frames are priorities. The broader finding is methodological: the input field and aggregation protocol can dominate the moment formula. The paper contributes a reproducible protocol-cascade analysis, a cross-shape alignment diagnostic for functional-map compatibility, and concrete recommendations for designing and reporting training-free shape descriptors.

</details>


### [3] [Comparative evaluation of photogrammetric reconstruction methods and 3D Gaussian Splatting for road surface roughness analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29452)
*Marouane Elmegdar, Teng Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (47.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文比较了COLMAP、Meshroom、Metashape和3D Gaussian Splatting四种基于图像的三维重建方法在道路表面粗糙度估计中的表现，发现COLMAP对微观纹理最敏感，Meshroom重建均衡，Metashape几何最平滑，3DGS能捕捉可见不规则性但噪声较高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统道路表面评估依赖专用传感器设备，成本高昂。本文探索利用智能手机图像进行低成本三维重建来评估道路粗糙度的可行性，并系统比较不同重建方法的效果。

Method: 使用智能手机采集道路图像，分别用COLMAP、Meshroom、Metashape和3D Gaussian Splatting四种管线进行三维重建。所有点云在CloudCompare中经过统一的对齐、分割、法线估计和粗糙度计算流程，在0.2、0.4、0.6模型单位邻域半径下评估粗糙度。

Result: COLMAP对微观纹理最敏感，粗糙度变化最大；Meshroom重建结果均衡，粗糙度变化适中；Metashape因内部滤波产生最平滑的几何结构；3DGS能捕捉可见不规则性但噪声较高且点云密度较低。

Conclusion: 开源重建管线可用于相对粗糙度评估，为低成本路面监测提供了实用方案。不同方法在纹理敏感度和几何平滑度上各有优劣，需根据具体应用场景选择。

Classification reason: 论文比较了包括3D Gaussian Splatting在内的多种三维重建方法用于道路表面粗糙度分析，属于3D场景重建与评估应用，映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Focuses on road surface roughness, not aligned with user's priority subtopics in generative AI and video/image generation. Signals: 3D reconstruction comparison, road surface analysis, photogrammetry vs 3DGS, low-cost pavement monitoring, not generative AI

Abstract: Image-based 3D reconstruction offers a low-cost alternative to traditional sensor-based techniques for road surface assessment. This study compares four reconstruction pipelines--COLMAP, Meshroom, Metashape, and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)--to evaluate their ability to estimate road surface roughness from smartphone imagery. All point clouds were processed in CloudCompare using a consistent workflow involving orientation alignment, segmentation, normal estimation, and roughness computation at neighborhood radiuses of 0.2, 0.4, and 0.6 model units. The results show that COLMAP provides the highest sensitivity to micro-texture, while Meshroom yields balanced reconstructions with moderate roughness variation. Metashape produces the smoothest geometry due to its internal filtering, and 3DGS captures visible irregularities but exhibits higher noise and lower density. The comparison demonstrates that open-source pipelines are viable for relative roughness evaluation, offering a practical approach for low-cost pavement monitoring.

</details>


### [4] [Learning Representations from 3D Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29549)
*Julia Farganus, Krzysztof Żurawicki, Arkadiusz Gaweł, Weronika Jakubowska, Halina Kwaśnicka*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (53.76) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文对基于3D Gaussian Splatting的场景表征学习进行了系统比较研究，评估了点云和图神经网络等多种几何深度学习架构在3DGS场景分类任务上的表现，揭示了不同架构族和输入特征配置对表征质量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Gaussian Splatting主要用于视图合成，其在场景理解任务（如分类）中的潜力尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统评估不同几何深度学习架构在3DGS场景表征学习中的有效性。

Method: 作者将3D场景通过Gaussian Splatting嵌入为潜在表征，然后使用点云模型和图神经网络等多种几何深度学习架构进行端到端分类、线性探测和聚类分析，在传统点云数据集和专用3DGS数据集上进行基准测试。

Result: 不同架构族在3DGS场景分类任务上表现出一致的性能差异，Gaussian特有属性（如协方差、透明度等）对表征质量有显著影响。

Conclusion: 3D Gaussian Splatting不仅适用于视图合成，其表征也可有效用于场景理解任务；选择合适的几何深度学习架构和输入特征配置对学习有效的3DGS表征至关重要。

Classification reason: 论文研究基于3D Gaussian Splatting的场景表征学习与分类，属于3D场景理解与表征方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）紧密相关，因为3DGS是该子主题的核心技术之一。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS representation learning for classification, not directly on user's priority generation or efficiency topics. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, scene understanding, representation learning, geometric deep learning, not autoregressive or diffusion

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is a recent approach for scene rendering. Although primarily designed for view synthesis, its potential for scene understanding tasks remains underexplored. In this work, we conduct a comparative evaluation of various geometric deep learning architectures for the classification of 3D scenes represented using Gaussian Splatting. We benchmark point-based and graph-based models across both traditional point cloud datasets and dedicated Gaussian Splatting datasets. Scenes are embedded into latent representations, which are evaluated through end-to-end classification, linear probing, and clustering analysis. Our study provides insight into the suitability of different geometry-aware architectures and input feature configurations for learning effective 3D Gaussian Splat representations. The results highlight consistent differences between architectural families and reveal the impact of Gaussian-specific attributes on the quality of representation.

</details>


### [5] [SuperVoxelGPT: Adaptive and Ordered 3D Tokenization for Autoregressive Shape Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29655)
*Yuan Li, Congyi Zhang, Xifeng Gao, Xiaohu Guo*

Main category: cs.GR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 高优先级 (88.77) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SuperVoxelGPT 提出一种自适应且有序的超体素 token 化方法，通过显著性引导的质心 Voronoi 划分实现形状自适应分区，结合 SuperVoxelVAE 和微调 MLLM 进行自回归 3D 生成，在 Trellis-500K 上以均匀体素 12.8% 的序列长度达到 SOTA 质量并实现 10 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归 3D 生成方法受限于不充分的 3D token 化：基于集合的紧凑表示缺乏确定性空间顺序导致序列预测歧义，而均匀或八叉树体素网格虽保持顺序但存在严重冗余和过长序列。这种结构权衡限制了稳定高效的自回归 3D 生成。

Method: 首先根据文本提示预测粗粒度几何显著性分布，然后利用显著性引导的质心 Voronoi 划分构建形状自适应的超体素分区（复杂区域细粒度、平滑区域粗粒度）。在此基础上引入 SuperVoxelVAE，并微调预训练 MLLM 以自回归方式生成超体素 token。

Result: 在 Trellis-500K 数据集上，SuperVoxelGPT 将 token 序列长度降至均匀体素 token 化的 12.8%，同时达到 SOTA 生成质量，相比先前方法平均实现 10 倍加速。

Conclusion: SuperVoxelGPT 通过自适应有序超体素 token 化有效解决了自回归 3D 生成中序列长度与空间顺序保持之间的权衡，为高效高质量的自回归 3D 生成提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于自适应有序超体素（supervoxel）token化的自回归3D生成方法，属于3D物体生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel adaptive 3D tokenization for autoregressive generation directly addresses efficiency and quality trade-offs in a priority subtopic. Signals: autoregressive 3D generation, efficient inference, adaptive tokenization, MLLM fine-tuning, 10x speedup

Abstract: Autoregressive multimodal large language models (MLLMs) enable 3D generation but struggle to scale to high-resolution shapes due to inadequate 3D tokenizations. Compact set-based representations discard deterministic spatial ordering, leading to ambiguous sequence prediction, while uniform or octree-based voxel grids preserve ordering at the cost of severe redundancy and excessively long sequences. This structural trade-off limits stable and efficient autoregressive 3D generation. We present SuperVoxelGPT, a representation-first framework that resolves this tension through adaptive and deterministically ordered supervoxel tokenization. Given a prompt, we first predict a coarse geometric saliency distribution and construct a shape-adaptive supervoxel partition using saliency-guided centroidal Voronoi tessellation, allocating fine-grained cells to complex regions and larger cells to smooth regions. Conditioned on the text and ordered supervoxel layout, we introduce a SuperVoxelVAE and fine-tune a pretrained MLLM to autoregressively generate supervoxel tokens. Experiments on Trellis-500K show that SuperVoxelGPT reduces token sequence length to 12.8% of uniform voxel tokenization while achieving state-of-the-art generation quality and an average 10$\times$ speedup over prior methods.

</details>


### [6] [DGSG-Mind: Dynamic 3D Gaussian Scene Graphs for Long-Term Scene Understanding and Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29879)
*Luzhou Ge, Xiangyu Zhu, Jinyan Liu, Xuesong Li*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Importance: 普通相关 (52.09) - 未入选精读

Conclusion: DGSG-Mind 通过混合实例感知3D高斯动态场景图系统有效解决了动态场景中长期理解与定位的挑战，为具身智能场景理解提供了新范式。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D scene graphs and robotics, not directly aligned with user's priority subtopics like autoregressive video generation or diffusion models. Signals: 3D scene graph, dynamic scene understanding, zero-shot 3DVG, robotics, not video generation

Abstract: Integrating open-vocabulary semantic information into dynamic 3D scene representations is essential for long-term embodied scene understanding. However, existing methods often suffer from fragile instance association due to incomplete cross-view cues, while their limited ability to handle object-level topological changes restricts long-term robotic task execution. Moreover, current 3D scene understanding methods either rely on simple feature matching without explicit spatial reasoning or assume offline ground-truth 3D geometry. To address these challenges, we present DGSG-Mind, a hybrid instance-aware 3D Gaussian dynamic scene graph system with an embodied reasoning agent. Our system couples a probabilistic voxel grid with explicit 3D Gaussians to enable robust cross-modal instance fusion and incremental semantic mapping. It handles dynamic changes through Gaussian-based visual relocalization and localized masked refinement guided by geometric-semantic consistency. Built on the instance Gaussian map, DGSG-Mind further constructs a hierarchical scene graph and develops the 3D Gaussian Mind, which integrates structural relations, spatial-semantic information, and visually annotated RoI Gaussian renderings for multimodal reasoning. Extensive experiments show that DGSG-Mind achieves the best zero-shot 3DVG performance among methods operating on self-reconstructed maps, while also delivering strong performance in 3D open-vocabulary semantic segmentation and scene reconstruction. We further deploy DGSG-Mind on real-world robots to demonstrate its target-oriented reasoning and dynamic update capabilities. The project page of DGSG-Mind is available at

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 DVSM，一种仅解码器架构的大规模新视角合成模型。通过将场景隐式表示为 KV-cache，在更少参数和相同渲染复杂度下超越编码器-解码器变体。进一步引入基础模型先验和分阶段 patch 尺寸策略，在多个基准上达到 SOTA，部分场景甚至超越逐场景优化的 3DGS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模新视角合成模型（LVSMs）普遍采用编码器-解码器架构，将重建和渲染分离为不同网络。本文质疑这种设计是否最优，探索更简洁高效的解码器-only 架构。

Method: 提出 DVSM（Decoder-only View Synthesis Model），核心思想是用解码器-only 架构将场景隐式表示为 KV-cache，共享颜色输入重建网络与仅相机输入渲染网络之间的权重，使两者在同一视角下的特征对齐。此外，引入基础模型先验（foundation model priors）和分阶段 patch 尺寸策略（stage-wise patch sizing）以提升效率-质量权衡。

Result: 在多个新视角合成基准上达到 SOTA，部分场景下甚至超越逐场景优化的 3DGS（3D Gaussian Splatting）。解码器-only 架构在更少参数和相同渲染复杂度下优于编码器-解码器变体。

Conclusion: 解码器-only 架构在大规模新视角合成中是一种更优的设计选择，通过隐式 KV-cache 场景表示和权重共享机制，可以在更高效的同时取得更好的合成质量。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种仅解码器架构的新视角合成模型（DVSM），属于3D场景的新视角渲染与生成任务，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel decoder-only view synthesis with KV-cache implicit scene representation advances multimodal generation efficiency. Signals: decoder-only architecture, KV-cache scene representation, foundation model priors, stage-wise patch sizing, SOTA novel-view synthesis

Abstract: Recent Large View Synthesis Models (LVSMs) advocate an encoder-decoder architecture that separates reconstruction and rendering into distinct networks. We re-examine this design. Through controlled experiments, we show that a decoder-only architecture, which represents scenes implicitly as a KV-cache, outperforms encoder-decoder variants while using fewer parameters at identical rendering complexity. Further analysis shows that sharing weights between the color-input reconstruction network and the camera-only rendering network better aligns their features at the same viewpoint, facilitating image synthesis. Building on this finding, our model, dubbed DVSM, further incorporates foundation model priors and stage-wise patch sizing for an improved efficiency-quality tradeoff. Our results establish a new state of the art for novel-view synthesis across multiple benchmarks, in some cases even outperforming per-scene-optimized 3DGS under dense input views.

</details>

*Yihang Tao, Yu Guo, Zhengru Fang, Haonan An, Yuguang Fang*

TL;DR: FRUC 是一个前馈式 3D 高斯泼溅框架，用于从无标定的多车辆协同驾驶视角进行动态场景重建。它通过视觉几何 Transformer 实现一次性无标定推理，引入以自我为中心的因果遮挡场建模遮挡先验，并将跨智能体融合建模为确定性残差去噪过程，在真实驾驶数据集上实现了 SOTA 渲染质量和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体场景重建方法依赖精确的空间标定和逐场景优化，效率低下且难以部署。本文旨在解决无标定多车辆协同视角下的动态场景重建问题，核心挑战在于：在不破坏自车准确观测的可见几何的前提下，通过协作增强自车被遮挡区域的几何信息，同时保持重建效率。

Method: FRUC 基于视觉几何 Transformer 骨干网络，支持从任意数量多车辆视角进行一次性无标定推理。首先引入以自我为中心的因果遮挡场（ego-centric causal occlusion field），通过建模智能体间的时空关联显式推导遮挡演化作为潜在先验；然后在这些遮挡先验引导下，将跨智能体融合建模为确定性残差去噪过程，通过零初始化注入（zero-initialized injection）将困难的跨智能体融合转化为有界残差学习，实现鲁棒的协同盲区补全。

Result: 在真实世界 V2XReal 和 UrbanIng-V2X 数据集上的大量实验表明，FRUC 在渲染质量和效率方面均显著优于现有方法，成为动态协同驾驶场景重建的新 SOTA。

Conclusion: FRUC 通过前馈式无标定框架有效解决了多车辆协同动态场景重建中的效率与精度问题，为自动驾驶中的协同感知与场景理解提供了新的技术路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于3D高斯泼溅的前馈式动态场景重建框架，用于多车辆协同驾驶视角下的场景重建，属于3D场景生成与重建范畴，与 canonical subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel feed-forward 3D reconstruction from uncalibrated multi-vehicle views, but peripheral to core priority subtopics. Signals: feed-forward 3D gaussian splatting, uncalibrated collaborative driving, dynamic scene reconstruction, occlusion field, residual denoising fusion

Abstract: We present FRUC, a feed-forward 3D Gaussian splatting framework for dynamic scene reconstruction from uncalibrated collaborative driving views. Existing multi-agent reconstruction frameworks are often hindered by rigid prerequisites, demanding precise spatial calibration and slow per-scene optimization. In this paper, we rethink this task by conceptualizing a distributed multi-vehicle network as a spatio-temporally unstructured ego-centric multi-camera system, where the core challenge lies in enhancing ego-centric occluded geometry through collaboration without degrading the ego's accurately observed visible geometry, while preserving reconstruction efficiency. For efficient reconstruction, FRUC is built upon a visual grounded geometric Transformer backbone to enable one-shot, calibration-free inference from a flexible number of multi-vehicle views. To achieve non-destructive geometric supplementation under uncalibrated cross-agent misalignment, FRUC first introduces an ego-centric causal occlusion field that explicitly derives occlusion evolution as latent priors by modeling agent-wise spatio-temporal correlations. Guided by these occlusion priors, it further formulates cross-agent integration as a deterministic residual denoising process via zero-initialized injection, turning challenging cross-agent fusion into bounded residual learning for robust collaborative blind-spot completion. Through extensive evaluations on the real-world V2XReal and UrbanIng-V2X datasets, FRUC is shown to be a new state-of-the-art for the scene reconstruction of dynamic collaborative driving environments, significantly outperforming existing methods in both rendering quality and efficiency.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D风格迁移面临数据稀缺问题——每个模型仅在一个场景上训练，可用的内容图像数量有限，严重制约了风格化性能。现有方法因缺乏足够的内容-风格图像对来提供监督信号，导致风格化质量不佳。

Method: 将大规模2D图像数据集上预训练的编解码器集成到3D风格迁移流程中，利用解码器从大量内容-风格图像对中学习到的先验知识。结合特征高斯泼溅（feature Gaussian splatting）与延迟风格化（deferred stylization），将视角相关操作统一为视角无关过程，在保证多视角一致性的同时实现高质量风格化。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，DS-StyleGaussian在视觉质量上优于现有的零样本3D风格迁移方法。

Conclusion: 2D预训练可以作为3D任务的有效增强手段，弥合2D与3D之间的数据差距，为数据稀缺的3D任务提供新的解决思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究零样本3D风格迁移，使用3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）实现多视角一致的风格化渲染，属于3D场景生成与神经渲染范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"高度匹配。

Importance reason: 3D style transfer is not a priority subtopic, and the paper lacks strong signals for the user's boosted keywords. Signals: 3D style transfer, 2D pre-trained priors, zero-shot, feature Gaussian splatting, deferred stylization

Abstract: In this work, we focus on zero-shot 3D style transfer that can generate multi-view consistent stylized views of the 3D scene given an arbitrary style image. We primarily tackle the issue of data scarcity in 3D style transfer, which arises when each model is trained on only a single scene, thereby limiting the number of available content images. This scarcity significantly hampers stylization performance, as model optimization relies on a sufficient number of content-style image pairs to provide supervisory signals. Our core idea is to integrate a decoder pre-trained on large-scale 2D image datasets into the 3D style transfer pipeline, thereby leveraging the prior knowledge encoded in the decoder from learning over numerous content-style image pairs. Our method combines feature Gaussian splatting and deferred stylization, enabling high-quality stylization with the data-sufficient decoder network while ensuring view consistency by unifying view-dependent operations into a view-invariant process. Experiments demonstrate that our Data-Sufficient StyleGaussian (DS-StyleGaussian) model outperforms existing zero-shot 3D style transfer methods in terms of visual quality across various datasets. This work also suggests that 2D pre-training can serve as a strong enhancement for 3D tasks, bridging the data gap between 2D and 3D.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自监督视觉模型和文生图扩散模型的特征主要从2D图像目标中学习，缺乏显式的3D感知能力，容易混淆对称物体侧面、重复部件以及视觉相似但3D结构不同的区域。作者希望引入3D基础模型的先验知识来弥补这一不足。

Method: 给定一张图像，首先使用SAM3D估计物体几何和姿态，然后通过渲染-比较优化精炼姿态；接着从重建的几何中渲染PartField描述子到图像平面，生成几何感知的特征图；利用重建形状上的测地距离可靠地过滤候选对应点；最后用过滤后的匹配作为监督信号，在DINO和Stable Diffusion特征之上训练一个轻量适配器用于语义对应估计。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在语义对应估计任务上优于现有方法，同时减少了人工几何标注的需求。代码和模型已开源。

Conclusion: 本文证明了3D基础模型提供的几何先验可以有效增强2D基础特征的语义对应能力，为无需人工标注的3D感知视觉对应学习提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文利用3D基础模型（SAM3D）估计物体几何和姿态，通过渲染-比较优化重建3D结构，并利用重建形状上的测地距离过滤对应点，以增强语义对应学习。核心贡献在于引入3D先验（几何与姿态）来改进2D特征，属于3D几何与重建技术在视觉任务中的应用，因此映射到[多模态生成]方向下的[3D 生成与重建]规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D geometric priors for semantic correspondence, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in autoregressive/diffusion video generation and unified multimodal models. Signals: 3D foundation priors, semantic correspondence, post-training framework, SAM3D, DINO/Stable Diffusion adapter

Abstract: Foundation features from self-supervised vision models and text-to-image diffusion models have proven effective for semantic correspondence estimation. However, because these features are learned primarily from 2D image objectives, they lack explicit 3D awareness and often confuse symmetric object sides, repeated parts, and visually similar structures that are distinct in 3D. We introduce a 3D-aware post-training framework that goes beyond available 2D foundation features by incorporating priors from 3D foundation models. Given an image, our method uses SAM3D to estimate object geometry and pose, and refines the pose through render-and-compare optimization. Subsequently, we render PartField descriptors from the reconstructed geometry into the image plane based on the estimated object pose. The resulting geometry-aware feature maps complement DINO and Stable Diffusion features, while geodesic distances on the reconstructed shapes enable reliable filtering of candidate correspondences. We use the filtered matches as supervision to train a lightweight adapter on top of DINO and Stable Diffusion for semantic correspondence. In contrast to prior post-training approaches that require pose annotations and rely on coarse spherical geometry, our method automatically obtains instance-specific 3D structure and uses it to guide correspondence learning. Experiments show that our approach improves semantic correspondence over the prior methods while reducing manual geometric supervision. Code and model can be found at .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度补全方法在稀疏观测下性能有限，且通常依赖复杂架构设计或相机内参。作者希望构建一个简单、通用且鲁棒的框架，能够从任意稀疏观测（如 LiDAR 点云、SfM 点云等）生成度量精确的稠密深度图，并具备良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Method: (1) 利用现有单目基础模型（如 Depth Anything）提升稀疏深度输入的质量；(2) 提出泊松深度初始化策略，从稀疏观测生成均匀的粗稠密深度图作为结构先验；(3) 用点图回归头（point map head）替代传统深度头，直接回归相机空间下逐像素 3D 坐标，使模型学习底层 3D 场景结构而非像素级深度恢复，同时消除对相机内参的依赖。

Result: 在多个基准数据集（如 NYUv2、KITTI、ScanNet 等）和不同稀疏度下，LDCM 在深度补全和点图估计任务上均一致超越现有最先进方法，展现出优异的泛化能力。

Conclusion: LDCM 验证了结合单目基础模型先验与点图回归训练目标的有效性，为深度补全提供了一种简洁而强大的新范式，未来可进一步扩展到更广泛的 3D 感知任务。

Classification reason: 该论文提出大深度补全模型（LDCM），从稀疏观测生成稠密深度图与3D点图，属于3D场景重建与深度估计范畴，与[3D 生成与重建]（three_d_generation）这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on depth completion, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's configured direction weights. Signals: Transformer-based depth completion, Poisson depth initialization, point map head, no camera intrinsics needed, strong generalization

Abstract: This work presents the Large Depth Completion Model (LDCM), a simple, effective, and robust framework for single-view metric depth estimation with sparse observations. Without relying on complex architectural designs, LDCM generates metric-accurate dense depth maps using a transformer. It outperforms existing approaches across diverse datasets and sparse observations. We achieve this from two key perspectives: (1) leveraging existing monocular foundation models to improve the quality of sparse depth inputs, and (2) reformulating training objectives to better capture geometric structure and metric consistency. Specifically, a Poisson-based depth initialization strategy is first introduced to generate a uniform coarse dense depth map from diverse sparse observations, providing a strong structural prior for the network. Regarding the training objective, we replace the conventional depth head with a point map head that regresses per-pixel 3D coordinates in camera space, enabling the model to directly learn the underlying 3D scene structure instead of performing pixel-wise depth map restoration. Moreover, this design eliminates the need for camera intrinsic parameters, allowing LDCM to naturally produce metric-scaled 3D point maps. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LDCM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across multiple benchmarks and varying sparsity levels in both depth completion and point map estimation, showcasing its effectiveness and strong generalization to unseen data distributions.

</details>


### [12] [Déjà View: Looping Transformers for Multi-View 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30215)
*Alessandro Burzio, Tobias Fischer, Sven Elflein, Qunjie Zhou, Riccardo de Lutio, Jiawei Ren, Jiahui Huang, Shengyu Huang, Marc Pollefeys, Laura Leal-Taixé, Zan Gojcic, Haithem Turki*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (59.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DéjàView 提出一种循环Transformer架构用于多视图3D重建，通过单个循环块重复K次迭代细化视图特征，在推理时可调节计算量，以远少于大规模前馈模型的参数在五个重建基准上达到或超越其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式3D重建Transformer模型参数规模已超十亿，但研究表明连续Transformer层的行为类似于重复应用相似操作，深度部分买的是"迭代"而非真正的表达能力。作者希望将迭代显式化到架构中，用更少的参数实现同等或更好的重建质量。

Method: DéjàView 采用单个循环Transformer块，对每个视图的特征进行K次递归细化。训练时使用固定的K步展开，推理时K可作为计算量调节旋钮。循环块与独立参数的多层变体在相同训练数据和计算量下进行对比，验证显式迭代的归纳偏置优势。

Result: 在室内、室外、物体中心、驾驶场景等五个重建基准上，DéjàView 以远少于前馈基线的参数和相当或更低的计算量，达到或超越了更大规模模型的表现。循环块变体优于相同架构但使用独立每步参数的变体。

Conclusion: 显式迭代不仅是计算高效的容量替代方案，更是多视图3D重建中更强的归纳偏置。循环架构在推理时提供了灵活的计算-质量权衡，为高效3D重建模型设计提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多视图3D重建的循环Transformer架构，属于3D重建与生成方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a compute-efficient looping transformer for multi-view 3D reconstruction, aligning with model efficiency and autoregressive-like iterative refinement. Signals: efficient inference, iterative refinement, multi-view 3D reconstruction, transformer loop, compute-performance trade-off

Abstract: Recent feed-forward 3D reconstruction transformers have scaled to over a billion parameters, following the broader trend of increasing model capacity in computer vision. Yet emerging evidence suggests that contiguous transformer layers often behave like repeated applications of similar operations, and multi-view reconstruction transformers refine their predictions progressively across decoder depth. We posit that model depth partially buys iteration, paid for inefficiently in unique parameters, and instead make that iteration explicit in architecture. Our model, DéjàView, applies a single looped transformer block recurrently to per-view features for K refinement steps. Trained once, it exposes K as an inference-time compute knob, matching or outperforming substantially larger feed-forward baselines across five reconstruction benchmarks spanning indoor, outdoor, object-centric, and driving scenes, while using a fraction of their parameters and comparable or lower compute. Importantly, the same looped block formulation outperforms an otherwise identical variant with independent per-step parameters under matched training data and compute, suggesting that explicit iteration is not merely a compute-efficient substitute for capacity but a stronger inductive bias for multi-view 3D reconstruction.

</details>

Result: 该方法能够从重建场景中恢复干净的、可仿真的物体表示，支持场景内多个物体同时进行物理一致的交互仿真，在真实场景数据上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: SAM3D-Phys 通过结合场景重建与生成式3D先验，有效解决了真实场景中物体几何不完整的问题，为基于物理的交互仿真提供了可行的解决方案。

Importance reason: Strong on 3D generation and physical simulation, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like autoregressive video generation or diffusion video generation. Signals: 3D generation, physical simulation, scene reconstruction, SAM3D, multi-object interaction

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of recovering complete, simulatable object geometry from reconstructed real-world scenes, enabling physics-based interaction with objects embedded in the scene. While modern multi-view reconstruction methods can produce visually accurate environments, objects are often incomplete due to occlusions and limited observations, making them unsuitable for physics simulation. To address this limitation, we propose SAM3D-Phys, a framework that integrates scene reconstruction with generative 3D priors of SAM3D to recover physically simulatable objects. Our approach first reconstructs the scene from multi-view images to obtain scene geometry and partial observations of objects. We then leverage SAM3D to infer complete object geometry from these partial observations. To ensure that the recovered objects remain consistent with the reconstructed scene, we restore scene-consistent object states through two complementary strategies: a physics-constrained spatial optimization algorithm that iteratively aligns the recovered object to its original location, and a mask-guided appearance distillation module that refines texture fidelity based on the observed images. By recovering complete object geometry and restoring its pose and appearance within the scene, SAM3D-Phys produces clean object representations suitable for physics-based simulation, enabling simultaneous and physically consistent interactive simulation of multiple objects within a reconstructed scene. *Alex Lazarovich, Ofir Itzhak Shahar, Gur Elkin, Ohad Ben-Shahar*

Main category: cs.LG

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高密度脑电图（HD-EEG）能精细测量皮层活动，但硬件昂贵、准备时间长，限制了临床和研究应用。现有低密度EEG空间超分辨率方法性能有限，且缺乏可解释性。因此需要一种能从稀疏电极信号重建高密度EEG的可微、可解释框架。

Method: EMAG 将脑电信号源建模为各向异性4D（空间+时间）高斯混合模型。在球形脑网格的每个点放置多个高斯分量，每个分量由4×4精度矩阵参数化，实现各向异性空间扩散和时空耦合。通过可微高斯场在电极位置的贡献渲染头皮EEG信号，实现端到端训练，无需显式源定位监督。

Result: 在 Localize-MI、SEED 和 SEED-IV 三个公开EEG基准上，以2倍到8/16倍超分辨率因子进行评估。EMAG 在大多数超分辨率因子下超越了当前最先进的EEG超分辨率方法。

Conclusion: EMAG 通过可微4D高斯混合泼溅实现了有效的EEG空间超分辨率，其显式高斯参数化还提供了脑源配置的可视化和可解释性，为源定位和生物标志物发现等临床与神经科学应用开辟了新途径。

Classification reason: 论文提出 EMAG，用各向异性4D高斯混合模型对脑电信号进行空间超分辨率重建，核心是4D高斯泼溅（4D Gaussian Splatting）的可微渲染框架，与 canonical 的 "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）中 3DGS/4D高斯泼溅技术高度契合。

Importance reason: Novel differentiable 4D Gaussian splatting for EEG super-resolution, but far from user's core generative AI and video/image generation focus. Signals: EEG super-resolution, 4D Gaussian splatting, differentiable rendering, medical signal processing, low clinical relevance per user config

Abstract: High-density electroencephalography (HD-EEG) enables fine-grained measurement of cortical activity but requires expensive hardware and lengthy setup times, limiting its clinical and research accessibility. We propose EMAG (EEG Mixture of Anisotropic Gaussians), a differentiable framework that reconstructs HD-EEG signals from a sparse subset of low-density (LD) electrodes by representing brain electrical sources as a mixture of anisotropic 4D space-time Gaussians. EMAG places a mixture of multiple Gaussians at each point of a spherical brain grid, each parameterized by a full 4 x 4 precision matrix, enabling anisotropic spatial spreads and explicit coupling between spatial and temporal dimensions. The forward model renders scalp EEG via differentiable Gaussian field contributions at electrode locations, enabling end-to-end training without explicit source localization supervision. We evaluate EMAG on three public EEG benchmarks (Localize-MI, SEED, and SEED-IV) at super-resolution factors of 2x through 8/16x. EMAG outperforms the current state-of-the-art EEG super-resolution method at most super-resolution factors on three standard benchmarks (Localize-MI, SEED, SEED-IV). The explicit Gaussian parameterization further enables direct visualization and interpretability of learned brain source configurations, potentially opening avenues for clinical and neuroscientific applications, such as source localization or biomarker discovery.

</details>

TL;DR: City-Mesh3R 提出了一种可扩展的城市级3D网格重建框架，采用分治策略从大规模无序多视图图像集合中直接重建水密表面网格，通过拓扑图像聚类、几何感知相机选择和曲率感知自适应顶点密度重网格化等步骤，生成适用于仿真的高质量3D城市网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市级3D重建方法（基于NeRF、Gaussian Splatting等）难以恢复可直接用于仿真的3D网格，存在几何不完整、表面不规则噪声等问题；而小规模重建方法扩展到大规模城市场景时计算复杂度过高。因此需要一种可扩展的端到端框架来生成高质量、水密的城市级3D网格。

Method: 采用分治策略：(1) 通过拓扑图像聚类、聚类独立稀疏SfM和地图合并重建稀疏城市地图，无需穷举图像特征匹配；(2) 对地图进行空间划分，执行几何感知相机选择；(3) 进行稠密表面重建；(4) 使用曲率感知自适应顶点密度重网格化进行表面细化；(5) 将各分区网格拼接成全局城市网格。整个流程支持分布式处理以扩展到任意大规模场景。

Result: 在城市级重建数据集上的定性和定量实验表明，该方法能生成高保真水密3D网格，具有规则几何结构，捕捉精细表面细节，且由于端到端分布式处理，可扩展到任意大规模场景。

Conclusion: City-Mesh3R 提供了一种可扩展的端到端城市级3D网格重建方案，解决了现有方法在几何完整性和表面质量方面的不足，为城市仿真等下游应用提供了高质量的3D网格输入。

Classification reason: 该论文研究城市级多视图图像到3D网格重建，属于3D场景重建与生成范畴，与 canonical 子主题 "3D 生成与重建" (three_d_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D reconstruction for simulation, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key interests in autoregressive/diffusion video generation or unified multimodal models. Signals: 3D mesh reconstruction, city-scale, simulation-ready, divide-and-conquer, not autoregressive/diffusion

Abstract: City-scale 3D surface reconstruction from multiview images for downstream 3D simulation, poses highly challenging problems due to the scale and complexity of urban scenes. Existing city-scale 3D reconstruction methods based on NeRF, Gaussian Splatting etc. often fail to recover 3D meshes ready for simulation due to incomplete/missing geometry and irregular, noisy surfaces. Scaling existing small-scale 3D reconstruction methods to arbitrarily large urban scenes is highly infeasible due to their computational complexity. We present City-Mesh3R, a scalable framework for reconstructing watertight surface meshes directly from large unordered image collections. Unlike recent methods which use global sparse SfM point-cloud initialization followed by a distributed 3D dense reconstruction of large-scale scenes, our method follows an end-to-end images-to-mesh 3D reconstruction approach using a divide-and-conquer strategy. The sparse city map is reconstructed via topological image clustering, cluster-wise independent sparse SfM and map merging, without need for exhaustive image feature matching. Then this map is partitioned spatially to perform geometry-aware camera selection, followed by dense surface reconstruction and surface refinement using curvature-aware adaptive vertex density remeshing. These partition meshes are then stitched together to produce the global mesh of the city. The proposed end-to-end framework is evaluated on city-scale reconstruction datasets. As demonstrated by our qualitative and quantitative results, our proposed method yields high-fidelity watertight 3D meshes with regular geometry, capturing fine surface details, and is suitable for scaling to arbitrarily large scenes owing to the end-to-end processing in a distributed setting.

</details>


### [16] [Supercharging Thermal Gaussian Splatting with Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30328)
*Manoj Biswanath, Chenxin Cai, Hannah Schieber, Daniel Roth, Benjamin Busam*

TL;DR: 本文提出 Thermal-to-Depth Gaussian Splatting (TDg)，一种仅使用热红外图像和深度估计的单模态3D场景表示方法。该方法在3D高斯泼溅框架中引入深度估计，无需依赖可见光或多模态数据，在RGBT-Scenes和ThermalMix数据集上相比MSMG基线在LPIPS、SSIM、PSNR上分别提升1.12%、0.034%、0.01%，同时训练时间减少55%（约12分47秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景表示方法通常依赖RGB可见光图像，但在低光照、夜间等条件下可见光受限。热红外图像可在无光照条件下工作，但多模态融合（热+可见光）会引入额外复杂性和计算开销。本文旨在仅使用热红外单模态数据，结合深度估计，实现高效且鲁棒的3D场景表示。

Method: 基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）框架，使用热红外图像作为输入，并引入深度估计模块来辅助辐射场学习。具体地，TDg架构仅使用热红外单模态数据，通过深度估计网络获取场景深度信息，将其与热辐射场结合进行3D高斯优化，从而无需依赖可见光或多模态融合。

Result: 在RGBT-Scenes和ThermalMix数据集上，TDg相比MSMG基线在LPIPS上提升1.12%，SSIM提升0.034%，PSNR提升0.01%。训练时间从约23分钟降至约10分钟，减少55%（12分47秒）。

Conclusion: 本文成功证明了仅使用热红外单模态结合深度估计即可有效进行3D场景辐射场重建，在保持或提升渲染质量的同时大幅降低训练时间。该方法在监控、搜救、工业检测等需要热源识别的场景具有应用潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Thermal-to-Depth Gaussian Splatting (TDg)，使用热红外图像和深度估计进行3D场景表示与辐射场渲染，属于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的改进方法，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Thermal Gaussian Splatting with depth estimation is a niche 3D reconstruction method, not aligned with priority subtopics like video/image generation or world models. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, thermal imaging, depth estimation, single-modality, training speed improvement

Abstract: Efficient and robust 3D scene representation is crucial in autonomous driving, robotics, and related fields. While RGB images provide valuable content for 3D reconstruction, other modalities like thermal or depth can enable additional information on the environment. Lately, novel view synthesis methods like 3D Gaussian Splatting have started using multiple modalities to further boost their performance. But fusing or combining multimodal data can make the process slower and can bring in additional challenges. Therefore, our project aims to use single modality based on thermal infrared domain, by removing the reliance on visible light as much as possible. This single modality can be expected to be faster as it does not rely on multimodal data. We propose a method, Thermal-to-Depth Gaussian Splatting (TDg), that uses only thermal images and depth estimation in its architecture to derive the radiance fields. Our TDg method outperforms the MSMG (Multiple Single-Modal Gaussians) baseline in most cases on our test datasets, RGBT-Scenes and ThermalMix. On average, the rendering quality metrics such as learned perceptual image patch similarity (LPIPS), structural similarity index measure (SSIM), and peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) of TDg are 1.12%, 0.034%, and 0.01% better than the baseline MSMG values. It also reduces the training time significantly, by 12 mins 47 secs (55% improvement). Overall, our method is successful in deriving these thermal radiance fields, which can ultimately have several applications, such as identifying heat sources critical in surveillance, search or rescue operations, and industrial inspections where temperature is widely used to monitor machines.

</details>


### [17] [REST3D: Reconstructing Physically Stable 3D Scenes from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30338)
*Xiaoxuan Ma, Jiashun Wang, Nicolas Ugrinovic, Yehonathan Litman, Kris Kitani*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: REST3D提出一种从单张RGB图像重建物理稳定3D场景的框架，通过智能体物理场景理解构建场景树表示，结合图像到3D模型初始化与物理约束优化，显著减少物体漂浮和穿透等物理不一致问题，支持VR人机交互等沉浸式应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像3D重建方法能生成几何合理的场景，但缺乏物理结构理解，常出现物体漂浮、穿透等物理不一致问题，导致物理模拟不稳定。而图像条件场景生成方法虽改善了物理合理性，但依赖强场景先验，生成结果与输入图像不一致。因此需要一种既能保持视觉一致性又能保证物理稳定性的重建方法。

Method: REST3D包含三个核心步骤：(1) 智能体物理场景理解——利用大语言模型智能体从单张图像中构建场景树表示，从重力-支撑角度捕捉物体物理状态和物体间关系；(2) 场景初始化——使用图像到3D模型生成每个物体的3D几何，并通过场景树引导的对齐进行初始摆放；(3) 物理约束优化——通过物理约束优化解决物体漂浮、穿透等物理违规，同时保持与输入图像的视觉一致性。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，REST3D显著减少了物理错误（物体漂浮、穿透等），提高了物理模拟稳定性，同时保持了良好的重建质量。论文还展示了在VR人机交互中的应用，证明了其在沉浸式应用中的潜力。

Conclusion: REST3D通过将物理场景理解与物理约束优化相结合，有效解决了单图像3D场景重建中的物理不一致问题，为将日常图像转化为可用于物理模拟的数字资产提供了可行方案，在沉浸式交互和内容创作领域具有应用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文从单张RGB图像重建物理稳定的3D场景，属于3D场景生成与重建范畴，与[多模态生成]方向下的[3D 生成与重建]（CANONICAL）高度匹配。同时该方法涉及内容创作应用，也匹配[AIGC]方向。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with multimodal generation and 3D reconstruction, but lacks direct connection to top-priority autoregressive/diffusion video generation or world models. Signals: 3D scene reconstruction, physical stability, single image to 3D, scene tree representation, physics-constrained optimization

Abstract: Reconstructing physically stable 3D scenes from a single RGB image enables casual images to be converted into simulation-ready digital assets for applications such as immersive interaction and content creation. However, existing single-image reconstruction methods fall short in capturing the physical structure of a scene. As a result, they often produce geometrically plausible but physically inconsistent results, including object floating and penetration, which lead to unstable behavior in physics simulations. Image-conditioned scene generation methods improve physical plausibility but often rely on strong scene priors, yielding plausible yet inaccurate object arrangements that fail to match the input image. We propose REST3D, a single-image reconstruction framework that can reconstruct physically stable 3D scenes by integrating physical scene understanding with physics-constrained refinement. We first introduce an agentic physical scene understanding technique that constructs a scene-tree representation capturing object physical states and inter-object relationships from a gravity-support perspective, providing a structural prior for reconstruction. Leveraging this structure, we initialize the scene using image-to-3D models, followed by scene-tree-guided alignment and physics-constrained optimization to resolve physical violations while preserving visual consistency with the input image. Experiments show that our method significantly reduces physical errors and improves simulation stability on both synthetic and real-world datasets while maintaining strong reconstruction quality. We further demonstrate the reconstructed scenes in VR-based human-object interaction, showing their potential for immersive applications.

</details>


### [18] [Uncertainty-driven 3D Gaussian Splatting Active Mapping via Anisotropic Visibility Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30342)
*Shangjie Xue, Jesse Dill, Dhruv Ahuja, Frank Dellaert, Panagiotis Tsiotras, Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (62.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出GAVIS框架，通过各向异性可见性场（Anisotropic Visibility Field）对3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）进行不确定性量化，并基于最大信息增益实现主动建图，在多种环境中显著优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在训练视角未见区域会产生不可靠的预测，现有方法缺乏高效的不确定性量化手段来指导主动建图（active mapping），因此需要一种既能实时量化不确定性又能指导下一最佳视角选择的框架。

Method: 提出各向异性可见性场（Anisotropic Visibility Field），用球谐函数表示每个高斯粒子相对于训练视角的可见性；将该可见性场集成到基于贝叶斯网络的不确定性感知3DGS光栅化器中，实现实时（200 FPS）不确定性量化；在此基础上构建最大信息增益框架进行主动建图。

Result: 在多种不同环境下的实验中，GAVIS在准确性和效率上均一致且显著优于先前方法；此外，该方法可作为后处理模块提升现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: GAVIS为3DGS提供了一种高效、原理性的不确定性量化与主动建图方案，在实时性和精度上均取得突破，且具有良好的通用性和可扩展性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的不确定性量化与主动建图框架，核心属于3D场景表示与重建，与[多模态生成]方向下的[3D 生成与重建]（three_d_generation）规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong 3DGS uncertainty and active mapping work, but not directly aligned with top priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, active mapping, uncertainty quantification, anisotropic visibility field, real-time 200 FPS

Abstract: We present Gaussian Splatting Anisotropic Visibility Field (GAVIS), a novel framework for uncertainty quantification and active mapping in 3DGS. Our key insight is that regions unseen from the training views yield unreliable predictions from the 3DGS. To address this, we introduce a principled and efficient method for quantifying the visibility field in 3DGS, defined as the anisotropic visibility of each particle with respect to the training views, and represented using spherical harmonics. The resulting visibility field is integrated into a Bayesian Network-based uncertainty-aware 3DGS rasterizer, enabling real-time (200 FPS) uncertainty quantification for synthesized views. Active mapping is further performed within a maximum information gain framework building on this formulation. Extensive experiments across diverse environments demonstrate that GAVIS consistently and significantly outperforms prior approaches in both accuracy and efficiency. Moreover, beyond standalone use, our method can be applied post-hoc to improve the performance of existing approaches.

</details>

*Omer Benishu, Gal Fiebelman, Sagie Benaim*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

TL;DR: PhyGenHOI提出一个物理感知的4D人-物交互生成框架，将运动扩散模型（MDM）驱动的人体语义智能体与物质点法（MPM）模拟的物理物体智能体通过3D高斯统一表示耦合，结合窗口吸引损失、接触驱动重模拟和掩码视频SDS目标，生成物理一致且视觉逼真的动态人-物交互场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D人-物交互生成方法通常缺乏物理一致性，导致生成的动作与物体交互时出现穿透、不自然的运动或物理上不可能的接触。作者旨在解决生成物理准确且视觉真实的动态人-物交互这一挑战。

Method: PhyGenHOI将人体建模为运动扩散模型（MDM）驱动的语义智能体，将物体建模为物质点法（MPM）模拟的物理智能体，两者均使用3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）作为统一可微表示。通过三种耦合机制监督交互：(1) 窗口吸引损失（Windowed Attraction Loss）使生成运动在时间上同步以拦截物体；(2) 接触驱动重模拟（Contact-Driven Re-simulation）在碰撞时触发物理一致的动量传递；(3) 掩码视频SDS目标（Masked Video-SDS）注入视频先验以增强接触保真度。

Result: 实验表明PhyGenHOI能够在多种动作、人体和物体上生成物理一致的4D人-物交互，在物理合理性和视觉质量上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: PhyGenHOI通过将生成式人体运动与显式物理物体模拟相结合，有效解决了4D人-物交互生成中的物理一致性问题，为动态场景生成提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究动态人-物交互的4D生成，涉及3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）和动态场景合成，与[multimodal_generation]方向下的[动态_4d_generation]（4D与动态场景生成）高度匹配，该子主题为CANONICAL条目，直接复用。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with multimodal generation and video world model priorities via physically-aware 4D human-object interaction generation. Signals: multimodal_generation, 4D dynamic scene generation, physical simulation, video diffusion, world model

Abstract: We address the task of generating physically accurate and visually faithful 4D Human-Object Interaction (HOI). Given a static 3D human and target object represented as 3D Gaussian Splats (3DGS), our goal is to synthesize dynamic scenes where the human actively engages with the object through actions, such as punching or kicking, in accordance with a given input text. To this end, we introduce PhyGenHOI, a novel framework that couples generative human motion with an explicit physical object simulation. We model the human as a semantic agent driven by a Motion Diffusion Model (MDM) and the object as a physical agent simulated via the Material Point Method (MPM), utilizing 3D Gaussians as a unified, differentiable representation. We supervise their interaction through three coupled mechanisms: (1) A Windowed Attraction Loss that temporally synchronizes generative motion to intercept the object; (2) A Contact-Driven Re-simulation step that triggers physically consistent momentum transfer upon impact; and (3) A Masked Video-SDS objective that injects video-based priors to enhance contact fidelity. Experiments show PhyGenHOI generates physically consistent 4D HOI across diverse actions, humans, and objects, outperforming baselines. Project page and videos:

</details>


### [20] [NeuROK: Generative 4D Neural Object Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30347)
*Chen Geng, Guangzhao He, Yue Gao, Yunzhi Zhang, Shangzhe Wu, Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.GR, cs.CV

TL;DR: NeuROK提出了一种数据驱动的4D神经运动学参数化方法，通过学习物体状态的潜在空间和形变解码器，实现从静态物体到物理条件下动态形变的生成式模拟，在多种动态物体类型上优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D动态生成方法大多依赖预定义的物理模型和系统辨识来估计参数，限制了方法的类别泛化能力和数据规模。本文希望克服这些限制，提出一种数据驱动的运动学状态参数化方法，实现通用、可扩展的4D动态生成。

Method: 提出Neural Object Kinematics (NeuROK)框架：1) 学习一个潜在空间表示物体的所有可能状态；2) 学习一个解码器将任意采样的潜在向量映射为合理的形变形状。采用基于Transformer的编码器-解码器架构，在大规模4D数据集上训练。从拉格朗日力学视角出发，将动力学建模简化为低维潜在空间中的动态演化。

Result: 在多种动态物体类型上展示了方法的有效性和泛化能力，相比先前方法具有明显优势，能够生成逼真的4D动态形变。

Conclusion: NeuROK通过数据驱动的神经运动学参数化，成功实现了通用、可扩展的4D动态物体生成，为构建全面的3D世界模型提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究4D动态物体生成（静态物体在物理条件下的时变形变），属于多模态生成方向下的[4D 与动态场景生成]规范子主题，该子主题明确涵盖动态3D/4D场景生成。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and video generation priorities via 4D dynamic simulation, despite not being a direct video generation method. Signals: 4D dynamics, world model, neural simulation, generative kinematics, data-driven physics

Abstract: Data-driven approaches have revolutionized 3D vision, enabling transformers to effectively reconstruct and generate static 3D objects. However, generating simulative 4D dynamics -- realistic temporal deformations of static objects under various physical conditions -- remains challenging and often ad hoc, despite its importance in building comprehensive 3D world models. Most existing methods assume a predefined physical model and use system identification to estimate parameters, restricting these methods to specific categories and small-scale datasets. We propose that these restrictions can be overcome by learning a data-driven kinematic state parameterization for object-centric physical systems. Specifically, we learn both a latent space representing all possible states of the object and a decoder that maps any sampled latent to a plausibly deformed shape of the object. We refer to this parameterization as Neural Object Kinematics (NeuROK), and learn a transformer-based encoder-decoder model on a curated large-scale 4D dataset. This formulation and the learned model significantly simplify the generation of simulative dynamics since we only need to consider the dynamics within a low-dimensional latent space from the Lagrangian mechanics' perspective in classical physics. We demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of this neural simulation framework across diverse dynamic object types, showing clear advantages over prior works. Project page:

</details>

*Yihang Tao, Yu Guo, Senkang Hu, Yanan Ma, Zihan Fang, Sam Kwong, Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: V2XCrafter是首个面向多智能体协同驾驶场景的可控街景生成框架。它基于单智能体扩散模型，通过渐进式多智能体扩散和跨智能体注意力模块，生成跨车辆视角一致的高保真街景图像，有效提升下游协同3D目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同驾驶系统依赖V2X通信进行多智能体协同感知以提升驾驶安全性，但真实世界V2X驾驶数据集标注稀缺且泛化能力有限。现有图像生成方法仅适用于单车辆多视角场景，在多智能体驾驶场景中面临两个挑战：(1) 学习目标扩展导致生成质量下降；(2) 智能体间高度动态变化使得联合观测物体的物理属性（如颜色、类别）难以保持一致性。

Method: 提出V2XCrafter框架：(1) 渐进式多智能体扩散模型——基于单智能体扩散骨干，利用相邻智能体的潜在状态作为参考信号，逐步引导从单智能体到多智能体的扩散过程；(2) 跨智能体注意力模块——利用协作视图图（collaboration view graph）和可学习的联合观测物体表征，建模动态的跨智能体相机视角关系，从而解决跨车辆不一致性问题。

Result: 实验表明V2XCrafter能够生成高保真、可控且跨智能体一致的街景图像，有效增强下游协同3D目标检测任务的性能。

Conclusion: V2XCrafter是首个面向多智能体协同驾驶场景的可控生成框架，通过渐进式扩散和跨智能体注意力机制解决了多视角一致性问题，为V2X驾驶场景的数据增强提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出V2XCrafter，用于生成多智能体协同驾驶场景的街景图像，核心目的是为协同感知任务生成合成训练数据以进行数据增强。这与multimodal_generation方向下的aigc_synthetic_data（合成数据与数据集蒸馏）子主题高度匹配，该子主题涵盖合成训练数据生成与数据增强。

Importance reason: Novel multi-agent controllable generation for driving scenes, but outside core priority subtopics and lacks strong boost keywords. Signals: multi-agent diffusion, controllable street view generation, cross-agent consistency, collaborative perception data augmentation, single-to-multi diffusion

Abstract: Collaborative driving systems leverage vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication for multi-agent collaborative perception to enhance driving safety, yet they remain constrained by scarce annotated real-world V2X driving datasets and limited generalization across diverse driving conditions. While image generation technology offers a feasible solution for data augmentation, existing methods tailored for single-vehicle multi-view scenarios face two fundamental challenges in multi-agent driving settings: (1) the expansion of the learning objective degrades generation quality, and (2) the highly dynamic variations across agents hinder the modeling of consistency for physical attributes (e.g., color, category) in jointly observed objects. To bridge this gap, we propose V2XCrafter, the first framework for generating controllable and realistic collaborative driving scene across agents' camera views. For effective learning, we develop a progressive multi-agent diffusion model based on a single-agent backbone, using neighboring agents' latent states as reference signals to progressively guide the single-to-multi diffusion. To address cross-vehicle inconsistency, we propose a cross-agent attention module that leverages a collaboration view graph and learnable jointly observed object representation to model the dynamic cross-agent camera view relationships. Experiments have shown that V2XCrafter can generate high-fidelity and controllable street views with consistency across agents, thereby effectively enhancing the downstream collaborative 3D object detection tasks.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: GiPL 包含两个分支：(1) 迭代伪标签自训练分支：在支持集上进行零样本推理生成可靠伪标注，与真实标签融合后迭代优化模型；(2) 生成式数据增强分支：利用大视觉语言模型合成领域对齐、包含多目标标注的图像，丰富训练样本并抑制过拟合。

Result: 在 RUOD、CARPK、CarDD 三个跨域小样本目标检测数据集上，在 1/5/10-shot 设置下，GiPL 一致性地显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: GiPL 通过迭代伪标签自训练和生成式数据增强两个互补策略，有效解决了跨域小样本目标检测中支持集利用不足和过拟合问题，验证了利用大视觉语言模型进行数据增强在小样本场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language foundation models have shown promising zero-shot generalization for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Object Detection (CD-FSOD). However, they face two critical challenges in fine-tuning: insufficient support set utilization due to sparse single-instance annotations, and severe overfitting under extremely limited target-domain samples. To address these issues, this paper proposes GiPL, an efficient two-branch training the first branch, we design an iterative pseudo-label self-training paradigm, which performs zero-shot inference on the support set to generate reliable pseudo-annotations, fuses them with ground-truth labels, and iteratively optimizes the model to fully exploit support set data. In the second branch, we introduce generative data augmentation pipeline using large vision-language models, which synthesizes domain-aligned, multi-object annotated images to enrich training samples and suppress overfitting. Extensive experiments on three challenging CD-FSOD datasets (RUOD, CARPK, CarDD) under 1/5/10-shot settings demonstrate that GiPL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods with significant performance is available at \href{ }{CDiscover}.

*Thenukan Pathmanathan, Kanchan Keisham, Thangarajah Akilan*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 跨模态知识蒸馏能够有效利用2D图像的纹理信息增强3D点云分割，在标注稀缺场景下具有实用价值，为数据高效的3D场景感知提供了可行方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出跨模态知识蒸馏框架，利用2D图像特征增强3D点云分割，属于利用多模态信息进行数据高效的3D场景感知。这与多模态生成方向下的"合成数据与数据集蒸馏"（aigc_synthetic_data）子主题相关，因为其核心是通过知识蒸馏（一种数据增强/合成标注的方式）来提升3D感知性能，且该子主题已在multimodal_generation方向下存在。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D LiDAR segmentation, not aligned with user's priority on video/image generation or efficient multimodal models. Signals: cross-modal knowledge distillation, 3D point cloud segmentation, LiDAR-only baseline, SemanticKITTI, 2% mIoU improvement

Abstract: Point cloud segmentation is a fundamental task in 3D scene understanding. Its progress is constrained by the high cost and time required for dense 3D annotations, making labeled samples difficult to obtain. Beyond annotation scarcity, different sensing modalities face inherent limitations. 2D images provide rich texture and appearance cues, yet they lack explicit depth and geometric structure. In contrast, 3D point clouds capture accurate spatial geometry but are sparse and contain no texture information. As a result, relying on a single modality restricts the richness of learned representations and weakens generalization. Although recent multi-modal methods that combine 3D point clouds with 2D images have demonstrated strong performance in tasks such as classification and retrieval, they typically depend on large-scale labeled datasets and have not been fully exploited for data-efficient dense prediction. To address these limitations, we propose a novel cross-modal knowledge distillation framework, xModel-KD, for 3D point cloud segmentation. Our method exploits the complementary strengths of 2D texture and 3D geometry by learning unified per-point representations through cross-modal alignment. Specifically, we design a cross-modal fusion encoder trained with a contrastive objective that enforces feature consistency between corresponding 2D and 3D representations across multiple views. By integrating powerful pre-trained backbones with a targeted fusion strategy, the proposed framework effectively transfers appearance cues from images to geometry-aware point features. Experimental results show that cross-modal fusion achieves a 2% absolute improvement in mIoU over a LiDAR-only baseline, demonstrating the benefit of leveraging complementary multi-modal information for scalable and annotation-efficient 3D scene understanding.

</details>

*Mingshu Cai, Miao Zhang, Chenghe Yang, Yixuan Li, Osamu Yoshie, Yuya Ieiri*

TL;DR: KGEdit提出了一种基于歧义感知知识图谱（AAKG）的无训练视频生成与编辑框架，通过结构化语义注入模块（SSIM）和时序感知语义控制（TASC）模块，在扩散Transformer中实现细粒度语义控制，有效解决了复杂文本指令下的语义歧义、概念绑定错误和跨帧不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练视频生成方法在处理复杂文本指令时存在语义歧义、概念绑定错误和跨帧不一致等问题，亟需一种结构化语义控制方法来实现精确的视频生成与编辑。

Method: 1) 构建歧义感知知识图谱（AAKG），将输入提示分解为身份、关系、属性和负约束四种结构化语义；2) 设计结构化语义注入模块（SSIM），将语义信号注入扩散Transformer的关键层；3) 引入时序感知语义控制（TASC）模块，根据去噪过程的阶段特性动态调度语义目标。

Result: 实验表明KGEdit在编辑精度和时间稳定性上优于现有方法，在文本驱动交互场景中具有更高的效率和可控性。

Conclusion: KGEdit通过结构化语义控制有效提升了视频生成与编辑的语义对齐和时序一致性，为无训练视频生成提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出KGEdit框架，用于文本到视频扩散模型的精确视频生成与编辑，核心是解决语义歧义和跨帧不一致问题，属于视频编辑与可控生成范畴。因此映射到multimodal_generation方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing"（图像与视频编辑）。

Importance reason: Directly addresses controllable video generation and editing with a novel knowledge graph approach, aligning with high-priority video generation and diffusion transformer topics. Signals: training-free video generation, knowledge graph for semantic control, diffusion transformer, temporal consistency, controllable generation

Abstract: In recent years, training-free video generation has progressed remarkably. However, when handling complex textual instructions, existing methods still suffer from semantic ambiguity, incorrect concept binding, and cross-frame inconsistency. To address these issues, we propose KGEdit, a structured semantic control framework for text-to-video (T2V) diffusion models. Specifically, we first construct an ambiguity-aware knowledge graph (AAKG) to disentangle and disambiguate the input prompt, converting it into four types of structured semantics: identity, relation, attribute, and negative constraints. We then design a structured semantic injection module (SSIM) to inject these semantic signals into key layers of the diffusion Transformer, enabling fine-grained semantic control. In addition, we introduce a temporal-aware semantic control (TASC) module that dynamically schedules semantic objectives according to the stage-wise characteristics of the denoising process, further improving semantic alignment and temporal consistency. Experiments show that KGEdit outperforms existing methods in editing precision and temporal stability, while offering higher efficiency and controllability in text-driven interaction scenarios.

</details>

*Kecheng Han, Yuchen Zhang, Bingqing Liu, Boqiang Guo, Wenbin Zheng, Shiyuan Pei*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频运动放大方法在复杂几何变换下存在结构不一致问题，且 CNN 全局上下文有限、Transformer 计算成本高；同时现有训练数据以简单线性运动为主，缺乏真实世界中的几何与成像复杂性。

Method: 提出 GeoMag 框架，基于状态空间模型（SSM）实现全局一致的运动放大，具有线性计算复杂度；同时构建 Geo-200K 大规模合成数据集，引入丰富的几何变换和传感器级真实退化，提升训练信号的多样性和真实性。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准上，GeoMag 在视觉保真度和计算效率上持续优于先前方法，产生更少的伪影和更好的结构一致性。

Conclusion: GeoMag 通过 SSM 的全局建模能力和大规模几何感知数据集，有效解决了视频运动放大中的结构一致性和计算效率问题，为视频运动放大提供了新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频运动放大（Video Motion Magnification），属于视频编辑与增强范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）" 高度相关。

Importance reason: Novel SSM-based video motion magnification with geometric awareness, but niche editing task not in top priority subtopics. Signals: state space model, video motion magnification, geometric-aware, synthetic dataset Geo-200K, linear complexity global modeling

Abstract: Video Motion Magnification (VMM) reveals imperceptible dynamics but often suffers from structural inconsistencies under complex geometric transformations. Existing learning-based methods generally face a trade-off between the limited global context of CNNs and the high computational cost of Transformers. In addition, current training protocols, largely dominated by simple linear motion, fail to capture the geometric and imaging complexities encountered in real-world videos. To address these issues, we propose GeoMag, a geometric-aware VMM framework built upon State Space Models to achieve globally consistent motion amplification with linear complexity. We further construct Geo-200K, a large-scale synthetic dataset that introduces rich geometric transformations together with sensor-realistic degradations, improving the diversity and realism of training signals. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that GeoMag consistently outperforms prior methods in visual fidelity and computational efficiency, while producing fewer artifacts and better structural consistency.

</details>


### [26] [GenEraser: Generalizable Video Object Removal via Balanced Text-Mask Guidance and Decoupled Locator-Preserver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30045)
*Yuqing Chen, Lin Liu, Haisu Wu, Xiaopeng Zhang, Yaowei Wang, Yujiu Yang, Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 值得关注 (65.78) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GenEraser 提出了一种基于扩散 Transformer 的通用视频物体与效果移除框架，通过多条件混合专家（MC-MoE）、可学习深度 CFG 融合（LD-CFG）和解耦定位器-保持器架构，解决了现有方法在跨域场景中难以同时移除目标物体及其物理效果（如烟雾、反射、涟漪）的问题，在多个基准上取得 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频物体移除方法主要依赖空间掩码，难以捕获与目标物体弱相关的物理效果（如烟雾、反射、光晕、涟漪），且显式文本引导的潜力未被充分挖掘。此外，移除模型在高层次语义泛化与精确像素级背景保持之间存在固有的优化冲突。

Method: GenEraser 包含三个核心设计：(1) 多条件混合专家（MC-MoE）配合二分图文本引导，充分利用扩散 Transformer 的多模态先验增强复杂效果识别；(2) 可学习深度 CFG 融合机制（LD-CFG），自适应平衡掩码和文本条件在不同场景下的主导权重；(3) 解耦专家架构，包含定位器（Locator）和保持器（Preserver），分别负责语义泛化和像素对齐，缓解两者之间的权衡。

Result: 在 ROSE Benchmark 和 VOR-Eval 上分别提升 2.16 dB 和 1.44 dB，在开放世界场景中展现出优异的泛化鲁棒性，超越现有 SOTA 方法。

Conclusion: GenEraser 通过多条件引导和解耦架构有效解决了视频物体移除中的跨域泛化和物理效果移除难题，验证了文本引导在视频编辑任务中的潜力，为通用视频编辑提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频物体移除（video object removal），属于视频编辑任务，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）" 高度匹配。同时涉及 AIGC 方向下的视频内容编辑应用。

Importance reason: Strong technical novelty in video object removal with diffusion transformers, but subtopic is not a priority and lacks direct alignment with user's key topics. Signals: Diffusion Transformer, video object removal, multimodal guidance, decoupled architecture, SOTA performance

Abstract: Video object removal frequently struggles to simultaneously eliminate target objects and their associated physical effects (e.g., smoke, reflections, light, and ripples) in out-of-domain scenarios due to complex spatiotemporal ambiguities. While existing methods primarily rely on spatial masks, they often fail to capture weakly correlated effects, and the potential of explicit textual guidance remains underexplored. Furthermore, a fundamental optimization conflict exists in removal models between high-level semantic generalization and precise pixel-level background preservation. To address these challenges, we propose GenEraser, a novel framework for generalized and high-fidelity video object and effect removal. First, we introduce a Multi-Conditional Mixture-of-Experts (MC-MoE) paired with Bipartite Text guidance to fully exploit the multimodal priors of Diffusion Transformers, significantly enhancing the identification of complex effects. Second, a Learnable Deep ``CFG'' Fusion mechanism (LD-CFG) is developed to adaptively balance the relative dominance of mask and textual conditions across diverse scenarios. Finally, we propose a Decoupled Expert Architecture, comprising a Locator and a Preserver, to mitigate the inherent trade-off between semantic generalization and pixel alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our GenEraser surpasses recent state-of-the-art approaches, achieving significant quantitative improvements (e.g., $2.16$ dB and $1.44$ dB on the ROSE Benchmark and VOR-Eval, respectively) while maintaining exceptionally robust generalization in open-world scenarios.

</details>


### [27] [LiveSVG: Zero-Shot SVG Animation via Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30174)
*Matan Levy, Ran Margolin, Bar Cavia, Dvir Samuel, Yael Pritch, Shmuel Peleg, Alex Rav Acha, Ariel Shamir, Dani Lischinski*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

TL;DR: LiveSVG 提出了一种零样本 SVG 动画方法，利用冻结的图像到视频扩散模型生成目标视频，再通过可微渲染将原始 SVG 拟合到视频帧上，实现复杂、非刚性的矢量动画，无需骨架先验或类别特定知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SVG 动画方法在处理复杂运动时存在局限：基于 LLM 的代码合成难以表达精细的非刚性贝塞尔变形，而 SDS 方法提供噪声梯度且常需类别特定的骨架先验。作者希望提出一种无需骨架、无需类别先验的零样本 SVG 动画方法。

Method: 给定输入 SVG 图像和运动提示，使用冻结的图像到视频扩散模型生成可预览的目标视频；然后通过可微渲染将原始 SVG 拟合到该视频的每一帧。拟合阶段采用双层运动表示：每组的单应性矩阵用于粗粒度刚体运动，每条路径的贝塞尔控制点偏移用于局部变形。为解决颜色引起的对应歧义，提出球体填充重着色策略。

Result: 在 AniClipart 和 ChallengeSVG 基准上，LiveSVG 显著优于现有方法，能够生成更复杂、更自然的 SVG 动画，且保持矢量图形的可编辑性。

Conclusion: 直接参考视频拟合是一种实用且鲁棒的 SVG 动画路径，能够实现与提示对齐且完全可编辑的矢量动画，无需骨架先验或类别特定知识。

Classification reason: 该论文利用视频扩散模型生成目标视频，再通过可微渲染将SVG图像拟合到视频帧上实现动画，属于图像/视频编辑与可控编辑范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel zero-shot SVG animation via video diffusion, but tangential to user's core priorities in autoregressive/diffusion video generation and world models. Signals: zero-shot SVG animation, video diffusion model, differentiable rendering, non-rigid motion, no skeleton prior

Abstract: We introduce LiveSVG, a zero-shot approach for generating Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) animations using video diffusion models. Current SVG animation methods struggle with complex motions: LLM-based code synthesis fails to express fine, non-rigid Bézier deformations, while Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) provides noisy gradients and often requires category-specific priors like skeletons. In contrast, LiveSVG fits vector geometry directly to an explicitly generated target video. Given an input SVG image and a motion prompt, we generate a previewable target video using a frozen image-to-video model, then fit the original SVG to this video via differentiable rendering. Our fitting stage is skeleton-free, utilizing a dual-level motion representation that combines per-group homographies for coarse articulation with per-path Bézier control-point offsets for local deformations. To resolve color-induced correspondence ambiguities during pixel-wise fitting, we introduce a novel sphere-packing recolorization strategy. We also present ChallengeSVG, a benchmark of complex, multi-object scenes that exposes the limitations of prior work. Evaluations demonstrate that LiveSVG significantly outperforms existing methods on both AniClipart and ChallengeSVG, establishing direct reference-video fitting as a practical, robust route to prompt-aligned and fully editable vector animation.

</details>


### [28] [Stable-Layers: Fine-Tuning Image Layer Decomposition Models with VLM-Scored Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30257)
*Ciara Rowles, Reshinth Adithyan, Nikhil Pinnaparaju, Vikram Voleti, Mark Boss*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (57.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Stable-Layers 提出一种无需配对监督的强化学习框架，利用 VLM 评分反馈微调预训练的图层分解模型，通过 Flow-GRPO 和两阶段评估管道（逐样本评分+网格校准）解决奖励信号方差不足的问题，在 Crello 数据集上实现了更好的图层分离效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图层分解模型通常需要配对数据（如前景/背景分离的标注）进行监督训练，获取这类数据成本高昂且难以扩展。作者希望利用预训练 VLM 的视觉理解能力作为奖励信号，通过强化学习微调模型，从而摆脱对配对标注的依赖。

Method: 基于 Qwen-Image-Layered 预训练模型，采用 Flow-GRPO 算法结合 LoRA 适配进行微调。对每张输入图像采样多个候选分解结果，使用 VLM 进行评分。为解决 VLM 独立评分时判别力不足的问题，设计了两阶段评估管道：(1) 逐样本评分阶段，VLM 按五个编辑相关标准（如图层分离度、伪影等）对每个候选独立打分；(2) 网格校准阶段，VLM 对所有候选进行并排对比重新评分，以增强组内方差，为 GRPO 提供有效的相对优势信号。

Result: 在 Crello 数据集上，Stable-Layers 相比基线模型（Qwen-Image-Layered）实现了更强的图层分离、更少的空白或伪影图层，以及更低的逐图层重建误差。

Conclusion: 本文证明了利用 VLM 作为奖励信号进行强化学习可以有效微调图层分解模型，无需配对监督数据。两阶段评分策略解决了 VLM 评分方差不足的问题，为无监督图像编辑模型的训练提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究图像层分解（layer decomposition），属于图像编辑与可控编辑的范畴，与 canonical 子主题 "visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel RL fine-tuning for layer decomposition, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like autoregressive video generation or world models. Signals: VLM-scored reinforcement learning, layer decomposition, Flow-GRPO, no paired supervision, Crello dataset

Abstract: We present Stable-Layers, a reinforcement learning framework that eliminates the need for paired supervision by fine-tuning a pretrained layer decomposition model using only feedback from a vision-language model (VLM). Starting from Qwen-Image-Layered, we apply Flow-GRPO with LoRA adaptation, sampling multiple candidate decompositions per image, scoring them with a VLM, and optimising the policy from group-relative advantages. The key challenge lies in designing a reliable reward signal: VLMs scoring samples in isolation tend to compress their judgements into a narrow band, leaving GRPO with little within-group variance to learn from. We address this with a two-stage evaluation pipeline that pairs structured per-sample scoring across five edit-centric criteria with a grid-based calibration step in which the VLM re-scores all candidates side-by-side. Stable-Layers produces decompositions with stronger layer separation, fewer blank or artifact-heavy layers, and lower per-layer reconstruction error on the Crello dataset compared to the base model.

</details>

### [29] [UniNote: A Unified Embedding Model for Multimodal Representation and Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29287)
*Jinghan Zhao, Wenwei Jin, Anqi Li, Jintao Tong, Luya Mo, Jiawei Li, Bin Li, Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (57.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UniNote 是一个面向工业级 Item-to-Item 检索的统一多模态嵌入模型，通过两阶段训练（对比 SFT + RL 对齐）和 Matryoshka 表示学习，在内容平台（小红书）实现了检索质量与成本效率的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态嵌入方法在 I2I 检索场景中存在三个关键问题：全局内容表示与细粒度局部检索难以平衡、解耦的嵌入-排序流水线系统效率低下、模型精度与服务延迟之间存在固有权衡。亟需一个统一的、端到端的工业级 I2I 检索模型。

Method: UniNote 提出定制化的检索策略以支持不同粒度下的多模态内容表示学习，并采用两阶段训练范式：第一阶段通过对比 SFT 建立稳健的基础嵌入，第二阶段通过强化学习（RL）对齐内容相关性以优化排序质量。同时集成 Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) 实现灵活的嵌入维度压缩。

Result: UniNote 在多种 I2I 任务上达到 SOTA 性能。在小红书大规模部署后，检索质量和成本效率均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: UniNote 验证了统一嵌入模型结合两阶段训练（对比学习+RL对齐）在工业级多模态 I2I 检索中的有效性，为内容平台的推荐、审核等核心流程提供了高效且可部署的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出统一多模态嵌入模型用于工业级 Item-to-Item 检索，属于多模态检索方向。multimodal_generation 和 multimodal_understanding_generation 两个方向下均有现成的 "多模态检索" 非 canonical 子主题，直接复用。

Importance reason: Strong industrial multimodal retrieval work but not aligned with priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: multimodal embedding, I2I retrieval, two-stage training, RL alignment, Matryoshka representation

Abstract: Item-to-Item (I2I) retrieval is a fundamental part of modern content platforms, supporting critical industrial workflows from recommendation engines to content auditing. While multimodal embedding methods have advanced general retrieval, they often falter in I2I scenarios due to the challenges of balancing global content representation with fine-grained local retrieval, the systemic inefficiency of decoupled embedding-and-ranking pipelines, and the inherent trade-offs between model precision and serving latency. To solve these issues, we propose \textbf{UniNote}, a unified embedding model designed for industrial I2I retrieval. Tailored retrieval strategies are introduced to support representation learning over complex, multimodal content at varying granularities. To operationalize these strategies, UniNote employs a two-stage training paradigm: the first stage leverages contrastive SFT to establish robust base embeddings, while the second stage refines ranking quality through a reinforcement learning (RL) process that aligns the model with content relevance. Our results show that UniNote achieves SOTA performance across diverse I2I tasks. Deployed at Xiaohongshu and integrated with Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL), UniNote achieved significant improvements in retrieval quality and cost efficiency in large-scale applications.

</details>


### [30] [No More K-means:Single-Stage Sparse Coding for Efficient Multi-Vector Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30120)
*Lixuan Guo, Yifei Wang, Tiansheng Wen, Aosong Feng, Stefanie Jegelka, Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.IR, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 值得关注 (68.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SSR（Single-stage Sparse Retrieval），用稀疏自编码器（SAE）替代 K-means 聚类，将 token 嵌入投影到高维稀疏表示，从而绕过向量聚类直接使用倒排索引进行高效多向量检索。在 BEIR 基准上，SSR 相比 ColBERTv2 将索引时间减少 15 倍，检索延迟减半，同时提升了检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MVR 模型（如 ColBERT）虽通过细粒度 token 级交互提升了检索精度，但面临巨大的存储和检索效率瓶颈。现有方案依赖降维和 K-means 聚类，导致索引延迟高且压缩过程丢失语义信息。本文旨在解决这些效率与精度之间的权衡问题。

Method: 提出 SSR 范式，利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）将 token 嵌入投影到高维但高度稀疏的表示空间，从而无需向量聚类，直接采用倒排索引实现精确高效的检索。该方法是一种单阶段稀疏编码方案，替代了传统的两阶段聚类+压缩流程。

Result: 在 BEIR 基准上的实验表明，SSR 相比 ColBERTv2 实现了索引时间减少 15 倍、检索延迟减半，同时检索性能优于现有领先基线方法。

Conclusion: SSR 通过稀疏编码替代聚类，实现了多向量检索在效率与精度上的"三重提升"（索引加速、检索加速、性能提升），为大规模多向量检索提供了一种新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多向量检索（multi-vector retrieval），属于多模态检索范畴。现有 taxonomy 中 multimodal_retrieval 子主题（多模态检索）已存在于 multimodal_generation 和 multimodal_understanding_generation 两个方向下，直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Novel sparse coding approach for efficient multi-vector retrieval, but outside user's core generative model priorities. Signals: Efficient retrieval, Sparse autoencoder, Indexing speedup, BEIR benchmark, Not generative model

Abstract: Multi-vector retrieval (MVR) models, exemplified by ColBERT, have established new benchmarks in retrieval accuracy by preserving fine-grained token-level interactions. However, this granularity imposes prohibitive storage and retrieval efficiency bottlenecks: to manage the immense memory footprint and computational overhead of billion-scale token vectors, state-of-the-art systems are forced to rely on aggressive dimension reduction and complex clustering (e.g., K-means). This compromise introduces two critical limitations: excessive indexing latency of clustering large-scale corpora and semantic information loss inherent to compression. In this paper, we propose Single-stage Sparse Retrieval (SSR}, a paradigm shift that replaces expensive clustering with efficient sparse coding. Instead of compressing features into low-dimensional dense vectors, we utilize Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) to project token embeddings into a high-dimensional but highly sparse representation. This transformation enables us to bypass vector clustering entirely and leverage inverted indexing for precise, high-throughput retrieval. Extensive experiments on the BEIR benchmark demonstrate that SSR achieves a "trifecta" of improvements: it reduces indexing time by 15x compared to ColBERTv2, halves retrieval latency, and simultaneously improves retrieval performance over leading baselines.

</details>

### [31] [SalsaAgent: A multimodal embodied language model for interactive dance generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29219)
*Payam Jome Yazdian, Zoe Stanley, Angelica Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 普通相关 (55.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SalsaAgent 是一个多模态具身语言模型，能够根据人类领舞者的动作和音乐背景，实时生成富有表现力的全身萨尔萨舞蹈动作。它将交互建模为非语言运动 token 传递，扩展 LLM 词汇表以处理离散运动 token、成对关系 token 和音频，并通过两阶段 token-to-diffusion 流水线生成高质量的双人舞蹈动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互中的双向非语言反应、协调与同步对于社交机器人至关重要。现有舞蹈生成方法多关注单人舞蹈，缺乏对双人交互中领舞-跟舞关系的建模，且难以同时协调音乐、动作和双人空间关系。

Method: 提出 SalsaAgent，将交互建模为"非语言运动 token 传递"过程。主要贡献包括：(1) 扩展 LLM 词汇表以处理离散运动 token、成对关系 token 和音频 token；(2) 利用自动生成的骨架动力学文本描述进行 token grounding 的 LLM 微调；(3) 两阶段 token-to-diffusion 流水线，先由 LLM 预测跟舞者的运动 token，再通过扩散模型生成高质量连续动作。

Result: 主观和客观评估表明，SalsaAgent 在动作质量、音乐协调性、双人协调性和一致的双人空间行为方面均显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: SalsaAgent 有效实现了交互式双人舞蹈生成，证明了将 LLM 与运动 token 和扩散模型结合用于社交机器人交互的可行性，为未来社交感知机器人及交互式虚拟智能体提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究交互式萨尔萨舞蹈动作生成，属于人体动作生成与虚拟人交互范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "avatar_motion_generation (数字人与动作生成)" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on niche dance generation, not core video/image generation or world models. Signals: multimodal embodied language model, interactive dance generation, token-to-diffusion pipeline, nonverbal motion token passing, human-robot interaction

Abstract: Interaction between humanoids involves bidirectional and nonverbal reactivity, coordination and synchrony. Toward socially aware robots and interactive virtual agents, we present SalsaAgent, a language model that generates expressive, full-body salsa dance motions in reaction to a human leader and against a contextual music backdrop. We formulate interaction as nonverbal motion token passing, extending the vocabulary of a large language model (LLM) to process discrete motion tokens, pairwise relation tokens, and audio. Our contributions include new tokens for full-body and motion relations, LLM fine-tuning using automatically derived text descriptions of skeleton dynamics for token grounding, and a two-stage token-to-diffusion pipeline. Subjective and objective evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in terms of motion quality, music and partner coordination, and consistent two-person spatial behavior, with significant improvements over baselines.

</details>

*Xuangeng Chu, Yuan Gan, Ziteng Cui, Shuhong Liu, Jian Wang, Bing Zhou, Tatsuya Harada*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频驱动3D面部动画方法能生成同步唇动，但通常依赖预定义的身份或风格隐特征，用户无法自由控制说话风格；且固定风格/身份无法适应音频中的情感变化。因此需要一种能独立控制风格和情感、并支持动态情感变化的生成框架。
