Result: 模型能够实时生成与文本描述匹配的高度同步的唇动和面部表情，支持推理过程中情感动态变化，在同步性和表现力上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CapTalk 通过文本引导实现了风格与情感的独立控制，为音频驱动3D面部动画提供了更灵活、更具表现力的生成方案，并验证了动态情感控制的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究音频驱动的3D说话头生成，支持文本引导的风格和情感控制，属于数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）这一[CANONICAL]子主题的典型范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D head animation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key boost keywords. Signals: 3D facial animation, speech-driven, style-emotion decoupling, text-guided control, not video generation

Abstract: Audio-driven 3D facial animation aims to generate synchronized lip movements and vivid facial expressions from arbitrary audio clips. While existing methods can produce synchronized lip motions, they often rely on predefined identity or style latent features, which limits users' ability to freely control speaking styles. Moreover, applying a fixed style or identity to an entire audio segment typically results in facial animation styles that do not adapt to the emotional content of the audio. To address these challenges, we revisit the entanglement between style and emotion, construct a large-scale dataset with textual descriptions of both style and emotion, and propose a novel talking head generation framework that enables separate control over style and emotion. Our model takes as input both textual descriptions of speaking style and character emotion, as well as the driving audio stream, enabling real-time generation of highly synchronized lip movements and facial expressions that match the provided descriptions. Furthermore, our model supports dynamic emotion control during inference, allowing it to handle scenarios where the target emotion changes throughout the speech.

</details>


### [33] [AnyMo: Scaling Any-Modality Conditional Motion Generation with Masked Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29488)
*Yiheng Li, Zhuo Li, Ruibing Hou, Yingjie Chen, Hong Chang, Hao Liu, Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建OmniHuMo大规模多模态对齐动作数据集（5000+小时、320万序列，含文本/语音/音乐/轨迹标注）；提出AnyMo框架，包含Residual FSQ动作分词器将动作量化为离散token，以及可扩展的掩码建模Transformer，支持任意模态组合条件下的动作生成。

Result: 实验表明AnyMo在多种模态组合条件下均能实现高质量的动作合成，支持对空间和风格属性的灵活控制，展示了多模态条件动作生成的扩展性。

Conclusion: AnyMo通过大规模数据集和统一框架有效解决了多模态条件动作生成中的跨模态交互和扩展性问题，为任意模态组合的人体动作生成提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态条件的人体动作生成，属于[数字人与动作生成]（avatar_motion_generation）这一[CANONICAL]子主题的范畴。

Importance reason: Strong multimodal generation framework with masked modeling, but subtopic is digital human motion generation, not a top priority. Signals: multimodal generation, masked modeling, motion generation, large-scale dataset, residual FSQ tokenizer

Abstract: Conditional human motion generation remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision and robotics. Despite significant progress, current methods are often constrained by fixed modality configurations and task-specific architectures, leaving cross-modal interactions and the scaling laws of multimodal-conditioned synthesis largely underexplored. A key bottleneck is the scarcity of large-scale modality-aligned motion data, limiting generalization across diverse control signals. In this work, we introduce OmniHuMo, a large-scale, high-quality dataset comprising over 5,000 hours of motion and 3.2 million sequences with precisely aligned multimodal annotations (e.g., text, speech, music, and trajectory). Leveraging OmniHuMo, we propose AnyMo, a unified multimodal framework combining a Residual FSQ-based motion tokenizer with a scalable masked modeling transformer, enabling high-quality motion synthesis under arbitrary modality combinations. Extensive experiments show that AnyMo achieves high-fidelity synthesis while offering flexible control over both spatial and stylistic attributes.

</details>

*Hao Wu, Xiangyang Luo, Hao Wang, Jiawei Zhang, Yi Zhang, Jinwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 普通相关 (55.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免微调的扩散模型说话人脸生成框架，利用预训练的Stable Diffusion和IP-Adapter，并设计了三个无需可训练参数的组件（Structurist、Structure Controller、Noise Sensor）来解决身份漂移、同步误差和时间不稳定性问题，在唇音同步精度和视觉保真度上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的说话人脸生成方法需要针对特定任务进行微调并依赖大规模音视频数据集，计算成本高，限制了方法的可扩展性和可访问性。作者希望提出一种免微调的范式，直接利用预训练模型实现高质量的说话人脸生成。

Method: 提出免微调框架，以预训练的Stable Diffusion和IP-Adapter为骨干，利用IP-Adapter的视觉嵌入能力从预训练模型中挖掘唇部相关语义。设计三个无需可训练参数的组件：(1) Structurist——显式解耦并重组唇部和外观特征以缓解身份漂移和外观失真；(2) Structure Controller——基于准单调运动趋势自适应优化嵌入以实现精确唇音同步；(3) Noise Sensor——引入高斯先验检测并抑制闪烁和抖动伪影，增强时间一致性。

Result: 在唇音同步精度（PCLD提升至少0.16）和视觉保真度（FID提升至少0.7）上均优于现有SOTA方法，建立了免微调的扩散说话人脸生成新框架。

Conclusion: 本文证明了利用预训练扩散模型和IP-Adapter可以在无需微调的情况下实现高质量的说话人脸生成，为扩散模型在该任务上的可扩展性和可访问性提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究说话人脸生成（talking face generation），属于数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）这一[CANONICAL]子主题的典型范畴。同时涉及AIGC方向下的视频音频生成应用。

Importance reason: Novel fine-tuning-free diffusion framework for talking face generation, but outside user's priority subtopics and contains no boost keywords. Signals: diffusion-based talking face generation, fine-tuning-free, IP-Adapter, Stable Diffusion, lip-sync accuracy

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of diffusion models, talking face generation has made remarkable progress. However, existing diffusion-based methods still require task-specific fine-tuning and large-scale audiovisual datasets, resulting in high computational costs that hinder scalability and accessibility of diffusion-based approaches across the research community. To address this, we propose a finetuning-free paradigm that directly performs talking face generation using the pretrained weights of Stable Diffusion and IP-Adapter. This backbone leverages the visual embedding capability of IP-Adapter to mine lip-related semantics from the pretrained Stable Diffusion. To address the challenges of identity drift, synchronization errors, and temporal instability, we also design three trainable-parameterfree components: (1) the Structurist, which explicitly disentangles and reassembles lip and appearance features to mitigate identity drift and appearance distortion; (2) the Structure Controller, which adaptively refines embeddings based on quasi-monotonic motion trends for precise lip synchronization; and (3) the Noise Sensor, which introduces Gaussian prior to detect and suppress flicker and jitter artifacts and enhance temporal consistency. Experimental results show that our method outperforms existing SOTA approaches in both lip-sync accuracy (at least 0.16 gain in PCLD) and visual fidelity (at least 0.7 improvement in FID), establishing a novel fine-tuning-free diffusion framework for talking face generation.

</details>


### [35] [Archon: A Unified Multimodal Model for Holistic Digital Human Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30311)
*Chong Bao, Shichen Liu, Lijun Yu, David Futschik, Stylianos Moschoglou, Shefali Srivastava, Ziqian Bai, Feitong Tan, Guofeng Zhang, Zhaopeng Cui, Sean Fanello, Yinda Zhang*

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Archon 是一个面向数字人全模态生成的统一自回归模型，通过模态专用分词器、语义视频重参数化（4倍token压缩）和"模态思维链"推理，在说话头生成、音频驱动动画等任务上达到领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字人生成方法通常针对单一模态（如仅文本到视频或音频到面部），缺乏一个统一的模型来联合建模文本、音频、动作和视觉等多种模态的联合分布，难以实现高保真、可控的全方位数字人生成。

Method: Archon 包含：(1) 模态专用分词器将7种模态（文本、音频、面部动作、身体动作、面部视频、身体视频、面部语义）统一为离散token；(2) 原生自回归统一多模态模型，在同步多模态数据和72个多样化任务上预训练；(3) 语义视频重参数化，通过语义引导的token压缩实现4倍降采样；(4) 语义驱动的视频扩散解码器用于高保真重建；(5) "Thinking in Modality" 策略，将模糊的跨模态任务分解为逐步的模态链推理。

Result: 在多个数字人生成任务（说话头生成、音频驱动面部动画、文本驱动动作生成等）上，Archon 达到与现有专用方法相当或更优的性能，验证了统一框架的有效性。

Conclusion: Archon 证明了统一多模态模型在数字人全模态生成中的可行性，通过语义token压缩和模态链推理有效解决了高保真视频生成中的token爆炸和跨模态歧义问题，为沉浸式交互的数字人系统提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出统一多模态模型 Archon，用于数字人（digital human）的全方位生成，涵盖文本、音频、动作、视觉等多种模态。这与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "数字人与动作生成" (avatar_motion_generation) 高度匹配，该子主题明确包含虚拟人、说话头、肖像动画等方向。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with unified multimodal model and autoregressive generation priorities, with high practical value for digital human creation. Signals: unified multimodal model, autoregressive generation, digital human generation, token compression, multimodal chain-of-thought

Abstract: Digital humans are fundamental to immersive interaction, yet creating a unified model for holistic modalities, including text, audio, motion, and visual content, remains an open challenge. In this paper, we present Archon, a fully pretrained, human-centric unified multimodal model for holistic avatar generation. Archon unifies seven modalities with modality-specific tokenizers, and a native autoregressive unified multimodal model pretrained on synchronized modalities and 72 diverse tasks to model holistic joint distributions. To address the token explosion challenge in high-fidelity talking videos, we introduce a memory-efficient semantic video reparameterization, achieving 4x token reduction while preserving fine-grained dynamics, coupled with a semantic-driven video diffusion decoder. We further propose a "Thinking in Modality" that decomposes ambiguous cross-modal tasks into stepwise thinking in an alternative chain of modality, progressively enhancing fidelity and controllability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Archon achieves superior or comparable performance across diverse digital human generation tasks, validating the effectiveness of our unified framework. Project page: .

</details>


### [36] [VLA-Pro: Cross-Task Procedural Memory Transfer for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29562)
*Shengyu Si, Yuanzhuo Lu, Ruimeng Yang, Ziyi Ye, Zuxuan Wu, Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 普通相关 (62.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VLA-Pro提出了一种即插即用的框架，通过存储任务相关的LoRA适配器作为程序性记忆，在推理时基于多模态上下文检索并动态融合这些记忆，从而提升VLA模型在机器人操作任务中的跨任务泛化能力。在RoboTwin、RLBench和真实世界实验中，该方法显著提升了跨任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在泛化到未见过的任务时表现不佳，难以跨物体、场景和动作模式迁移相关经验。作者希望设计一种机制，让模型能够在训练时存储任务相关的操作经验，并在推理时根据当前上下文检索和利用这些经验，从而提升跨任务泛化能力。

Method: VLA-Pro包含两个阶段：(1) 训练阶段，为每个任务训练特定的LoRA适配器作为参数化的程序性记忆；(2) 推理阶段，基于当前多模态上下文（视觉和语言指令）检索最相关的程序性记忆，并通过动态融合机制将这些记忆与基础模型结合，生成当前的动作块。该方法采用即插即用设计，不改变基础VLA模型架构。

Result: 在RoboTwin、RLBench和真实世界操作任务上，VLA-Pro在多个基础模型上一致提升了跨任务泛化能力。仿真环境中相对提升最高达207%，真实世界操作成功率从5.8%提升至65.0%。

Conclusion: 程序性记忆检索与自适应融合为将操作经验迁移到新任务提供了一种有效的机制，同时保持了模块化和执行稳定性。该方法证明了存储和检索任务相关经验是提升VLA模型跨任务泛化能力的有前景方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLA模型在机器人操作中的跨任务泛化，涉及动作生成与操作技能迁移，与[数字人与动作生成]（avatar_motion_generation）中的人体动作生成、操作动作相关，且该方向包含机器人操作动作生成内容。

Importance reason: Strong cross-task generalization for VLA models via procedural memory, but subtopic is outside priority areas. Signals: LoRA adapter memory, cross-task transfer, robotic manipulation, modular VLA, real-world success rate boost

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action~(VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robotic manipulation, yet they still struggle to generalize to unseen tasks that necessitate transferring relevant experience across objects, scenes, and action patterns. This paper proposes VLA-Pro, a plug-and-play framework designed to enhance cross-task generalization by storing task-relevant procedural memories at training time and transferring these memories during inference. Specifically, VLA-Pro stores task-specific LoRA adapters as parameterized procedural memories during training. At inference time, VLA-Pro retrieves relevant procedural memories based on the current multi-modal context and dynamically fuses these memories for generating the current action chunk. Experiments on RoboTwin, RLBench, and real-world manipulation tasks show that VLA-Pro consistently improves cross-task generalization across multiple backbones, achieving up to a 207% relative improvement in simulation and increasing real-world success rate from 5.8% to 65.0%. These results suggest that procedural memory retrieval and adaptation provide an effective mechanism for transferring manipulation experience to novel tasks while preserving modularity and execution stability.

</details>


### [37] [Before the Shutter: Aesthetic and Actionable Portrait Photography Planning in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30318)
*Ruixiang Jiang, Chang Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: 本文提出3D美学人像摄影规划任务，在3D场景中生成人体姿态、相机、光照和曝光方案，以在拍摄前获得视觉上吸引人且物理可行的人像照片。方法构建摄影场景图（Photographic Scene Graph）表示场景可供性、主体-场景关系和光照结构，并通过美学引导的比较规划生成最优方案。实验表明该方法在人类和MLLM评估者中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算摄影方法大多聚焦于拍摄后的2D图像后处理（如修图、重光照、编辑），而拍摄前的规划（姿态、相机、灯光）这一关键环节尚未被充分探索。本文旨在将计算摄影从"事后修正"转向"事前规划"。

Method: 构建摄影场景图（Photographic Scene Graph）来编码场景可供性、主体-场景关系和光照结构；基于该表示，通过美学引导的比较规划机制，在先前尝试和当前取景器观察之间进行对比优化，生成最优的人体姿态、相机参数、光照和曝光方案。

Result: 在多种室内外场景上的实验表明，该方法生成的人像在人类评分者和MLLM评估者中均优于竞争基线，同时保持较高的物理可行性。

Conclusion: 本文展示了从拍摄后修正走向拍摄前计算人像摄影规划的可行路径，为计算摄影领域提供了新的研究方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究3D场景中的人像摄影规划，涉及人体姿态生成、相机与光照规划，属于数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）范畴下的肖像摄影规划任务，与[CANONICAL]子主题"数字人与动作生成"中的人体动作、肖像动画方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Novel pre-capture planning task with aesthetic 3D scene reasoning, but tangential to core generative modeling priorities. Signals: 3D scene planning, aesthetic photography, human pose generation, camera and lighting optimization, physical plausibility

Abstract: Portrait photography is largely decided before the shutter opens: the subject's pose, the camera configuration, and the lighting devices must be coordinated within the surrounding 3D scene. In contrast, most existing computational methods focus on post-production in 2D image space, such as retouching, relighting, or editing images that already exist; pre-capture photographic planning remains largely unexplored. We introduce 3D aesthetic portrait planning, the task of generating human pose, camera, lighting, and exposure plans that produce visually compelling portraits while satisfying geometric and photometric feasibility in a 3D scene. Our approach builds a Photographic Scene Graph that represents scene affordances, subject-scene relations, and portrait-relevant lighting structure. Built on this representation, we perform aesthetic-guided comparative planning over previous attempts and current viewfinder observations. Experiments across diverse indoor and outdoor scenes show that our method produces portraits preferred by human raters and MLLM evaluators over competitive baselines, while maintaining high physical plausibility. Together, our results suggest a path from post-capture correction toward pre-capture computational portrait planning. Project repository:

TL;DR: 本文提出 AdaState，用自适应隐状态替代固定首帧锚点，使自回归视频扩散模型在生成过程中动态更新场景参考，从而增强视频动态性、运动丰富度和场景自然演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散模型将首帧作为固定锚点，其 KV 缓存中的首帧表征占据主导注意力，导致生成视频动态性不足、运动受限、场景演进被抑制。作者希望打破这种静态一致性偏好，让模型能够生成随时间自然演进的视频。

Method: 提出 AdaState，用一个可学习的自适应隐状态替代固定的首帧锚点。该状态在每个生成块中与视频内容一同去噪，但从不渲染为可见帧。模型通过同时关注前一状态和当前内容来生成新的场景锚点，使参考随生成内容动态演化。时间被处理为相对而非绝对，每个生成步骤看到相同的位置结构，状态转移在每个块中保持一致，从而在生成过程中引入循环机制。

Result: 实验表明，自适应状态显著提升了视频动态性，生成了更丰富的运动和更自然的场景演进，相比固定首帧锚点的基线方法在视频质量与动态性指标上均有提升。

Conclusion: AdaState 通过自适应隐状态替代固定首帧锚点，有效解决了自回归视频扩散模型中因首帧过度锚定导致的动态性不足问题，为流式视频生成提供了一种简洁而有效的改进方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出自回归视频扩散模型中的自适应状态机制以改进流式视频生成，属于视频扩散生成方法，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "文生视频与视频扩散"。

Importance reason: Directly addresses autoregressive video generation's core limitation of static scene anchoring, aligning with top-priority subtopics and boost keywords. Signals: autoregressive video generation, video diffusion, adaptive state, scene dynamics, streaming video

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models generate streaming video by producing frames sequentially, conditioning each chunk on previously generated content. These models are structurally anchored to the first frame: its key-value representation occupies a privileged position in the attention cache and serves as the primary scene reference throughout generation. As the cleanest and most error-free position in the cache, this anchor draws disproportionate attention, suppressing video dynamics, and locking scene composition to the initial viewpoint even as the scene naturally evolves. The result is a temporally shallow video in which motion, camera movement, and scene progression are dampened in favor of static consistency. To address this, we replace the static anchor with an adaptive state, a hidden latent that the model denoises alongside content at every chunk but never renders. Rather than referencing a frozen first frame, the model generates its own scene anchor at each step by attending to both the previous state and the current content, producing a reference that evolves with the generated content. Unlike standard video generation, which encodes an absolute notion of time, our formulation treats time as relative: every generation step sees the same positional structure regardless of how far generation has progressed, and the state transition is identical at every chunk. Together, these properties introduce a recurrence into the generation process, where denoising serves as the transition function, and the KV cache serves as the carrier, requiring no external module. Experiments demonstrate that the adaptive state substantially improves video dynamics, enabling richer motion and natural scene progression within generated videos.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型概念擦除方法中，训练式方法效果好但计算成本高，编辑式方法高效但难以同时实现精确擦除和保持生成能力。作者发现根本原因在于编辑式方法依赖加法参数更新，而加法更新会纠缠神经元方向、幅度和角度几何，导致概念擦除与整体生成性能之间产生非预期干扰。

Method: OCE 从几何角度将编辑式擦除重新表述为乘法参数更新。具体地，OCE 对每层参数应用闭式解导出的正交变换，实现精确的概念擦除同时保持神经元幅度和角度几何不变。针对多概念擦除中的冲突约束，OCE 引入子空间级目标函数和结构化子空间操作，实现更有效和可扩展的擦除。

Result: 在单概念和多概念擦除任务上的大量实验表明，OCE 在概念擦除效果和非目标保持方面均优于现有方法，可在 4.3 秒内擦除多达 100 个概念。

Conclusion: OCE 通过正交变换的乘法参数更新范式，有效解决了编辑式概念擦除中加法更新带来的方向-幅度-角度纠缠问题，在保持生成能力的同时实现了高效、精确且可扩展的多概念擦除。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散模型中的概念擦除（concept erasure），属于生成模型安全方向，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "概念擦除与遗忘学习"（aigc_unlearning_erasure）完全匹配。该子主题在 aigc 和 multimodal_generation 方向下均有收录。

Importance reason: Strong method for controllable generation via concept erasure, but not directly aligned with top priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: orthogonal concept erasure, diffusion model safety, multiplicative parameter update, multi-concept erasure, efficient editing-based method

Abstract: Concept erasure has emerged as a promising approach to mitigate undesired or unsafe content in diffusion models, yet existing methods still face significant limitations. While training-based methods are effective, their high computational cost limits scalability. Editing-based methods are more efficient and deployment-friendly, yet they struggle to simultaneously achieve precise concept erasure and preserve overall generative capacity. We identify this core limitation of the editing-based methods as reliance on additive parameter updates. Our empirical analysis reveals that concept semantics primarily depend on neuron direction rather than neuron magnitude, while overall generative capacity relies on the angular geometry of neurons. As additive updates inherently entangle direction, magnitude, and angular geometry, they inevitably introduce unintended interference between concept erasure and overall generation performance. To address this, we propose Orthogonal Concept Erasure (OCE), which reformulates editing-based erasure as multiplicative parameter updates from a geometric perspective. Specifically, OCE applies layer-wise orthogonal transformations derived from a closed-form solution to the parameters, enabling precise concept erasure while preserving the neuron magnitude and angular geometry. Furthermore, to address conflicting constraints in multi-concept erasure, OCE introduces a subspace-level objective with structured subspace manipulation, yielding a more effective and scalable erasure. Extensive experiments on single- and multi-concept erasure demonstrate that OCE outperforms existing methods in concept erasure and non-target preservation, erasing up to 100 concepts in 4.3 s. Code: .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑方法（如ROME和MEMIT）主要通过输出行为评估编辑效果，但其内部机制尚未被充分探索。作者希望探究不同事实编辑是否依赖共同的机制，以及编辑如何影响模型内部的知识表征，从而理解编辑失败（如无法传播到相关事实）的根本原因。

Method: 作者提出训练一个紧凑的二进制掩码来识别编辑权重中共享的功能子空间。该掩码通过优化学习，能够逆转编辑效果。作者在多个编辑实例上训练掩码，并在未见过的编辑上进行测试。此外，作者通过将掩码注入编辑过程来验证该子空间的必要性，并分析掩码如何通过消除后层过度注意力来逆转编辑。

Result: 掩码能够逆转80%的训练集编辑和超过70%的测试集编辑，证明不同编辑共享共同的功能结构。将掩码注入编辑过程会使编辑成功率从98%降至38%，表明该机制对编辑成功至关重要。分析发现编辑通过抑制而非覆盖知识来工作，这解释了编辑无法传播到相关事实的原因。

Conclusion: 知识编辑方法（ROME和MEMIT）实际上是通过抑制而非覆盖知识来修改模型输出，不同编辑共享相同的功能子空间。这一发现不仅解释了编辑无法传播到相关事实的局限性，还为检测和防御不想要的编辑提供了新的方向。

Classification reason: 论文研究知识编辑（ROME/MEMIT）对模型内部机制的隐藏影响，发现编辑并未覆盖知识而是抑制了知识，并训练二进制掩码来逆转编辑。这与概念擦除/遗忘学习（aigc_unlearning_erasure）高度相关，因为论文揭示了编辑的可逆性机制，可用于检测和防御不想要的编辑，属于生成模型安全与遗忘学习范畴。同时该论文也涉及生成模型对齐与奖励（generation_alignment）中的可控性方面，但核心更贴近概念擦除。

Importance reason: Focuses on editing internal mechanisms rather than generation quality or efficiency. Signals: knowledge editing, transformer interpretability, ROME, MEMIT, weight masking

Abstract: Knowledge editing methods such as ROME and MEMIT update factual associations in transformer models by modifying MLP weights. While evaluated mainly by output behavior, their internal mechanism remains underexplored. We investigate whether edits rely on a common mechanism, regardless of which fact is modified. Despite fact-specific weight changes, we argue that ROME and MEMIT target the same subset of weights critical for maintaining edits. To isolate this subset, we train a compact binary mask over the edited weights. The mask reverses 80% of edits on the training set and over 70% on the test set, confirming that diverse edits share a common functional structure. Our analysis reveals that the mask reverses edits by eliminating overattention in later layers. Additionally, we show that injecting the mask during editing drops editing success from 98% to 38%, demonstrating that this mechanism is necessary for edits to succeed. Our finding that edits suppress rather than overwrite knowledge explains why ROME and MEMIT fail to propagate changes to related facts. The identified common functional subspace informs detection and defense against unwanted edits.

</details>

*Yurong Gao, Zicheng Zhang, Congying Han, Tiande Guo, Xinmin Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: 本文发现扩散桥模型在接近目标分布时存在严重的端点欠拟合问题，根源在于网络输入与回归目标之间的噪声水平差异过大。为此提出噪声对齐扩散桥（NADB），通过均值网络提供更干净的回归目标并引入噪声对齐映射关系，有效解决了噪声不匹配和端点欠拟合问题，在图像恢复与翻译任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散桥模型通过模仿标准扩散模型的分数匹配公式来学习两个分布之间的连接，但本文发现这种方式在接近目标端点（t→0）时会出现严重的欠拟合现象，表现为预测方差和方向显著漂移。作者分析认为这是由于网络输入与回归目标之间的噪声水平存在巨大差异所致，亟需解决这一结构性问题。

Method: 提出 Noise-Aligned Diffusion Bridge (NADB)，核心包含两个设计：(1) 引入均值网络（mean network）来提供更干净的回归目标，替代原始噪声目标；(2) 设计噪声对齐映射关系（noise-aligned mapping），使网络输入与回归目标的噪声水平相匹配，消除噪声不匹配问题。通过这种重新公式化，解决了端点附近的欠拟合。

Result: 在多个图像恢复（如去噪、超分）和图像翻译任务上进行实验，NADB 相比现有扩散桥方法在目标端点附近显著减少了预测方差和方向漂移，生成质量更高，验证了噪声对齐策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了扩散桥模型中一个被忽视的端点欠拟合问题，并提出了噪声对齐的解决方案。NADB 通过均值网络和噪声对齐映射从根本上解决了噪声不匹配问题，为扩散桥模型的训练提供了更稳定的框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Noise-Aligned Diffusion Bridge (NADB)，解决扩散桥模型在目标端点附近的欠拟合问题（噪声不匹配导致预测方差和方向漂移），属于扩散模型训练与采样过程的优化方法，与 canonical 子主题「生成模型加速与高效化」中扩散模型效率优化方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Addresses a critical underfitting issue in diffusion bridges with a novel noise alignment method, highly relevant to efficient generative models and video generation. Signals: diffusion bridge improvement, noise alignment, endpoint underfitting, image restoration/translation, efficient inference potential

Abstract: Diffusion bridge models offer a powerful framework for connecting two data distributions, such as in image restoration and translation. Many existing methods learn this bridge by mimicking the score-matching formulation of standard diffusion models. In this work, we find that this way leads to an anomalous underfitting phenomenon near the target endpoint, as the process approaches the target distribution ($t \to 0$). This underfitting, characterized by significant drift in the predicted variance and direction, results from an excessively large discrepancy in noise levels between the network's input and its regression resolve this issue, we propose the Noise-Aligned Diffusion Bridge (NADB).Our approach reformulates the diffusion bridge by first employing a mean network to provide a cleaner conditional target, and then introducing a novel, noise-aligned mapping relationship. This new formulation resolves the noise mismatch and corrects the underfitting near the target endpoint. Experimental validation across multiple image restoration and image translation tasks demonstrates the effectiveness of our approach. Code is available at .

</details>

Conclusion: 上下文蒸馏可以形式化为隐式记忆管理问题，模块化记忆库结合显式选择与Self-Gating机制能有效提升蒸馏效果和鲁棒性，缓存共享进一步提高了推理效率。

Importance reason: Directly addresses model efficiency and memory management for generative models, aligning with high-weight subtopics. Signals: efficient inference, model efficiency, compression, LoRA adapter, cache sharing

Abstract: Context distillation compresses contextual information into model parameters, yet existing methods often ignore how multiple distilled latent memories should be stored, retrieved, and safely activated in non-oracle settings. We formulate context distillation as a latent memory management problem. We distill each context into an independent LoRA adapter, forming a modular memory bank that enables explicit memory selection. Given a query, our framework retrieves candidate memories, routes the query to the most suitable adapter, and uses a Self-Gating mechanism to decide whether latent memory should be activated. To improve efficiency, we further introduce cache sharing to reduce management overhead during inference. Experiments show that our method substantially outperforms baselines with retrieval, while Self-Gating improves robustness by deactivate unnecessary latent memories.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）作为一种有前景的非自回归生成范式，全参数微调计算成本过高，因此参数高效微调（PEFT）成为标准做法。然而，现有PEFT方法（如LoRA）最初为自回归模型设计，使用与噪声水平无关的静态参数，忽略了扩散过程中输入分布和生成难度沿去噪轨迹显著变化的固有动态特性，导致次优性能。

Method: NaRA引入一个低秩核心矩阵，该矩阵由一个轻量级、全局共享的超网络以噪声水平为条件生成。超网络将噪声步编码为条件嵌入，通过一个小型MLP输出低秩核心矩阵，再分解为LoRA的A和B矩阵。这样，更新矩阵可以沿扩散过程连续变化，同时保持参数和延迟开销极小。论文还提供了NaRA框架的理论证明。

Result: 在常识推理、数学推理和代码生成基准上，NaRA一致优于噪声无关的基线方法（如标准LoRA），验证了噪声感知适配对dLLM微调的有效性。

Conclusion: NaRA通过噪声感知的低秩适配，有效解决了传统PEFT方法在扩散LLM中忽略噪声动态的问题，为dLLM的参数高效微调提供了更优方案。该方法在多个推理任务上取得一致改进，且额外开销极小。

Classification reason: 该论文提出NaRA，一种针对扩散LLM（dLLM）的参数高效微调方法，属于生成模型效率优化范畴。虽然dLLM是文本生成模型，但其核心是扩散过程，论文关注噪声感知的低秩适配以提升微调效率，与[multimodal_generation]下的[canonical] subtopic "efficient_generation"（生成模型加速与高效化）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient fine-tuning for diffusion LLMs, a key multimodal generation topic with high personal weight. Signals: noise-aware LoRA, diffusion LLM fine-tuning, parameter-efficient, dynamic low-rank adaptation, consistent benchmark improvements

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have emerged as a promising non-autoregressive generative paradigm. Given the prohibitive computational cost of full fine-tuning, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) has become the standard approach. However, existing PEFT methods (e.g., LoRA), originally tailored for autoregressive models, rely on static parameters that are agnostic to the noise level. Consequently, they ignore the intrinsic dynamics of the diffusion process, where input distributions and generation difficulty shift significantly along the denoising trajectory, rendering them suboptimal for dLLMs. To address this, we propose Noise-aware Low-Rank Adaptation (NaRA), which introduces a low-rank core matrix generated by a lightweight, globally shared hypernetwork conditioned on the noise level. This design enables the update matrices to vary continuously along the diffusion process while keeping parameter and latency overhead negligible. We provide a theoretical justification for the proposed NaRA framework and empirically demonstrate consistent improvements over noise-agnostic baselines across commonsense reasoning, mathematical reasoning, and code generation benchmarks. Our code is available at .

</details>

Importance reason: Directly addresses model efficiency via quantization, a priority subtopic, with a novel method that improves generation quality for long outputs. Signals: model efficiency, quantization, LLM generation quality, logit alignment, final-block optimization

Abstract: As large language models continue to scale, low-bit weight-only post-training quantization (PTQ) offers a practical solution to their memory-efficient deployment. Although block-wise PTQ is capable of matching the full-precision (FP) baseline on basic language modeling and understanding, its quality is degraded for generative tasks -- especially at longer responses and extended chains of thought, which is critical in boosting task accuracy. We attribute this shortfall to two factors: (i) the omission of the unembedding layer (the LM head) in block-wise optimization and (ii) the reliance on the mean squared error (MSE) objective. Both factors cause the token probability distribution of the quantized model to misalign with that of the FP model, yielding notable accuracy drops on text generation benchmarks. To rectify the discrepancy, we introduce Logit-aware Final-block Quantization (LFQ), a simple yet effective enhancement to block-wise PTQ that quantizes the final Transformer block by minimizing the cross-entropy between the logits of the FP model and those of its quantized counterpart. By aligning token probabilities at the logit level in the final block, LFQ consistently improves the accuracy of complex generation tasks over state-of-the-art block-wise PTQ across diverse model families, while maintaining parity with FP baselines on language modeling and understanding.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 Draft-OPD，一种用于推测解码草稿模型训练的在策略蒸馏方法。针对监督微调（SFT）训练草稿模型时存在的"离线-推理不匹配"问题（即训练时学习固定目标轨迹，推理时评估自身策略生成的块），Draft-OPD 利用目标辅助展开（target-assisted rollout）生成稳定续写，并从验证暴露的错误位置重放草稿生成，使草稿模型能从目标模型对接受和拒绝提案的反馈中学习。实验表明，该方法在多种任务上实现超过 5 倍无损加速，相比 EAGLE-3 和 DFlash 分别提升 23% 和 13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码草稿模型通常使用监督微调（SFT）在目标模型生成的固定轨迹上训练，但这种方法很快陷入平台期——草稿模型在测试数据上的接受长度不再提升。原因是离线训练与在线推理之间存在不匹配：SFT 训练时草稿模型学习固定轨迹，而推理时它需要在自己策略生成的块上进行评估。这促使研究者探索在策略蒸馏（on-policy distillation）方法。

Method: Draft-OPD 包含两个关键设计：(1) 目标辅助展开（target-assisted rollout）：利用目标模型辅助草稿模型生成稳定续写，避免草稿模型独立生成时的不稳定性；(2) 从验证暴露的错误位置重放草稿生成（replaying drafting from verification-exposed error positions）：让草稿模型从被拒绝的 token 位置重新生成，从而学习目标模型对接受和拒绝提案的反馈，聚焦于限制推测接受率的草稿诱导错误。

Result: 实验表明，Draft-OPD 在多种任务上实现超过 5 倍的无损加速（lossless acceleration），在推理模型上表现尤为突出。相比 EAGLE-3 和 DFlash，分别提升 23% 和 13% 的加速效果。

Conclusion: Draft-OPD 通过引入在策略蒸馏机制，有效解决了 SFT 训练草稿模型时的离线-推理不匹配问题，显著提升了推测解码的加速效果。该方法为构建更高效的草稿模型提供了新的训练范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Draft-OPD，一种用于推测解码（speculative decoding）中草稿模型（draft model）的在策略蒸馏方法，旨在加速大语言模型推理。这属于生成模型推理加速与效率优化，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for autoregressive models, a high-priority subtopic, with a novel on-policy distillation method that significantly improves speculative decoding. Signals: speculative decoding, on-policy distillation, efficient inference, autoregressive generation, model acceleration

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by pairing a target model with a lightweight draft model whose proposed tokens are verified in parallel. A common way to build draft models, like EAGLE3 or DFlash is supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on target-generated trajectories. However, we observe that SFT quickly plateaus: the draft model's acceptance length on test data stops improving. The reason is an offline-to-inference mismatch: In SFT, the drafter learns from fixed target-generated trajectories, whereas during speculative decoding it is evaluated on blocks proposed under its own policy. This motivates on-policy distillation (OPD), where the target model supervises the drafter on draft-induced states. Yet OPD remains difficult for draft models, as they cannot reliably roll out complete sequences independently, whereas target-assisted generation makes the collected sequences follow the target distribution and thus eliminates the on-policy signal. We therefore propose Draft-OPD, which uses target-assisted rollout for stable continuations and replays drafting from the verification-exposed error positions. This allows the drafter to learn from target feedback on both accepted and rejected proposals, focusing training on the draft-induced errors that limit speculative acceptance. Experiments show that Draft-OPD achieves over $5\times$ lossless acceleration for thinking models across diverse tasks, improving over EAGLE-3 and DFlash by 23\% and 13\%.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出SLAD（Shared LoRA Adapters for Task Specific Distillation），通过共享LoRA适配器参数策略，在教师和学生模型之间实现更好的特征对齐，从而提升任务特定蒸馏的效果。该方法不仅提升了学生模型的性能，也提升了教师模型的性能，同时训练速度比全参数微调快2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限环境（如嵌入式系统）中，将小型基础模型适配到下游任务的需求日益增长。任务特定蒸馏利用大模型（教师）辅助小模型（学生）的适配，但现有研究发现微调教师模型会导致教师与学生之间的特征表示不对齐，反而降低蒸馏效果。本文旨在解决这一特征不对齐问题，提升蒸馏效率。

Method: 首先利用低秩适配（LoRA）替代全参数微调，以更好地保留预训练知识并改善特征对齐。进一步提出参数共享策略（SLAD），在联合训练过程中让教师和学生编码器共享LoRA适配器参数，从而增强两者之间的特征对齐。通过多个分类和分割数据集进行实验验证。

Result: SLAD在多个分类和分割数据集上取得了任务特定蒸馏框架下的最优性能（SOTA）。相比全参数微调，训练速度提升2倍。不仅学生模型性能提升，教师模型性能也得到改善，验证了更好的特征对齐对知识迁移的促进作用。

Conclusion: SLAD通过共享LoRA适配器的参数共享策略，有效解决了任务特定蒸馏中教师与学生模型特征不对齐的问题，实现了更高效的知识迁移，同时加速了训练过程。该方法为资源受限场景下的模型适配提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究任务特定蒸馏（task-specific distillation），通过共享LoRA适配器实现大模型（教师）到小模型（学生）的知识迁移，属于生成模型效率优化中的蒸馏方法，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on efficient model adaptation via shared LoRA, which aligns with model efficiency but lacks direct connection to top-priority generative video/image topics. Signals: efficient inference, model compression, LoRA adaptation, knowledge distillation, feature alignment

Abstract: In the context of resource-constrained environments such as embedded systems, adapting reduced-size foundation models to downstream tasks has become increasingly popular. This has recently motivated the emerging setting of task-specific distillation, where a larger and a smaller version of the same foundation model are both adapted to the same downstream task, with the goal of transferring knowledge from the former to the latter. Recent work has demonstrated the benefits of using a larger version of the same foundation model to assist the adaptation of a smaller one. Typically, the larger model (teacher) is first adapted via fine-tuning or linear probing before its knowledge is distilled into the smaller model (student). While fine-tuning the teacher often increases its performance, recent work showed that probing it leads to better knowledge distillation to the student. Our findings show that this is mainly due to a mis-alignment in feature representation between the teacher and the student which occurs during the teacher's fine-tuning. Inspired by existing efforts to preserve previously learned knowledge, we first propose leveraging low-rank adaptation, resulting in better feature alignment and therefore better knowledge transfer. Drawing from this insight, we further enhance the feature alignment through a parameter-sharing strategy of the adapters between the two encoders during joint training. Our proposed method, SLAD, shows better feature alignment between the teacher and student, which results in increased performance for not only the student but also the teacher model, while being 2x faster to train than fine-tuning. Through extensive experiments on multiple classification and segmentation datasets, we demonstrate the improved accuracy and transfer efficiency of our method, achieving state-of-the-art performance in the task-specific distillation framework.

</details>

*Jiayi Luo, Qiyan Liu, Tengyang Wang, JunHao Liu, Jiayu Chen, Cong Wang, Hanxin Zhu, Chen Gao, Xiaobin Hu, Qingyun Sun, Zhibo Chen*

TL;DR: 本文提出Future Forcing，一种无需训练的、面向未来的KV缓存策略，用于加速自回归视频生成。通过利用自回归视频模型中RoPE前查询分布的近似平稳性，构建未来查询代理来评估KV缓存token的重要性，并在仿射子空间中合并冗余token对，从而在有限缓存下提升长视频生成的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成中，KV缓存随生成步长增长导致内存膨胀和误差累积，限制了模型扩展到更长序列。现有KV缓存压缩方法仅基于当前或历史短时信号评估token重要性，容易忽略早期看似不重要但后续关键帧所需的token。

Method: 首先发现自回归视频模型中RoPE前的查询分布具有近似平稳性，据此从历史统计中构建未来查询代理；然后利用该代理对KV缓存token进行重要性评分；最后在未来查询诱导的仿射子空间内合并冗余token对，实现无需额外训练的KV缓存压缩。

Result: 在VBench-Long基准上，对于60秒视频生成，Future Forcing在有限KV缓存下将主体一致性（subject consistency）提升高达1.49，优于现有自回归视频KV缓存策略。

Conclusion: Future Forcing通过利用自回归视频模型中查询分布的平稳性，实现了无需训练的、面向未来的KV缓存策略，有效提升了长视频生成的一致性和效率，为自回归视频生成的可扩展性提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种无需训练的KV缓存策略（Future Forcing）来加速自回归视频生成，属于生成模型推理效率优化，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for autoregressive video generation, a top priority subtopic, with a novel training-free KV cache policy. Signals: autoregressive video generation, efficient inference, training-free KV cache, long-video consistency, VBench-Long improvement

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video generation has emerged as a promising paradigm for long-horizon video synthesis, where each frame is generated conditioned on previously generated tokens. To accelerate inference, the KV cache is used to avoid redundant recomputation across generation steps. Nevertheless, its growth with generation length introduces increasing memory and error accumulation, limiting the scalability of AR models to even longer sequences. Existing KV cache compression methods mitigate this issue by selectively retaining only video tokens deemed important. However, most existing methods assess token importance using short-horizon signals derived from the current or historical generation context, making these methods prone to overlooking tokens that appear unimportant at early steps but later become critical for future frames. In this work, we identify an important property of trained AR video models: although RoPE-modulated queries evolve across autoregressive steps, the underlying canonical pre-RoPE query distribution remains remarkably stable throughout the video generation process. This approximate stationarity implies that future query distributions are estimable from historical statistics, enabling principled future-aware cache decisions without any additional training. Building on this insight, we propose Future Forcing, a training-free future-aware KV cache policy for AR video generation. Specifically, Future Forcing first constructs a future query proxy from historical statistics, then scores KV cache tokens by their importance under this proxy, and finally merges redundant token pairs within the affine subspace induced by the future query. Extensive experiments show that Future Forcing improves long-horizon consistency under limited KV caches, achieving up to 1.49 improvement in subject consistency on VBench-Long for 60s generation over existing AR video KV cache policies.

</details>

TL;DR: SGMD 提出了一种新的视频扩散蒸馏方法，通过分数梯度匹配（Score Gradient Matching）替代传统的分布匹配蒸馏（DMD），引入双势能机制（负残差NR和残差收缩RC）分别用于外环校正和内环追踪，在4步视频生成中实现约3倍训练加速，同时显著提升运动动态质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMD 风格的视频蒸馏面临两个耦合挑战：假分数需要追踪持续演化的生成器，导致频繁更新时训练成本高昂；而反向KL匹配可能过于保守，难以保留强运动动态。现有方法在训练效率和生成质量之间存在权衡。

Method: SGMD 采用假分数视角，直接优化假分数使其逼近教师模型，同时使用教师停止梯度的 Fisher 信息作为稳定的分布匹配目标。在此基础上引入双势能机制：负残差（NR）用于外环校正，残差收缩（RC）用于内环追踪，从而在保持时序一致性的同时提升运动动态。

Result: 与 DMD2 相比，SGMD 在4步蒸馏模型上实现了约3倍训练加速，显著改善了运动动态质量，同时保持了时序一致性。人工评估表明 SGMD 在运动质量和整体偏好上更受青睐，视觉质量和文本对齐保持可比水平。

Conclusion: SGMD 通过分数梯度匹配和双势能机制有效解决了视频扩散蒸馏中训练效率与运动动态保持之间的权衡，为少步视频生成提供了一种更高效的蒸馏范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出视频扩散模型的少步蒸馏加速方法（SGMD），属于生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）这一规范主题，该主题涵盖扩散加速、少步采样、蒸馏等效率优化方法。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient video diffusion inference with a novel training acceleration method, highly relevant to user's priority on diffusion video generation and model efficiency. Signals: Video diffusion distillation, Few-step generation, Training acceleration, Score gradient matching, Motion dynamics improvement

Abstract: Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) is a widely used paradigm for accelerating inference in few-step video diffusion models. However, DMD-style video distillation faces two coupled challenges: the fake score must track a continuously evolving generator, making training costly when frequent updates are required, while reverse-KL-style matching can be mode-seeking and conservative for preserving strong motion dynamics. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{Score Gradient Matching Distillation (SGMD)}. SGMD adopts a fake-score perspective by directly optimizing the fake score toward the teacher, while using teacher stop-gradient Fisher as a stable distribution-matching objective. We provide a gradient analysis that motivates this objective choice under ideal tracking. Building on this, SGMD introduces a pair of dual potentials: negative-residual (NR) for outer-loop correction and residual-contraction (RC) for inner-loop tracking. Empirically, compared to DMD2, SGMD achieves an approximately $\sim 3\times$ training speedup and substantially improves motion dynamics for 4-step distilled models while preserving temporal consistency. A human study confirms that SGMD is preferred in motion quality and overall preference, while visual quality and text alignment remain comparable. Code is available at .

</details>

TL;DR: 本文从几何视角分析稀疏表示分类（SRC）的稳定性，提出基于类条件子空间投影残差的残差间隔理论，并设计几何塑形目标（掩码自表达、跨类重建抑制、子空间对齐惩罚）来学习有利于稳定残差推理的表示，在图像、文本和EEG数据上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SRC等基于重建残差的分类方法在实际应用中可靠性不足，其性能高度依赖于所学表示的几何结构。现有方法通常将SRC作为可微模块进行端到端训练，但本文希望严格分离训练与推理，在固定SRC推理规则下学习几何上更稳定的表示。

Method: 提出基于子空间水平的理论框架，定义残差间隔（residual margin）并刻画子空间重叠、主导性和小主角度等几何障碍。据此设计三类几何塑形目标：掩码类内自表达（masked within-class self-expressiveness）、跨类重建路径抑制（discourage cross-class reconstruction pathways）、类间子空间对齐惩罚（inter-class span alignment prevention），在训练中不依赖SRC残差或预测。

Result: 在COIL-100图像、TREC文本和EEG连接性数据上，使用固定SRC/OMP推理评估所有表示，报告了残差间隔和几何诊断指标。与交叉熵基线相比，所提几何塑形目标在保持分类准确率的同时显著提升了残差间隔和推理稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文从几何视角揭示了SRC稳定性的关键因素，提出的几何塑形目标在不改变推理规则的前提下有效提升了表示的残差推理稳定性，为理解和发展基于重建的分类方法提供了理论指导。

Classification reason: 该论文研究稀疏表示分类（SRC）的几何稳定性，提出几何塑形目标以提升残差推理的稳定性与效率，属于表示学习与推理效率优化方向，与"生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）中关于高效推理、稀疏化等主题相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on theoretical stability of sparse representation classification, not on generative model acceleration or video/image generation. Signals: theoretical analysis, sparse representation, classification stability, not generative, no video/image generation

Abstract: Reconstruction-based inference assigns a class by comparing class-wise reconstruction residuals; Sparse Representation Classification (SRC) is a canonical instance whose reliability depends on the geometry of the learned representation. We adopt a strict training-inference separation: SRC is used only as a fixed test-time rule and is never differentiated, unrolled, or optimized during training. In a span-level idealization based on class-conditional spans and their associated projection residuals, we formalize residual-ordering stability through a residual margin and characterize geometric obstructions -- span overlap, dominance, and near-overlap via small principal angles -- that can collapse this margin in worst-case directions. This span-level theory is primary: it specifies when the idealized residual family is well-separated, and it provides a conditional solver-level interpretation for practical residual approximations (e.g., OMP) insofar as they remain close to the span-level residual ordering. Under explicit coverage and separation assumptions, we derive a quantitative lower bound on the (idealized) residual margin. Guided by these targets, we propose geometry-shaping objectives that promote masked within-class self-expressiveness, discourage cross-class reconstruction pathways and inter-class span alignment, and prevent collapse -- without invoking SRC residuals or predictions during training. Experiments on images (COIL-100), text (TREC), and EEG connectivity evaluate all representations under identical fixed SRC/OMP inference and report residual margins and geometric diagnostics; cross-entropy is included only as a reference geometry under the same evaluation protocol.

</details>

Abstract: This book provides a compact, derivation-oriented introduction to the mathematical foundations of modern generative artificial intelligence. Rather than surveying every recent architecture or implementation detail, it develops a coherent route through the ideas connecting major families of generative models, from PCA, probabilistic PCA, variational autoencoders, and diffusion models to normalising flows, autoregressive factorisations, GANs, Wasserstein GANs, and energy-based models. The aim is to make the structure of generative modelling more accessible without removing the mathematical substance needed to understand how these models are derived and related. The book is intended as a foundation-building primer for mathematically curious researchers, practitioners, and students.

</details>

*Daniil Shlenskii, Nikita Gushchin, Lev Novitskiy, Dmitry V. Dylov, Alexander Korotin*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: 本文提出 Midpoint Generative Models (MGM)，一种基于流匹配中点对称性的单步生成模型训练框架。通过利用线性插值流匹配中当两端分布相同时中点漂移场为零的性质，定义中点散度（Midpoint Divergence）作为分布差异度量，并扩展为随机翻转插值和对称随机插值，最终导出可训练的变分目标，实现高效的单步生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型（如扩散模型）通常需要多步采样才能生成高质量样本，推理成本高。单步生成模型虽有潜力但训练不稳定或理论不充分。本文旨在提出一个理论上严谨且实用的单步生成模型训练框架。

Method: MGM 基于流匹配（Flow Matching）中线性插值的对称性：当两端分布相同时，中点 t=1/2 处的漂移场为零。该漂移场的范数定义了一种分布差异度量——中点散度（Midpoint Divergence）。进一步引入随机翻转插值和对称随机插值（stochastic interpolants）进行推广，得到广义中点散度，并推导出其变分形式作为可训练的目标函数，用于训练单步生成器。

Result: 在多个标准图像生成数据集上，MGM 在单步生成设置下取得了与现有最先进单步生成方法（如一致性模型、对抗蒸馏等）具有竞争力的性能，验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: MGM 提供了一个理论上严谨且实用的单步生成模型训练框架，通过流匹配中点对称性导出可训练的散度目标，在保持生成质量的同时大幅减少采样步数，为高效生成建模提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Midpoint Generative Models (MGM)，一种训练单步生成模型（one-step generative model）的框架，属于生成模型加速与高效化（少步采样/蒸馏）方向，与 canonical subtopic "efficient_generation" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel one-step generative model training framework with strong theoretical grounding, directly relevant to model efficiency and acceleration priorities. Signals: one-step generation, flow matching symmetry, midpoint divergence, efficient inference, generative model acceleration

Abstract: We introduce Midpoint Generative Models (MGM), a principled framework for training one-step generative models. MGM is based on a simple symmetry of Flow Matching with linear interpolation: when the two endpoint distributions coincide, the corresponding drift field vanishes at the midpoint time, $t=1/2$. We show that the norm of this field defines a valid discrepancy between distributions, which we call the Midpoint Divergence. We extend this discrepancy beyond the midpoint by introducing randomly flipped interpolations and further generalize it by replacing deterministic linear Flow Matching interpolations with symmetric stochastic interpolants, yielding a generalized Midpoint Divergence. Finally, we derive a variational formulation of our generalized divergence, yielding a tractable objective for training a one-step generator. The resulting MGM algorithm offers an effective and theoretically grounded approach to generative modeling, achieving competitive performance against existing one-step generative modeling methods.

</details>

*Ziwen Xu, Haiwen Hong, Linsong Yu, Benglei Cui, Longtao Huang, Hui Xue, Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出参数记忆定律（Parametric Memory Law），揭示了LoRA微调中损失降低与有效参数、序列长度之间的幂律关系，并发现预测概率p>0.5是贪婪解码下逐字回忆的充分条件。基于此提出MemFT，一种阈值引导的优化策略，动态将训练预算分配给低于阈值的token，提升记忆保真度和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM需要在动态环境中持续学习和更新知识，LoRA被广泛用于此类记忆更新，但现有研究主要依赖定性下游评估，缺乏对精确参数记忆的定量容量限制和底层动力学的系统探索。

Method: 将LoRA作为潜空间中的受控记忆容量探针，系统量化精确参数记忆。提出参数记忆定律（幂律关系），在token级别进行细粒度分析，发现预测概率p>0.5是贪婪解码下逐字回忆的充分条件。基于此设计MemFT，一种阈值引导的优化策略，动态地将训练预算重新分配给低于阈值的token。

Result: 实验表明MemFT能够提升记忆保真度和效率，验证了参数记忆定律的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统量化了LoRA微调中的精确参数记忆容量，揭示了幂律关系和相变条件，提出的MemFT策略有效提升了记忆效率，为LLM持续学习中的参数更新提供了理论指导和实用方法。

Classification reason: 论文研究LoRA微调中的参数记忆容量与效率优化，提出MemFT阈值引导优化策略动态分配训练预算。这与多模态生成方向下的"生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）子主题高度相关，因为该子主题涵盖蒸馏、量化、稀疏化等效率优化方法，LoRA本身就是一种高效微调方法，MemFT进一步优化了训练预算分配。同时与AIGC方向下的"AI智能体与生成工作流"（aigc_agent_workflow）也有一定关联，但主要归属efficient_generation。

Importance reason: Provides a quantitative memory law for LoRA finetuning, directly relevant to model efficiency and controllable generation. Signals: LoRA memory law, efficient finetuning, token-level phase transition, MemFT optimization, model efficiency

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) must continuously learn and update knowledge to remain effective in dynamic real-world environments. While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is widely used for such memory updates, existing studies mainly rely on qualitative downstream evaluations, leaving the quantitative capacity limits and underlying dynamics of exact parametric memory largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we employ LoRA as a controlled memory capacity probe within the latent space to systematically quantify exact parametric memory. We introduce the Parametric Memory Law, a robust power law linking loss reduction Delta L to effective parameters and sequence length. At the token level, fine-grained analysis reveals a deterministic phase transition, demonstrating that a prediction probability of p > 0.5 constitutes a sufficient condition for verbatim recall under greedy decoding. Driven by these insights, we introduce MemFT, a threshold-guided optimization strategy that dynamically redistributes the training budget toward sub-threshold tokens. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that MemFT can enhance memory fidelity and efficiency. Code will be released at .

</details>


### [53] [Veda: Scalable Video Diffusion via Distilled Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30325)
*Shihao Han, Hao Yang, Xinting Hu, Xiaofeng Mei, Yi Jiang, Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (95.71) - Top 3 精读候选

TL;DR: Veda提出一种蒸馏稀疏注意力框架，通过将tile选择建模为从完整注意力中的显式重建问题，结合统计感知的tile评分和头感知tile划分，实现视频扩散Transformer中自注意力的高效稀疏化。在Waver和Wan2.1等大规模视频扩散模型上，Veda在720P 10秒视频生成中实现了5.1倍端到端加速和10.5倍自注意力加速，且无明显质量下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer在生成高分辨率长视频时，自注意力的计算复杂度随序列长度呈二次增长，成为主要瓶颈。现有稀疏注意力方法在高稀疏率下会导致生成质量严重下降，无法在保持质量的同时实现有效加速。

Method: Veda框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 统计感知tile评分（Statistics-aware Tile Scoring），利用注意力统计信息估计每个tile的重要性；(2) 头感知tile划分（Head-aware Tiling），减少结构不匹配；(3) 硬件高效的tile跳过核（Hardware-efficient Tile-skipping Kernel），将理论稀疏性转化为实际加速。整体将tile选择形式化为从完整注意力中的显式重建问题。

Result: 在Waver-T2V-12B模型上生成720P 10秒视频，Veda实现5.1倍端到端加速和10.5倍自注意力加速，将注意力开销从92%降至50%。加速增益随序列长度增加而增大，表明Veda在时空分辨率上具有良好的可扩展性。在Wan2.1等模型上也验证了有效性。

Conclusion: Veda通过蒸馏稀疏注意力框架有效解决了视频扩散Transformer中自注意力的计算瓶颈，实现了大幅加速且不牺牲生成质量。该方法在多种大规模视频扩散模型上均有效，且加速比随序列长度增长而提升，为高分辨率长视频生成的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出Veda，一种基于蒸馏稀疏注意力机制的扩散Transformer加速方法，核心贡献在于通过稀疏注意力降低自注意力计算成本，实现视频扩散模型的高效推理。这直接对应多模态生成方向下的[CANONICAL]子主题"生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation），该子主题涵盖扩散加速、稀疏化等效率优化方法。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for video diffusion transformers, a top priority subtopic with strong acceleration results. Signals: 5.1x end-to-end speedup, 10.5x self-attention speedup, distilled sparse attention, scales with sequence length, applied to large-scale video diffusion models

Abstract: Scaling Diffusion Transformers to generate high-resolution, long videos is constrained by the quadratic cost of self-attention, and existing sparse attention methods degrade under high sparsity. We show empirically that generation quality is determined not by the sparsity ratio itself, but by how well the sparse mask aligns with the tile-wise geometry of full attention. Based on this insight, we propose Veda, a distilled sparse attention framework that formulates tile selection as an explicit reconstruction problem from full attention. Veda integrates statistics-aware tile scoring with head-aware tiling to reduce estimation error and structural mismatch, enabling aggressive sparsity. A hardware-efficient tile-skipping kernel converts theoretical sparsity into practical wall-clock speedups. Experiments on large video diffusion models, including Waver and Wan2.1, demonstrate substantial acceleration with no noticeable degradation in generation quality. To generate 720P 10-second videos on Waver-T2V-12B, Veda achieves a 5.1$\times$ end-to-end speedup and a 10.5$\times$ self-attention speedup, reducing attention overhead from 92% to 50%. Notably, the gains increase with sequence length, indicating that Veda scales favorably with spatiotemporal resolution across models.

</details>


### [54] [Colored Noise Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30332)
*Hadar Davidson, Noam Issachar, Sagie Benaim*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 值得关注 (69.18) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Colored Noise Sampling (CNS)，一种无需训练的扩散模型随机采样器。它利用扩散模型生成轨迹中的频谱偏置（低频先、高频后），通过动态的、时间步和频率相关的彩色噪声注入策略，更高效地将能量分配到未解析的频率波段，从而引导生成分布更接近真实数据流形。在SiT、JiT、FLUX等架构上，CNS显著优于标准ODE/SDE采样器，在ImageNet-256上实现无引导FID从8.26降至6.27（SiT-XL/2）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型的SDE求解器在整个生成过程中注入均匀白噪声，忽略了生成轨迹固有的频谱偏置（低频结构先形成，高频细节后生成），导致有限能量预算的浪费。作者希望建立一种数学框架，将SDE推理重新理解为频率解耦的能量转移过程，并据此设计更高效的噪声注入策略。

Method: 提出Colored Noise Sampling (CNS)，一种即插即用的训练自由随机求解器。核心思想是：在扩散模型逆过程中，根据当前时间步和频率成分，动态分配彩色噪声（而非均匀白噪声），将注入能量优先导向尚未充分解析的频率波段。具体地，CNS利用生成轨迹的频谱偏置特性，设计了一个时间步和频率相关的噪声调度，使得低频成分在早期获得较少噪声扰动（已基本形成），高频成分在后期获得更多能量注入（需要精细刻画）。该方法可直接替换现有扩散模型的采样器，无需重新训练。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上，CNS显著降低了无引导FID：SiT-XL/2从8.26降至6.27，JiT-B/16从32.39降至26.69，JiT-H/16从11.88降至8.31。在Classifier-Free Guidance设置下也取得一致的相对FID改进。实验覆盖了SiT、JiT、FLUX等多种架构，验证了方法的通用性和即插即用特性。

Conclusion: CNS通过利用扩散模型的频谱偏置特性，设计频率解耦的彩色噪声注入策略，在无需重新训练的前提下显著提升了扩散模型的采样质量。该方法作为一种通用的即插即用采样器替换方案，可广泛应用于各类扩散模型架构，为扩散模型的高效采样提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种无需训练的彩色噪声扩散采样方法（CNS），通过频率解耦的能量分配策略改进SDE求解器，显著提升了扩散模型的采样效率和质量。这属于扩散模型采样加速与效率优化的范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances efficient inference for diffusion video generation, a top-priority subtopic, with strong empirical gains. Signals: training-free sampler, diffusion model acceleration, spectral bias exploitation, strong FID improvements on SiT/FLUX, aligns with efficient inference priority

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve state-of-the-art image synthesis, with their generative trajectories fundamentally exhibiting a spectral bias, resolving low-frequency global structures early and high-frequency fine details later. Conventional stochastic differential equation (SDE) solvers fail to account for this dynamic, naively injecting uniform white noise throughout the entire process and misusing the finite energy budget. In this work, we establish a mathematical framework that reconsiders SDE inference as a targeted, frequency-decoupled energy transfer. Leveraging this framework, we introduce Colored Noise Sampling (CNS), a novel, training-free stochastic solver. Rather than injecting uniform white noise, CNS utilizes a dynamic, timestep- and frequency-dependent schedule that more efficiently allocates injected energy toward structurally unresolved frequency bands. By actively exploiting the model's inherent spectral bias, CNS systematically steers the generated distribution toward the true data manifold. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CNS significantly outperforms standard ODE and SDE baselines as a strictly plug-and-play, inference-time sampler substitution across diverse architectures (SiT, JiT, FLUX). Compared to standard sampling on ImageNet-256, CNS achieves substantial unguided FID reductions, improving from 8.26 to 6.27 on SiT-XL/2, 32.39 to 26.69 on JiT-B/16, and 11.88 to 8.31 on JiT-H/16, while yielding consistent relative FID improvements with Classifier-Free Guidance. Project page is available at .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列自回归视频扩散模型依赖固定大小的滑动窗口KV缓存，但逐头的KV布局是流式内存和延迟的主要贡献者，此前未被充分优化。作者希望在不牺牲生成质量的前提下大幅压缩KV缓存，以支持分钟级长视频生成。

Result: 在VBench上，VideoMLA在短视界流式视频扩散基线中表现相当，在长视界下取得所有评估方法中的最佳总体分数；在单张B200上吞吐量提升1.23倍；KV内存减少92.7%。

Conclusion: VideoMLA证明了MLA在视频扩散中的有效性，即使预训练注意力不满足低秩假设，MLA瓶颈仍能保持生成质量并大幅压缩KV缓存，为长视频自回归生成提供了高效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出VideoMLA，将低秩潜在KV缓存（MLA）引入视频扩散模型，显著减少每token KV内存（92.7%）并提升吞吐量（1.23x），属于生成模型效率优化（加速与内存压缩），因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation)。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for autoregressive video generation, a top priority, with a novel low-rank KV cache method. Signals: First application of MLA to video diffusion, 92.7% KV memory reduction, matches baseline quality, 1.23x throughput gain, challenges low-rank assumption in video attention

Abstract: Long-rollout causal video diffusion has converged on a fixed-size sliding-window KV cache, with recent progress innovating within this layout by changing which tokens occupy the window or how their positions are encoded. The per-head KV layout itself, a dominant contributor to streaming memory and latency, has been mostly left unchanged. In this paper, we present the first study of Multi-Head Latent Attention (MLA) in video diffusion. VideoMLA replaces per-head keys and values with a shared low-rank content latent and a shared decoupled 3D-RoPE positional key, reducing per-token KV memory by 92.7% at every cached layer. We further investigate why MLA succeeds in video diffusion even though the spectral assumption often used to motivate it in language models does not hold: pretrained video attention is not low-rank, with 99%-energy effective rank far above any practical latent dimension. VideoMLA retains quality at compression ratios where direct spectral approximation would predict large reconstruction error. We show that the MLA bottleneck, rather than the pretrained spectrum, determines the effective rank: both spectral and random initialization occupy nearly the full rank budget from initialization, and training preserves this budget while adapting within it. On VBench, VideoMLA matches short-horizon streaming video diffusion baselines, achieves the best overall score at long horizons among evaluated methods, and improves throughput by 1.23x on a single B200.

TL;DR: 本文揭示了掩码扩散语言模型（MDM）中置信度解码策略的一个根本性缺陷：该策略倾向于优先预测局部"容易"的 token，而忽略长距离依赖关系，导致在复杂推理任务（如多位数加法）上产生高置信度错误。置信度对齐训练会进一步放大这一问题，而随机掩码策略虽看似低效，却能更好地保留推理轨迹条件，在困难样本上表现更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前掩码扩散语言模型（MDM）普遍采用置信度解码作为默认推理策略，且训练方案也倾向于对齐这种解码模式。然而，这种策略是否适合需要复杂逻辑推理的任务尚未被充分研究。作者希望揭示置信度解码与复杂推理之间的根本性不匹配问题。

Method: 作者以多位数加法为切入点，系统分析了置信度解码在 MDM 中的推理失败模式。通过对比置信度解码与随机掩码解码在五种不同推理任务上的表现，并分析置信度对齐训练（confidence-aligned training）与随机掩码训练的效果差异，揭示了置信度解码优先预测局部简单 token 而忽略长距离依赖的机制。

Result: 置信度解码在复杂推理任务上产生高置信度错误，置信度对齐训练将错误率放大一个数量级。随机掩码策略虽然看似低效，但在困难样本上保持了较低的失败率。该模式在五种推理任务中一致出现，但严重程度因任务而异。

Conclusion: 置信度解码与复杂逻辑推理所需的轨迹存在根本性不匹配，置信度对齐训练会加剧这一问题。随机掩码作为一种更鲁棒的替代方案，能够更好地保留推理轨迹条件。这为 MDM 的推理策略设计提供了重要启示。

Classification reason: 该论文研究掩码扩散语言模型（MDM）中置信度解码策略与复杂推理任务之间的对齐问题，属于生成模型的对齐与可控性范畴。现有 subtopic "generation_alignment（生成模型对齐与奖励）" 涵盖生成模型对齐、偏好优化、guidance 与可控性，与该论文主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Reveals a critical failure mode in confidence-based decoding for masked diffusion models, directly impacting autoregressive and diffusion-based generation quality. Signals: masked diffusion language models, confidence-based decoding failure, reasoning trajectory misalignment, high-confidence errors, random masking robustness

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDMs) uniquely support any-order generation, with confidence-based decoding currently serving as the de facto standard inference policy. To optimize for this, recent training schemes attempt to align training mask patterns directly with those observed during generation. However, we argue that confidence-based decoding is inherently misaligned with the logical-flow trajectories required for complex reasoning, and that confidence-aligned training actively entrenches this misalignment. We make this concrete using multi-digit addition, where the decoding strategy prematurely predicts locally easy digits before resolving their long-range dependencies, producing high-confidence errors on challenging inputs. While traditional random masking keeps the failure rate low on this challenging tail, confidence-aligned training amplifies the error rate by an order of magnitude. Across five distinct reasoning tasks, this same pattern emerges with task-dependent severity: confidence-based decoding induces failures on highly complex inputs, and confidence-aligned training exacerbates them. In contrast, random masking -- despite its perceived inefficiency -- robustly preserves the reasoning-trajectory conditionals essential for solving the challenging tail.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作表明在单模型自消费训练中引入人类筛选可以改善模型对齐，但实际场景中多个模型会相互消费彼此生成的合成数据。这种多模型交互如何影响人类筛选的效果尚不清楚，需要理论分析。

Method: 形式化定义了多模型自消费训练框架，建立动力系统模型分析收敛性；理论推导了人类筛选对自身模型（self-influence）和跨模型（cross-influence）的影响，并分析了跨模型交互如何导致对齐效果反转的条件。

Result: 证明了在多模型自消费循环中，人类筛选的效果可能被跨模型交互削弱甚至反转，导致长期对齐退化。给出了系统收敛到稳定点的条件，以及人类筛选产生负面效应的边界条件。

Conclusion: 多模型自消费训练中的人类偏好对齐需要谨慎设计，简单的筛选策略在跨模型交互下可能适得其反，为实际部署中的合成数据训练策略提供了理论指导。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模型自消费训练循环中人类偏好对齐的反向效应，核心关注生成模型的对齐问题。multimodal_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）直接涵盖对齐、偏好优化等主题，是最佳匹配。同时该工作涉及合成数据训练（自消费循环），也与 aigc 方向相关。

Importance reason: Directly addresses alignment dynamics in multi-model self-consuming loops, highly relevant to multimodal generation and model efficiency priorities. Signals: multimodal_generation, self-consuming training, human curation backfire, cross-model interaction, alignment dynamics

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly trained on synthetic data generated by prior model iterations rather than exclusively on real data. This self-consuming training paradigm can lead to model collapse, divergence, or bias amplification. Recent work (Ferbach et al., 2024) shows that incorporating human curation into the loop can steer a self-consuming model toward human-aligned behavior, but these analyses focus on a single, isolated model that solely consumes its own outputs. In practice, however, models often interact and train on input-output pairs produced by other models. This paper studies self-consuming training in the multi-model regime. We first formalize a framework for interacting self-consuming models and characterize when the resulting dynamical system converges to a stable point. We then examine how human curation of one model affects its own alignment (self-influence) and how such effects propagate to other models (cross-influence). Unlike isolated settings where human curation always enhances model alignment, we show that cross-model interactions can dampen or even invert this effect, ultimately degrading long-term alignment.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于组优势的强化学习方法（如GRPO、DAPO）在数学推理和文生图等领域表现良好，但它们依赖样本级奖励，对所有token进行均匀信用分配，无法捕捉细粒度的token级贡献。这限制了模型在关键语义区域（如与文本提示对齐的视觉区域或推理轨迹中的关键关键词）上的学习效率。

Method: 提出Guidance Contrastive Policy Optimization (GCPO)，核心思想是通过对比正提示（positive prompt）和负提示（negative prompt）下的模型预测差异来分配token级别的优势值。具体地，对于每个token，其优势值正比于正负提示下预测概率的差异，从而让模型更关注与正提示语义对齐的关键区域。该方法不依赖于额外的奖励模型或人工标注，可直接替代GRPO/DAPO中的均匀信用分配策略。

Result: 在文生图任务上，GCPO在多个指标上一致优于GRPO和DAPO基线，生成的图像在语义对齐和视觉质量上均有提升。在思维链推理任务上，GCPO同样表现出更优的推理准确率，特别是在需要关键推理步骤的任务中。实验表明GCPO能有效突出与正提示相关的语义区域和推理轨迹中的关键token。

Conclusion: GCPO是一种通用且可扩展的离散策略优化策略，通过对比引导的逐token信用分配有效提升了生成模型的对齐质量。该方法在文生图和思维链推理两个不同领域均展现出优势，为生成模型的对齐优化提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出GCPO算法，通过对比正负提示的预测差异实现token级别的信用分配，用于优化文本到图像生成和思维链推理中的离散策略学习。核心贡献是生成模型的对齐与奖励优化方法，与[multimodal_generation]下的[generation_alignment]（生成模型对齐与奖励）高度匹配，该子主题涵盖RLHF、奖励建模、偏好优化等。同时该方法也适用于多模态理解与生成方向的对齐。

Importance reason: Directly improves autoregressive image generation alignment via token-level credit assignment, a key priority. Signals: autoregressive image generation, controllable generation, reinforcement learning for generation, token-level credit assignment, outperforms GRPO/DAPO

Abstract: Group-advantage-based reinforcement learning methods, such as GRPO and DAPO, have demonstrated strong performance across diverse domains, including mathematical reasoning and text-to-image generation. However, their reliance on sample-level rewards introduces a key limitation as uniform credit assignment across all tokens fails to capture fine-grained, token-level contributions. To address this issue, we propose Guidance Contrastive Policy Optimization (GCPO), a novel algorithm that enables per-token credit assignment by contrasting model predictions under positive and negative prompts. Rather than uniformly broadcasting sample-level advantages, GCPO assigns token-level advantages proportional to the difference between these contrastive predictions, allowing more precise and informative learning signals. Empirically, we find that GCPO emphasizes semantically relevant regions such as visual areas aligned with textual prompts in text-to-image generation, and critical keywords within reasoning traces for chain-of-thought tasks. Through extensive experiments, GCPO consistently outperforms GRPO and DAPO baselines on both text-to-image generation and chain-of-thought reasoning benchmarks, demonstrating its effectiveness as a general and scalable optimization strategy for discrete policy learning.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的奖励函数在稀疏结构化RL任务中常出现奖励泛滥、语义/API误解等失败模式，一次性生成效果不佳。现有方法多依赖种群搜索（高成本）或简单重试，缺乏系统性的诊断与修正框架。

Method: 提出诊断驱动的迭代奖励函数优化方法：首先对LLM生成的奖励函数进行PPO训练，收集训练诊断指标（如奖励分布、成功率等）；然后基于预定义的失败模式分类法（奖励泛滥、语义/API误解、弱塑形）指导LLM进行针对性修正；迭代此过程直至收敛。在MiniGrid（DoorKey-8x8、KeyCorridor等）和MuJoCo上进行评估。

Result: DoorKey-8x8从2.3%提升至97.6%，KeyCorridor从31.2%提升至86.7%。消融实验表明分类法提示是主要机制，静态词汇控制可恢复大部分性能（87.6%/70.7%）。在连续控制任务（MuJoCo）上，基于成功率的诊断在密集奖励场景中可能失效。

Conclusion: LLM奖励设计应视为调试而非一次性生成，诊断驱动的迭代优化在稀疏结构化RL任务中有效。该方法受限于PPO框架下的稀疏结构化任务，在密集奖励连续控制场景中效果有限。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM奖励设计在稀疏结构化RL中的失败模式与诊断驱动迭代优化，核心是奖励函数设计与对齐问题，属于生成模型对齐与奖励（generation_alignment）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM reward debugging for sparse RL, not directly on user's priority generative modeling or efficient inference topics. Signals: LLM reward design, sparse RL, diagnostic refinement, MiniGrid, not generative modeling

Abstract: For sparse, structured reinforcement-learning tasks with semantic reward-function interfaces, LLM-generated reward shaping is better framed as debugging than one-shot generation. We study PPO-trained agents using MiniGrid as core evaluation and MuJoCo as boundary stress test. Our audit finds two dominant one-shot failure modes -- reward flooding and semantic/API misunderstanding -- plus a rarer weak-shaping case. We propose diagnostic-driven iterative refinement, where training diagnostics and a failure-mode taxonomy guide targeted reward-function revision. Refinement improves DoorKey-8x8 from 2.3% to 97.6% and KeyCorridor from 31.2% to 86.7% with high seed-to-seed variance. Controls show these gains are not from retrying or extra training: metrics-only re-prompting yields large drops, while a static-vocabulary control recovers much of the gap (87.6%; 70.7%), showing the taxonomy prompt is a major mechanism and dynamic labels provide only partially isolated incremental evidence. Budget-matched and Best-of-3 comparisons separate refinement from selection and training-time effects. Component-removal tests, sensitivity analyses, and an audit against author labels provide converging evidence for the debugging interpretation while revealing calibration limits. Continuous-control results show the boundary: success-based diagnostics can misfire in dense-reward locomotion, and return-trend feedback removes one false-positive mechanism without robust gains. The low-call protocol is a cost contrast with population-based reward search, not a benchmark comparison. In four crossed-variance-design environments, point estimates suggest larger gains when LLM reward-function variance dominates but bootstrap intervals are wide. The method is bounded to sparse structured tasks with reliable interfaces under PPO; fields like event_text may help, hurt, or be neutral.

</details>

*Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem, Kevin Zhang, Wei Cui, Marc T. Law, Kin Kwan Leung*

Main category: cs.LG, stat.ML, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (62.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Conf-Gen（共形生成），一个将共形风险控制（CRC）扩展到生成任务的通用框架，放宽了理论假设，统一并推广了先前将共形预测应用于 LLM 的尝试，并在图像生成（非记忆化）、对话系统（充分提问）和 AI 智能体（输出正确性）等新领域展示了共形保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测（CP）和共形风险控制（CRC）在监督学习中提供了形式化的不确定性量化保证，但无法直接应用于无监督生成模型（如 LLM 和图像生成器）。现有将 CP 应用于 LLM 的方法缺乏统一框架，且无法扩展到图像生成、对话系统等更广泛的生成任务。因此需要一种通用的方法将共形保证引入生成模型。

Method: Conf-Gen 通过放宽 CRC 的理论假设（如可交换性假设），将其适配到生成任务。核心思想是将生成过程视为一个可控制的风险函数，通过校准集构建预测集，使得生成结果以一定概率满足期望性质（如非记忆化、充分提问、输出正确）。框架统一了先前将 CP 应用于 LLM 的多种尝试，并提供了新的理论扩展。

Result: 在三个新颖的应用场景中展示了 Conf-Gen 的有效性：(1) 图像生成器产生非记忆化图像的共形保证；(2) 对话 AI 系统提出足够澄清问题的共形保证；(3) AI 智能体输出正确性的共形保证。实验表明 Conf-Gen 能够为生成模型提供形式化的不确定性量化保证。

Conclusion: Conf-Gen 为生成模型的不确定性量化提供了一个通用且理论严谨的框架，填补了共形预测方法在无监督生成任务中的空白。该工作不仅统一了现有方法，还拓展了共形保证在 AI 生成内容中的新应用场景，对提升生成模型的可控性和可靠性具有重要意义。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Conf-Gen 框架，将共形预测（CP）和共形风险控制（CRC）扩展到生成模型（LLM、图像生成器等），提供不确定性量化保证。这与 multimodal_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度相关，因为该 subtopic 涵盖生成模型的可控性、guidance 与对齐，而 Conf-Gen 正是通过形式化保证来提升生成模型的可控性和可靠性。同时该工作也涉及 AIGC 方向下的安全与评测相关主题。

Importance reason: Provides a novel theoretical framework for uncertainty quantification in generative models, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: Conformal prediction for generative models, Uncertainty quantification, LLM and image generation guarantees, Theoretical framework extension, No direct video or world model application

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) and its extension, conformal risk control (CRC), are established frameworks for quantifying uncertainty in supervised machine learning through formal guarantees. However, recent breakthroughs in artificial intelligence (AI) have been driven by unsupervised generative models, such as large language models (LLMs) and image generators, which are not directly compatible with CP or CRC. In this work we introduce conformal generation (Conf-Gen), a general framework adapting CRC to generative tasks while relaxing its theoretical assumptions. Conf-Gen unifies and generalizes previous attempts to apply CP to LLMs, and extends conformal methodology to entirely new domains. We demonstrate the flexibility of Conf-Gen through some novel applications, including obtaining conformal guarantees on: image generators producing non-memorized images, conversational AI systems having asked enough clarifying questions, and the output of AI agents being correct.

</details>

Method: 在 MM-DiT 的注意力输出空间中，将负提示的注意力特征相对于正提示特征进行正交化分解，仅减去正交分量，从而抑制不想要的语义同时保留期望的语义。该方法无需训练，支持多概念抑制和可调节抑制强度。

Conclusion: 正交负向引导是一种有效的训练无关概念抑制方法，在注意力特征空间中的正交化操作能够更好地分离正负语义，为 T2I 模型的可控生成提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种训练无关的负向引导方法，通过在注意力特征空间中对齐与正交化操作，实现对特定概念的抑制与生成可控性。这与 multimodal_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配，该 subtopic 涵盖 guidance 与可控性。同时，该方法也属于 AIGC 方向下的生成内容安全与可控生成范畴。

Importance reason: Novel training-free negative guidance method for T2I, but not aligned with user's priority subtopics like autoregressive video generation or world models. Signals: Training-free concept suppression, MM-DiT attention feature space, FLUX model, Human evaluation outperforms baselines, Multi-concept suppression

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models have become increasingly capable of generating high-quality images. Yet, enforcing the explicit absence of a specified object or attribute remains a fundamentally challenging problem. Existing approaches, including prompt negation, post-hoc editing, and negative guidance, remain insufficient for explicit concept suppression, often failing to remove the target concept or degrading overall image quality. To this end, we propose Orthogonal Negative Guidance in attention feature space, a training-free method that operates in the attention output space of MM-DiT-based T2I transformers. Our method orthogonalizes negative-prompt attention features with respect to positive-prompt features and subtracts only the orthogonal component, suppressing unwanted concepts while preserving desired semantics. Experiments on FLUX-dev and FLUX-schnell show that our method achieves favorable trade-offs between concept suppression, prompt alignment, and image quality. In human evaluation, our method outperforms the second-best baseline by 18.78%. We further show that our method supports multi-concept suppression and adjustable concept suppression.

</details>

*Yuxiao Yang, Weitong Zhang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件序列模型（CSM）将return-to-go（RTG）作为控制信号，但往往仅将其视为简单数值输入，未能确保RTG与策略实际性能之间的对齐，导致高RTG不一定对应高回报轨迹。

Method: 提出Q-ALIGN DT框架，利用Q函数为CSM提供密集引导，并通过RTG扰动技术联合微调Q函数与CSM，确保输入RTG与输出策略的Q值一致，使更高RTG映射到更高期望回报的轨迹。

Result: 在D4RL基准上，Q-ALIGN DT实现了优越的可控性和性能，有效学习到结构化的策略族，保持精确对齐，并能泛化到速度追踪等先前方法失败的任务。

Conclusion: Q-ALIGN DT通过Q值引导的对齐机制，有效解决了CSM中RTG与策略性能不一致的问题，理论上可学习到近似最优策略，实验验证了其在可控性和泛化性上的优势。

Classification reason: 该论文提出Q-ALIGN DT框架，通过Q函数确保条件序列模型中return-to-go与策略性能的对齐，属于生成模型对齐与奖励优化范畴，与multimodal_generation下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "generation_alignment"（生成模型对齐与奖励）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on RL alignment for sequence models, not directly on user's priority generative or video/image generation topics. Signals: Q-guided alignment, return-conditioned supervised learning, D4RL benchmark, RTG perturbation, controllable policy learning

Abstract: Conditioned Sequence Models (CSMs) learn policies by treating return-to-go (RTG) as a control signal. However, existing CSMs often treat the RTGs as simple numerical inputs rather than aligning them with the performance of their policies. In this paper, we propose Q-ALIGN DT, a framework that enforces this alignment by ensuring the $Q$-value of the output policy is consistent with the input RTG. By leveraging a $Q$ function to provide dense guidance to CSMs and further fine-tuning it using an RTG-perturbation technique with the CSM, our method ensures that higher RTGs are consistently mapped to trajectories with higher expected returns. Theoretically, we show that Q-ALIGN DT can efficiently learn the desired policy and output a near-optimal one when the RTG is sufficiently high. Empirically, we demonstrate through extensive experiments that Q-ALIGN DT achieves superior controllability and performance across the D4RL benchmark. Remarkably, our model effectively learns a structured family of policies that maintains precise alignment and generalizes to tasks like velocity-tracking where prior methods fail.

</details>

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI
