TL;DR: 本文提出OISD（On-Policy Internal Self-Distillation）框架，在GRPO强化学习后训练过程中，通过将最终层的预测信号蒸馏到中间层（包括logit对齐和注意力对齐），提升语言模型的推理能力，在数学推理任务上取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RL后训练方法主要使用稀疏的结果级奖励来优化最终输出策略，而忽略了中间表示中蕴含的预测信号。作者希望利用这些中间层的预测信息来进一步提升模型的推理能力。

Method: OISD框架在GRPO rollout和优化过程中，将最终层同时作为策略网络和中间层的内部教师（detached），通过两种对齐机制进行蒸馏：1）logit对齐——传递高层推理行为（如何思考）；2）注意力对齐——确保一致的注意力模式（关注哪里）。使用带符号优势加权的Jensen-Shannon散度进行对齐，在保持策略一致性的同时蒸馏信息丰富的中间表示。

Result: 在四个数学推理任务上，OISD相比强推理RL基线取得了显著且一致的改进，验证了内部自蒸馏对提升推理能力的有效性。

Conclusion: OISD通过将最终层的预测信号蒸馏到中间层，有效提升了语言模型的推理能力，为RL后训练提供了一种新的范式，且无需外部特权信息。

Classification reason: 该论文提出OISD框架，通过内部自蒸馏方法改进GRPO强化学习后训练，属于生成模型的对齐与奖励优化范畴，与multimodal_generation方向下的generation_alignment（生成模型对齐与奖励）子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on internal distillation for math reasoning, not directly on user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: internal self-distillation, GRPO, logit alignment, attention alignment, math reasoning

Abstract: Recent reinforcement learning (RL) post-training approaches primarily optimize the final output policy using sparse outcome-level rewards, while largely overlooking predictive signals encoded in intermediate representations. In this paper, we introduce a new paradigm called on-policy internal self-distillation and propose the OISD framework, which improves reasoning by transferring on-policy predictive signals from the final layer to intermediate representations. During rollout and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) optimization, the final layer acts as both the policy and a detached internal teacher for selected intermediate layers, which are guided to align with it through two complementary mechanisms: logit alignment, which transfers high-level reasoning behaviors (how to think), and attention alignment, which enforces consistent attention patterns (where to look) from the final layer to the selected intermediate layer, both without requiring external privileged information. Our OISD, together with GRPO, employs signed advantage-weighted Jensen--Shannon alignment to distill informative intermediate representations while preserving policy consistency under a unified acting policy. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of OISD, with substantial and consistent improvements over strong reasoning RL baselines across four mathematical reasoning tasks. The code will be released at

</details>


### [64] [Inferring the Size of Large Language Models From Popular Text Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29223)
*Ivica Nikolic*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (47.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种黑盒方法，仅通过分析模型对流行文本（如经典文学、宗教文献）的下一词预测准确度，来推断大语言模型的参数量下限。该方法利用记忆信号构建准确率轮廓向量，通过成对统计检验和基于PCA的缩放律估计器，在闭源模型上有效恢复了产品层级结构并揭示了不同厂商的规模策略差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多广泛使用的LLM的参数量被开发者隐瞒，而模型规模是理解其能力和成本的关键参考指标。现有方法往往需要内部信息或大量计算资源，因此需要一种仅通过黑盒API访问就能推断模型规模的方法。

Method: 基于一个关键观察：流行文本（经典文学、宗教文本等）几乎存在于所有大规模预训练语料中，模型对不同长度文本片段的下一词预测准确度反映了其记忆程度，而记忆能力受限于总参数量。作者将不同文本和片段长度的记忆信号聚合成准确率轮廓向量，构建了两种互补推断方法：(1) 成对统计检验，判断两个模型哪个更大；(2) 基于PCA的缩放律估计器，从轮廓向量中提取一维潜在指标映射到参数量。

Result: 在开源模型上验证了两种方法的准确性和可靠性。应用于闭源模型时，该方法恢复了内部产品层级结构，并揭示了不同厂商的规模策略差异：一些厂商有显著的代际参数量增长，而另一些则受严格的参数量上限约束。

Conclusion: 仅通过文本输出就能可靠推断LLM的参数量下限，即使在严格的API限制下也能系统性地探测隐藏的设计选择。该方法为理解闭源模型的能力和成本提供了新的工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出黑盒方法从文本记忆信号推断LLM参数量，属于生成模型的可控性/可解释性分析，与multimodal_generation方向下的"生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）子主题相关，该子主题涵盖guidance与可控性。

Importance reason: Focuses on model size inference via memorization, not directly on generation or efficiency. Signals: black-box inference, memorization signal, scaling-law estimator, closed-weight model analysis, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: The parameter counts of the most widely used large language models (LLMs) are often withheld by their developers, leaving model size -- a primary reference point for interpreting capabilities and costs -- largely undisclosed. We propose a black-box method to infer conservative lower bounds on LLM size from generated text outputs alone, requiring nothing beyond the ability to submit text fragments and observe next-token predictions. Our approach is grounded in a key observation: popular, widely-circulated texts -- such as classical literature, religious texts, and foundational documents -- are present in virtually every large-scale pretraining corpus, and how accurately a model predicts the next word across text fragments of varying length is a reliable signal of how much it has memorized them, which in turn is fundamentally limited by its total parameter count. We aggregate this memorization signal across a diverse corpus of texts and fragment lengths into a single accuracy profile vector per model, and build two complementary inference methods on top of it: a pairwise statistical test that determines which of two models is larger, and a scaling-law estimator that extracts a one-dimensional latent index from these vectors via Principal Component Analysis (PCA) to map the aggregated signal to a parameter count. Validated on a broad set of open-weight models, both methods produce accurate and reliable lower bounds. When applied to popular closed-weight models, our framework recovers internal product hierarchies and reveals a clear divergence in industry scaling strategies: while some developers yield significantly higher bounds indicative of large generational parameter growth, others operate under strict parameter ceilings, demonstrating that hidden design choices can be systematically probed even under strict API limitations.

</details>

*Xiaohang Tang, Keyue Jiang, Che Liu, Qifang Zhao, Xiaoxiao Xu, Sangwoong Yoon, Ilija Bogunovic*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 ELBO 的扩散语言模型 RL 方法存在训练-推理不匹配（TIM）偏差，因为 ELBO 作为似然代理与真实推理过程不一致，导致性能下降。作者希望设计一种无需似然代理的直接蒸馏方法，以更稳定有效地优化扩散语言模型。

Method: GDSD 从逆向 KL 正则化 RL 的闭式最优解中推导出一个优势引导的自教师模型，然后通过无归一化的目标函数将扩散语言模型的去噪器 logits 直接与教师模型对齐，将 RL 问题转化为无似然的自我蒸馏过程，从而绕过 TIM 偏差。

Result: 在 LLaDA-8B 和 Dream-7B 模型上，针对规划、数学和代码基准测试，GDSD 在测试准确率上比现有最优的 ELBO 方法提升高达 +19.6%，且训练奖励动态更加稳定。

Conclusion: 直接去噪器自蒸馏无需依赖 ELBO 似然代理，可以为扩散语言模型提供更稳定、更有效的 RL 训练过程，是一种有前景的替代范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究使用强化学习（RL）优化扩散语言模型（dLLM）的策略，提出引导去噪器自蒸馏（GDSD）方法，属于生成模型的对齐与奖励优化范畴。multimodal_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "generation_alignment"（生成模型对齐与奖励）完全匹配，故复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Novel RL method for diffusion language models that avoids training-inference mismatch, with strong empirical gains on planning and coding tasks. Signals: diffusion language model alignment, RL without ELBO bias, self-distillation from advantage-guided teacher, +19.6% test accuracy, LLaDA-8B and Dream-7B benchmarks

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can be used to improve the policy (denoiser) of diffusion large language models (dLLMs), while being hindered by the intractability of the policy likelihood. A dominant and efficient family of methods replaces the likelihood in standard RL with its evidence lower bound (ELBO), estimated from randomly masked sequences. Despite being well aligned with pre-training, these approaches introduce bias through training--inference mismatch by using the ELBO as a likelihood surrogate, which can degrade performance. In this work, we propose Guided Denoiser Self-Distillation (GDSD) to directly distill the denoiser of dLLMs from an advantage-guided self-teacher, derived from the closed-form optimum of reverse-KL regularized RL. GDSD matches the dLLM's denoiser logits to the teacher's via a normalization-free objective, which reduces RL to likelihood-free self-distillation and thus bypasses the TIM biases. Recent ELBO-based methods emerge as instances of applying different distillation divergences, but with diagnosable pathologies that GDSD avoids. On planning, math, and coding benchmarks with LLaDA-8B and Dream-7B, GDSD consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art ELBO-based methods with a more stable training reward dynamics, achieving test-accuracy improvements of up to $+19.6\%$. These results suggest that direct denoiser self-distillation, without relying on an ELBO likelihood surrogate, can provide a more stable and effective RL procedure for dLLMs. Code is available at .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）作为一种新兴的生成范式，具有独特的解码特性，但现有的引导方法大多依赖辅助模型或专为自回归解码设计，难以直接应用于DLM。因此需要一种简单、无需训练且适用于DLM的引导方法。

Conclusion: DLM-SWAI是一种简单有效的扩散语言模型引导方法，填补了现有引导方法在扩散语言模型上的空白，为DLM的可控生成提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种无需训练的扩散语言模型引导方法（DLM-SWAI），通过token级风格分数在去噪过程中偏置分布，实现风格和安全控制。这属于生成模型的可控性与对齐（guidance/steering）范畴，与multimodal_generation下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "generation_alignment"（生成模型对齐与奖励）高度匹配，该subtopic涵盖guidance与可控性。同时aigc方向下的同名subtopic也匹配。

Abstract: Steering language model generation toward desired textual properties is essential for practical deployment, and inference-time methods are particularly appealing because they enable controllable generation without retraining. Recent work has also highlighted diffusion language models as an emerging generation paradigm with distinct decoding properties. However, most existing steering approaches either rely on auxiliary models or are designed for autoregressive next-token decoding, making them difficult to apply to diffusion language models DLMs, which generate text through iterative denoising of partially masked sequences. Therefore, we propose DLM-SWAI, a simple training-free steering method that biases the token distribution at each denoising step using pre-computed token-level style scores. Experiments on style and safety control tasks show that DLM-SWAI effectively steers diffusion language models while preserving generation quality and requiring minimal computational overhead. Ablations further reveal a controllable trade-off between steering strength and fluency, and our analysis links class-wise steerability to the strength of token-level attribute cues.

*Zihao Xue, Yan Wang, Zhen Bi, Long Ma, Zhonglong Zheng, Zeyu Yang, Bingyu Zhu, Longtao Huang, Jie Xiao, Jungang Lou*

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeDIG框架，针对扩散Transformer（DiT）文生图模型的安全引导问题，通过位置感知的稀疏特征迁移实现鲁棒且可泛化的安全控制。该方法构建稀疏自编码器（SAE）对DiT不同干预位置进行建模，利用鲁棒性感知预训练路由选择在源-目标风险偏移下稳定的干预点，并通过冻结SAE编码器作为可复用稀疏安全字典、仅适配解码器的方式实现跨域迁移。在FLUX.1 Dev和SD3.5 Large上的实验表明，SafeDIG能有效降低目标域和整体的不安全生成率，同时保持源域安全性和图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer（DiT）的层次化跨模态生成过程使得安全控制与传统的提示过滤或输出检测截然不同——有害语义可能在文本表示中弱表达，逐步绑定到视觉隐空间，最终与渲染动态纠缠。固定层的安全引导不稳定，且从已知风险学到的引导机制难以可靠迁移到未知的目标风险域。因此需要一种鲁棒且可泛化的安全引导方法。

Method: SafeDIG框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 在DiT功能不同的干预位置上构建稀疏自编码器（SAE）；(2) 鲁棒性感知预训练路由，优先选择在源-目标风险偏移下保持稳定的干预位置；(3) 冻结SAE编码器作为可复用稀疏安全字典，仅适配解码器到目标域激活流形，实现可迁移安全特征与域特定激活几何的分离。推理时通过Blend（将不安全激活拉向安全流形）和Repel（将不安全激活推离有害稀疏方向）两种操作进行安全引导。

Result: 在FLUX.1 Dev和Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large上的实验表明，SafeDIG一致地降低了目标域和整体的不安全生成率，同时保持了源域安全性和图像质量。该方法在跨域迁移场景下表现出良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: SafeDIG通过位置感知稀疏特征迁移有效解决了DiT文生图模型的安全引导问题，实现了鲁棒且可泛化的安全控制。该框架分离了可迁移安全特征与域特定激活几何，使得安全引导能够从已知风险域迁移到未知目标域，为扩散模型的安全对齐提供了新的技术路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SafeDIG框架，通过稀疏自编码器实现文生图扩散Transformer的安全引导（safety steering），属于生成模型的安全对齐与可控性方向。multimodal_generation下的generation_alignment（生成模型对齐与奖励）是[CANONICAL]子主题，涵盖guidance与可控性，与该论文高度匹配。同时该论文也涉及AIGC安全治理（aigc_safety_governance），但核心方法是生成模型的对齐引导，因此主方向为multimodal_generation。

Importance reason: Directly addresses safety steering for diffusion transformers in text-to-image generation, a high-priority subtopic with strong alignment to user's multimodal generation and AIGC interests. Signals: diffusion transformer safety, robust generalization, sparse feature transfer, FLUX.1 Dev, SD3.5 Large

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers have become a powerful backbone for text-to-image generation, but their layered and cross-modal generation process makes safety control fundamentally different from prompt-level filtering or output-level detection. Harmful semantics may be weakly expressed in text representations, progressively bound to visual latents, and finally entangled with rendering dynamics. As a result, safety steering at a fixed layer can be unstable, and a steering mechanism learned from known risks may not transfer reliably to a shifted target risk domain. We propose SafeDIG, a safety steering framework that formulates DiT safety adaptation as position-aware sparse feature transfer. SafeDIG first constructs Sparse Autoencoders over functionally distinct DiT intervention positions and uses robustness-aware pre-training routing to prioritize intervention sites that are expected to remain stable under source-target risk shift. It then separates transferable safety features from domain-specific activation geometry by freezing the SAE encoder as a reusable sparse safety dictionary and adapting only the decoder to the target-domain activation manifold. During inference, SafeDIG combines Blend and Repel operations to steer unsafe activations toward transferred safety manifolds or away from harmful sparse directions. Experiments on FLUX.1 Dev and Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large show that SafeDIG consistently reduces target-domain and overall unsafe generation rates while preserving source-domain safety and image quality.

</details>

*Vinay Samuel, Yapei Chang, Mohit Iyyer*

TL;DR: REDIPO是一种离线DPO数据构建流程，通过从base和instruct模型采样、重写base响应、过滤安全质量、构建边际多样性偏好对，在保持对齐性能的同时恢复LLM输出的多样性。在Qwen3-4B、OLMo-3-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B上，NoveltyBench distinct_k分别提升134%、33%和44%，同时MTBench、IFEval和Arena-Hard性能基本保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多开放式指令存在多个有效答案，用户可以从不同答案中受益。但后训练（post-training）往往将LLM的输出空间收窄到少数规范响应，导致多样性丧失。如何在保持对齐收益的同时恢复输出的多样性是一个重要问题。

Method: REDIPO是一个离线DPO数据构建流程：(1) 对每个提示从base模型和instruct模型分别采样多个响应；(2) 用instruct模型重写base模型的响应；(3) 过滤候选响应以确保安全性和指令遵循质量；(4) 在具有相似指令遵循奖励的候选响应中，构建偏好对以偏好边际上更多样的响应。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B、OLMo-3-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B上，REDIPO使NoveltyBench distinct_k分别提升134%、33%和44%（相对instruct checkpoint），DivPO多样性变化分别为0%、-6%和-4%。MTBench、IFEval和Arena-Hard性能基本保持，HarmBench攻击成功率降低。

Conclusion: 通过精心构建的偏好数据，可以在保持后训练对齐收益的同时，重新引入base模型生成中的多样化有效答案。边际多样性对选择和base响应重写是多样性提升的关键驱动因素，而过滤和质量约束配对有助于维持对齐。

Classification reason: 该论文研究通过DPO偏好优化在保持对齐的同时恢复LLM输出的多样性，核心是偏好优化与对齐方法，与multimodal_generation下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配。同时该工作也涉及AIGC方向的对齐与可控生成。

Importance reason: Addresses diversity loss in post-trained LLMs, a key issue for controllable generation and alignment, but is more NLP-focused than the user's core multimodal generation priorities. Signals: DPO data construction, diversity recovery, alignment preservation, offline pipeline, base-model rewriting

Abstract: Many open-ended instructions have multiple valid answers that users can benefit from seeing, but post-training often narrows an LLM's output space toward a small set of canonical responses. We introduce REDIPO, an offline DPO data-construction pipeline for recovering distinct valid answer modes while preserving the alignment benefits of the instruct model. For each prompt, REDIPO samples responses from both base and instruct models, rewrites base-model responses with the instruct model, filters candidates for safety and instruction-following quality, and builds preference pairs that favor marginally diverse responses among candidates with similar instruction-following reward. Across Qwen3-4B, OLMo-3-7B, and LLaMA-3.1-8B, REDIPO improves NoveltyBench distinct_k by 134%, 33%, and 44% relative to the instruct checkpoints, while DivPO changes diversity by 0%, -6%, and -4% on the same models. These gains largely maintain MTBench, IFEval, and Arena-Hard performance, and reduce direct-category HarmBench attack success rate. Ablations show that marginal-diversity pair selection and base-response rewriting drive the diversity gains, while filtering and quality-bounded pairing help maintain alignment. Overall, our results show that diverse valid answers from base-model generations can be reintroduced through carefully constructed preference data while retaining the alignment benefits of post-training. We release our code and data at .

</details>


### [69] [VPG: Visual Prefix Guidance for Autoregressive Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30317)
*Xinyao Liao, Qiyuan He, Yicong Li, Jiayin Zhu, Xiaoye Qu, Wei Wei, Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (95.0) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VPG 是一种无需训练的推理时引导方法，通过对比生成前缀与损坏前缀下的模型输出，外推 logits 以增强生成前缀的后验支持，从而缓解自回归图像/视频生成中的曝光偏差和前缀漂移问题，在 VAR、Infinity 和 InfinityStar 上均提升了生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归图像和视频生成器在训练时使用教师强制（teacher-forcing）的历史序列，但在推理时必须基于自身生成的前缀进行采样，导致曝光偏差（exposure bias）和前缀漂移（prefix drift）。现有方法要么修改训练过程，要么在采样时施加引导，但这些引导主要针对外部语义条件（如类别标签或文本提示），而非检验下一步预测是否为已生成前缀提供强后验支持。

Method: VPG 是一种训练-free的推理时引导方法。对于自回归生成中的每一步，VPG 将模型在生成前缀下的输出与在损坏前缀（corrupted prefix）下的输出进行对比，然后外推（extrapolate）logits 朝向那些能增强生成前缀后验支持的候选 token，从而改进下一步预测的质量。

Result: 在 VAR 的类别条件图像生成上，FID 平均降低 0.36；在 Infinity 的文本到图像生成和 InfinityStar 的文本到视频生成上，均提升了基准性能，且无需重新训练基础模型。

Conclusion: VPG 作为一种简单有效的训练-free推理时引导方法，能够显著缓解自回归视觉生成中的曝光偏差问题，提升生成质量，且具有良好的通用性，可适用于不同自回归生成框架（图像和视频）。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种训练-free的推理时引导方法（Visual Prefix Guidance），通过对比生成前缀与损坏前缀下的模型输出来改进自回归图像/视频生成的质量，属于生成模型在推理时的可控性与对齐优化。这与 multimodal_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "generation_alignment（生成模型对齐与奖励）" 高度匹配，该 subtopic 涵盖 guidance 与可控性。

Importance reason: Directly addresses autoregressive video and image generation with a training-free inference-time guidance method, aligning with top priority subtopics and boost keywords. Signals: autoregressive video generation, autoregressive image generation, training-free guidance, exposure bias mitigation, inference-time method

Abstract: Autoregressive image and video generators are trained with teacher-forced histories but must sample from their own generated prefixes at inference time, making them vulnerable to exposure bias and prefix drift. Existing remedies either modify training or apply sampling-time guidance aimed primarily at external semantic conditions, such as class labels or text prompts, rather than testing whether a next-step prediction provides strong posterior support for the generated prefix itself. We propose Visual Prefix Guidance (VPG), a training-free inference-time guidance method for autoregressive image and video generation. VPG improves next-step prediction by contrasting the model's output under the generated prefix with its output under a corrupted prefix, then extrapolating logits toward candidates that strengthen the posterior support of the generated prefix. Across class-conditional image generation with VAR, text-to-image generation with Infinity, and text-to-video generation with InfinityStar, VPG improves generation quality without retraining the base model, reducing FID on VAR by 0.36 on average and improving benchmark performance on both image and video generation.

</details>


### [70] [Alignment-Guided Score Matching for Text-to-Image Alignment in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30038)
*Jaa-Yeon Lee, Yeobin Hong, Taesung Kwon, Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级、免奖励的后训练方法，通过将对比对齐引导直接融入扩散模型的分数匹配目标来优化软文本 token，从而改善文本-图像对齐。该方法解决了 SoftREPA 等对比学习方法中过度惩罚负样本导致的过计数和重复问题，在 GenEval 基准上计数准确率提升超过 35%，且可无缝应用于 SD1.5、SDXL、SD3 等扩散骨干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型生成的图像质量高，但文本-图像对齐精度不足。现有后训练方法依赖外部奖励或人类偏好信号，性能受限于奖励质量且未直接解决扩散过程中的对齐问题。免奖励方法 SoftREPA 通过对比学习优化软文本 token 改善了表示对齐，但其对比公式过度惩罚负样本，导致过计数和重复等失败案例。

Method: 提出 Alignment-Guided Score Matching (AGSM) 方法，在扩散模型的分数匹配目标中引入对比对齐引导。具体地，在训练过程中为每个文本 token 分配对齐方向（alignment direction），在分数级别（score level）引导生成过程朝向更准确的文本-图像对齐，从而缓解对比学习中的过度惩罚问题。该方法仅需微调软文本 token，是一种轻量级免奖励后训练方法。

Result: 在 GenEval 基准上，该方法与 SoftREPA 性能相当，但在其失败案例上显著改进，计数准确率提升超过 35%。方法可无缝应用于 SD1.5、SDXL 和 SD3 等主流扩散模型，且与现有的基于 RL 的扩散后训练方法互补。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 AGSM 方法通过将对齐引导融入分数匹配目标，有效改善了扩散模型的文本-图像对齐，特别是解决了对比学习方法中的过计数和重复问题。该方法轻量、免奖励、与现有扩散骨干和后训练方法兼容，为扩散模型对齐提供了一种新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种免奖励的文本-图像对齐方法，通过将对比对齐引导融入扩散模型的分数匹配目标来改善文本-图像对齐。这属于生成模型对齐（text-to-image alignment）的核心范畴，与 multimodal_generation 下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "generation_alignment（生成模型对齐与奖励）" 高度匹配。同时该方法也属于 AIGC 领域的生成内容质量优化，因此也匹配 aigc 方向。

Importance reason: Novel alignment method for diffusion models, but not directly on priority subtopics like autoregressive video generation or world models. Signals: diffusion model alignment, reward-free post-training, counting accuracy improvement, GenEval benchmark, SD1.5/SDXL/SD3 compatible

Abstract: Diffusion models generate highly realistic images but often struggle with precise text-image alignment. While recent post-training methods improve alignment using external rewards or human preference signals, their performance heavily depends on reward quality and does not directly address alignment within the diffusion process itself. Recent reward-free approaches such as SoftREPA demonstrate that optimizing soft text tokens via contrastive learning can effectively improve text-image representation alignment, outperforming standard parameter-efficient fine-tuning baselines. However, the contrastive formulation can excessively penalize negative pairs, which manifests as characteristic failure cases such as over-counting and repetition. To address this issue, we propose a lightweight, reward-free post-training method that refines soft tokens by integrating contrastive alignment guidance directly into the score-matching objective of diffusion models. By assigning alignment directions at the score level, our approach mitigates these limitations and yields more coherent and semantically faithful generations. Experiments show that our method matches SoftREPA while substantially improving its failure cases, achieving over 35% improvement in counting accuracy on the GenEval benchmark. Our method is seamlessly applicable to existing diffusion backbones (SD1.5, SDXL, and SD3), and is complementary to existing RL-based diffusion post-training methods. Project page:

</details>

TL;DR: 本文发现在线自蒸馏（OPSD）方法中，初始学生-教师性能差距与最终性能提升之间存在稳定的线性关系，该关系跨上下文类型和模型家族成立，且随模型规模扩展保持，为基于世界反馈的后训练提供了一种可预测的缩放定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越简单标量奖励、利用更丰富的世界反馈进行可扩展的 RL 后训练是自然的发展方向。OPSD 是一种有前景的方法，但其相对于 GRPO 等成熟方法的可靠性尚不明确，需要建立可预测的理论基础。

Method: 通过大量实验分析 OPSD 中初始学生-教师性能差距与最终性能提升之间的关系，验证其线性相关性在不同上下文类型（如数学推理、代码生成）和不同模型家族中的普适性，并研究该关系随模型规模扩展的行为。

Result: 发现初始学生-教师性能差距与最终性能提升之间存在高度一致的线性相关性，该关系跨不同上下文类型和模型家族成立，并且随模型规模扩展保持线性可预测性。

Conclusion: OPSD 的性能可以在训练前被预测和调优，为将世界反馈作为后训练流程的一等公民提供了一种原则性方法，并可能为更大模型的经验缩放定律奠定基础。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于世界反馈的在线自蒸馏（OPSD）方法，属于生成模型的后训练对齐与奖励优化范畴，与 multimodal_generation 和 multimodal_understanding_generation 两个方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a predictive scaling law for on-policy self-distillation from world feedback, which is highly relevant to efficient post-training and model alignment. Signals: scaling law for OPSD, world feedback for post-training, linear predictability across model scales, relevant to efficient inference and model efficiency, no direct video/image generation focus

Abstract: Moving beyond simple scalar rewards toward richer world feedback is a natural path to more scalable RL post-training. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is a promising recent approach that uses arbitrary feedback as learning signal, yet its reliability compared to established methods, such as GRPO, remains unclear. We identify a strikingly consistent linear correlation between the initial student-self-teacher performance gap and the final performance improvement in OPSD. This relationship holds across context types and model families, providing a powerful predictive law for anticipating the outcome of an OPSD configuration without running the full training procedure. Interestingly, we show that this linear predictability holds with model scale, suggesting a potential basis for new empirical scaling laws on larger models with stronger in-context learning capabilities. In essence, our findings show that OPSD performance can be predicted and tuned before training, offering a principled way to incorporate world feedback as a first-class component of the post-training pipeline.

</details>


### [72] [HPO: Hysteretic Policy Optimization for Stable and Efficient Training under Sparse-Reward Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30201)
*Mohamed Sana, Nicola Piovesan, Antonio De Domenico, Fadhel Ayed, Haozhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (52.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出滞回策略优化（HPO）及其自适应变体A-HPO，针对GRPO在稀疏可验证奖励场景下的失败模式——负优势更新过多且长度归一化不当——进行最小化修改。HPO降低负优势更新的权重，并用均值长度归一化替代逐响应长度归一化；A-HPO基于批次优势符号统计自动设置滞回权重。在TeleLogs和Countdown任务上，A-HPO显著优于GRPO、SAPO和GSPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO风格的强化学习在稀疏可验证奖励场景下存在一个常见但未被充分关注的失败模式：早期更新中负优势的响应远多于正优势的响应，而逐响应级别的长度归一化使得更新幅度与输出长度挂钩，导致训练不稳定且效率低下。本文旨在通过最小化修改解决这一问题。

Method: 提出HPO（Hysteretic Policy Optimization），对GRPO进行两处修改：(1) 引入滞回权重η，降低负优势更新的权重；(2) 用均值长度归一化替代逐响应长度归一化。进一步提出A-HPO（Adaptive HPO），根据批次中正负优势的统计比例自动确定滞回权重η，无需手动调参。

Result: 在TeleLogs任务上，A-HPO最终奖励达0.84，优于SAPO（5%）、GSPO（11%）和GRPO（15%），且响应长度相当。在Countdown任务上，A-HPO在1.5B-7B模型的最难初始配置中取得最大增益。消融实验表明，A-HPO的优势来自更好地平衡正负优势贡献，而非单纯的正向或完全对称更新。

Conclusion: HPO和A-HPO通过简单有效的修改显著提升了GRPO在稀疏奖励场景下的训练稳定性和效率，验证了合理处理负优势更新和长度归一化对强化学习训练的重要性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出HPO和A-HPO，改进GRPO在稀疏奖励下的强化学习训练，核心是优化生成模型的奖励信号处理与偏好优化，属于生成模型对齐与奖励（generation_alignment）范畴。该subtopic在multimodal_generation和aigc下均有存在，且为[CANONICAL]条目，优先复用。

Importance reason: Focuses on RL training stability for sparse rewards, not directly on generation quality or efficiency. Signals: RL for LLM alignment, sparse reward, GRPO improvement, not generation-focused, no video/image results

Abstract: We investigate a narrow but common failure mode of GRPO-style reinforcement learning in the context of sparse verifiable rewards: early updates contain more responses with negative advantages than those with positive advantages, while response-level length normalization ties the magnitude of the update to the length of the output. We propose Hysteretic Policy Optimization (HPO), a minimal modification of GRPO that reduces the weight of negative-advantage updates and replaces per-response length normalization with mean-length normalization. We further introduce Adaptive HPO (A-HPO), which sets the hysteretic weight based on batch-level advantage-sign statistics, thereby removing the need for tuning a fixed hysteretic weight. In our TeleLogs and Countdown experiments, A-HPO improves the reward per update compared to GRPO, with the largest gains in early sparse reward regimes. On TeleLogs, A-HPO achieves a final reward of 0.84, outperforming SAPO by 5%, GSPO by 11%, and GRPO by 15%, while maintaining a comparable response-length. On Countdown, A-HPO achieves the largest gains in initial and most difficult configurations across 1.5B-7B models. Ablation studies on the hysteretic weight show that the gains of A-HPO come from better balancing the contributions of positive and negative advantages compared to positive-only or fully symmetric updates.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 FlatSounds 基准，通过受控反事实对和单视频模式测试来审计视频到音频生成模型的物理推理能力。实验发现现有模型更依赖文本描述而非视觉流来推断物理属性，文本描述虽提升物理和语义准确性但会降低时间对齐精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频到音频生成模型能产生逼真的音轨，但缺乏对其是否真正捕捉底层物理过程的评估。已有评测侧重于感知真实性，忽略了在受控干预下的物理正确性。

Method: 提出 FlatSounds 基准，包含两类测试：(1) 受控反事实对——改变单一物理因素（如材质、速度、距离）生成对比视频对；(2) 单视频模式测试——探测内部一致性和方向趋势。通过物理指标评估生成音频是否反映正确的物理属性和时序。

Result: 现有模型存在一致性的权衡：模型更依赖文本描述而非视觉流来推断物理和语义信息；文本描述总体上提升物理和语义准确性，但反而降低了时间对齐精度。基于物理的指标与人类偏好测试高度相关。

Conclusion: 需要超越音频质量评估，推动模型直接从像素中学习物理过程。物理指标与人类偏好强相关表明该评测方向的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 FlatSounds 基准，用于评测视频到音频生成模型在物理正确性方面的表现，属于生成模型的评测基准，因此映射到 multimodal_generation 下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集" (generation_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Provides a novel physics-grounded benchmark for video-to-audio generation, revealing critical limitations in current models' physical reasoning. Signals: video-to-audio generation, physical reasoning benchmark, controlled counterfactual evaluation, text reliance vs. visual inference, temporal alignment trade-off

Abstract: Generative video-to-audio (V2A) models produce highly plausible soundtracks, but it remains unclear whether they capture the underlying physical processes. Existing evaluations emphasize perceptual realism and overlook physical correctness under controlled interventions. In this paper, we introduce FlatSounds, a benchmark that audits the physical reasoning of V2A models through: 1) controlled counterfactual pairs in which a single physical factor is varied, and 2) single-video pattern tests that probe internal consistency and directional trends. These settings test whether the generated audio correctly reflects specific physical properties and timings. Our evaluation of state-of-the-art models reveals a consistent trade-off: models rely more on text captions than the visual stream to infer physics and semantics. Captions generally improve physical and semantic accuracy, but paradoxically degrade temporal alignment. Our results highlight the need to move beyond audio quality toward learning physical processes directly from pixels. Finally, we find that our physics-based metrics correlate strongly with human preference tests on our own data. Project webpage:

</details>


### [74] [MiraBench: Evaluating Action-Conditioned Reliability in Robotic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29360)
*Tianzhuo Yang, Zihan Shen, Zirui Mi, Zhaoyi Zhang, Jiayi Zhou, Jiaming Ji, Juntao Dai, Jiawei Chen, Boyuan Chen, Yaodong Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 高优先级 (89.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MiraBench是一个用于评估机器人世界模型动作条件可靠性的分层基准，将可靠性分解为物理一致性、动作跟随保真度和乐观偏差检测三个层次，基于16,000+人工标注评估了12种模型配置，发现视觉保真度不能代表动作保真度、模型规模扩大不改善动作跟随、乐观偏差普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人世界模型评测主要关注视觉保真度，缺乏对模型预测在给定动作条件下是否物理合理、是否忠实于动作指令、以及在动作不应成功时是否校准失败的系统性评估。需要建立以"动作条件可靠性"为核心的评测基准。

Method: 提出MiraBench分层基准，包含三个递进层次：(1) Physics Adherence——无参考的物理一致性评估；(2) Action-Following Fidelity——预测是否尊重任务相关动作输入；(3) Optimism Bias Detection——检测在失败诱导动作下预测成功结果的倾向。构建了16,000+人工标注语料库，评估12种代表性世界模型配置。

Result: 视觉保真度是动作保真度的糟糕代理；增加模型规模并不能可靠改善动作跟随能力；乐观偏差在现有系统中普遍存在。

Conclusion: MiraBench将评测从外观质量转向动作条件可靠性，为评估和改进机器人世界模型作为忠实模拟器提供了诊断基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出MiraBench，一个用于评估机器人世界模型动作条件可靠性的分层基准，属于生成模型评测范畴。匹配到multimodal_generation下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "generation_benchmark"（生成模型评测与数据集），该子主题涵盖生成模型评测基准、新数据集和评估指标。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model reliability evaluation, a high-priority subtopic, with actionable findings for model improvement. Signals: world model evaluation, action-conditioned reliability, optimism bias, physical consistency, video generation benchmark

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models are increasingly used as scalable simulators for robot learning, yet current evaluations provide limited evidence that their predictions are reliable under the actions they condition on. Existing benchmarks largely emphasize visual fidelity, leaving unclear whether predicted futures are physically plausible, faithful to commanded actions, and calibrated to failure when actions should not succeed. We introduce \textsc{MiraBench}, a hierarchical benchmark that defines \emph{action-conditioned reliability} as a core evaluation target for robotic world models. MiraBench decomposes this target into three progressively demanding levels: \emph{Physics Adherence}, which evaluates reference-free physical consistency; \emph{Action-Following Fidelity}, which measures whether predictions respect task-relevant action inputs; and \emph{Optimism Bias Detection}, which probes the tendency to predict successful outcomes under failure-inducing actions. To support this evaluation, we curate a human-annotated corpus with over 16,000 judgments across tasks, failure categories, and leading world models. We evaluate 12 representative model configurations spanning vector-conditioned robotic world models, text-conditioned generative world models, open-weight systems, closed-source systems, and multiple model scales. Across this broad model landscape, MiraBench reveals three central findings: visual fidelity is a poor proxy for action fidelity; increasing model scale does not reliably improve action following; and optimism bias is pervasive across current systems. By shifting evaluation from appearance to action-conditioned reliability, MiraBench provides a diagnostic foundation for assessing and improving robotic world models as faithful simulators.

</details>


### [75] [Toward Ethical Facial Age Estimation: A Generalized Zero-Shot Benchmark Without Training on Children's Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29230)
*Caio Petrucci, Leo Sampaio Ferraz Ribeiro, Sandra Avila*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向人脸年龄估计的广义零样本评测基准，在训练中完全排除未成年人数据，仅使用18-59岁样本训练，将18岁以下样本作为零样本测试集，60岁以上作为未见验证集。评估9种SOTA方法发现所有方法均无法泛化到未见年龄组，平均性能下降46.4%，且存在明显的已见类偏置问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面部年龄估计模型通常使用包含未成年人图像的数据集进行训练，这引发了严重的伦理、法律和隐私问题。作者希望在不使用儿童数据的前提下，仍能评估模型对年轻人群的年龄估计能力，从而推动更负责任的数据使用实践。

Method: 作者重新审视了6个广泛使用的年龄估计数据集，制定了严格的年龄组分离标准划分：18-59岁用于训练/验证/测试，18岁以下仅用于零样本评估，60岁以上作为分布偏移下的未见验证集。对于含身份标注的数据集，采用身份互斥划分防止身份泄露。在此协议下评估了9种SOTA年龄估计方法。

Result: 所有评估方法均无法泛化到未见年龄组，性能平均下降46.4%，最高达52.8%。模型并非随机退化，而是系统性地将未见年龄的预测锚定到邻近的已见类别上，表现出广义零样本学习中典型的已见类偏置。

Conclusion: 本文通过将"不使用儿童数据的年龄估计"形式化为现有数据集上的广义零样本基准，揭示了当前建模实践与现实伦理约束之间的关键差距。该基准为受限数据场景下的模型评估提供了原则性基础，鼓励开发对分布偏移鲁棒且符合负责任数据使用的方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向人脸年龄估计的广义零样本评测基准，明确排除儿童数据训练但仍评估对年轻人群的泛化能力，属于生成模型/回归模型的评测基准与数据集构建，映射到 multimodal_generation 下的 canonical 子主题「生成模型评测与数据集」。

Importance reason: Ethical AI benchmark for age estimation is valuable but far from user's core generative model priorities. Signals: ethical AI benchmark, zero-shot age estimation, strong performance degradation, seen-class bias, no generative model focus

Abstract: Age estimation from facial images typically relies on training data that includes images of minors, a practice that raises serious ethical, legal, and privacy concerns. In this work, we propose a generalized zero-shot benchmark for facial age estimation that explicitly excludes children's data during training while still assessing model performance on younger populations. We revisit six widely used datasets and introduce standardized splits with strict age-group separation: samples aged 18-59 for training, validation, and testing; samples under 18 reserved exclusively for zero-shot evaluation; and samples 60+ as an unseen validation set for model selection under distribution shift. For datasets with identity annotations, subject-exclusive splits prevent identity leakage and better reflect real-world deployment conditions. Evaluating nine state-of-the-art age estimation methods under this protocol reveals that all evaluated methods consistently fail to generalize to unseen age groups, suffering substantial performance degradation -- on average 46.4%, and up to 52.8% -- relative to the supervised baseline. Moreover, models do not simply degrade: they systematically anchor predictions for unseen ages to nearby seen classes, a manifestation of the well-known seen-class bias in generalized zero-shot learning. By formalizing age estimation without children's data as a generalized zero-shot benchmark on existing datasets, this work highlights a critical gap between current modeling practices and real-world ethical constraints. Our benchmark provides a principled basis for evaluating models under restricted data regimes and encourages the development of methods that are robust to distribution shift and aligned with responsible data use.

</details>

TL;DR: EarthShift 是首个面向遥感领域多种真实分布偏移（时间、地理、尺度、传感器等）的鲁棒性评测基准，对 8 个地理空间基础模型和 11 个任务进行评测，发现模型在分布外场景下性能平均下降 15-20%，且鲁棒性与通用视觉基础模型及全监督模型相当，揭示了当前模型在分布鲁棒性方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球观测基准主要关注多样任务上的分布内泛化性能，但模型在实际部署中需要面对多种分布外场景（如新时间、新地理区域、新尺度、新传感器），而缺乏系统评测模型在真实分布偏移下鲁棒性的公开基准。

Result: 地理空间基础模型在分布外场景下性能平均下降 15-20%，且该下降趋势在不同模型架构、大小、预训练或微调策略下一致；地理空间基础模型的鲁棒性与通用视觉基础模型及全监督模型相似，并未表现出更好的分布鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 当前地理空间基础模型在真实分布偏移下的鲁棒性不足，未来研究应不仅关注性能提升，还需致力于提高分布鲁棒性，EarthShift 可作为相关研究的评测平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 EarthShift，一个用于遥感领域分布偏移鲁棒性评测的基准测试平台，属于生成模型/基础模型的评测基准，与 canonical 子主题「生成模型评测与数据集」(generation_benchmark) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on remote sensing robustness, which is outside your priority subtopics and has negative keyword overlap. Signals: Remote sensing benchmark, distribution shift, geospatial foundation models, negative keyword "remote sensing"

Abstract: Current Earth observation benchmarks focus on measuring performance on diverse tasks and applications, typically measuring generalization in-distribution. But when models are deployed, they must generalize to myriad out-of-distribution scenarios, such as new time periods, geographies, scales, and sensors. We introduce EarthShift: the first public testbed for benchmarking robustness across multiple realistic distribution shifts encountered in remote sensing. EarthShift enables users to measure distributional robustness by comparing performance in- and out-of-distribution using datasets from paired datasets from different sources, temporal windows, geographic locations, and sensors. Our experiments on 8 geospatial foundation models (GFMs) and 11 tasks covering 5 shift types show that GFMs consistently perform 15-20% worse out-of-distribution on average regardless of model architecture, size, pre-training or fine-tuning strategy. We show that GFM robustness is similar to that of generic vision foundation models, and even fully-supervised models. This highlights a need for future research to strive for improvements in distributional robustness, not just performance, which can be benchmarked using EarthShift. We release our code and datasets to provide a testbed to guide future work to create foundation models that are robust and reliable in real-world applications. Code and data for EarthShift are available at:

</details>


### [77] [Rethinking FID Through the Geometry of the Reference Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29335)
*Yunghee Lee, Byeonghyun Pak*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (62.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了FID指标与样本质量之间的不一致性问题，发现参考数据集的几何特性（分布密度和有效秩）显著影响FID随样本质量变化的趋势。密集数据集上FID趋势更有利，而分散数据集上FID可能随样本质量提升反而变差。研究通过六个数据集、精度/召回归因以及替代特征空间和距离的消融实验验证了这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FID是图像生成领域最广泛使用的评测指标之一，但实践中发现更低的FID并不总是对应更好的样本质量。这种不一致性困扰着生成模型的评估和比较，需要深入理解其根本原因。

Method: 在六个不同几何特性的数据集上进行受控实验，分析分布密度和有效秩如何解释FID随样本质量的变化趋势。通过精度（precision）和召回（recall）归因分析，以及使用替代特征空间和距离度量进行消融实验，验证结论的稳健性。

Result: 分布密度和有效秩显著解释了FID随样本质量变化的行为：密集（concentrated）数据集倾向于产生更有利的FID趋势（质量提升时FID下降），而分散（dispersed）数据集可能导致FID在样本质量提升时反而变差。精度/召回归因和替代特征空间/距离的消融实验支持相同结论。

Conclusion: 分布度量指标（如FID）应结合参考数据集的几何特性进行解读，以实现更可靠的生成模型基准评测。这为理解和改进生成模型评估方法提供了重要指导。

Classification reason: 该论文研究FID这一图像生成评测指标的局限性，分析参考数据集几何特性对FID的影响，属于生成模型评测与基准（generation_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。同时与AIGC方向下的AIGC评测与基准（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）也相关。

Importance reason: Directly challenges a core evaluation metric (FID) for multimodal generation, relevant to your priority on model efficiency and reliable benchmarking. Signals: FID inconsistency, reference dataset geometry, evaluation reliability, generative model benchmarking, precision/recall attribution

Abstract: Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) is widely used to evaluate image generators, yet lower FID does not always correspond to better sample quality. We show that this mismatch depends in part on the geometry of the reference dataset. In a controlled study across six datasets, distributional density and effective rank significantly explain how FID changes as sample quality improves. Concentrated datasets tend to yield more favorable FID trends, whereas more dispersed datasets can make FID worsen despite better samples. Attribution to precision and recall and ablations with alternative feature spaces and distances support the same conclusion. These results suggest that distributional metrics should be interpreted together with the geometry of the reference dataset for more reliable benchmarking.

</details>


### [78] [Do Physics Foundation Models Learn Generalizable Physics? A Bias-Aware Benchmark Across Physical Regimes and Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29283)
*Mengdi Chu, Yang Liu, Ayan Biswas, Han-Wei Shen*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (54.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含8种物理动力学、3种训练数据混合和25种测试场景的基准，系统评估了5种物理基础模型架构的泛化能力。结果表明当前物理基础模型是条件性而非通用性的泛化者，其能力受物理机制、时间尺度、初始条件、预训练、模型规模和架构等多因素影响，仅靠扩大数据和模型规模无法可靠消除能力偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理基础模型声称具有通用时空预测能力，但其评估往往将性能压缩为单一平均分数，难以判断模型是否真正学到了可泛化的物理动力学，还是仅在特定设置下表现良好。

Method: 构建了一个包含8种物理动力学（如扩散、对流、波动等）、3种训练数据混合策略和25种测试场景（涵盖分布内、分布偏移和分布外设置）的基准。评估了5种物理基础模型架构（如Earthformer、SimVP等），每种架构包含4种变体（从头训练和3种不同规模的预训练模型），共产生60,000个测量结果。

Result: 当前物理基础模型是条件性而非通用性的泛化者：其泛化能力依赖于物理机制、时间尺度、初始条件设置、预训练、模型规模和架构。改善训练数据分布只能部分缓解这一局限，预训练和模型缩放也无法可靠消除其能力偏差。

Conclusion: 改进物理基础模型需要超越简单的模型缩放或数据扩展，转向学习能够跨物理机制、时间尺度和分布偏移传递可迁移物理知识的机制。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了一个包含8种物理动力学、3种训练数据混合和25种测试场景的基准，用于评估物理基础模型的泛化能力，属于生成模型评测与数据集（generation_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Evaluates foundation model generalization in physics, but the domain is far from the user's priority in generative AI and video/image generation. Signals: Physics foundation model benchmark, Generalization under distribution shift, Conditional vs universal generalist, Bias-aware evaluation, Not aligned with user's generative AI focus

Abstract: Recent physics foundation models claim general spatiotemporal forecasting ability, yet their evaluations often collapse performance into a single average score under a fixed training distribution. This makes it difficult to determine whether a model has learned generalizable physical dynamics or only performs well under particular settings. We construct a benchmark with 8 physical dynamics, 3 training-data mixtures, and 25 test regimes induced by dynamic-scale and initial-condition complexity shifts, covering in-distribution, distribution-shift, and out-of-distribution settings. We evaluate five physics foundation model architectures and four model variants per architecture (scratch and three pretrained sizes), resulting in 60,000 measurements. Our results show that current physics foundation models behave as conditional rather than universal generalists: their generality depends on the physical regime, temporal scale, initial-condition setting, pretraining, model size, and architecture. Improving the training data distribution only partially mitigates this limitation. Pretraining and scaling are also unable to reliably remove their ability biases. We argue that improving physics foundation models requires moving beyond scaling models or expanding data, toward learning mechanisms that better capture transferable physical knowledge across regimes, temporal scales, and distribution shifts.

</details>


### [79] [DirectorBench: Diagnosing Long-Form Video Generation with Personalized Multi-Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30090)
*Jiamin Chen, Qianben Chen, Jiawen Zhang, Yidi Wu, Yuchen Li, Xiaokun Zhang, Wangchunshu Zhou, Chen Ma*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

TL;DR: DirectorBench 是一个面向长视频生成的多智能体诊断评测基准，包含80个结构化元数据条目、7个用户画像和40个检查点标准，覆盖脚本、视觉、音频、跨模态和稳定性五个维度，能够定位生成工作流中的瓶颈并支持用户画像感知的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频生成评测基准主要关注局部视觉质量、短时序一致性或通用提示对齐，无法诊断工作流中的具体失败模式，也无法反映用户个性化偏好。因此需要一种能够定位瓶颈、支持用户画像感知的诊断性评测方法。

Method: 构建 DirectorBench，包含80个结构化元数据条目、7个用户画像和40个检查点标准，覆盖脚本、视觉、音频、跨模态和稳定性五个维度。采用多智能体评估框架，对4种长视频生成工作流、6种基础LLM和7种用户画像进行系统评估，并通过14名标注员进行人工验证。

Result: 揭示了工作流间的"片段间瓶颈"：过渡质量平均仅0.256，最佳工作流也仅达0.356；提示级用户需求满足度平均为0.71。人工评估验证了 DirectorBench 与人类判断的一致性，并发现聚合评分无法揭示的、依赖于工作流和用户画像的失败模式。

Conclusion: 长视频生成评测需要诊断性和用户画像感知的基准方法，DirectorBench 能够有效定位生成工作流中的具体瓶颈，揭示聚合评分所隐藏的细粒度质量问题。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了长视频生成的诊断性评测基准 DirectorBench，属于生成模型评测与数据集方向，与 multimodal_generation 下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "generation_benchmark（生成模型评测与数据集）" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses long-form video generation evaluation, a critical gap for your high-priority video generation and world model topics. Signals: long-form video generation, diagnostic benchmark, multi-agent evaluation, user profile-aware, workflow bottleneck analysis

Abstract: Long-form video generation is rapidly moving from short, single-scene synthesis toward minute-long, multi-shot creation with narrative structure, cinematic control, audio, and cross-modal synchronization. However, evaluating such videos remains challenging, since existing benchmarks largely focus on local visual quality, short-horizon temporal consistency, or generic prompt alignment, and provide limited diagnosis of workflow failures and user-dependent preferences. We introduce DirectorBench, a personalized multi-agent diagnostic benchmark for long-form video generation. DirectorBench evaluates generated videos with respect to 80 structured metadata entries, 7 user profiles, and 40 checkpoint criteria across 5 dimensions: script, visual, audio, cross-modal, and stability. Instead of reducing quality to a single aggregate score, DirectorBench localizes checkpoint-level bottlenecks and supports profile-aware evaluation. We evaluate 4 long-form video generation workflows, 6 base LLMs, and 7 user profiles. Across workflows, DirectorBench reveals a between-unit bottleneck: transition quality averages only 0.256 and reaches 0.356 for the best workflow, while prompt-level user demand fulfillment averages 0.71. We further conduct human evaluation with 14 annotators to validate the alignment between DirectorBench and human judgment. The results show that DirectorBench captures human-perceptible quality differences and reveals workflow- and profile-dependent failure modes that are hidden by aggregate scoring. These findings highlight the importance of diagnostic and profile-aware benchmarking for long-form video generation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉生成数据集（如 LAION、DataComp）存在许可不明确、稳定性差、规模受限等问题，难以支持可扩展的视觉生成建模研究。作者希望构建一个大规模、稳定、许可明确且易于访问的图像数据集，以推动视觉生成领域的可扩展性研究。

Method: 从互联网收集大量许可图像，使用最先进的视觉语言模型进行标注，构建了包含1亿训练样本、20万验证样本和100万测试样本的数据集。数据集经过安全过滤和去重处理，并集中托管在 Hugging Face 上。同时提供了像素空间流匹配（pixel-space flow matching）的参考基线模型和评测协议。

Result: 构建了约28万亿像素的 GPIC 数据集，所有图像均许可用于研究和商业用途。提供了基准测试协议和像素空间流匹配的参考基线，数据集、基准和模型均已开源。

Conclusion: GPIC 为视觉生成研究提供了一个大规模、许可明确、稳定可访问的数据集资源，有助于推动可扩展视觉生成建模方法的研究。该数据集和配套基准有望成为视觉生成领域的重要基础设施。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个大规模许可图像数据集 GPIC（约28万亿像素），包含1亿训练样本、基准测试协议和像素空间流匹配基线。这属于生成模型评测与数据集（generation_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题，因为论文核心贡献是构建大规模生成数据集并提供评测基准。同时，该数据集也可服务于 AIGC 领域的合成数据与数据集蒸馏（aigc_synthetic_data）方向。

Importance reason: 大规模许可图像数据集是视觉生成模型的基础，但非用户核心优先级。 Signals: 大规模许可图像数据集, 28万亿像素, 1亿训练样本, 流匹配基线, 安全过滤去重

Abstract: Studying scalable methods for visual generative modeling requires large, accessible, and stable datasets. We introduce GPIC, a Giant Permissive Image Corpus of approximately 28 trillion pixels. GPIC comprises diverse internet images captioned by a state-of-the-art vision-language model, including 100M training, 200K validation, and 1M test examples. Moreover, all GPIC images are permissively licensed for both research and commercial use. GPIC is safety-filtered, deduplicated, and centrally hosted on Hugging Face. We provide a benchmarking protocol for generative modeling on GPIC. Finally, we provide a reference baseline for pixel-space flow matching on GPIC. Our dataset, benchmark, and models are available at . Evaluation toolkit and code are available at

### [81] [YoCausal: How Far is Video Generation from World Model? A Causality Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30346)
*You-Zhe Xie, Yu-Hsuan Li, Jie-Ying Lee, Kaipeng Zhang, Yu-Lun Liu, Zhixiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 高优先级 (92.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: YoCausal 是一个受认知科学中"期望违背"范式启发的两层级视频生成模型因果理解评测基准。通过零成本的时间反转构造自然反事实样本，Level 1 用反向惊奇指数（RSI）量化时间箭头感知，Level 2 用因果认知指数（CCI）借助 VLM 区分因果与非因果子集，从而解耦真正的因果推理与时间偏置。对 13 个 SOTA 视频扩散模型的评测表明，感知时间箭头不等于理解因果关系，模型与人类因果认知仍有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型评测基准多依赖合成数据，存在 sim-to-real 差距，且无法有效区分模型是否真正理解因果关系还是仅仅过拟合了统计时间模式。作者希望从因果视角系统评估视频生成模型的世界模型能力。

Method: 受认知科学中"期望违背"（VoE）范式启发，提出两层级评测框架：(1) Level 1：通过时间反转真实视频构造反事实样本，定义反向惊奇指数（RSI），用去噪损失量化模型对时间箭头方向的感知能力；(2) Level 2：利用 VLM 将数据集分层为因果子集和非因果子集，定义因果认知指数（CCI），解耦真正的因果推理与时间偏置。整个协议可任意扩展且零成本。

Result: 对 13 个 SOTA 视频扩散模型的评测显示：(1) 模型能感知时间箭头（RSI 有效），但感知时间箭头不等于理解因果关系；(2) 模型在因果子集上的表现显著弱于非因果子集，表明存在严重的时间偏置；(3) 模型与人类水平的因果认知仍有显著差距。

Conclusion: 视频扩散模型在因果理解方面与人类水平存在显著差距，当前模型更多依赖时间统计模式而非真正的因果推理。YoCausal 为从因果视角评估视频生成模型提供了可扩展的评测框架，有助于推动视频生成向世界模型演进。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 YoCausal，一个用于评估视频扩散模型因果理解能力的基准，属于生成模型评测范畴。现有 canonical subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）完美匹配，该 subtopic 涵盖生成模型评测基准、新数据集和自动评测指标。

Importance reason: Directly addresses the critical gap between video generation and world model causality, aligning with top-priority world model and video generation subtopics. Signals: world model evaluation, causality benchmark, video diffusion models, cognitive science inspired, zero-cost counterfactual

Abstract: As video diffusion models (VDMs) advance toward world models, a key question arises: do they truly understand causality, or merely overfit to statistical temporal patterns? Existing benchmarks mostly rely on synthetic data, limiting real-world generalization due to the sim-to-real gap. We present YoCausal, a two-level benchmark inspired by the Violation of Expectation (VoE) paradigm from cognitive science. By temporally reversing real-world videos at zero cost as natural counterfactual samples, YoCausal establishes an arbitrarily extensible evaluation protocol. Level 1 introduces the Reverse Surprise Index (RSI), quantifying arrow-of-time perception via denoising loss. Level 2 introduces the Causality Cognition Index (CCI), which leverages a VLM to stratify datasets into causal and non-causal subsets, disentangling genuine causal reasoning from temporal bias. Evaluation of 13 state-of-the-art VDMs reveals that perceiving the arrow of time does not imply understanding causality, and a significant gap persists relative to human-level causal cognition.

</details>

### [82] [Mind-Omni: A Unified Multi-Task Framework for Brain-Vision-Language Modeling via Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29591)
*Yizhuo Lu, Changde Du, Qingyu Shi, Hang Chen, Jie Peng, Liuyun Jiang, Shuangchen Zhao, Huiguang He*

TL;DR: Mind-Omni 是首个将脑信号、视觉、语言三种模态统一到离散扩散框架中的多任务模型，通过 Brain Tokenizer 将连续脑信号转化为离散 token，在共享语义空间中实现7种编码和解码任务的统一，并构建了脑信号问答指令微调数据集 BQA，在多项任务上达到或超越专用大模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑机接口研究多采用专用单任务模型，缺乏灵活性和跨任务协同能力。作者希望构建一个统一的框架，能够同时处理脑信号到文本/图像的解码、文本/图像到脑信号的编码以及脑信号问答等多种任务，实现多模态间的相互理解与生成。

Method: 提出 Mind-Omni 框架，核心创新包括：(1) Brain Tokenizer，将异质连续脑信号（fMRI、EEG等）离散化为标准 token；(2) 基于离散扩散的统一生成范式，在共享语义空间中实现任意模态间的 token 级交互；(3) 构建脑信号问答（BQA）指令微调数据集，增强推理能力。框架统一了7种任务：脑→文本、脑→图像、文本→脑、图像→脑、脑→脑、脑问答、多模态脑问答。

Result: 在多任务统一框架中达到新 SOTA，性能与更大规模的专用模型相当甚至更优，验证了多任务协同的有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: Mind-Omni 为脑信号建模提供了一种新的统一范式，证明了离散扩散和多任务协同在脑-视觉-语言建模中的有效性，为神经活动基础模型的研究铺平了道路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Mind-Omni，将脑信号、视觉、语言三种模态统一到离散扩散框架中，实现理解与生成一体化（7个编码/解码任务），属于统一多模态模型方向。该 subtopic 在 multimodal_generation 和 multimodal_understanding_generation 两个方向下均已存在，且为 [CANONICAL] 条目，直接复用。

Importance reason: Novel unified multimodal framework for brain signals, but outside user's core generative AI focus. Signals: unified multimodal, discrete diffusion, brain-computer interface, multi-task synergy, instruction-tuning dataset

Abstract: Modeling the interplay between external stimuli and internal neural representations is a pivotal research area for Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs). A major limitation of prior work is the prevailing paradigm of specialized, single-task models, which curtails versatility and neglects inter-task synergies. To address this, we propose Mind-Omni, the first versatile framework that unifies seven distinct encoding and decoding tasks through a discrete diffusion paradigm. At its core is a novel Brain Tokenizer that transforms heterogeneous, continuous brain signals into standardized, discrete tokens. This enables direct, token-level interactions for mutual understanding and generation between any two or more modalities within a shared semantic space. To unlock advanced reasoning capabilities, we further curate a specialized Brain Question Answering (BQA) instruction-tuning dataset. Our model not only establishes a new state-of-the-art among multi-task unified frameworks but also provides strong evidence for multi-task synergy. By demonstrating performance competitive with, and at times superior to, larger specialized models, our work offers a powerful new paradigm for neural modeling and paves the way for foundation models of neural activity. The code is publicly available at .

</details>

### [83] [Emergent Semantic Representations in World Models through Physical Interaction without Linguistic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28865)
*Jiayi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (89.38) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过实验证明，基于VAE的世界模型在无语言监督的随机具身探索中，其潜在空间会自发形成反映物理世界几何结构的语义表征。方向准确率0.677（随机编码器0.547），位置RSA 0.192（随机编码器0.029，提升6.6倍）。通过双敲除实验（标准KL正则化）验证了预测性能与语义对齐共同提升的共享驱动假说，表明物理世界几何结构是世界模型表征的组织原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索世界模型在无语言监督条件下，仅通过物理交互能否自发学习到有意义的语义表征，以及这种表征的组织原则是什么。

Method: 训练基于VAE的世界模型，在随机具身探索数据上进行学习。通过方向准确率、位置表征相似性分析（RSA）等指标评估潜在空间的语义结构。设置20个时间检查点追踪预测性能与语义对齐的共变关系，并通过双敲除实验（标准KL正则化beta=0.1 vs 降低beta=0.001）验证共享驱动假说。

Result: 方向准确率0.677±0.029（随机编码器0.547），位置RSA 0.192±0.047（随机编码器0.029，提升6.6倍）。预测性能与语义对齐共改善（Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004）。标准KL正则化（beta=0.1）导致两者同时崩溃至接近随机水平，降低beta至0.001则恢复。

Conclusion: 物理世界几何结构是世界模型表征的组织原则，这对设计具有语义基础的具身智能体具有直接启示意义。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于物理交互训练的世界模型（world model），探讨其潜在空间如何自发形成反映物理几何结构的语义表征。这与多模态生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视频世界模型"（video_world_model）高度匹配，该子主题涵盖世界模型、可交互模拟器、物理世界建模等核心内容。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model representation emergence through physical interaction, a top priority subtopic with weight 1.6, and aligns with boost keywords "world model" and "neural simulator". Signals: world model, semantic representation emergence, physical interaction, geometric structure, VAE-based

Abstract: What does a world model learn from physical exploration, without any linguistic supervision? We argue the answer is organized by a single principle: the geometric structure of the physical world. Training a VAE-based world model on random embodied exploration, we find that its latent space develops spatial semantic structure that mirrors physical geometry -- direction accuracy 0.677+-0.029 versus 0.547 for a randomly initialized encoder, and position RSA 0.192+-0.047 versus 0.029 for random encoders (6.6x improvement), showing that training induces genuine structural organization beyond CNN inductive bias. Across 20 temporal checkpoints, prediction performance and semantic alignment co-improve (Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004), consistent with the shared-driver account. We confirm this through a double knockout: standard KL regularization (beta=0.1) forces the encoder away from geometric structure, and both prediction performance and semantic alignment collapse simultaneously to near-chance by step 50,000 -- exactly as the shared-driver account predicts. Reducing beta to 0.001 restores geometric access and recovers both capabilities together. These findings establish physical world geometry as the organizing principle of world model representations, with direct implications for the design of semantically grounded embodied agents.

</details>


### [84] [minWM: A Full-Stack Open-Source Framework for Real-Time Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30263)
*Min Zhao, Hongzhou Zhu, Bokai Yan, Zihan Zhou, Yimin Chen, Wenqiang Sun, Kaiwen Zheng, Guande He, Xiao Yang, Chongxuan Li, Fan Bao, Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: minWM 是一个全栈开源框架，提供从数据构建、可控微调、自回归训练、少步蒸馏到流式推理的端到端流水线，将现有双向文生视频/图生视频基础模型转化为相机可控的少步自回归视频世界模型，支持实时交互式 rollout。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散基础模型在高质量视频生成上取得了显著进展，但将其转化为实时交互式视频世界模型仍面临挑战。交互式世界模型需要可控性、因果性和低延迟 rollout，这在实际中需要完整的数据构建、可控微调、自回归训练、少步蒸馏和流式推理流水线，而目前缺乏一个统一、可复现的开源框架。

Method: minWM 提出 Causal Forcing / Causal Forcing++ 流水线：首先对双向视频扩散模型进行相机控制微调，然后通过 AR 扩散训练、因果 ODE 或因果一致性蒸馏、以及非对称 DMD 将其蒸馏为少步自回归生成器。框架模块化且架构可扩展，在 Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B（交叉注意力条件注入）和 HY1.5-TI2V-8B（MMDiT 架构）上实例化，并支持适配现有视频世界模型。

Result: 提供了完整的可运行脚本、检查点、文档和推理代码，并在相机轨迹质量、可控性训练步数、最小 batch size 等方面进行了实用消融实验，验证了框架的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: minWM 提供了一个可复现且可扩展的配方，用于构建和适配实时交互式视频世界模型，填补了从视频扩散基础模型到交互式世界模型之间的工程和方法论空白。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一个构建实时交互式视频世界模型的全栈开源框架 minWM，将双向视频扩散模型转化为可交互、因果、低延迟的视频世界模型。这直接对应 multimodal_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "video_world_model"（视频世界模型），描述完全匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses the user's top-priority video world model and autoregressive video generation subtopics with a full-stack open-source framework. Signals: video world model, autoregressive video generation, real-time interactive rollout, camera control, few-step distillation

Abstract: Recent video diffusion foundation models have achieved remarkable progress in high-quality video generation, yet turning them into real-time interactive video world models remains challenging. Interactive world models require controllable, causal, and low-latency rollout, which in practice demands a full pipeline spanning data construction, controllable fine-tuning, autoregressive training, few-step distillation, and streaming inference. In this work, we present minWM, a full-stack open-source framework for building real-time interactive video world models. minWM provides an end-to-end pipeline that converts existing bidirectional T2V/TI2V video foundation models into camera-controllable few-step autoregressive world models. Specifically, minWM first fine-tunes a bidirectional video diffusion model with camera control, and then applies the Causal Forcing / Causal Forcing++ pipeline, including AR diffusion training, causal ODE or causal consistency distillation, and asymmetric DMD, to distill it into a few-step autoregressive generator for low-latency rollout. The framework is modular and architecture-extensible: we instantiate it on representative open backbones, including Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B and HY1.5-TI2V-8B, covering both cross-attention-based condition injection and MMDiT-style architectures. minWM also supports adapting existing video world models, such as HY-WorldPlay, to new data distributions, training recipes, and latency targets. Beyond releasing runnable scripts, checkpoints, documentation, and inference code, we provide practical ablations on camera trajectory quality, controllability training steps, and minimal batch-size requirements. We hope minWM serves as a reproducible and extensible recipe for building and adapting real-time interactive video world models. Project Page: [ ]( )

</details>


### [85] [Chess-World-Model: A 10M-Game Benchmark for Exact State Tracking from Chess Move Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30100)
*Benjamin Walker, Terry Lyons*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (84.79) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Chess-World-Model，一个基于 1000 万真实国际象棋对局的大规模状态跟踪基准，用于评估世界模型在动作序列中精确跟踪棋盘状态的能力。实验表明，循环神经网络（Mamba-3、Gated DeltaNet、SLiCE）在 3M 和 8M 参数下显著优于因果 Transformer；真实对局性能在 18M 参数以上饱和，但随机均匀走棋的分布外测试在 40M 参数下仍具区分力，能暴露规模掩盖的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型的状态跟踪基准多为合成或基于语言的，缺乏对结构化状态更新在真实领域中的测试能力。作者希望构建一个大规模、真实场景的状态跟踪基准，以评估模型是否真正学习到了状态转换规则而非数据中的捷径。

Method: 基于 1000 万真实国际象棋对局构建基准，包含真实对局和随机均匀合法走棋两个数据划分。在统一接口和训练协议下，对比了因果 Transformer、块对角 SLiCE、Mamba-3 和带负特征值的 Gated DeltaNet 四种模型，评估其从合法走棋序列预测精确棋盘状态的能力。

Result: 循环模型在 3M 和 8M 参数下显著优于 Transformer。真实对局性能在 18M 参数以上饱和，但随机均匀走棋的分布外测试在 40M 参数下仍具区分力。消融实验表明，表达能力较弱的状态转换机制会降低所有三种循环模型在分布外划分上的性能。

Conclusion: Chess-World-Model 是一个实用的大规模状态跟踪基准，能够暴露模型规模所掩盖的失败。循环模型在状态跟踪任务上优于 Transformer，且分布外测试是评估模型是否真正学习到状态转换规则的关键指标。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Chess-World-Model，一个基于国际象棋棋局序列的状态跟踪基准，用于评估世界模型（world model）在动作序列中维持准确隐状态的能力。这与多模态生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题"视频世界模型"（video_world_model）高度相关，因为世界模型的核心能力正是状态跟踪，且该基准旨在测试模型对状态转换规则的学习，而非仅依赖常见人类棋局的捷径。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for world model state tracking, but chess domain is less directly aligned with video world models. Signals: world model state tracking benchmark, recurrent models beat transformers, out-of-distribution test reveals scale-masked failures, 10M real games, Mamba-3, Gated DeltaNet, SLiCE

Abstract: World models require state tracking, which is the ability to maintain a correct latent state across action sequences. Existing benchmarks are often synthetic or language-based, limiting their value as tests of structured state updates in realistic domains. We introduce Chess-World-Model, a large-scale state-tracking benchmark built from 10 million real chess games, where models predict the exact board state reached after a sequence of legal moves. Alongside a held-out real-game split, we include an out-of-distribution split from uniformly random legal play, which tests whether models learn the transition rules rather than shortcuts from common human positions. Prior theoretical and empirical work has shown that Transformers struggle to state-track, while input-dependent linear RNNs require expressive state-transition matrices to do so. We therefore benchmark a causal Transformer, block-diagonal SLiCE, Mamba-3, and Gated DeltaNet with negative eigenvalues under a matched interface and training protocol. The recurrent models strongly outperform the Transformer at 3 and 8 million parameters. Real-game performance saturates above 18 million parameters, but the random-uniform split remains discriminative up to 40 million, exposing failures otherwise hidden by scale. Additionally, ablations show that less expressive state-transition mechanisms reduce performance on the out-of-distribution split for all three recurrent models. Together, these results establish Chess-World-Model as a practical large-scale benchmark for state tracking that exposes failures model scale would otherwise conceal.

</details>

### [86] [Native Audio-Visual Alignment for Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30073)
*Longbin Ji, Guan Wang, Xuan Wei, Chenye Yang, Xiangrui Liu, Zhenyu Zhang, Shuohuan Wang, Yu Sun, Jingzhou He*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 高优先级 (89.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: NAVA 提出了一种原生音视频对齐框架（Native Audio-Visual Alignment），采用 Align-then-Fuse MMDiT 架构，先建立音视频对应关系再联合去噪，并引入 Timbre-in-Context Conditioning 实现可控语音音色，仅用 6.3B 参数即取得优越的音视频同步质量和生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源音视频联合生成方法存在两个问题：双塔设计（dual-tower）后对齐方式削弱了细粒度的音视频协同演化；统一三模态设计将语义条件与底层同步耦合在一起，限制了生成质量。因此需要一种原生对齐机制来更好地建模音视频之间的对应关系。

Method: 提出 NAVA 框架，核心是 Align-then-Fuse MMDiT 架构：先通过模态感知的音视频对齐（modality-aware alignment）建立音视频对应关系，再过渡到模态共享的联合去噪（modality-shared joint denoising）。此外引入 Timbre-in-Context Conditioning，将参考音色线索与对应语音片段关联，实现可控语音音色生成。

Result: 在 Verse-Bench 和 Seed-TTS 上的实验以及用户研究表明，NAVA 在仅 6.3B 参数下实现了优越的视频质量、精确的音视频同步、有竞争力的音频质量以及更强的参考音色可控性。

Conclusion: NAVA 通过原生音视频对齐设计有效解决了现有方法中音视频协同不足的问题，验证了 Align-then-Fuse 架构在音视频联合生成中的有效性，为可控音视频生成提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究音视频联合生成（audio-video generation），属于多模态生成方向。现有 canonical subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）精确匹配论文主题，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with multimodal generation and video generation priorities, with novel alignment architecture and controllable timbre. Signals: multimodal_generation, Align-then-Fuse MMDiT, audio-video synchronization, controllable speech timbre, 6.3B parameter efficiency

Abstract: Joint audio-video generation aims to synthesize temporally synchronized and semantically coherent visual-acoustic content. However, existing open-source methods mainly rely on either dual-tower designs with posterior alignment or fully unified tri-modal designs that mix textual context, audio and video in one shared space. The former weakens fine-grained audio-video co-evolution, while the latter couples semantic conditioning with low-level synchronization. To address these limitations, we propose NAVA, a Native Audio-Visual Alignment framework for joint audio-video generation. NAVA is built upon context-conditioned native audio-visual alignment: it first establishes audio-video correspondence in a dedicated interaction space, and then uses external context to condition the joint denoising process. Specifically, NAVA is instantiated with an Align-then-Fuse MMDiT architecture, which transitions from modality-aware audio-video alignment to modality-shared joint denoising. Furthermore, we introduce Timbre-in-Context Conditioning to associate reference timbre cues with corresponding speech spans to achieve controllable speech timbre. Experiments on Verse-Bench and Seed-TTS, together with a user study, demonstrate that NAVA achieves superior video quality, precise audio-visual synchronization, competitive audio quality, and stronger reference-timbre controllability using only 6.3B parameters.

</details>


### [87] [MELD: Mel-Spectrogram-Based Speech Language Modeling with Discrete Latent Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29859)
*Sung-Lin Yeh, Wei Zhou, Gil Keren, Duc Le, Zhong Meng, Hao Tang, Jay Mahadeokar, Ozlem Kalinli, Alexandre Mourachko*

Main category: cs.CL, eess.AS

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 普通相关 (53.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出MELD，一种在梅尔频谱图上联合优化编码器和自回归语音语言模型的离散潜变量方法，在零样本TTS和STT任务上优于基于编解码器和其他梅尔频谱图的基线方法，并有效缓解了自回归梅尔频谱建模中的长时间静音和单词遗漏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音语言模型通常使用独立于自回归模型优化的编码器，导致提取的表示可能不适用于下游任务。作者希望解决编码器与语言模型分离优化带来的次优问题。

Method: 提出MELD，在梅尔频谱图上引入离散潜变量模型，将编码器与自回归语音语言模型进行联合优化，使编码器能够感知下游任务目标。

Result: 在零样本TTS和STT任务上，MELD优于基于编解码器和其他梅尔频谱图的基线方法，并有效缓解了自回归梅尔频谱建模中的长时间静音和单词遗漏问题。

Conclusion: 联合优化编码器和语音语言模型能够提升表示质量，改善下游任务性能，并解决自回归梅尔频谱建模中的常见缺陷。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于梅尔频谱图的离散潜变量语音语言模型，用于零样本TTS和STT任务，属于语音生成与多模态联合生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on speech language modeling with mel-spectrograms, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's boost keywords. Signals: Speech language model, Mel-spectrogram, Discrete latent variable, Zero-shot TTS/STT, Joint optimization

Abstract: Recent speech language models rely on encoders that are optimized separately from autoregressive models. Since these encoders are unaware of the downstream objectives, the extracted representations may not be optimal for downstream tasks. To address this limitation, we introduce a discrete latent variable model on mel spectrograms that jointly optimizes the encoder and the speech language model. Joint optimization not only brings improvements over codec-based and other mel-spectrogram-based baselines on zero-shot Text-to-Speech (TTS) and Speech-to-Text (STT) tasks, but also effectively alleviates common issues in autoregressive mel-spectrogram modeling, such as prolonged silence generation and word omissions.

</details>


### [88] [HoliTok:A Coutinuous Holistic Tokenization with Robust Dual Capabilities of Speech Generation and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29948)
*Bohan Li, Shi Lian, Hankun Wang, Yiwei Guo, Yu Xi, Zhihan Li, Da Zheng, Colin Zhang, Kai Yu*

Main category: eess.AS, cs.SD, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 普通相关 (55.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HoliTok 是一种连续全息语音分词模型，将 48kHz 语音压缩为 25Hz 的 128 维潜变量序列，通过渐进式训练策略同时保持信号保真度、语义信息和潜变量可学习性。基于该分词器构建的统一 AR+DiT 模型在语音合成与识别任务上均表现优异，是唯一无需额外优化技巧即可在统一生成-理解架构中稳健运行的表示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音分词器难以同时满足语言模型可学习性和高质量波形解码的需求，导致统一语音基础模型架构复杂、训练设计繁琐。作者希望设计一种既能保持信号级保真度又能融入语义信息且具有良好潜变量可学习性的全息语音分词器，以简化统一生成-理解建模。

Method: 提出 HoliTok 连续全息语音分词模型，将 48kHz 语音编码为 25Hz 的 128 维连续潜变量序列。采用渐进式训练策略：第一阶段训练自编码器重建信号保真度；第二阶段引入语义信息（如 HuBERT 特征蒸馏）；第三阶段优化潜变量的可学习性。基于该分词器构建统一 AR+DiT 模型，其中自回归（AR）部分负责生成建模，扩散 Transformer（DiT）部分负责高质量波形解码，支持语音合成与识别等统一任务。

Result: HoliTok 在重建保真度上达到竞争性水平；在生成任务中展现出优越的可学习性，支持高质量可控语音合成；在统一生成-理解架构中，HoliTok 是唯一无需额外优化技巧即可稳健运行的表示方法，而其他对比表示均需要特殊处理。

Conclusion: HoliTok 是一种有效的语音分词器，可作为统一口语语言建模的基础表示接口，为简化统一语音基础模型的架构设计提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出连续语音分词器 HoliTok，支持语音生成与理解统一建模，属于音频生成与多模态联合生成方向，匹配 [CANONICAL] subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成" (audio_av_generation)。同时其统一生成-理解建模也涉及多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Strong in speech tokenization but peripheral to user's core focus on visual generation and world models. Signals: Speech tokenization, Unified generation-understanding, Continuous latent representation, AR+DiT architecture, High-quality reconstruction

Abstract: Unified speech foundation models require a holistic tokenization space that is both learnable by language models and decodable into high-quality waveforms. Existing speech tokenizers, however, often fail to satisfy these requirements simultaneously, leading to increased architectural complexity and more involved training designs. We propose HoliTok, a continuous Holistic speech Tokenization model designed for unified generation-understanding modeling. HoliTok encodes 48~kHz speech into a compact 25~Hz sequence of 128-dimensional latents. It is trained with a progressive strategy that jointly preserves signal-level fidelity, incorporates semantic information, and maintains strong latent learnability. Based on this tokenization, we build a unified AR+DiT model for speech synthesis and recognition, where the same latent sequence supports both generation-specific and unified generation-understanding tasks. Experiments show that HoliTok achieves competitive reconstruction fidelity, improves generative learnability for high-quality and controllable synthesis, and, among the evaluated representations, is the only one that operates robustly in our unified generation-understanding architecture without additional optimization tricks. These results suggest that HoliTok serves as an effective speech tokenizer and a foundational representation interface for unified spoken language modeling. The code is available at: .

</details>

### [89] [ViASNet: A Video Ad Saliency Network for Predicting Dynamic Saliency and Viewer Engagement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29302)
*Jianping Ye, Michel Wedel*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (46.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出ViASNet，一种基于3D U-Net架构的视频广告显著性预测模型，能够预测动态眼动注视模式，并通过预测显著图的熵来诊断广告场景的观众参与度，在151个视频广告数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频广告在电视、社交媒体和电商平台中日益普及，理解观众在观看广告时的视觉注意力分布对于广告设计和优化至关重要。现有显著性预测模型多针对自然图像或通用视频，缺乏专门针对视频广告场景的深度动态显著性预测模型。

Method: 提出ViASNet（Video Ad Saliency Network），基于3D U-Net架构，能够同时处理时空信息，并融合音频特征和场景语义信息。模型以视频帧序列为输入，输出逐帧的动态显著性图。此外，利用预测显著图的熵作为诊断工具，逐帧计算熵值以识别未能吸引观众注意力的广告和场景。

Result: 在151个视频广告数据集上评估，每个广告约有20名观看者的眼动追踪数据。通过消融实验分析了影响模型性能的关键因素，并在15个未见广告的测试数据上展示了熵诊断工具的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于ViASNet等深度显著性模型的自动化系统可以显著加速广告设计和测试流程，为视频广告的内容优化提供量化工具。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频广告的显著性预测模型，属于广告/营销领域的垂直应用，与"领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）这一现有子主题匹配，该子主题涵盖医疗、法律、科学、代码等垂直领域生成应用，广告内容优化可归入此类。

Importance reason: Focuses on a narrow ad saliency application with no connection to user's priority generative or efficiency topics. Signals: video saliency prediction, 3D U-Net, ad engagement, no generative modeling, no autoregressive/diffusion methods

Abstract: The digital media landscape has seen a pervasive shift toward short-form video advertising on TV, social media and e-commerce platforms. The present study focuses on deep saliency prediction for short-form video advertising. Deep saliency models have been used to generate predictions of human eye fixation patterns with the purpose of enhancing user interaction with digital technology and optimizing its design. For video ads, dynamic saliency maps capture where and when viewers are looking, revealing why video ads are effective, and how their content should be optimized. We develop and test a new deep dynamic saliency prediction model called ViASNet (Video Ad Saliency Network), which has an architecture founded on the 3D U-Net, and accommodates the influence of audio and the semantic meaning of scenes. We assess the model's performance on 151 video ads, each seen by about 20 viewers wile their eye movements were tracked, and explore the critical factors influencing model performance through ablation experiments. We calculate the entropy of the predicted saliency maps frame-by-frame as a diagnostic tool to identify ads and scenes that fail to engage viewers, and illustrate its use on test data of 15 unseen ads. Our study reveals that ad design and testing can be sped up considerably through automated systems built on deep saliency models such as ViASNet.

</details>


### [90] [Probabilistic bias adjustment of seasonal forecasts using generative machine learning: A case study of Arctic sea ice predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29172)
*Parsa Gooya, Reinel Sospedra-Alfonso*

Main category: cs.LG, physics.ao-ph

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (48.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于条件变分自编码器（cVAE）的概率性后处理框架，用于对季节性气候预测（以北极海冰预测为例）进行偏差校正。通过用生成器替代高斯参数化解码器、使用连续排序概率评分（CRPS）替代均方误差损失、以及采用更高分辨率的目标数据集，该方法能够生成任意大规模、校准良好、偏差校正的预测集合，同时改善标准cVAE的模糊性问题，提升预测的锐度和谱能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 季节性气候预测对规划与风险管理至关重要，但集合预报存在系统性偏差和时空误差，且随预报提前时间增长而加剧。传统后处理校准方法（如标准cVAE）存在预测模糊、丢失细尺度能量的问题。因此需要一种能够生成大规模、校准良好且保持细尺度特征的偏差校正方法。

Method: 基于条件变分自编码器（cVAE）的概率后处理框架，核心改进包括：(1) 用生成器（generator）替代标准cVAE中的高斯参数化解码器；(2) 在目标函数中使用连续排序概率评分（CRPS）替代均方误差（MSE）；(3) 使用比原始预报更高分辨率的目标数据集。模型学习以有偏模型预测为条件的观测分布，从而生成任意大规模、校准良好的偏差校正预报集合。

Result: 实验表明，调整后的预报在校准性、与观测分布的一致性以及误差方面均优于基准预测。相比标准cVAE，该方法在提升原始预报分辨率的同时，改善了预测的锐度和谱能量，有效缓解了标准cVAE的模糊性问题。

Conclusion: 基于生成式机器学习（cVAE）的概率偏差校正框架能够有效提升季节性气候预测的质量，生成大规模、校准良好且保持细尺度特征的预报集合。该方法为气候预测的后处理提供了一种有前景的生成式解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文将生成式机器学习（条件变分自编码器）应用于气候科学领域中的北极海冰季节性预测偏差校正，属于垂直领域（气候/地球科学）的生成应用。虽然该论文不涉及图像/视频等典型多模态生成，但其核心方法是生成模型（cVAE）在科学预测中的领域应用，与 multimodal_generation 方向下的非 canonical 子主题 "领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on climate forecast bias correction, not aligned with user's priority subtopics in generative AI and video/image generation. Signals: climate science, seasonal forecasting, cVAE, bias correction, not generative AI core

Abstract: Seasonal climate predictions support planning and risk management by offering early information of the most likely-to-occur climate conditions in the coming months, and associated uncertainties. Ensemble forecasts enable this by simulating many plausible outcomes, allowing predictions to be expressed as usable probabilities. Large ensembles and high-resolution forecasts strengthen this guidance by better sampling uncertainty and capturing finer-scale processes but come with significant computational cost. Moreover, forecast ensembles drift and exhibit systematic biases and spatio-temporal errors that grow with lead time, requiring careful post-processing and calibration. A probabilistic post-processing framework based on conditional Variational Autoencoders (cVAEs) was developed at the Canadian Center for Climate Modeling and Analysis to generate large ensembles of bias adjusted seasonal predictions of Arctic sea ice. The generative model was designed to learn the observational distribution conditioned on the biased model prediction. This enables generation of arbitrarily large ensembles of well-calibrated, bias corrected forecasts with improved skill. Here, we extend this framework to address the loss of fine-scale energy and the characteristic blurriness in predictions, a known limitation of standard cVAEs. Specifically, we employ a generator in place of the Gaussian parametrized decoder in the cVAE and use Continuous Ranked Probability Score in the objective function instead of the Mean Square Error. We further use a higher resolution target dataset compared to the raw forecast. We show that the adjusted forecasts are better calibrated, more consistent with the observational distribution, and exhibit smaller errors than benchmark predictions, while also enhancing the resolution of the raw forecasts and improving sharpness and spectral power relative to the standard cVAE.

</details>

Result: 在感知、分割和规划等多个自动驾驶任务上，CityGen 生成的跨城市场景数据一致提升了模型的跨城市鲁棒性，验证了该框架作为可扩展、标签高效的自动驾驶通用化基础的有效性。

Conclusion: CityGen 提供了一种无需目标域标签的跨城市自动驾驶场景生成方案，通过 HD-map 条件与城市视觉提示引导扩散模型合成目标城市数据，有效缓解了跨城市部署中的域偏移问题，为通用化自动驾驶建立了可扩展的基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于扩散模型的自动驾驶跨城市场景生成框架 CityGen，属于自动驾驶这一垂直领域的生成应用。虽然 multimodal_generation 方向下也有 aigc_domain_application 子主题，但该子主题在 aigc 方向下为 [CANONICAL] 条目，且论文核心是生成内容（合成驾驶场景），与 AIGC 的领域内容生成应用高度匹配，因此复用该 canonical 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on domain-specific autonomous driving generation, not core multimodal generation or video generation. Signals: diffusion-based generation, HD-map conditioned synthesis, cross-city autonomous driving, zero-label adaptation, not autoregressive or video generation

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems are commonly trained and evaluated within limited geographic regions, which hinders their scalability when deployed in new cities. However, significant domain shifts in appearance, road topology, and traffic patterns often cause severe performance degradation under cross-city deployment. Existing approaches based on domain adaptation, data augmentation, or synthetic data generation typically rely on labeled target data, city-specific annotations, or task-specific designs, limiting their scalability and effectiveness for holistic evaluation. In this paper, we introduce CityTransfer-Bench, a geographically disjoint benchmark for evaluating cross-city generalization across perception, segmentation, and planning, and propose CityGen, a diffusion-based generative framework that performs zero-label city adaptation via HD-map-conditioned synthesis guided by city-level visual prompts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CityGen consistently improves cross-city robustness across multiple tasks, establishing a scalable and label-efficient foundation for generalizable autonomous driving.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经退行性疾病进展预测系统通常只产生标量临床评分，忽略了纵向神经影像的丰富结构信息；而传统生成方法则存在解剖细节丢失和模糊化问题，难以捕捉细微的疾病进展模式。

Method: 提出治疗条件扩散框架（Treatment-Conditioned Diffusion），以患者基线DaTscan影像和一年内左旋多巴等效日剂量（LEDD）为条件，预测未来脑部SPECT影像。使用Transformer编码器建模非线性的时间依赖药理学动态，并设计多权重区域兴趣（ROI）掩码，在扩散生成过程中聚焦生物关键脑区。

Result: 相比基线方法，该方法在MSE上降低14.0%，MAE降低7.2%，SSIM提高4.9%，能够保持清晰的解剖边界并显著提升临床保真度。

Conclusion: 治疗条件扩散框架能够有效预测神经退行性疾病进展中的高保真脑部影像，为个性化治疗干预和长期规划提供了有前景的工具。

Abstract: Forecasting the progression of neurodegenerative diseases, such as Parkinson's disease, is essential for effective long-term planning and personalized therapeutic intervention. Existing systems typically produce scalar clinical scores that ignore the rich structure of longitudinal neuroimaging, while traditional generative approaches suffer from a loss of anatomical details and blurring subtle progression patterns. To address this, we introduce a novel treatment-conditioned diffusion framework that predicts high-fidelity future brain states by conditioning the generative process on patients' screening DaTscan images and levodopa equivalent daily dose over one year. The pipeline uses a Transformer-based encoder to represent non-linear, time-dependent pharmacological dynamics and optimizes generation through a multi-weight region-of-interest mask that focuses on biologically critical areas. Experimental evaluation shows that our framework maintains sharp anatomical boundaries and significantly improves clinical fidelity relative to the baseline, achieving 14.0% lower MSE, 7.2% lower MAE, and 4.9% higher SSIM.

</details>
