Abstract: Multi-component LLM agents assemble probabilistic claims from components that each see only part of a joint problem; the composition can violate basic probability axioms even when every component is locally coherent. We formalise this locally coherent, globally incoherent failure via the compositional residual eps*, the L2 distance from the composed quote to the joint coherent polytope, computable at runtime from system output and the declared cross-component coupling constraints. A product-structure dichotomy characterises when local coherence suffices, and a Rayleigh-quotient prediction matches the observed residual within 7% on three of four relation classes. A hierarchical Boyle-Dykstra projection repairs the composition deterministically; an anytime-valid e-process gives sequential coherence monitoring. Across 1,876 ensemble cliques on a four-LLM mid-tier panel (frontier-panel rerun in Section 5.5), eps* > 0 on 33-94% of cliques, translating to +0.115 nats per bet of regret on 1,770 resolved bets under the proportional allocation rule (the gain collapses to +0.006 under bettors that themselves coherentise). Three intuitive LLM-side mitigations(retrieval, partition-aware prompting, aggregator-LLM) each fail or regress.

</details>


### [122] [Towards Verifiable Multimodal Deep Research: A Multi-Agent Harness for Interleaved Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29861)
*Chenghao Zhang, Guanting Dong, Yufan Liu, Tong Zhao, Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 普通相关 (53.1) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Ptah，一个多智能体框架，用于生成可验证的多模态深度研究报告。Ptah 通过规划、研究和写作三个阶段，利用视觉感知规划、基于证据的检索、视觉工作记忆和声明式多模态工具使用，生成交织文本与图像证据的网页报告。验证智能体确保事实依据、引用忠实性和跨模态一致性。同时提出 PtahEval 评估协议，实验表明 Ptah 生成的报告比强基线更可靠、视觉信息更丰富、更易用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 驱动的深度搜索能检索简洁的事实答案，但深度研究需要将分散证据综合成长篇报告，且多模态深度研究面临两大挑战：开放式的综合缺乏确定性真实值，以及需要将文本论证与视觉证据交织呈现。目前缺乏能够生成可验证、视觉信息丰富的多模态深度研究报告的系统。

Method: Ptah 是一个多智能体框架，包含规划、研究和写作三个阶段。规划阶段：视觉感知规划智能体分析用户查询并生成视觉感知的研究计划。研究阶段：证据收集智能体基于声明进行检索，维护源对齐的图像在"视觉工作记忆"中。写作阶段：通过声明式多模态工具使用（如插入图像、引用来源）生成交织文本与图像的网页报告。验证智能体作为验收函数，在整个工作流中强制执行事实依据、引用忠实性和跨模态一致性。同时提出 PtahEval 评估协议，在现有基准上增加图像级和呈现级评估。

Result: 在深度研究基准上的实验表明，Ptah 生成的报告比强基线更可靠、视觉信息更丰富、更易用。具体来说，Ptah 在事实准确性、引用质量、视觉信息丰富度和整体可用性方面均优于对比方法。

Conclusion: Ptah 通过多智能体编排和验证机制，有效解决了可验证多模态深度研究中的关键挑战，生成了更可靠、视觉信息更丰富的多模态报告。该工作为自动化深度研究提供了新的范式，验证智能体的引入确保了生成内容的事实性和跨模态一致性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多智能体框架 Ptah，用于自动化生成交织文本与视觉证据的深度研究报告，属于 AI 智能体驱动的生成工作流，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic aigc_agent_workflow（AI 智能体与生成工作流）高度匹配。同时涉及多模态理解与生成方向（报告生成需理解图文并生成内容）。

Importance reason: Strong multi-agent framework for verifiable multimodal report generation, but focuses on AI agents and workflows rather than core generative model advances. Signals: multimodal deep research, multi-agent framework, verifiable report generation, visual working memory, PtahEval evaluation protocol

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced autonomous agents from deep search, which retrieves concise factual answers, to deep research, which synthesizes scattered evidence into long-form reports. However, verifiable multimodal deep research remains challenging due to open-ended synthesis without deterministic ground truth and the need to interleave textual arguments with visual evidence. We propose \textsc{Ptah}, a multi-agent harness for interleaved report generation. \textsc{Ptah} orchestrates the lifecycle from user query to rendered web report through planning, research, and writing stages, where specialized agents construct visual-aware plans, collect claim-grounded evidence, maintain source-aligned images in a \textit{Visual Working Memory}, and compose reports through declarative multimodal tool use. A verifier agent serves as the harness's acceptance function, enforcing factual grounding, citation fidelity, and cross-modal consistency throughout the workflow. We further introduce \textsc{Ptah}Eval, an evaluation protocol that augments existing benchmarks with image-level and presentation-level assessments. Experiments on deep research benchmarks show that \textsc{Ptah} produces more reliable, visually informative, and usable human-facing multimodal reports than strong baselines.

</details>


### [123] [GenClaw: Code-Driven Agentic Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30248)
*Junyan Ye, Jun He, Zilong Huang, Dongzhi Jiang, Xuan Yang, Rui Chen, Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 值得关注 (77.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GenClaw 提出了一种代码驱动的智能体图像生成范式，将图像生成过程分解为概念构思、代码草图渲染和纹理补充三个阶段，利用代码（SVG/HTML）作为可控中间画布，将语言推理与生成模型的视觉表现力相结合，实现了更可控、可解释的图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成智能体受限于底层黑盒模型，工作流陷入反复改写提示词的循环，无法直接操控画布，缺乏对视觉构建的精确控制能力。LLM 作为真正"画笔"的潜力尚未被充分挖掘。

Method: GenClaw 将图像生成分为三个阶段：(1) 概念构思阶段——智能体通过搜索和推理构建概念知识与上下文；(2) 代码草图阶段——利用代码（如 SVG、HTML）渲染可执行的视觉草图，代码作为可控中间画布；(3) 纹理补充阶段——使用图像生成模型补充纹理、材质和照片级真实感。代码桥接了语言推理与像素合成，实现了程序化逻辑与生成模型视觉表现力的无缝集成。

Result: GenClaw 将图像生成从黑盒范式转变为类似人类创作的分阶段过程，在可控性和可解释性方面取得了进展，实现了更精确的视觉构建。

Conclusion: GenClaw 通过代码驱动的分阶段生成范式，为高度可控和可解释的视觉生成系统迈出了重要一步，展示了将程序化逻辑与生成模型相结合的巨大潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 GenClaw，一种代码驱动的智能体图像生成范式，将图像生成分解为概念构思、代码草图（SVG/HTML）和纹理渲染三个阶段，属于 AI 智能体驱动的自动化创作工作流，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 aigc_agent_workflow（AI 智能体与生成工作流）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a code-driven agentic image generation paradigm that enhances controllability and interpretability, aligning with user's priority on controllable generation and AI agents. Signals: code-driven generation, agentic image generation, controllable generation, SVG/HTML intermediate canvas, staged generation process

Abstract: Image generation models have evolved from text-conditioned pixel synthesis toward multimodal agents endowed with visual comprehension and tool invocation capabilities. Yet, existing agents remain at the mercy of underlying black-box image models. Their workflow is trapped in a repetitive cycle of prompt rewriting for generation refinement, leaving them with no mechanism to directly manipulate the canvas. In essence, the potential of LLMs to serve as a genuine "brush" for precise visual construction remains largely untapped. In this paper, we propose GenClaw, a code-driven agentic image generation paradigm that empowers the agent to create like a human artist: first conceptualizing, then sketching, and finally coloring. Specifically, the agent first constructs the conceptual knowledge and context through search and reasoning. It then utilizes code (e.g., SVG, HTML, ) to render executable visual sketches. Finally, it employs an image generation model to supplement textures, materials, and photorealism. In this workflow, code serves as a controllable intermediate canvas bridging linguistic reasoning and pixel synthesis, seamlessly integrating programmatic logic with the visual expressiveness of generative models. By transforming image generation from a black-box paradigm into a staged process akin to authentic human creation, GenClaw offers a step toward for highly controllable and interpretable visual generation systems.

</details>


### [124] [CONCAT: Consensus- and Confidence-Driven Ad Hoc Teaming for Efficient LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29612)
*Ziyang Ma, Dingyi Zhang, Sichu Liang, Jiajia Chu, Pengfei Xia, Hui Zang, Deyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.MA

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 普通相关 (45.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CONCAT提出一种无需训练的LLM多智能体协作框架，通过基于共识和置信度的临时组队机制，在保持性能的同时显著提升效率，减少50%以上的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统虽然性能优于单智能体，但智能体间大量通信导致巨大计算开销。先前方法需要额外训练稀疏图或规划器，不仅增加成本还限制了泛化能力。因此需要一种无需训练、高效且通用的多智能体协作框架。

Method: CONCAT框架包含三个步骤：(1) 基于智能体初始答案进行聚类，并根据置信度选择每个聚类的领导者；(2) 基于心理理论设计启发式函数，根据领导者的答案和置信度预测两两协作的收益；(3) 根据预测收益裁剪部分通信，构建临时多智能体网络。整个过程无需任何任务特定训练。

Result: 在三个LLM和三个基准上的实验表明，CONCAT相比LLM-Debate实现了高达2.02倍的效率提升（准确率/延迟比），优于AgentDropout等训练感知方法，在Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct上平均延迟降低50.1%。

Conclusion: CONCAT证明了无需额外训练即可高效组织多智能体协作，通过共识和置信度驱动的临时组队策略，在保持甚至提升任务性能的同时大幅降低通信开销，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于LLM的多智能体协作框架CONCAT，属于LLM智能体与生成工作流范畴，与[AIGC]方向下的[AI 智能体与生成工作流]（aigc_agent_workflow）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel training-free efficiency method for LLM multi-agent systems, but outside user's core generative model priorities. Signals: multi-agent efficiency, training-free collaboration, latency reduction, consensus-driven, theory of mind

Abstract: Although large language model (LLM) based multi-agent systems (MAS) show their capability to solve complex tasks and achieve higher performance over single agent systems, they lead to huge computational overheads because of heavy communication between agents. Previous research has made efforts to train a sparse multi-agent graph or fine-tune a planner to orchestrate the workflow better. However, such extra training processes introduce computational costs and limit MAS to specific domains, therefore compromising their generalizability. In this paper, we propose CONCAT, a training-free multi-agent collaboration framework based on CONsensus and Confidence-driven Ad hoc Teaming to efficiently organize agent interactions. Specifically, agents are clustered based on their initial answers, and leaders of each cluster are selected based on the agents' confidence. Then, a heuristic function based on the Theory of Mind is designed to predict the collaboration benefits between every two leaders according to their answers and confidence. Finally, an ad hoc multi-agent network is organized after evicting a percentage of communications based on the predicted benefits. Experiments across three LLMs and three benchmarks show that CONCAT achieves up to 2.02x higher efficiency (accuracy/latency ratio) than LLM-Debate and outperforms training-aware methods such as AgentDropout, while reducing average latency by 50.1% on Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct, without any task-specific training.

</details>


### [125] [Evolve as a Team: Collaborative Self-Evolution for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29790)
*Zhezheng Hao, Tianfu Wang, Huanshuo Dong, Ziyan Liu, Hong Wang, Xiankun Lin, Qiang Lin, Can Wang, Hande Dong, Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.MA, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 普通相关 (43.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出Meta-Team框架，通过协作自演化机制让基于LLM的多智能体系统从自身执行经验中学习改进，包括行为级、协调级和团队级的多尺度演化，在多个长周期任务基准上优于单智能体、手工MAS和现有MAS演化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统在复杂长周期任务中常出现各种执行失败，且这些失败难以在设计阶段消除。现有方法缺乏让系统从自身执行经验中自动学习和改进的有效机制。

Method: 提出Meta-Team框架，包含两个核心设计：(1) 保留每个智能体的执行上下文并在任务后协调通信，使智能体能够交换分布式证据用于演化；(2) 多尺度自演化机制，将执行经验转化为三个层面的可复用改进：个体行为级、智能体间协调级和团队级组织。

Result: 在六个长周期智能体基准测试中，Meta-Team一致优于单智能体系统、手工设计的MAS以及先前的MAS演化方法。进一步分析表明Meta-Team实现了更可靠和可扩展的MAS自演化。

Conclusion: Meta-Team通过协作自演化有效提升了多智能体系统的性能和可靠性，为经验驱动的MAS演化提供了可行框架。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于LLM的多智能体系统的协作自演化框架，属于AI智能体与生成工作流范畴，与[AIGC]方向下的[AI 智能体与生成工作流]（aigc_agent_workflow）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on multi-agent system evolution, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in generation models. Signals: multi-agent systems, self-evolution, LLM-based agents, long-horizon tasks, collaborative learning

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as an effective paradigm for complex and long-horizon tasks. However, in real-world tasks, MAS often exhibit various failures during execution and such failures are difficult to eliminate during design. This motivates experience-driven MAS evolution, where a system improves based on its own execution experience. Yet such evolution is challenging because MAS experience is prolonged and intricate, interleaving multiple agents' execution chains and communication messages, which makes it difficult to identify what should be improved. To address this challenge, we propose Meta-Team, an experience-driven MAS evolution framework based on collaborative self-evolution. Meta-Team preserves the execution context of each agent and coordinates post-task communication, enabling agents to exchange distributed evidence for evolution. Building on this design, Meta-Team conducts multi-scale self-evolution, transforming execution experience into reusable improvements to agent behaviors, inter-agent coordination, and team-level organization. Across six long-horizon agent benchmarks, Meta-Team consistently outperforms single-agent systems, hand-crafted MAS, and prior MAS evolution methods; further analyses demonstrate that Meta-Team enables more reliable and scalable MAS self-evolution.

</details>


### [126] [Evolutionary Dynamics of Cooperation in Next-Generation LLM Agent Systems: A Cross-Provider Empirical Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29874)
*Francisco León Zúñiga Bolívar*

Main category: cs.GT, cs.MA, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Willis等人的基准研究，使用演化博弈论和迭代囚徒困境（IPD）框架，测试了2025-2026年发布的四个前沿LLM模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT-5.4 Mini）在多智能体竞争环境中的合作偏向。研究发现合作偏向在多数模型-提示组合中持续存在，但跨提供商差异显著：Gemini 2.5 Flash在偏向条件下达到77%的侵略性均衡，而GPT-5.4 Mini在Self-Refine提示下达到70%的合作均衡。提供商身份比模型代际更能预测均衡结果，噪声仍然是普遍挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证下一代LLM智能体是否继承了前代模型的合作偏向，以及模型规模扩大和提供商多样性是否会重塑多智能体竞争环境中的均衡行为。

Method: 采用与Willis等人相同的演化博弈论协议，使用迭代囚徒困境（IPD）作为博弈框架，在三种提示风格（Default、Prose、Self-Refine）和四种群体组成（平衡/偏向，有无噪声）条件下，对四个前沿LLM模型进行系统测试，通过Moran迭代（每条件500次）分析均衡结果。

Result: 合作偏向在9/12的模型-提示组合中持续存在；Gemini 2.5 Flash在偏向条件下达到77%侵略性均衡，GPT-5.4 Mini在Self-Refine下达到70%合作均衡；Self-Refine提升了所有模型的ICD（内部合作偏差），Claude Sonnet 4.6 Refine达到最高ICD（0.913）；噪声敏感性方面，Claude Sonnet 4.6约6个百分点，Claude 3.5 Sonnet约13个百分点，但差异未达统计显著性。

Conclusion: 提供商身份而非模型代际是均衡结果的最强预测因子；噪声仍然是所有模型面临的普遍挑战，不受模型规模或代际影响；Self-Refine提示能提升合作偏向但无法消除提供商间差异。

Classification reason: 论文研究下一代LLM智能体在多智能体博弈（囚徒困境）中的合作行为演化，属于LLM智能体行为分析与博弈研究，与AIGC方向下的"AI智能体与生成工作流"（aigc_agent_workflow）子主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM agent cooperation in game theory, not on the user's priority subtopics like video generation or model efficiency. Signals: LLM agent cooperation, evolutionary game theory, cross-provider comparison, noise robustness, not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Do next-generation LLM agents inherit the cooperative biases documented in their predecessors, or does scale and provider diversity reshape equilibrium behaviour in competitive multi-agent settings? Willis et al. established a benchmark for this question using evolutionary game theory and the Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma (IPD), finding consistent cooperative biases in ChatGPT-4o and Claude 3.5 Sonnet. We extend this benchmark to four frontier models released in 2025-2026 - Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and GPT-5.4 Mini - applying the identical protocol across three prompting styles (Default, Prose, Self-Refine) and four population compositions (balanced and biased, with and without noise). Cooperative bias persists across providers (H1): nine of twelve model-prompt combinations favour cooperative equilibria in balanced noiseless conditions. Cross-provider divergence is substantial (H3): Gemini 2.5 Flash reaches up to 77% aggressive equilibria under biased conditions, while GPT-5.4 Mini reaches 70% cooperative equilibria under Self-Refine. Support for aggressive capability parity is partial (H2): Self-Refine raises ICD in all models and Claude Sonnet 4.6 Refine achieves the highest ICD in the dataset (0.913), but Default and Prose prompts show no systematic narrowing. Evidence on noise robustness is directionally positive but not robustly confirmed (H4): with n=500 Moran iterations per condition, average noise sensitivity is approximately 6 percentage points for Claude Sonnet 4.6 versus 13 pp for Claude 3.5 Sonnet, but this cross-study gap is not statistically significant once the predecessor's unreported sampling error is propagated. Provider identity, rather than model generation, is the strongest correlate of equilibrium outcomes; noise remains a universal challenge regardless of model size or vintage.

</details>


### [127] [Agora: Toward Autonomous Bug Detection in Production-Level Consensus Protocols with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29910)
*Xiang Liu, Sa Song, Zhaowei Zhang, Huiying Lan, Jason Zeng, Ming Wu, Michael Heinrich, Yong Sun, Ceyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 普通相关 (35.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Agora 是一个面向生产级共识协议的领域感知多智能体框架，利用 LLM 智能体协作探索协议状态空间、合成攻击场景并迭代验证，在 Raft、EPaxos、HotStuff、BullShark 四个协议中发现了 15 个此前未知的协议级逻辑漏洞，而现有基于 LLM 的方法未能检测到任何此类漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共识协议是实现分布式系统和区块链的基础，其中的实现漏洞可能导致数据损坏和财务损失。现有基于 LLM 的代码分析方法难以检测涉及多执行阶段复杂状态依赖的深层协议级逻辑漏洞。

Method: Agora 采用领域感知的多智能体框架，包含多个专门智能体：状态空间探索智能体、攻击场景合成智能体（利用领域特定约束）和验证智能体，通过迭代精炼协作完成协议验证。智能体之间明确分工，使其能够推理全局协议不变量，超越单函数代码分析的能力。

Result: 在 Raft、EPaxos、HotStuff、BullShark 四个共识协议实现上，使用四种 SOTA LLM 进行评测。Agora 发现了 15 个此前未知的违反安全属性的协议级逻辑漏洞，而现有基于 LLM 的智能体方法未能检测到任何此类协议级逻辑漏洞。

Conclusion: 领域感知的多智能体协作对于检测复杂协议中的深层逻辑漏洞至关重要，Agora 框架证明了将假设驱动测试与 LLM 能力相结合进行系统性协议验证的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于多智能体框架的共识协议漏洞检测系统，属于 LLM 智能体在代码分析与自动化验证中的应用，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "AI 智能体与生成工作流" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM agents for consensus protocol bug detection, which is outside the user's core interests in generative AI and multimodal models. Signals: LLM agents, consensus protocol verification, bug detection, multi-agent framework, not generative AI

Abstract: Consensus protocols form the backbone of distributed systems and blockchains, where implementation bugs can cause data corruption and financial losses. While LLM-based approaches show promise in code analysis, they struggle with deep protocol-level logic bugs involving complex state-dependent behaviors across multiple execution stages. We present Agora, a domain-aware multi-agent framework that integrates hypothesis-driven testing with LLM capabilities for systematic protocol verification. Agora employs specialized agents that collaboratively explore protocol state spaces, synthesize attack scenarios using domain-specific constraints, and validate findings through iterative refinement. This explicit role separation enables reasoning about global protocol invariants beyond single-function code analysis. We evaluate Agora on four consensus implementations (Raft, EPaxos, HotStuff, BullShark) using four state-of-the-art LLMs. Agora discovers 15 previously unknown protocol-level logic bugs that violate safety properties, while existing LLM-based agents fail to detect any such protocol-level logic bugs. Our results demonstrate that domain-aware multi-agent collaboration is essential for detecting deep logic bugs in complex protocols.

</details>


### [128] [When Cloud Agents Meet Device Agents: Lessons from Hybrid Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30102)
*Corrado Rainone, Davide Belli, Bence Major, Arash Behboodi*

Main category: cs.MA, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 普通相关 (45.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了混合多智能体系统（Hybrid MAS）的设计空间，探索了云端大语言模型（LLM）与设备端小语言模型（SLM）协同推理的权衡。作者将两种代表性MAS架构适配为支持混合推理，分析了任务准确率、货币成本和边缘能耗之间的帕累托前沿，发现SLM能从LLM辅助中获益，但最优架构高度依赖具体任务，且更大的前沿计算并不总能带来更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合多智能体系统（云端LLM+设备端SLM）在任务准确率、成本和能耗之间存在复杂的耦合关系，目前缺乏通用的设计原则，通常只能通过特定领域的临时决策引入混合组件。本文旨在系统性地探索这一设计空间，为混合MAS提供更通用的设计指导。

Method: 作者将两种代表性MAS架构（可能是基于LLM协调的架构和基于投票/共识的架构）适配为支持混合推理模式，通过系统性地改变设计选择（如任务分配策略、模型选择、通信协议等），在帕累托前沿上研究功率、成本和性能之间的权衡关系。

Result: 研究发现：SLM可以有效受益于LLM的辅助；最优架构高度依赖具体任务；更大的前沿级计算并不总能带来更好的性能提升；混合组件的引入需要在多个维度上进行精细权衡。

Conclusion: 混合多智能体系统的设计是一个复杂的多目标优化问题，不存在通用的最优方案。未来的混合MAS设计需要根据具体任务需求，在性能、成本和能耗之间找到合适的平衡点。

Classification reason: 论文研究混合多智能体系统（云端LLM + 设备端SLM）的设计空间，属于AI智能体与生成工作流范畴，与aigc_agent_workflow（AI智能体与生成工作流）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on hybrid multi-agent system design for cloud/device LLM/SLM, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in generative models and video/image generation. Signals: Hybrid multi-agent systems, cloud-device LLM/SLM tradeoffs, Pareto frontier analysis, task-dependent optimal architecture, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: The design space of agentic AI inference spans two extremes: frontier large language models (LLMs), typically hosted in the cloud and offering strong performance across a wide range of tasks at substantially high cost, and more cost-efficient small language models (SLMs), which are amenable to on-device inference. Hybrid multi-agent systems (MASs) combining on-device and cloud models offer a promising middle ground, but they also introduce a complex and poorly understood design space in which task accuracy, monetary cost, and edge energy consumption are tightly coupled; in the absence of general design principles, hybrid components, although not the most prevalent choice, are typically introduced through ad hoc decisions tailored to specific domains. In this work, we examine this design space more systematically. We adapt two representative MAS architectures to support hybrid inference and study how individual design choices shift the operating point along the Pareto frontier of power, cost, and performance. Our findings paint a nuanced picture of hybrid MAS design: while SLMs can effectively benefit from LLM assistance, the optimal architecture is highly task-dependent, and greater frontier-level compute does not consistently translate to better performance.

</details>


### [129] [Unifying Temporal and Structural Credit Assignment in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30227)
*Wenwu Li, Yuran Song, Mingze Zhao, Bo Jin, Wenhao Li*

Main category: cs.MA, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 普通相关 (45.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向LLM多智能体系统的时序与结构信用分配方法，通过状态空间瓶颈识别关键轮次、稳态角色策略隔离智能体贡献，并引入离散化的言语化块坐标下降算法进行迭代优化，在多个推理基准上显著降低查询复杂度并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统（MAS）虽能通过协作交互增强LLM的复杂推理能力，但其计算图离散不可微且全局监督信号稀疏，现有黑盒优化方法难以将轨迹级失败归因到具体局部组件，导致优化效率低、方差大。因此需要引入结构归纳偏置来解耦误差信号。

Method: 提出时序与结构信用分配方法：(1) 时序信用——利用状态空间瓶颈识别关键轮次；(2) 结构信用——利用稳态角色策略隔离各智能体贡献。基于分解后的信号，引入离散化的言语化块坐标下降算法，交替优化角色提示和聚合协议，使用LLM生成的"代理梯度"仅针对识别出的薄弱环节进行迭代改进。

Result: 在多个推理基准上，该方法显著降低了查询复杂度，同时提升了性能，提供了可解释的自改进路径。

Conclusion: 本文为LLM多智能体系统的优化提供了一种有原则、可解释的方法，通过时序与结构信用分解实现了更高效的迭代优化，为自改进MAS的发展奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于LLM的多智能体系统（MAS）的提示优化方法，属于AI智能体与生成工作流范畴，与[AI 智能体与生成工作流]（aigc_agent_workflow）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on multi-agent prompt optimization, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in generation and efficiency. Signals: multi-agent systems, credit assignment, prompt optimization, LLM reasoning, block coordinate descent

Abstract: While Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) empower Large Language Models to tackle complex reasoning tasks through collaborative interaction, optimizing their dynamics remains a formidable challenge due to the discrete, non-differentiable nature of the computation graph and the sparsity of global supervisory signals. Existing black-box optimizers struggle to attribute trajectory-level failure to specific local components, resulting in inefficient, high-variance exploration. We argue that tractable MAS optimization needs structural inductive biases to disentangle error signals. We propose temporal and structural credit assignment, which decomposes the objective along two axes: (i) temporal credit, using state-space bottlenecks to identify critical rounds, and (ii) structural credit, using stationary role policies to isolate agent contributions. Leveraging these decomposed signals, we introduce a discrete, verbalized block coordinate descent algorithm for iterative refinement. Rather than indiscriminate global updates, it alternates between optimizing role prompts and aggregation protocols, using LLM-generated "proxy gradients" to target only the identified weak links. Across diverse reasoning benchmarks, our approach substantially reduces query complexity while improving performance, providing a principled and interpretable path toward self-improving MAS.

</details>

### [130] [Auditing Training Data in Generative Music Models via Black-Box Membership Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29202)
*Yi Chen Liu, Jiawei Yu, Kexin Cao, Syed Irfan Ali Meerza, Trishika Movva, Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 检测与溯源

Importance: 普通相关 (45.62) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种黑盒成员推断方法，用于审计生成式音乐模型的训练数据。核心思路是：训练过的样本在给定文本描述条件下，与模型生成结果之间具有更强的语义和结构对齐。通过影子模型构建配对样本，训练一个"音乐审计器"来分类成员与非成员，在多个SOTA音乐生成模型上达到最高98.6%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到音乐生成模型通常在大规模数据集上训练，但数据来源和训练透明度不足，缺乏实际机制来验证特定音频样本是否被用于训练。这引发了关于数据来源、同意和训练透明度的担忧。

Method: 利用训练成员与非成员在候选音频与模型条件生成输出之间的语义/结构对齐差异。具体地：1) 用候选音频的文本描述查询目标模型获得生成音频；2) 在学习的特征空间中度量候选音频与生成音频之间的关系；3) 使用影子模型构建配对样本（候选音频+条件生成），训练一个"音乐审计器"来分类成员身份。该方法完全黑盒，无需访问模型参数或训练元数据。

Result: 在多个SOTA音乐生成模型上，该方法达到最高98.6%的准确率，假阳性率低至1.9%，假阴性率低至1.0%，证明了在实际部署场景中可靠的训练数据审计是可行的。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在完全黑盒设置下对生成式音乐模型进行训练数据成员推断是可行的，为AI生成内容的训练数据溯源和透明度审计提供了有效工具。

Classification reason: 该论文研究生成式音乐模型的训练数据成员推断（membership inference），属于对AI生成模型训练数据的溯源与归因问题，与AIGC检测与溯源（aigc_detection_attribution）这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on music model auditing, not aligned with user's priority subtopics in video/image generation or model efficiency. Signals: Membership inference, generative music, black-box auditing, training data provenance, shadow model

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-music generation enable high-fidelity synthesis of structured musical audio, raising growing concerns about data provenance, consent, and training transparency. These models are typically trained on large-scale corpora with little disclosure, leaving no practical mechanism to verify whether a particular audio sample was included in training. In this paper, we investigate black-box membership inference for generative music models, aiming to determine whether a candidate music sample was used during training, given only query access to the deployed system. Our key insight is that training membership induces systematically stronger semantic and structural alignment between a candidate sample and the model's generation conditioned on its caption. We query the target model with the associated caption and measure the relationship between the candidate audio and the generated output in a learned feature space. To capture features that separate members from non-members, we construct paired examples consisting of each track and its caption-conditioned generation from shadow models, and train a music auditor to classify membership. The auditor captures alignment patterns characteristic of training membership and generalizes to unseen target models in a fully black-box setting without access to model parameters or training metadata. Across multiple state-of-the-art music generators, our method achieves up to 98.6% accuracy, with false-positive and false-negative rates as low as 1.9% and 1.0%, demonstrating that reliable training-data auditing is feasible in realistic deployment scenarios.

</details>


### [131] [FakeVLM-R1: Internalizing Physical Laws via CoT for Synthetic Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30062)
*Leqi Zhu, Junyan Ye, Kaiqing Lin, Zhiyuan Yan, Conghui He, Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 检测与溯源

Importance: 普通相关 (48.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FakeVLM-R1 提出了一种结合 GRPO 强化学习与批判性思维链（Critical Thinking CoT）的合成图像检测方法，通过"双向辩证推理"机制让模型在提出伪造假设的同时利用物理常识构建真实性反证，解决了现有方法缺乏因果推理能力和对真实图像过度拒绝的问题，在多个基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大多模态模型（LMM）的合成图像可解释检测方法仍依赖大量伪造数据的模仿学习，缺乏真正的因果推理能力，容易产生解释性幻觉（explanatory hallucinations），且对真实图像存在过度拒绝偏差（over-rejection bias）。

Method: 在监督微调（SFT）基础上，引入组相对策略优化（GRPO）与批判性思维链（Critical Thinking CoT）机制。推理时执行"双向辩证推理"：提出伪造假设的同时，调用物理常识构建真实性反证。同时构建了 FakeClue++ 数据集，引入基于真实图像物理规律的高质量标注，提供统一的真实性锚点。

Result: 在多个基准上达到 SOTA 性能，实现了高精度、逻辑可解释的检测，解决了对真实图像的过度拒绝偏差，展现出良好的泛化性和对扰动的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FakeVLM-R1 通过强化学习与批判性思维链的结合，赋予了模型类似人类的因果推理能力，为合成图像检测提供了一种更可靠、可解释的新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究合成图像检测（synthetic image detection），属于 AIGC 检测方向，与 canonical 子主题 aigc_detection_attribution（AIGC 检测与溯源）高度匹配。同时，方法中使用了 LMM 和 CoT 推理，也与多模态理解与生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on synthetic image detection, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to generation or efficiency. Signals: GRPO reinforcement learning, critical thinking CoT, synthetic image detection, physical law reasoning, over-rejection bias

Abstract: The development of generative artificial intelligence technologies has propelled the visual realism of synthetic images to an unprecedented level. Although current interpretable detection methods based on Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have made certain progress, they still rely on imitation learning derived from massive volumes of forged data. Consequently, they lack genuine causal reasoning capabilities and are prone to explanatory hallucinations. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose FakeVLM-R1, aiming to endow the model with human-like critical thinking capabilities when performing synthetic detection tasks. Building upon Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), this framework integrates Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a Critical Thinking Chain-of-Thought (CoT) mechanism. During the inference phase, the model executes a "bidirectional dialectical reasoning" process: while proposing a forgery hypothesis, it must simultaneously invoke physical commonsense to construct an authenticity counter-proof. Furthermore, we constructed the FakeClue++ dataset with high-quality samples, which extensively introduces annotations guided by the physical laws of authentic images, providing a unified authenticity anchor for the model. Experiments confirm that FakeVLM-R1 achieves SOTA performance the evaluated models across multiple benchmarks. It not only achieves high-precision, logically interpretable detection but also resolves the over-rejection bias of existing methods against real images, demonstrating generalization and robustness against perturbations.

</details>


### [132] [DeepFake Forensics AI: A Multi-Modal Detection and Blockchain-Anchored Evidence Management Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29353)
*Naisha Minnah*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.CV

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 检测与溯源

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 DeepFake Forensics AI，一个统一的多模态深度伪造检测与区块链证据管理平台。系统训练了四个独立神经网络（EfficientNet-B4 图像检测、Bi-LSTM 视频检测、ECAPA-TDNN 音频检测、GAN 指纹识别模块），并将证据哈希后存储于 IPFS 并锚定到以太坊区块链，提供角色权限控制的智能合约管理，适用于法医与法律场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测系统通常只处理单一模态，且缺乏防篡改的证据保存机制，无法满足法医和法律场景中对数字证据完整性的严格要求。

Method: 训练四个独立神经网络：EfficientNet-B4 用于图像检测（AUC=0.9868），双向 LSTM 用于视频检测（AUC=0.9628），ECAPA-TDNN 用于音频检测（EER=18.63%），以及一个 GAN 指纹识别模块（准确率 99.88%）用于识别生成架构。证据文件经 SHA-256 哈希后存储于 IPFS（通过 Pinata），并通过 Solidity 智能合约注册到以太坊区块链，支持基于角色的访问控制。前端使用 React，后端使用 FastAPI。

Result: 图像检测 AUC 0.9868，视频检测 AUC 0.9628，音频检测 EER 18.63%，GAN 指纹识别准确率 99.88%。系统成功实现了多模态深度伪造检测与区块链锚定的证据链管理。

Conclusion: 本文首次将多模态深度伪造检测与基于区块链的链式证据管理统一到一个平台中，为法医和法律工作流提供了可部署的解决方案，在检测性能和证据完整性方面均取得了良好效果。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多模态（图像/视频/音频）深度伪造检测平台，并包含生成架构指纹识别（溯源），与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "AIGC 检测与溯源" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on deepfake detection and blockchain evidence, which is outside the user's priority subtopics of generation and efficiency. Signals: Multi-modal detection, blockchain evidence, not generation-focused, low priority subtopic match, negative keywords absent

Abstract: The proliferation of AI-generated synthetic media poses a critical threat to the integrity of digital evidence in legal and forensic contexts. Existing deepfake detection systems typically address a single modality and provide no mechanism for tamper-proof evidence preservation. We present DeepFake Forensics AI, a unified platform that detects synthetic media across image, video, and audio modalities, identifies generative architecture fingerprints, and anchors forensic evidence immutably on the Ethereum blockchain. Our system trains four independent neural networks from scratch: an EfficientNet-B4 image detector (AUC = 0.9868), a Bidirectional LSTM video detector (AUC= 0.9628), an ECAPA-TDNN audio detector (EER = 18.63%), and a novel GAN fingerprinting module (accuracy = 99.88%) that identifies the generative architecture behind a fake image. Evidence files are hashed with SHA-256, stored on IPFS via Pinata, and registered on-chain via a Solidity smart contract with role-based access control. The platform provides a React frontend and FastAPI backend suitable for deployment in forensic and legal workflows. To our knowledge, this is the first system to unify multi-modal deepfake detection with blockchain-based chain-of custody management.

</details>


### [133] [Implicit Identity Technologies for LLMs: Fingerprinting and Watermarking across Datasets, Models, and Generated Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29245)
*Bing Liu, Shunping Wang, Yufan Zhu, Xinyi Yu, Jing Huang, Linkang Du, Hongbin Pei, Wei Luo*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 检测与溯源

Importance: 普通相关 (46.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了LLM指纹识别与水印技术，提出了"隐式身份"统一抽象概念，区分了指纹识别（非侵入式）和水印（主动嵌入），并基于生命周期构建了涵盖数据集、模型和生成内容的分类体系，同时建立了以可识别性、鲁棒性和可部署性为核心的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在数据、计算和专业知识上投入巨大，且越来越多地部署在高风险场景中，保护LLM相关资产并追溯其来源至关重要。然而现有工作分散在数据集溯源、模型所有权验证和生成内容检测等不同领域，术语使用不一致（指纹识别与水印常被混用），方法通常局限于孤立的资产特定场景，缺乏统一的系统性框架。

Method: 提出"隐式身份"（implicit identity）作为统一抽象概念，将指纹识别定义为从内在特征中提取的非侵入式身份信号，水印定义为主动嵌入数据、模型或生成内容中的侵入式身份信号。基于LLM生命周期（数据集→模型→生成内容）构建分类体系，进一步按验证语义分为基于相似性的归因和基于密钥的验证，并建立包含可识别性、鲁棒性和可部署性的评估框架。

Result: 系统梳理了数据集指纹/水印、模型指纹/水印、生成内容水印/指纹等各类方法，总结了代表性评估指标，并分析了不同访问权限和变换条件下的方法表现。论文本身是综述，未提出新方法，但提供了结构化的分类体系和评估框架。

Conclusion: 通过统一术语、生命周期阶段和评估目标，该综述为研究LLM身份技术提供了结构化基础，有助于开发更可靠的资产保护和溯源机制。未来工作可进一步探索跨资产类型的统一身份方案、更鲁棒的嵌入方法以及实际部署中的权衡。

Classification reason: 该论文是关于LLM指纹识别与水印技术的综述，核心关注模型所有权验证、数据集溯源和生成内容归因，属于AIGC检测与溯源范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "AIGC 检测与溯源"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Survey on LLM identity technologies is well-structured but falls outside user's core generative model and efficiency priorities. Signals: LLM fingerprinting and watermarking, lifecycle taxonomy, evaluation framework, not aligned with user's priority subtopics or boost keywords

Abstract: This paper presents a survey and taxonomy of LLM fingerprinting and watermarking for identity, ownership verification, provenance, and generated-content attribution. Large language models (LLMs) require substantial investments in data, computation, and expertise, and are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings, making it critical to protect LLM-related assets and trace their origins. Existing work has rapidly expanded across dataset provenance, model ownership, and generated-content detection, but the field remains fragmented: fingerprinting and watermarking are often used inconsistently, and methods are typically studied within isolated asset-specific settings. To address this gap, we introduce implicit identity as a unifying abstraction for verifiable but not directly observable identity signals in LLM systems. We distinguish fingerprinting as non-intrusive identity derived from intrinsic characteristics, and watermarking as intrusive identity deliberately embedded into data, models, or generated content. We then propose a lifecycle-based taxonomy that organises techniques across datasets, models, and generated content, and further separates them by verification semantics: similarity-based attribution and keyed verification. Finally, we establish an evaluation framework centred on identifiability, robustness, and deployability, summarising representative metrics under realistic access and transformation regimes. By unifying terminology, lifecycle stages, and evaluation objectives, this survey provides a structured foundation for studying LLM identity technologies and for developing more reliable mechanisms for asset protection and provenance.

</details>


### [134] [Audio Deepfake Detection with Half-Truth Localisation Using Cross-Attentive Feature Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29531)
*S. Sutharya, Remya K. Sasi*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.SD, cs.CV

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 检测与溯源

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CAFNet，一个仅 576k 参数的轻量级架构，用于音频深度伪造检测与半真音频（部分合成片段拼接）的篡改区域定位。该模型通过多特征融合（MFCC、LFCC、Chroma-STFT）与交叉注意力机制，联合完成三元分类（真实/全假/半真）和篡改边界回归，在 MLADDC 数据集上达到 92.71% 准确率和 0.075s 的边界定位误差，且参数量仅为 XLS-R 300M 的 1/500 以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频深度伪造检测大多作为二分类问题处理，但实际场景中存在更隐蔽的"半真"音频——短段合成语音被拼接进真实语音中。这类部分伪造的检测和篡改区域定位更具挑战性，现有方法难以同时完成检测与定位任务。

Method: 提出 CAFNet 架构：(1) 并行深度可分离卷积分支分别处理 MFCC、LFCC 和 Chroma-STFT 三种特征；(2) 通过交叉注意力机制融合多模态特征；(3) 使用 BiLSTM 回归头预测篡改区域的起止时间边界。模型在单次前向传播中同时完成三元分类（真实/全假/半真）和边界回归。

Result: 在 MLADDC T2+T3 测试集上，三元分类准确率 92.71%，宏 AUC 0.9910，边界定位 MAE 0.075s（中位误差 0.052s）。二分类准确率 96.76%，EER 3.20%，以不到 1/500 的参数量超越微调后的 XLS-R 300M（78.31%）和 AST 87M（93.03%）。跨数据集实验表明标准微调会破坏跨域表征。

Conclusion: CAFNet 以极小的参数量在音频深度伪造检测和半真音频定位任务上取得了优异性能，验证了多特征融合与交叉注意力机制的有效性。同时揭示了跨数据集泛化中标准微调方法的局限性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究音频深度伪造检测（audio deepfake detection），包括半真音频（部分伪造）的检测与篡改区域定位，属于 AIGC 检测与溯源（aigc_detection_attribution）这一规范子主题的范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on audio deepfake detection, which is outside the user's priority subtopics of video/image generation and world models. Signals: lightweight 576k model, cross-attentive feature fusion, half-truth localisation, outperforms large models, no autoregressive/diffusion/video generation focus

Abstract: Audio deepfake detection is well-studied as a binary problem, but partially manipulated speech, where a short synthesised segment is spliced into an otherwise genuine utterance, poses a harder and more realistic threat. Detecting such half-truth audio requires not only distinguishing it from real and fully fake speech, but also localising where the manipulation occurs. We present CAFNet, a 576k-parameter architecture that addresses both tasks jointly: it performs ternary classification (real, fully-fake, or half-truth) and regresses the temporal boundaries of the synthesised region in a single forward pass. CAFNet fuses Mel-Frequency Cepstral Coefficient (MFCC), Linear-Frequency Cepstral Coefficient (LFCC), and Chroma Short-Time Fourier Transform (Chroma-STFT) features through parallel depthwise-separable convolution branches with cross-attention, followed by a Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) regression head for boundary prediction. On the combined Multi-Lingual Audio Deepfake Detection Corpus (MLADDC) T2+T3 test set, CAFNet achieves 92.71% accuracy and macro Area Under the Curve (AUC) of 0.9910, with boundary localisation Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 0.075s and a median error of 0.052s. On binary detection, it achieves 96.76% accuracy and 3.20% Equal Error Rate (EER), outperforming fine-tuned XLS-R 300M (78.31%) and AST 87M (93.03%) at over 500 times fewer parameters. A cross-dataset study further shows that standard fine-tuning collapses cross-domain representations even under reduced backbone learning rates.

</details>


### [135] [KBF: Knowledge Boundary as Fingerprint for Language Model and Black-Box API Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29524)
*Yijia Fang, Yiqing Feng, Bingyu Li, Mingxun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 检测与溯源

Importance: 普通相关 (42.38) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 KBF 协议，利用 LLM 在知识边界附近的稳定数值召回作为指纹，对模型 API 进行低成本黑盒审计，可检测模型替换、混合路由攻击，并在真实平台审计中发现 7/27 的模型单元与参考端点不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中继和转售 API 越来越多地作为 LLM 访问的中间层，但用户无法验证所声称的端点是否真正提供广告中的模型。现有模型指纹方法成本高、易受部署变化影响，缺乏实用的黑盒审计方案。

Method: KBF 利用 LLM 在知识边界（模型确定知道与不知道之间的过渡区域）附近的稳定数值召回作为模型指纹。通过构造一组精心设计的查询，测量模型在知识边界上的召回模式，形成独特的指纹签名，用于黑盒审计和模型身份验证。

Result: 在 16 个生产 LLM 端点上，KBF 成功标记所有 155 个经济相关的模型替换，无假阳性；对部署变化保持稳定；可检测 5-10% 流量被替换的混合路由攻击；在六平台影子 API 审计中发现 7/27 的模型单元与参考端点统计不一致，且不一致集中在高级 Claude 端点上。

Conclusion: KBF 是一种低成本、高可靠性的 LLM API 黑盒审计协议，能够有效检测模型替换和混合路由攻击，揭示了当前 API 转售市场中存在模型不一致问题，为 LLM 服务审计提供了实用工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 KBF 协议，通过知识边界指纹对 LLM 模型 API 进行黑盒审计，检测模型替换与路由攻击。这属于 AIGC 检测与溯源（模型溯源/归因）范畴，与 canonical 子主题 aigc_detection_attribution 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM API auditing and security, which is outside the user's priority subtopics of video/image generation and model efficiency. Signals: Black-box auditing, LLM fingerprinting, API security, model substitution detection, low-cost protocol

Abstract: Relay and reseller APIs increasingly intermediate access to large language models (LLMs), but users have no direct way to verify that a claimed endpoint is actually serving the advertised model. We introduce KBF, a low-cost black-box auditing protocol that fingerprints model APIs using stable numerical recall near the knowledge boundary. Across 16 production LLM endpoints, KBF flags all 155 economically relevant substitutions without rejecting any same-model controls, remains stable under deployment variation, detects high-separation mixed-routing attacks when only 5-10% of traffic is substituted, and finds that 7 of 27 platform model cells in a six-platform shadow API audit are statistically inconsistent with their reference endpoints, with inconsistencies concentrated on premium Claude endpoints.

</details>


### [136] [Fingerprinting Inference Systems of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29979)
*Anna Wimbauer, Jonas Möller, Erik Imgrund, Konrad Rieck*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.LG

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 检测与溯源

Importance: 普通相关 (44.91) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM推理系统的不同组件（推理引擎、注意力后端、硬件平台）会在模型输出中留下可观测的数值偏差特征，并提出了一种基于提示-响应分析的指纹识别方法，能够可靠地识别这些组件，且防止指纹识别在根本上难以实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的行为不仅取决于模型本身，还受推理系统组件（推理引擎、注意力后端、硬件平台）的影响。这些组件实现上的细微差异会导致数值偏差，但此前工作仅从理论上证明了这些偏差的存在，其安全影响尚未被探索。本文旨在揭示这些偏差可被利用来识别推理系统组件，从而引发安全与隐私方面的担忧。

Method: 提出一种指纹识别方法，通过分析LLM的提示-响应行为来识别推理系统组件。具体地，向目标LLM发送精心设计的提示，收集其文本输出，然后利用输出中的数值偏差特征进行分类，识别出推理引擎、注意力后端和硬件平台。实验在非零温度设置下进行，以验证方法的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够可靠地识别推理引擎、注意力后端和底层硬件平台，即使在LLM以非零温度运行时依然有效。研究还发现，防止这种指纹识别在根本上非常困难，因为需要消除硬件和软件栈之间的数值差异。

Conclusion: LLM推理系统的组件指纹可以被外部攻击者通过查询模型的方式获取，这带来了安全与隐私风险。由于消除数值差异在根本上难以实现，本文提出了部分缓解措施并讨论了其影响，为LLM推理系统的安全防护提供了新的视角。

Classification reason: 该论文研究通过分析LLM的提示-响应行为来指纹识别推理系统组件（推理引擎、注意力后端、硬件平台），属于对AI生成模型/系统的溯源与归因，与AIGC检测与溯源（aigc_detection_attribution）这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel security analysis of LLM inference systems, but outside user's core generative modeling and efficiency priorities. Signals: LLM inference fingerprinting, numerical deviations, security implications, inference engine identification, attention backend detection

Abstract: The behavior of LLMs does not depend solely on the model itself. Components of the inference system, such as the inference engine, attention backend, and hardware platform, subtly influence how inputs are processed. These components differ in their implementations and thereby induce small numerical deviations across systems when running the same model. While prior work has established the theoretical existence of such deviations, their security implications have remained unexplored. In this paper, we show that these deviations are characteristic of specific components and propagate to observable textual outputs, exposing the inference system to any party that can query the model. Building on this observation, we introduce a fingerprinting method that analyzes the prompt-response behavior of LLMs to identify components of the inference system. Our empirical evaluation demonstrates that the inference engine, attention backend, and underlying hardware platform can be identified reliably, even when the LLM is operated at non-zero temperature. We show that preventing fingerprinting is fundamentally hard, as it would require eliminating numerical differences between hardware and software stacks. We therefore propose partial mitigations and discuss their impact.

</details>
