### [137] [When Models Disagree: Rethinking LLM Evaluation for Public Comment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29025)
*Aisha Najera, Alvin Moon, Vedant Srinivasan, Rajesh Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.CY, cs.HC, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (40.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文指出标准LLM评估仅依赖少量标注集上的准确率无法检测不同模型对同一公共评论语料产生实质性分类差异的问题，提出Interpretive Audit Pipeline，利用多模型分歧作为解释性复杂性的诊断信号，引导人工审查关注真正模糊的输入。在1,260条USDA联邦公共评论上的实验表明，模型间主题分歧超过模型内提示变化，专家评分标准会压制深层解释分歧而不解决它。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦机构使用LLM对公众评论进行分类，模型的分类结果直接影响政策制定者看到的内容。标准评估仅依赖少量验证集上的立场准确率，无法检测不同模型对同一输入产生实质性不同分类的问题，可能导致政策决策基于有偏或不可靠的模型输出。

Method: 提出Interpretive Audit Pipeline：1）使用多个LLM对公共评论进行分类，计算模型间的分歧程度；2）将分歧作为诊断信号，识别解释性复杂的评论；3）对分歧大的评论进行分层抽样，进行两阶段标注研究（独立标注→看到他人标注后修订），分析修订行为。

Result: 在1,260条USDA联邦公共评论上使用4个LLM的实验表明：模型间主题分歧超过模型内提示变化；专家评分标准会压制深层解释分歧而不解决它；两阶段标注研究中，不同标注者的修订行为差异显著，人类标注者的修订经常引入整体集成输出中不存在的框架。

Conclusion: 基于分歧的评估是准确率指标的必要补充，对于LLM辅助的解释性编码任务，应重视多模型分歧所揭示的解释复杂性，而非仅依赖单一准确率指标。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种基于多模型分歧的LLM评估方法（Interpretive Audit Pipeline），用于公共评论分析场景下的生成内容分类质量评估，属于AIGC评测范畴。映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "AIGC 评测与基准"（aigc_evaluation_benchmark），该子主题涵盖生成内容质量评测与评估方法。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM evaluation for public comment analysis, which is outside the user's core generative modeling and efficiency priorities. Signals: LLM evaluation, public comment analysis, interpretive audit, multi-model disagreement, human annotation revision

Abstract: Federal agencies are deploying large language models (LLMs) to categorize public comment corpora, where the model's organization of the record shapes what policymakers see and which arguments register. Standard evaluation, anchored on stance accuracy against a small validated set, cannot detect when different models produce materially different categorizations of the same public input. We propose an Interpretive Audit Pipeline that treats multi-model disagreement as diagnostic of interpretive complexity and directs human review toward genuinely ambiguous public input. Analyzing 1,260 public comments on a federal USDA docket across four LLMs, we find that inter-model thematic divergence exceeds within-model prompt variation, and that an expert rubric suppresses deep interpretive disagreement without resolving it. In a two-stage labeling study on a stratified 40-comment subsample, four LLMs and a human annotator labeled independently and then revised after seeing the others' labels. Revision behavior varied across labelers, and the human annotator's revisions frequently introduced framings absent from the ensemble's collective output. We argue disagreement-based evaluation is a necessary complement to accuracy metrics for LLM-assisted interpretive coding.

</details>


### [138] [Review Arcade: On the Human Alignment and Gameability of LLM Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28897)
*Hans Ole Hatzel, Sebastian Steindl, Jan Strich*

Main category: cs.MA, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (45.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过实验评估LLM生成的学术论文评审与人类评审的对齐程度，发现对齐有限且随提示词和模型变化显著；同时发现作者可以通过迭代修改论文来"博弈"LLM评审，在特定场景下可使最多35%的论文获得显著分数提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的论文评审正在被越来越多地使用，甚至被大型会议官方试点。然而，LLM评审与人类评审的对齐程度尚不明确，且作者可能利用LLM评审来针对性修改论文以获取更高分数。本文旨在系统评估这两种风险。

Method: 使用2025年ACL Rolling Review (ARR)的论文进行实证实验。从审稿人角度评估LLM评审与人类评审的对齐程度（跨不同提示词和模型）；从作者角度模拟迭代"草稿-修改"工作流，根据LLM评审改进论文，观察分数变化。

Result: 最佳情况下LLM评审与人类评审的对齐程度尚可，但不同提示词和模型下差异很大。作者"博弈"LLM评审在特定场景下有效，最多可使35%的论文获得统计显著的分数提升。

Conclusion: LLM评审与人类评审的对齐有限且不稳定，同时存在被作者利用的漏洞，需要更谨慎地使用LLM辅助评审并建立防护机制。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM生成的论文评审与人类评审的对齐程度以及可被"博弈"（gaming）的程度，属于对AI生成内容（LLM评审）的质量评测与人类偏好对齐评估，因此映射到AIGC方向下的[CANONICAL]子主题"AIGC 评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM review alignment and gaming, not on core generative model or video/image generation priorities. Signals: LLM review alignment, human evaluation, gaming potential, ARR dataset, prompt sensitivity

Abstract: LLM-generated reviews for scientific papers are gaining considerable traction and are even being officially piloted by major conferences. We have to assume that not only reviewers are using LLM-assistance, but also that authors use LLMs to revise their papers before submitting. In this work, we perform empirical experiments on papers from the 2025 ACL Rolling Review (ARR) to evaluate LLM reviews from both the author and the reviewer perspective. First, we identify a limited alignment of LLM reviews with human ones. In the best-case scenario, the alignment is reasonable. However, we also find that LLM-human alignment varies substantially across prompts and models. Finally, we investigate the scenario in which the author uses an iterative draft-revise workflow to improve the submission according to the LLM review. We find that this "gaming" of LLM reviews can be effective in specific scenarios, leading to a statistically significant increase of overall scores for up to 35\% of papers. We publish our code: .

</details>


### [139] [BEAMS: Benchmarking and Evaluating AI for Modeling and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28994)
*Sara Metcalf, William Schoenberg*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BEAMS 倡议旨在建立 AI 辅助建模与仿真的开放评测基准，通过自动化测试评估不同 LLM 在因果翻译、模型迭代、因果推理、一致性、行为解释等任务上的表现，发现当前 AI 工具在讨论和基础定性任务上表现较好，但在因果推理和定量错误修复上仍有不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 工具要支持真实世界的决策，必须能够构建仿真模型并提供可解释的建议。然而，目前缺乏系统化的基准来评估 AI 在建模与仿真中的能力，尤其是以人为中心的负责任 AI 实践。

Method: BEAMS 倡议通过开放数字与组织基础设施，建立多个评测类别（因果翻译、模型迭代、因果推理、一致性、模型行为解释、建模步骤建议、模型修复建议），并将这些评测实现为自动化测试，应用于 sd_ai 项目中的不同 LLM 引擎。

Result: AI 辅助建模工具在讨论和基础定性任务上表现较好，但在因果推理和定量错误修复上表现不佳。没有单一 LLM 在所有引擎类型上占优，不同任务存在速度与准确率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: BEAMS 倡议为 AI 辅助建模与仿真提供了首个系统化的评测框架，揭示了当前 AI 工具的能力边界，并计划未来纳入偏见评估和替代视角等更全面的基准。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 BEAMS 倡议，旨在建立 AI 辅助建模与仿真的评测基准（benchmark），属于 AIGC 方向下的「AIGC 评测与基准」规范主题，因此复用该 canonical subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on AI for modeling and simulation benchmarking, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like video/image generation or world models. Signals: AIGC 评测与基准, LLM evaluation, causal reasoning, simulation modeling, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: AI tools to support real world decision making must be able to build simulation models that inform their recommendations and render them interpretable. Tools that can automate aspects of modeling practice must complement human expertise, not replace it. The BEAMS Initiative aims to guide the development of AI tools for modeling and simulation toward forms that are responsible and ethical by establishing benchmarks for human centered modeling and simulation practices. The initiative uses open digital and organizational infrastructure to collaboratively evaluate AI tools for modeling and simulation. The open source sd ai project hosted by the initiative establishes transparency and enables contributions to be shared broadly. A steering group focuses on prioritizing potential benchmarks, while a technical group focuses on implementing the benchmarks in the form of automated tests. Tests for several distinct categories of evaluation have been implemented and applied to AI tools that support qualitative model building, quantitative model building, and model discussion. These include tests for causal translation, model iteration, causal reasoning, conformance, model behavior explanation, suggested model building steps, and suggested model fixes. When engines from the sd ai project are coupled with different LLMs, their performance on these evaluations reveals variability across different AI tools. The evaluations implemented by the initiative demonstrate that AI enabled modeling tools perform better at discussion and basic qualitative tasks than with causal reasoning and quantitative error fixing. No single LLM dominates across engine types, highlighting the importance of specific tasks and tradeoffs between speed and accuracy. Ongoing efforts of the initiative aim to incorporate benchmarks that address concerns about bias by considering alternative perspectives and human centered use cases.

</details>


### [140] [MetaRanker: Human-in-the-loop Active Ranking for Metalens Image Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29212)
*Yujin Park, Haejun Chung, Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.HC, cs.CV

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (40.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MetaRanker 是一个人机协同主动排序框架，用于评估金属透镜（metalens）图像质量。它通过概率偏好模型与不确定性感知的查询选择，结合视觉语言模型提供轻量级语义先验，以人类判断为主要监督信号，对金属透镜图像进行语义可解释性排序。实验表明，MetaRanker 的排序结果与人类评估高度一致，并将所需成对标注数量减少约 80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金属透镜成像系统的图像质量评估通常使用 PSNR 等失真导向的保真度指标，但这些指标与人类偏好和下游实用性的相关性较弱，存在感知-失真权衡问题。需要一种能够更好地反映人类对图像语义可解释性判断的评估方法。

Method: MetaRanker 结合了概率偏好模型（基于 Bradley-Terry 模型）与不确定性感知的主动查询选择策略，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）提供轻量级语义先验来指导信息量最大的成对比较采样，但人类判断始终作为主要监督信号。通过主动学习策略，系统选择最不确定的比较对让人类标注，从而高效学习人类偏好排序。

Result: 在真实世界和合成的金属透镜数据集上，MetaRanker 生成的排序与人类评估高度一致，同时将所需成对标注数量减少约 80%（相对于穷举成对评估）。标准图像质量评估指标（如 PSNR）与人类可解释性在金属透镜领域的对齐有限。

Conclusion: MetaRanker 为金属透镜图像质量提供了一种以人类感知为导向的评估方法，是向感知驱动的金属透镜评估与协同设计迈出的实用一步。该方法有效缓解了传统失真指标与人类偏好之间的差距。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MetaRanker，一个用于金属透镜图像质量评估的人机协同主动排序框架，核心是建立与人类偏好对齐的图像质量评测方法。这属于 AIGC 方向下的「AIGC 评测与基准」[CANONICAL] 子主题，因为该子主题涵盖生成内容质量评测与人类偏好对齐评估。

Importance reason: Focuses on metalens image quality ranking, which is niche and not aligned with user's priority subtopics in generative AI and efficient models. Signals: human-in-the-loop active ranking, metalens image quality, reduces pairwise annotations by 80%, weak correlation with standard IQA metrics, not generative AI or video/image generation

Abstract: Image quality in modern imaging systems emerges from the coupled effects of the sensor, optics, and computational reconstruction. Ultra-thin metalenses offer a path toward substantial miniaturization of optical modules, but practical designs often exhibit pronounced chromatic and field-dependent aberrations that necessitate computational reconstruction. In current metalens pipelines, reconstruction models are commonly trained and selected using distortion-based fidelity objectives, such as PSNR, yet these proxies can be weakly correlated with human preference and downstream utility, reflecting the well-known perception--distortion trade-off. We introduce MetaRanker, a human-in-the-loop active ranking framework that formalizes metalens image quality in terms of semantic interpretability, defined as the degree to which humans can reliably recognize objects and structures in the presence of optical artifacts. MetaRanker combines a probabilistic preference model with uncertainty-aware query selection, and leverages vision--language models to provide lightweight semantic priors. Importantly, these priors are used only to guide the sampling of informative comparisons; human judgments remain the primary supervision signal throughout. Across real-world and synthetic metalens datasets with distinct degradation profiles, MetaRanker produces rankings that align most closely with human assessments, while reducing the number of pairwise annotations required by approximately 80% relative to exhaustive pairwise evaluation. Finally, we show that standard image quality assessment metrics exhibit limited alignment with human interpretability in the metalens domain, positioning MetaRanker as a practical step toward perceptually grounded metalens evaluation and co-design.

</details>


### [141] [BenchTrace: A Benchmark for Testing Reflection Ability and Controlled Evolution in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29225)
*Jiahao Huang, Fei Cheng, Junfeng Jiang, Zefan Yu, Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (48.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 BenchTrace，一个用于评估 LLM 智能体自我进化能力的基准测试，包含反思评估和演化评估两个维度，并引入失败避免率（FAR）指标。实验表明 Qwen3-32B 和 GPT-4.1 在反思评估中端到端通过率均低于 30%，诊断是主要瓶颈；自我进化方法虽能提升 FAR，但存在遗忘早期经验和负迁移等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化智能体的评估仅关注任务得分，无法衡量反思质量，且依赖智能体自身的运行轨迹，缺乏针对特定失败模式的受控评估机制。

Method: 构建了一个包含 1,821 个标注片段的快照-反思数据集，覆盖六种不同任务。提出反思评估（通过目标 QA 任务探测失败识别能力）和演化评估（在受控自我进化模拟中测试失败经验是否转化为规避行为），并引入失败避免率（FAR）作为新指标。

Result: Qwen3-32B 和 GPT-4.1 在反思评估中端到端通过率均低于 30%，诊断是主要瓶颈。自我进化方法普遍能提升 FAR，但智能体会随着噪声片段累积遗忘早期经验，且反思难以泛化到不同上下文，导致负迁移。仅完全正确的反思与高 FAR 强相关。

Conclusion: BenchTrace 揭示了当前自我进化方法的局限性，提供了一个受控的、模型无关的框架用于针对性评估，为未来自我进化智能体的改进提供了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 BenchTrace，一个用于评估 LLM 智能体自我进化能力（反思与演化）的基准测试，属于 AIGC 方向下的智能体评测与基准。虽然论文聚焦于智能体反思能力评测，但 [CANONICAL] aigc_evaluation_benchmark 涵盖 AIGC 评测与基准，且该论文的核心贡献正是构建评测基准，因此映射到此规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM agent self-evolution evaluation, not directly on user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: LLM agent benchmark, self-evolution evaluation, reflection and evolution metrics, negative transfer findings, not aligned with user's core interests

Abstract: Self-evolving agents improve over time by reflecting on past failures, but existing evaluation is limited in two ways: it measures only task scores, leaving reflection quality unknown, and it relies on agents' own episode runs, offering no mechanism to target specific failure patterns. We present \textbf{BenchTrace}, a benchmark for evaluating self-evolution ability in LLM agents. BenchTrace is built on a snapshot-reflection dataset of 1,821 annotated episodes spanning six diverse tasks, and comprises a \textbf{Reflection Evaluation} that probes failure identification through targeted QA tasks, and an \textbf{Evolution Evaluation} that tests whether past failure experience translates into avoidance behavior in a controlled self-evolution simulation. Building on BenchTrace, we propose \textbf{failure avoidance rate (FAR)}, a new evaluation metric measuring the fraction of test cases in which the agent successfully avoids the target failure instance. Experiments with Qwen3-32B and GPT-4.1 reveal that both models fall below a 30\% end-to-end pass rate on reflection evaluation, with diagnosis as the primary bottleneck. Evolution evaluation shows that self-evolution methods generally improve FAR over the non-evolving baseline, but agents forget early lessons as noise episodes accumulate, and agents fail to generalize their reflections beyond the specific context, causing negative transfer across task contexts. Our correlation analysis further reveals that only a fully correct reflection is strongly associated with higher FAR. BenchTrace exposes concrete limits of current self-evolution approaches and provides a controlled, model-agnostic framework for targeted evaluation.

</details>


### [142] [Rethinking Literature Search Evaluation: Deep Research Helps, and Human Citation Lists Are Not a Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29234)
*Gaurav Sahu, Laurent Charlin, Christopher Pal*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (48.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文从两个角度研究大规模文献检索：一是实现Deep Research检索流程，通过广度优先扩展参考文献将召回率从低于20%提升至80%以上；二是利用LLM-as-a-judge评估人类引用列表作为评测标准的可靠性，发现仅51%的人类引用被评为中等相关以上，而AI重排序达到86-88%，且人类引用直接合作者的概率是AI的2.5倍。作者建议文献检索评测应联合报告召回率、主题相关性、列表多样性和合作者距离等多个指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献检索评测过度依赖人类引用列表作为ground truth，但人类引用存在偏差（如倾向于引用合作者），且传统API检索效果有限。作者希望重新审视文献检索评测标准，提出更全面的评测框架。

Method: 1) 构建Deep Research检索流程：处理完整查询论文，沿参考文献广度优先扩展检索结果；2) 使用LLM-as-a-judge（中性评估）判断人类引用的相关性；3) 在OpenAlex合作者图上分析引用偏差，比较人类与AI重排序的引用模式差异。

Result: Deep Research流程在RollingEval-Jun25基准上将召回率从低于20%提升至80%以上；仅51%的人类引用被评为中等相关以上，而AI重排序达到86-88%；人类引用直接合作者的概率是AI重排序的2.5倍。

Conclusion: 人类引用列表不适合作为文献检索评测的唯一ground truth；文献检索评测应联合使用召回率、主题相关性评分、排序列表多样性和合作者距离诊断等多个互补指标。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文献检索评测，核心贡献包括构建文献检索基准（RollingEval-Jun25）和评估人类引用列表作为评测标准（ground truth）的可靠性，属于AIGC评测与基准范畴。论文提出的Deep Research检索流程和LLM-as-a-judge评测方法均服务于生成式检索系统的评测，因此映射到[AIGC 评测与基准]（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on literature search evaluation, not on core generative modeling or efficiency priorities. Signals: Deep Research retrieval, LLM-as-a-judge evaluation, human citation bias, recall improvement, not generative model

Abstract: We study large-scale literature search from two complementary angles: improving the retrieval pipeline, and stress-testing the human reference list as an evaluation target. First, we implement a Deep Research pipeline that processes the full query paper and expands the retrieved results breadth-first along their bibliographies, and show that it substantially outperforms vanilla API-only search, raising recall on RollingEval-Jun25 (a 250-paper literature-search benchmark) from below 20% to above 80%. Second, we use a neutral LLM-as-a-judge to determine if human references are sound ground truth for the task. We find significant limitations: only 51% of human citations are judged moderately relevant or higher, against 86--88% for the strongest AI-based re-rankers. We study this gap on the OpenAlex co-authorship graph, finding that humans are 2.5x more likely than the best AI re-rankers to cite a direct collaborator. Together, our results argue against single-axis literature-search evaluation: recall, topical-relevance scoring, ranked-list diversity, and a co-authorship-distance diagnostic each measure complementary properties of citation quality and should be reported jointly.

</details>


### [143] [OpenClawBench: Benchmarking Process-side Anomalies in Real-world Agent Execution Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29253)
*Yibing Liu, Yangze Liu, Xiaolong Yin, Bin Wang, Chong Zhang, Hao Yin, Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (46.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OpenClawBench 是一个大规模数据集和基准，用于检测 AI 智能体执行轨迹中的过程侧异常（如未解决的歧义、不安全的外部写入、忽略的错误等），揭示了"结果-过程差距"——即使任务通过最终测试，执行过程中仍可能存在异常。论文基于 BFCL 驱动的 OpenClaw 会话构建了 31,264 条带注释轨迹，并提出了 FullTax 标注框架，实验表明仅以成功率为评估标准会遗漏大量过程侧失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 智能体评估主要关注任务最终是否成功（outcome），忽略了执行过程中的异常行为（process anomalies）。一个智能体可能通过最终任务测试，但过程中积累了未解决的歧义、不安全的外部写入、忽略的错误等问题。这种"结果-过程差距"导致对智能体可靠性的评估不完整，需要系统性的过程侧异常评测方法。

Method: 论文构建了 OpenClawBench 数据集，基于 BFCL 驱动的 OpenClaw 会话，由 6 个源模型生成 31,264 条带注释轨迹。提出了 FullTax 标注框架，将轨迹转化为结构化异常监督信息，包括：二分类标签、支持证据、异常起始/跨度定位、严重程度、可恢复性以及 5 类异常分类体系。使用 LoRA 微调 Gemma 3 12B 作为异常检测器进行验证。

Result: 在 31,135 条通过最终测试的执行轨迹中，有 2,904 条（约 9.3%）被 FullTax 标注为过程异常。LoRA 微调的 Gemma 3 12B 检测器在干净标签测试集上达到二分类 F1=0.729，验证了该基准的有效性和可操作性。

Conclusion: OpenClawBench 揭示了仅以任务成功率为评估标准会遗漏大量过程侧失败，为 AI 智能体执行可靠性研究提供了可审计、可复用的监督数据和方法。该工作将真实智能体执行日志转化为可用于研究、诊断和运行时监控的标准化资源。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 OpenClawBench，一个用于检测和标注 AI 智能体执行过程中过程侧异常（process-side anomalies）的大规模数据集和评测基准，属于 AIGC 评测与基准（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）范畴。虽然论文聚焦于智能体执行过程而非生成内容质量，但其核心贡献是构建评测基准来评估智能体执行可靠性，与 canonical 子主题"评测与基准"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on agent execution anomaly detection, not directly on user's priority generative modeling or efficiency topics. Signals: agent reliability, process-side anomaly, outcome-process gap, benchmark, not generative modeling

Abstract: Task success can hide process anomalies in real-world agent executions. An agent may pass the final task oracle while still accumulating unresolved ambiguity, unsafe external writes, ignored errors, weakly grounded commitments, or capability-boundary overcommitment. We study this mismatch as the Outcome-Process Gap and introduce OpenClawBench, a large-scale dataset for measuring and supervising process-side anomalies in real agent execution processes. OpenClawBench is built from BFCL-driven OpenClaw sessions produced by 6 source models and contains 31,264 annotated trajectories. It aligns task-oracle outcomes with structured process evidence. FullTax converts the aligned trajectories into structured anomaly supervision: binary labels, supporting evidence, onset/span localization, severity, recoverability, and a 5-class anomaly taxonomy. Using OpenClawBench, we make the Outcome-Process Gap measurable. Among 31,135 oracle-passing executions, 2,904 are still labeled process-anomalous under FullTax. These results show that success-only evaluation misses a concrete class of process-side failures in real agent executions. A LoRA-fine-tuned Gemma 3 12B detector trained on the high-confidence FullTax supervised pool reaches binary F1=0.729 on the cleaner-labels held-out test split. Together, OpenClawBench turns real agent execution logs into auditable and reusable supervision for studying, diagnosing, and operationally monitoring runtime agent reliability.

</details>


### [144] [Benchmarking Open-Source Safety Guard Models: A Comprehensive Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28830)
*Reetu Raj Harsh, Bhaskarjit Sarmah, Stefano Pasquali*

Main category: cs.SE, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (48.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了14个开源安全防护模型在79,331样本基准上的表现，涵盖8类NIST安全风险。发现Qwen Guard（4B）召回率最高（83.97%），而更大的模型如Llama Guard（12B）和GPT-OSS Safeguard（20B）可能漏检高达75%的有害内容，表明模型大小与安全检测性能无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在安全关键场景中的广泛部署，内容审核变得至关重要。然而，开源安全防护模型的性能缺乏系统性的比较评估，现有研究往往使用不同数据集和指标，难以进行公平对比。

Method: 收集14个开源安全防护模型，构建包含79,331个样本的基准数据集，整合HarmBench、StrongREJECT、RealToxicityPrompts和BeaverTails四个数据集，按NIST AI风险框架的8类安全类别（暴力、仇恨言论、骚扰、色情内容、自杀/自残、脏话、威胁、健康虚假信息）进行标注和评估。

Result: Qwen Guard（4B参数）以83.97%的召回率表现最佳；模型大小与安全检测性能无相关性；通用安全防护模型优于专用模型；召回率是安全应用的关键指标。

Conclusion: 开源安全防护模型性能差异显著，选择时应优先考虑召回率而非模型大小，通用模型比专用模型更适合实际部署。

Classification reason: 该论文对14种开源安全防护模型进行系统评测，构建了涵盖8类安全风险的基准数据集，属于AIGC内容安全评测范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "AIGC 评测与基准"（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Provides a practical benchmark for safety guard models, but is not directly aligned with your priority subtopics in generation and efficiency. Signals: Safety guard model evaluation, NIST risk framework, Qwen Guard outperforms larger models, model size not correlated with performance, recall-focused metric

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, robust content moderation becomes essential. We present a comprehensive evaluation of 14 open-source safety guard models on a curated benchmark of 79,331 samples spanning 8 NIST AI Risk Framework safety categories. Our benchmark aggregates four diverse datasets (HarmBench, StrongREJECT, RealToxicityPrompts, and BeaverTails), filtered to focus exclusively on safety-relevant content (violence, hate speech, harassment, sexual content, suicide/self-harm, profanity, threats, and health misinformation). We find that recall is the critical metric for safety applications, as missing harmful content poses greater risk than false positives. Our evaluation reveals surprising results: Qwen Guard (4B parameters) achieves the highest recall (83.97%) while larger models like Llama Guard (12B) and GPT-OSS Safeguard (20B) exhibit conservative behavior, missing up to 75% of unsafe content. We demonstrate that model size does not correlate with safety detection performance and that general-purpose guard models outperform specialized ones. These findings provide practical guidance for selecting safety guard models in production deployments.

</details>


### [145] [The Curse of Helpfulness: Inverse Scaling Law in Robustness to Distractor Instructions via DistractionIF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29491)
*Zeli Su, Zhankai Xu, Tianlei Chen, Longfei Zheng, Xiaolu Zhang, Jun Zhou, Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (50.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 DistractionIF 基准，用于评估 LLM 在 RAG 和智能体场景下对参考文本中干扰指令的鲁棒性。发现模型越大反而越不鲁棒（逆缩放现象），性能下降高达 30 个点。机制分析表明缩放模糊了鲁棒与分心行为的概率边界。GRPO 强化学习可恢复该边界，提升鲁棒性达 15.5% 且不影响指令跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在智能体和 RAG 系统中常需处理外部参考文本，这些文本可能包含类似指令的语义噪声（如编辑注释、系统日志），模型容易将其误当作指令执行。现有研究缺乏对此类干扰指令鲁棒性的系统评测，因此需要构建专门的基准来揭示这一问题。

Method: 构建 DistractionIF 基准，包含带有干扰指令的参考文本任务。通过困惑度分析研究模型规模与鲁棒性的关系，发现逆缩放现象。提出使用 GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）强化学习方法，在训练中强化模型区分数据与指令的能力，恢复鲁棒与分心行为之间的概率边界。

Result: 在多种模型上观察到一致的逆缩放现象：更大模型鲁棒性更差，性能下降高达 30 个点。GRPO 训练可将鲁棒性提升最多 15.5%，且不损害通用指令跟随能力。

Conclusion: 揭示了 LLM 在参考文本任务中指令跟随鲁棒性的关键缺口，表明模型规模扩大反而加剧了对干扰指令的敏感性。强化学习（GRPO）是缓解该问题的有效途径，有助于实现严格的数据-指令分离。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 DistractionIF 基准，用于评测 LLM 在参考文本中对抗干扰指令的鲁棒性，属于 AIGC 评测与基准（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）范畴。

Importance reason: Inverse scaling law in LLM robustness is a novel finding, but the topic is not in your priority subtopics. Signals: inverse scaling, LLM robustness, distractor instructions, GRPO reinforcement learning, AIGC benchmark

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in agentic and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems, where they must execute user-specified tasks over externally provided reference text. In practice, such context is often unstructured and contaminated with benign but instruction-like semantic noise, such as editorial comments and system traces, which should be treated strictly as data. We introduce DistractionIF, a benchmark designed to evaluate robustness against such distractor instructions in reference text. Across a broad range of models, we observe a consistent inverse scaling phenomenon: larger models are often less robust, with performance dropping by up to 30 points as scale increases. Mechanistically, our perplexity analysis reveals that scaling erodes the probabilistic boundary between robust and distracted behaviors, making models increasingly prone to over-interpreting noise as instructions. To address this, we demonstrate that reinforcement learning, specifically Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), can restore this boundary, improving robustness by up to 15.5% without compromising general instruction-following capability. Our findings highlight a critical instruction-following robustness gap in reference-grounded tasks and establish reinforcement learning as a promising path for enforcing strict data-instruction separation at scale.

</details>


### [146] [GPF-LiveNews: A Streaming Evaluation Protocol for Group-Conditioned Framing in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28848)
*Mohd Ariful Haque, Fahad Rahman, Kishor Datta Gupta, Roy George*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 GPF-LiveNews，一个流式评估协议和基准快照，用于审计大语言模型在开放输出中对不同受众群体的条件性框架（group-conditioned framing）。该协议以 BBC/Reuters 新闻为锚点，覆盖 42 个身份标签和 7 类提示，通过语义敏感性和情感差异信号评估模型输出。实验发现 Policy/Action 提示产生最强的语义变化，而情感变化较为平缓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态偏见基准无法反映 LLM 在非平稳环境（模型版本更新、检索层变化、安全系统调整、真实世界输入变化）下对新出现事件的框架方式，尤其是针对不同受众群体的输出差异缺乏流式审计方法。

Method: 提出 GPF-LiveNews 流式评估协议：以 BBC/Reuters 最新新闻为锚点，跨 42 个身份标签和 7 类提示族（如 Policy/Action 等）构建提示，收集模型输出后使用语义敏感性和情感差异信号进行评分，生成可审计的观测窗口信号供人工审查。

Result: 在 12 次监控运行和 23 个托管模型的试点实验中，Policy/Action 提示产生最强的语义移动，而情感变化在各维度和提示族间较为平缓。

Conclusion: GPF-LiveNews 提供了一种可扩展的流式审计方法，所有评分应视为供人工审查的观测窗口审计信号，而非永久性的公平性排名或有害偏见的直接证据。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个流式评估协议 GPF-LiveNews，用于审计 LLM 在不同受众群体条件下的输出框架（group-conditioned framing），属于 AIGC 评测与基准（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）范畴。同时涉及多模态理解与生成方向中的 LLM 输出评估，因此也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM bias evaluation, not on the user's priority generative modeling or efficiency topics. Signals: LLM bias audit, streaming evaluation protocol, group-conditioned framing, not generative model, not video/image generation

Abstract: Deployed language models are evaluated in a non-stationary environment: model versions, retrieval layers, safety systems, and real-world inputs all change over time. Static bias benchmarks remain useful, but they do not show how models frame newly emerging events for different prompted audiences. We introduce GPF-LIVENEWS, a streaming evaluation protocol and benchmark snapshot for auditing group-conditioned framing in open-ended LLM outputs. The protocol expands fresh BBC/Reuters news anchors across 42 identity labels and seven prompt families, then evaluates response bundles using semantic-sensitivity and sentiment-disparity signals. In a pilot over 12 monitoring runs and 23 hosted models, Policy/Action prompts produce the strongest semantic movement, while sentiment variation is flatter across dimensions and prompt families. The released artifact includes article metadata, prompt templates, instantiated prompts, model-output metadata, score tables, documentation, and reproduction scripts. We interpret all scores as observed-window audit signals for human review, not as permanent fairness rankings or direct proof of harmful bias.

</details>


### [147] [GrowLoop: Self-Evolving Conversation Evaluation Seeded by Human](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28882)
*Yihang Lin, Yunze Gao, Zeyang Lin, Dongbo Li, Kun Peng, Chenglong Song, Yue Liu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.SD, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GrowLoop 是一个自进化的对话评估系统，通过少量人工种子标注启动，利用 LLM 智能体迭代提取和优化评估准则（Heuristic Learning），并采用准则-案例协同进化机制，持续适应模型进步和场景变化，在开放域对话的人类相似性评估上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放域对话中的人类相似性评估面临三大挑战：1）人类判断标准是隐性知识，难以显式表达；2）人类判断存在分歧，有些一致有些合理不同；3）人类相似性的标准随模型能力和人类期望动态变化。现有方法（专家基准、奖励模型、自进化基准）均无法同时解决这三个挑战。

Method: GrowLoop 包含三个核心机制：1）Heuristic Learning：以少量人工种子标注为起点，LLM 智能体迭代提取和细化评估准则；2）Human-AI Agreement：在人类判断一致处要求人机一致，在分歧处仅要求合理性；3）Rubric-Case Co-evolution：准则与案例协同进化，当评估目标变化时通过新种子扩展，实现持续进化。

Result: 生成的评估准则在人类判断对齐度上显著优于现有方法，并能发现标注者忽略的问题。生成的基准能有效区分不同能力层级的模型，揭示模型不足之处，且能泛化到新场景并随模型进步自适应。

Conclusion: GrowLoop 将基准测试范式从手动更新或难度扩展转变为全面的、持续的自进化，为开放域对话评估提供了一种动态适应、持续改进的新方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出自进化的对话评估系统 GrowLoop，用于评估开放域对话中的人类相似性（human-likeness），属于生成内容质量评测与人类偏好对齐评估范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 的 aigc_evaluation_benchmark（AIGC 评测与基准）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on conversation evaluation, not core generative modeling or efficiency priorities. Signals: self-evolving evaluation, human-likeness, open-ended conversation, rubric-case co-evolution, heuristic learning

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models, evaluating human-likeness in open-ended conversation has become increasingly important. However, human-likeness is a form of tacit knowledge that humans perceive intuitively, yet the underlying criteria resist explicit formulation. Human judgments vary widely, with strong agreement on some cases and legitimate disagreement on others. Meanwhile, the criteria behind human judgments remain implicit, leaving no clear basis for constructing cases. Further, what counts as human-like is not static, but evolving with model capability and human expectations. Despite progress in evaluation methods such as expert-authored benchmarks, Reward Models, and self-evolving benchmarks, none addresses all three challenges simultaneously. Therefore, we propose GrowLoop, a self-evolving conversation evaluation system that continuously adapts as models advance and scenarios shift. With minimal human seed annotations as the first mover, LLM agents iteratively extract and refine evaluation rubrics through Heuristic Learning. Human-AI agreement is required where annotators converge, while only plausibility is expected where they diverge. Moreover, the Rubric-Case co-evolution mechanism enables continuous evolution, expanded through new seeds when the evaluation target moves. Applied to human-likeness evaluation in open-ended conversation, the generated rubrics not only substantially outperform existing methods in alignment with human judgments, but also uncover issues that annotators overlook. The resulting benchmark effectively discriminates models across capability tiers and reveals where they fall short, while generalizing to new scenarios and adapting as models advance. Our work shifts the benchmarking paradigm from manual updates or difficulty scaling to comprehensive, continuous self-evolution.

</details>


### [148] [PTCG-Bench: Can LLM Agents Master Pokémon Trading Card Game?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29653)
*Dongdong Hua, Yifei Sun, Renhong Huang, Feng Gao, Chunping Wang, Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 PTCG-Bench，一个基于宝可梦卡牌游戏（PTCG）的 LLM 智能体评测基准，从单局决策能力和经验驱动的自我进化能力两个层面评估智能体，并引入模块化消融分析以解耦智能体能力与模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测基准未能充分捕捉真实交互环境中策略性、不断演化的决策场景，缺乏对智能体自我进化能力的系统评估。

Method: 基于宝可梦卡牌游戏构建交互环境，设计两层评估体系：单局决策性能（复杂环境下的即时决策）和经验驱动的自我进化能力（通过积累经验提升表现）。引入模块化消融分析（harness ablation）以分离智能体架构设计与底层模型能力的影响。

Result: LLM 智能体在 PTCG 中能达到非平凡的游戏表现，但持续稳定的自我进化仍然困难，且性能对智能体架构设计（harness）敏感。

Conclusion: PTCG-Bench 为研究智能体架构感知和自我进化能力提供了有效评测平台，未来可推动更真实的交互式智能体研究。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个基于宝可梦卡牌游戏的 LLM 智能体评测基准 PTCG-Bench，属于 AIGC 方向下的「AIGC 评测与基准」规范子主题，因为该子主题涵盖 AIGC benchmark 和智能体评估。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM agent gaming benchmark, not aligned with user's priority on generative models and efficient inference. Signals: LLM agent benchmark, board game evaluation, self-evolution capability, modular ablation, not generative model

Abstract: Given a strategically complex board game, human players can quickly learn to devise strategies after playing a few rounds. Autonomous agents require similar capabilities in realistic interactive environments, yet existing agent benchmarks often fail to fully capture such strategic and evolving decision-making scenarios. We present PTCG-Bench, a benchmark built on the Pok'{e}mon Trading Card Game (PTCG) that evaluates LLM agents at two complementary levels: (1) their decision-making performance within a single complex environment, and (2) their ability to self-evolving through accumulated experience. We further include a modular harness ablation to better interpret agent performance without conflating it with model capability. Our experiments show that, although LLM agents can achieve non-trivial gameplay performance, sustained and stable self-evolution remains challenging, and performance is sensitive to harness design. We hope that PTCG-Bench will facilitate future research on harness-aware and self-evolving agents in realistic interactive environments.

</details>


### [149] [Notation Matters: A Benchmark Study of Token-Optimized Formats in Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29676)
*Lorenz Kutschka, Bernhard Geiger*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (58.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统评测了两种token优化格式TOON和TRON在智能体AI系统中的表现，在四个智能体基准和五个开源LLM上对比JSON基线，发现TRON可减少高达27%的token且准确率损失在14pp以内，TOON减少18%但存在多轮解析失败和并行工具调用崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JSON作为智能体系统中工具schema和执行结果交换的默认格式，其结构元素带来了大量token开销。虽然已有TOON和TRON等token优化替代格式被提出，但它们仅在孤立的理解或生成任务上被评估，在端到端智能体循环中是否仍能保持token缩减效果尚不清楚。

Method: 在四个智能体基准（BFCL, MCPToolBenchPP, MCP-Universe, StableToolBench）和五个开源LLM上，将输入压缩与输出压缩解耦，分别独立评测TOON和TRON的理解与生成能力，并与JSON基线进行对比。

Result: TRON最多减少27%的token，准确率损失在14个百分点以内；TOON最多减少18%的token，准确率损失约9个百分点，但在多轮交互中会出现解析失败级联效应，且对大多数模型会导致并行工具调用输出崩溃。

Conclusion: TRON在token效率与任务准确率之间取得了更好的平衡，是JSON在智能体系统中更有前景的替代方案；TOON虽然token缩减可观，但其在多轮和并行场景下的可靠性问题限制了实际应用。

Classification reason: 该论文在多个智能体基准（BFCL, MCPToolBenchPP, MCP-Universe, StableToolBench）上评测了TOON和TRON两种token优化格式在端到端智能体循环中的表现，属于对AIGC系统中结构化数据格式的评测研究，因此映射到[AIGC 评测与基准]（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Token optimization for agentic AI is a niche engineering concern, not a core generative model advance. Signals: token optimization, agentic AI, benchmark study, TOON/TRON formats, JSON baseline

Abstract: Large language models in Agentic AI systems consume tool schemas and execution results and emit tool invocations as structured data. The default language for that exchange, JSON, was designed for application-to-application interchange rather than token efficiency, so its structural elements impose substantial token overhead. Recent work proposes token-optimized alternatives such as TOON (Token-Oriented Object Notation) and TRON (Token Reduced Object Notation) as more compact replacements, but these formats have been evaluated only on isolated comprehension or generation tasks. Whether their token reductions hold inside end-to-end agentic loops therefore remains an open question. We evaluate TOON and TRON on four agentic benchmarks (BFCL, MCPToolBenchPP, MCP-Universe, StableToolBench) and five open-weight LLMs, decoupling input compression from output compression to measure comprehension and generation independently. TRON reduces tokens by up to 27% with accuracy within 14pp of the JSON baseline. TOON achieves up to 18% reduction at a similar 9pp accuracy cost, but additionally cascades on multi-turn parsing failures and collapses parallel tool-call output for most models.

</details>


### [150] [Citation-Closure Retrieval and Per-Rule Attribution for Real-World Regulatory Compliance Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29742)
*Yeong-Joon Ju, Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在法规合规问答中缺乏可追溯引用的问题，提出了 RegOps-Bench 基准和 RefWalk 框架。RegOps-Bench 基于国家研发法规构建了操作知识图谱，RefWalk 通过共享主题锚点遍历跨文档引用、多视角候选融合和逐规则归因，显著提升了检索召回率和引用准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 系统在处理法规合规问答时面临扁平化引用边、碎片化检索扩展和脆弱的归因机制等问题，无法满足多层级权威结构下的严格可追溯性要求。传统多跳问答和法律问答方法也不适用于这种需要结构化程序查找和证据集闭合的任务。

Method: 提出了 RefWalk 框架，核心包括：(1) 共享主题锚点驱动跨文档引用遍历；(2) 基于最大聚合的多视角候选融合；(3) 逐规则归因机制，将每个声明显式映射到来源。同时构建了 RegOps-Bench 基准，包含从复杂国家研发法规中提取的操作知识图谱。

Result: 在 RegOps-Bench 上取得了显著的检索召回率和引用准确性提升。在 HIPAA 数据集上的对比评估显示，现有系统在扁平结构规则上存在饱和现象，验证了 RegOps-Bench 的必要性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 RegOps-Bench 和 RefWalk 有效解决了法规合规问答中的引用可追溯性问题，为这一重要但被忽视的 NLP 任务建立了强基线，并揭示了现有系统在处理复杂层级法规结构时的局限性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 RegOps-Bench，一个面向法规合规问答的新基准，属于 AIGC 评测与基准（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）范畴。同时涉及 RAG 检索与多步推理，与多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态检索也有一定关联。

Importance reason: Focuses on regulatory compliance QA, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and boosted keywords. Signals: regulatory compliance, RAG, citation retrieval, benchmark, not aligned with user's core interests

Abstract: Deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) for regulatory compliance demands rigorous traceability via comprehensive citations across multi-tiered authority structures. Unlike traditional multi-hop or legal QA, this task requires structured procedural lookups and evidence-set closure rather than entity resolution or case-law reasoning. Existing RAG systems struggle here due to flattened citation edges, fragmented retrieval expansions, and fragile post-hoc attribution. We formalize Regulatory Compliance QA with RegOps-Bench, a novel benchmark featuring an Operational Knowledge Graph derived from complex national R\&D regulations. To address these bottlenecks, we propose RefWalk, a unified framework driven by a shared topic anchor. RefWalk traverses cross-document citations, fuses multi-view candidates via max-based aggregation, and enforces per-rule attribution to explicitly map claims to sources. We establish a strong baseline with substantial improvements in retrieval recall and citation accuracy. Finally, a contrastive evaluation on a U.S. health compliance dataset (HIPAA) reveals that existing systems exhibit saturation on flat-structure rules, underscoring the need for RegOps-Bench. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [151] [Croissant Tasks: A Metadata Format for Reproducible Machine Learning Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29786)
*Omar Benjelloun, Leonardo Martins Bianco, Isabelle Guyon, Thanh Gia Hieu Khuong, Jonathan Lebensold, Sebastian Lobentanzer, Luis Oala, Benedictus Kent Rachmat, Ihsan Ullah, Peyman Vahidi, Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Croissant Tasks，一种声明式、机器可执行的元数据格式，将底层实现细节抽象为高层规范，实现概念可复现性。论文贡献了格式规范、自动化 LLM 管道将现有基准转换为该格式，以及自主智能体从零生成可运行复现管线的实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习领域的可复现性面临严重挑战，主要原因包括执行细节描述不足和软件环境脆弱。现有的人工检查清单和手动验证方法虽然有效但成本高昂且难以规模化，亟需一种自动化、可扩展的解决方案。

Method: (1) 设计 Croissant Tasks 规范，形式化地将任务问题与解决方案解耦；(2) 开发自动化 LLM 管道，将现有基准数据集转换为该格式；(3) 通过自主智能体从零生成功能完整、准确的复现管线进行实证验证。

Result: 实验表明，自主智能体能够读取 Croissant Tasks 规范并成功生成可运行的复现管线，验证了该格式在实现概念可复现性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: Croissant Tasks 格式为机器学习中的自动化和概念可复现性提供了新的基础，有望推动更可靠、可验证的科研实践。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Croissant Tasks 元数据格式，旨在提升机器学习评估的可复现性，属于 AIGC 评测与基准（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）范畴，因为其核心贡献是构建可复现的评测基准格式与自动化评估流程。

Importance reason: Focuses on ML reproducibility metadata, not on the user's priority generative modeling or efficient inference topics. Signals: Reproducibility, metadata format, LLM pipeline, not generative, not efficient inference

Abstract: Reproducibility is fundamental to the scientific method, yet remains a critical challenge in machine learning. Contributing factors include underspecified execution details and brittle software environments. Human-centric remedies, such as checklists and manual verification, help but require intensive effort and fail to scale. To address this, we introduce Croissant Tasks: a declarative, machine-actionable metadata format that abstracts low-level implementation details into high-level specifications. This format enables conceptual reproducibility: verifying claims via independent, agent-generated implementations rather than brittle source code replication. We contribute: (1) the Croissant Tasks specification, formally decoupling task problem from solution; (2) an automated LLM pipeline that retrofits existing benchmarks into this format; and (3) empirical validation showing autonomous agents can ingest these specifications to generate functional, accurate reproduction pipelines from scratch. We envision this format as a new foundation for automated and conceptual reproducibility in machine learning.

</details>


### [152] [PRAIB: Peer Review AI Benchmark of Behaviour of LLM-Assisted Reviewing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29815)
*Krzysztof Żurawicki, Julia Farganus, Arkadiusz Gaweł, Mateusz Bystroński, Tomasz Jan Kajdanowicz*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出PRAIB（同行评审AI基准），一个用于衡量LLM辅助评审行为的框架，包含评审特异性、风格和行为参与度等指标。基于11,000条由5个模型对1,000篇ICLR/NeurIPS论文生成的评审，与人类评审进行对比，发现LLM评审存在评分变异性低、正向偏差、过度自信、交叉引用模式与人类不同等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着投稿数量激增，LLM被探索用于辅助同行评审以提高速度和可扩展性，但尚不清楚LLM是否像人类评审者一样真正理解科学稿件，还是仅仅生成看起来像评审的文本。需要系统性的基准来量化LLM评审行为与人类规范的差异。

Method: 提出了PRAIB框架，定义了评审特异性、风格和行为参与度三类指标。收集了11,000条由5个模型（GPT-4o、Claude 3.5 Sonnet、Gemini 1.5 Pro、Llama 3.1 70B、Mixtral 8x22B）对1,000篇ICLR/NeurIPS论文（2021-2025年）生成的评审，与原始人类评审进行对比分析，采用多种提示策略。

Result: LLM评审与人类评审存在显著差异：LLM评分变异性更低、存在正向偏差且过度自信；交叉引用模式依赖于具体模型且与人类不同；LLM倾向于生成更长更复杂的评审，但经常遗漏人类评审者指出的原子级弱点。

Conclusion: PRAIB为社区提供了一个诊断工具，用于识别LLM在同行评审中哪些方面可以可靠地辅助人类，哪些方面需要进一步改进才能部署。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个用于评估LLM辅助同行评审行为的基准框架（PRAIB），属于对AI生成内容（LLM生成的评审意见）的质量评测与基准构建，与AIGC方向下的[CANONICAL]子主题"AIGC 评测与基准"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM-assisted peer review evaluation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's core interests in generative models and efficiency. Signals: LLM evaluation benchmark, peer review analysis, not generative model, no video/image generation, no efficiency focus

Abstract: The growing number of submitted papers has motivated the exploration of Large Language Models (LLMs) as a means to support and augment the peer review process, particularly in terms of improving its speed and scalability. Yet, it remains unknown whether LLMs engage with scientific manuscripts in the same manner as human reviewers, or whether they merely produce review-looking text. To address this, we introduce the Peer Review AI Benchmark (PRAIB), a novel framework comprising thoroughly defined metrics that measure review specificity, style, and behavior of engagement. To complement the PRAIB framework, we conduct a large-scale empirical study leveraging a dataset of 11,000 reviews generated by five proprietary and open-source models for 1,000 ICLR and NeurIPS papers. Spanning the 2021--2025 period, these machine-generated reviews are compared against original human feedback across diverse prompting strategies to identify systematic behavioral divergences. Our analysis reveals that the generated reviews diverge significantly from feedback provided by human reviewers: LLM ratings are less variable, positively biased, and overconfident, and their cross-reference patterns are model-dependent and distinct from human norms. Furthermore, when evaluated through PRAIB, we observe that LLMs tend to generate longer, more complex reviews, yet frequently overlook the atomic weaknesses noted by human reviewers. By characterizing where and how LLMs reviewing behavior departs from human norms, PRAIB provides the community with a diagnostic tool for identifying which aspects of the review process LLMs can reliably support today and which require further development before deployment.

</details>


### [153] [Redundant or Necessary? A Benchmark for Detecting Redundant Steps in Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29893)
*Minyang Hu, Bo Yang, Zhinuo Zhou, Jiachen Liang, Guo Jiahao, Yiyang Yin, Xiongwei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (45.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了冗余步骤检测这一新研究方向，并构建了 RedundancyBench 基准，包含多样化的任务和精细标注的智能体轨迹，每个步骤根据其对任务完成的贡献被标注为冗余或必要。实验表明，最佳方法在检测冗余步骤上仅达到 24.88% 的分数，凸显了该任务的复杂性和进一步研究的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体评估主要关注任务是否成功完成，忽略了执行效率这一关键维度。实际应用中，智能体轨迹常包含大量冗余步骤，消耗大量资源但对任务完成贡献甚微。因此，需要系统性地研究智能体轨迹中的冗余步骤检测问题。

Method: 作者首先形式化定义了冗余步骤检测任务，然后构建了 RedundancyBench 基准，包含多种任务类型和人工精细标注的轨迹（每步标注为冗余或必要）。在此基础上，设计了三种代表性检测方法进行评估：基于规则的方法、基于微调的方法和基于大语言模型提示的方法。

Result: 最佳方法在检测冗余步骤上仅达到 24.88% 的分数，部分方法甚至不如随机猜测。这表明冗余步骤检测是一项极具挑战性的任务，现有方法远未达到实用水平。

Conclusion: 冗余步骤检测是一个重要但极具挑战性的研究方向，现有方法表现不佳，亟需更多研究投入。该基准为未来研究提供了标准化的评估平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 RedundancyBench，一个用于检测智能体轨迹中冗余步骤的新基准，属于 AIGC 评测与基准（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）范畴。虽然它评测的是智能体执行效率而非生成内容质量，但该基准本质上是对 AI 生成轨迹/行为的质量评估，与现有 canonical 主题合理相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on agent trajectory efficiency, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in generation models. Signals: new benchmark, redundant step detection, agent trajectories, low method performance, not generation-focused

Abstract: LLM-based agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in solving complex tasks through multi-step reasoning and tool use. However, existing evaluation protocols primarily focus on task success, overlooking a critical aspect of agent behavior: execution efficiency. In practice, agent trajectories often contain redundant steps that consume substantial resources while contributing little to task completion. In this work, we propose and formulate a new research area: \textbf{redundant step detection} for agent trajectories. To support this initiative, we introduce \textbf{RedundancyBench}, a new benchmark that contains diverse tasks with carefully annotated trajectories, where each step is labeled according to its contribution to task completion. Using RedundancyBench, we develop and evaluate 3 representative methods to answer whether a step within trajectory is redundant or necessary. Our results show that even the best-performing method achieves only 24.88\% score in detecting redundant steps, while some methods perform worse than random guessing. These results highlight the task's complexity and the need for further research in this area. \footnote{Code and dataset in this paper are both available in \href{ }{ }.}

</details>


### [154] [Cookie-Bench: Continuous On-screen Key Interaction Evaluation for Web Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30000)
*Haoyue Yang, Zhangxiao Shen, Fan Ding, Hangting Lou, Yifeng Kou, Haoqing Yu, Jingyao Li, Zhengfan Wu, Siqi Bao, Jing Liu, Hua Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (48.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Cookie-Bench，一个包含11个领域、1000个查询的Web代码生成评测基准，以及Cookie-Frame，一个基于元认知监控的三阶段自动化评测框架（静态感知→智能体驱动交互→动态评分），无需参考实现即可对交互式Web应用进行类人评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web代码生成评测依赖人工评判（如Arena）成本高且不可扩展，而自动化评测方法通常依赖参考实现、测试套件或固定检查清单，无法像人类评审者那样对实时交互会话进行综合推理。亟需一种无参考、自主驱动且整体推理的自动化评测方案。

Method: 提出Cookie-Bench基准（11领域、54子类、1000查询，涵盖静态展示和交互式应用任务，分三个难度等级和三种目标语言）和Cookie-Frame评测框架。Cookie-Frame基于Flavell的元认知监控理论，将证据收集与判断分离为三个阶段：静态感知（被动观察形成第一印象）、智能体驱动交互（自主探索应用并捕获连续屏幕视频/音频/截图）、动态评分（在完整证据链基础上给出整体功能和美学评分及结构化失败归因）。

Result: 在Cookie-Bench上，Cookie-Frame与专家人工评分高度一致，并揭示了13个前沿LLM在交互式Web生成任务上存在显著提升空间。

Conclusion: 本文提出的无参考、自主驱动、整体推理的评测范式能够有效评估交互式Web代码生成质量，与人类评判高度对齐，为Web生成领域提供了可扩展的自动化评测方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向Web代码生成的评测基准Cookie-Bench和评测框架Cookie-Frame，属于AIGC生成内容的质量评测与基准构建，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "AIGC 评测与基准"高度匹配。

Importance reason: A new web generation benchmark, but outside the user's core focus on video/image generation and model efficiency. Signals: Web code generation benchmark, reference-free evaluation, interactive application testing, AIGC evaluation, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Front-end web code has become a core product surface for every frontier LLM release, yet evaluating these interactive applications at development speed remains costly because human-judged leaderboards like Arena do not scale. Existing automated proxies typically lean on reference implementations, test suites, or rigid checklists, and tend to miss the reasoned synthesis a human reviewer performs over a live session. We articulate a new evaluation regime that is simultaneously reference-free, autonomously driven, and holistically reasoned, and instantiate it through two artifacts. \textbf{\dataname} is an 11-domain, 54-leaf, 1,000-query WebDev benchmark spanning both static-presentation and interactive-application tasks, balanced across three difficulty tiers and three target-language groups, with briefs rewritten to resist recall from circulated prompts. \textbf{\framename}, grounded in Flavell's metacognitive monitoring, separates evidence accumulation from judgment across three stages: Static Perception forms a first impression from passive observation; Agent-Driven Interaction explores the application autonomously while capturing continuous screen video, audio, and per-step screenshots; Dynamic Scoring issues holistic functionality and aesthetics verdicts with structured failure attribution only after the evidence chain is complete. On \dataname, \framename aligns closely with expert human ratings while surfacing substantial headroom across 13 frontier LLMs on interactive web generation. \noindenthttps://anonymous.

</details>


### [155] [RAISE: RAG Design as an Architecture Search Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30029)
*Zhen Chen, Yibing Liu, Weihao Xie, Yu Liang, Peilin Chen, Shiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (58.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 RAISE（RAG Intelligence Search Engine），将 RAG 系统设计形式化为架构搜索问题，构建了包含 13 种搜索算法、7 个文本与多模态数据集的标准化超参数优化评测框架，实验表明优化性能高度依赖任务，不存在通用最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 RAG 系统在查询重写、分块、检索深度、重排序、上下文压缩等环节存在大量设计选择，实践中常依赖启发式配置，缺乏系统化评估和可复现性。作者认为这一问题应形式化为 RAG 架构搜索问题，并需要标准化的评测平台。

Method: 构建 RAISE 框架，实现 13 种搜索算法（包括贝叶斯优化、进化算法、随机搜索等），在 7 个公开文本和多模态数据集上，使用标准化搜索空间和预算，通过 3 个随机种子进行系统评估。

Result: 实验表明优化性能高度依赖具体任务：在某数据集上表现优异的方法在其他数据集上未必泛化，提示不应将聚合排名视为通用最优策略的证据。

Conclusion: RAISE 为 RAG 超参数优化提供了公平、可复现、系统化的实验平台，揭示了任务依赖性的关键发现，为未来 RAG 系统设计提供了方法论基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 RAISE 框架，系统研究 RAG 系统的超参数优化问题，构建了标准化的搜索空间和评测基准，属于 AIGC 评测与基准（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）范畴。同时 RAG 涉及多模态数据检索与生成，也匹配多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on RAG system optimization, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key directions. Signals: RAG architecture search, hyperparameter optimization, task-dependent performance, no universal strategy, multimodal datasets included

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems expose numerous design choices spanning query rewriting, chunking, retrieval depth, reranking, and context compression. In practice, these choices are often configured through heuristics, hindering systematic evaluation and reproducibility across settings. We argue that this challenge is best formulated as RAG architecture search. To support controlled and reproducible study of this problem, we introduce the RAG Intelligence Search Engine (RAISE), a comprehensive framework and benchmark for RAG hyperparameter optimization, which evaluates optimization methods for RAG pipelines under standardized search spaces and budgets. RAISE implements 13 search algorithms and evaluates them across seven public text and multimodal datasets using three random seeds. Our experiments show that optimization performance is highly task-dependent: methods that perform strongly on one dataset may not generalize consistently across others, cautioning against interpreting aggregate rankings as evidence of universally superior strategies. RAISE provides a common experimental substrate for fair, reproducible, and systematic research on RAG hyperparameter optimization.

</details>


### [156] [Personalized Turn-Level User Conversation Satisfaction Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29711)
*Zhefan Wang, Zhiqiang Guo, Weizhi Ma, Min Zhang, Quanjia Yan, Hengliang Luo*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出个性化对话满意度评估方法，通过紧凑的用户记忆与目标轮次上下文结合生成满意度评分和不满理由，并构建 PersTurnBench 基准用于评估生成模型在个性化满意度上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动评估方法主要衡量通用响应质量，难以判断特定轮次下某个响应是否满足特定用户的个性化期望，因此需要个性化、轮次级别的对话满意度评估方法。

Method: 构建结合紧凑用户记忆与目标轮次上下文的满意度评估器，输出满意度分数和不满理由；采用事后分数校准提升序数一致性；基于该评估器构建 PersTurnBench 基准，通过回放固定对话状态实现不同生成模型的可控比较。

Result: 个性化记忆与事后分数校准在序数一致性和不满轮次检测上优于监督式、检索式和通用 LLM-as-a-judge 基线方法。

Conclusion: 个性化记忆和事后校准能有效提升对话满意度评估的准确性，PersTurnBench 可在无需为每个候选模型收集新用户反馈的情况下比较生成模型的个性化满意度表现。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了面向个性化对话满意度的评测基准 PersTurnBench 和评估器，属于 AIGC 生成内容（对话响应）的质量评测与基准构建，与 canonical 子主题 aigc_evaluation_benchmark（AIGC 评测与基准）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on a narrow evaluation benchmark for conversational AI, not aligned with user's priority on generative video/image models or efficiency. Signals: AIGC评测与基准, 个性化对话满意度, 用户记忆, 非生成模型核心方向, 无视频/图像生成关联

Abstract: User satisfaction with AI assistants is highly personalized: the same response may satisfy one user but disappoint another depending on what each user expects and what they have asked for before. Existing automatic evaluation methods mostly measure generic response quality, making it difficult to judge whether a response satisfies a user at a specific turn. We study this problem as personalized turn-level user conversation satisfaction evaluation. We build a conversation satisfaction evaluator that combines compact user memories with target-turn context to produce satisfaction scores and dissatisfaction-oriented rationales. Meta-evaluation against human satisfaction annotations shows that personalized memory and post-hoc score calibration improve ordinal agreement and dissatisfied-turn detection over supervised, retrieval-based, and generic LLM-as-a-judge baselines. We further introduce PersTurnBench, a personalized turn-level user conversation satisfaction benchmark that uses the verified evaluator to assess generation models via replay. By holding the replay state fixed, PersTurnBench enables controlled comparison of generic generation models and memory-augmented personalized systems without new human labels for every candidate model. The evaluator and benchmark let researchers compare candidate generation models on personalized satisfaction without collecting new user feedback for every model.

</details>


### [157] [HEART-Bench: Do LLM Agents Exhibit Human-like Psychology?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30058)
*Weihan Peng, Chenxu Zhang, Qianao Wang, Yuling Shi, Heng Lian, Qihong Mao, Jiahao Pang, Chunliang Feng, Bowen Li, Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了HEART-Bench，一个系统评估LLM智能体能否模拟连贯类人心理的基准。该基准基于大五人格构建了11个多样化角色，每个角色整合了1000个结构化自传体记忆，并设计了64个基于DIAMONDS心理学框架的决策场景，最终形成673道多选题，用于评估智能体在人格一致性、情感和价值一致行为决策方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体研究主要关注任务导向能力（规划、推理、行动），但很少将智能体视为具有完整人格的个体，忽视了情感维度的重要性。作者希望系统评估LLM智能体是否能模拟连贯的、类人的心理学特征。

Method: 基于大五人格理论构建11个正交人格角色，为每个角色整合1000个结构化自传体记忆（分布在理论驱动的不同人生发展阶段）；基于DIAMONDS心理学框架（包含Duty、Intellect、Adversity等8个维度）设计64个决策场景；通过系统的人类验证和筛选，最终得到包含673道多选题的基准测试集。

Result: 经过系统的人类验证和筛选，构建了一个包含673道多选题的基准测试集，可用于评估LLM智能体在人格一致性、情感和价值一致行为决策方面的表现。

Conclusion: HEART-Bench为研究LLM智能体中的类人情感、人格一致性和价值一致行为决策提供了一个有原则且可扩展的测试平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个评估LLM智能体是否能模拟类人心理的基准（HEART-Bench），属于AIGC方向下的智能体评测与基准，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "AIGC 评测与基准"（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM psychology simulation, not on the user's priority generative modeling or efficiency topics. Signals: LLM agent psychology, personality consistency, benchmark, not generative modeling, not video/image generation

Abstract: While LLM agents have demonstrated remarkable task-oriented abilities such as planning, reasoning, and action, few works have treated them as complete human personalities where emotional dimensions hold equal importance. In this paper, we introduce a novel benchmark to systematically assess whether LLM agents can simulate coherent, human-like psychology. Specifically, our benchmark constructs 11 diverse human characters grounded in orthogonal Big Five personality traits, with each profile deeply integrated with 1,000 structured autobiographical-style episodic memories distributed across theory-grounded developmental life stages. To rigorously evaluate the psychological manifestations of LLMs, we designed a curated suite of 64 decision-making scenarios, guided by the DIAMONDS taxonomy, a psychological framework that characterizes situations along eight dimensions: Duty, Intellect, Adversity, Mating, pOsitivity, Negativity, Deception, and Sociality. By subjecting agents to varying scenarios, the benchmark evaluates whether they can consolidate their innate personality traits and autobiographical memories to make behavioral decisions that are consistent with their specific psychological profiles. After systematic human validation and filtering, we obtained a benchmark consisting of 673 multiple-choice questions (MCQs). We believe this benchmark provides a principled and scalable testbed for studying human-like emotions, personality consistency, and value-consistent behavioural decision-making in LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [158] [LogDx-CI: Benchmarking Log Reduction Tools for LLM Root-Cause Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28876)
*Bowen Qin*

Main category: cs.SE, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 LogDx-CI 基准，在 35 个真实 GitHub Actions 失败案例上系统比较了 11 种日志缩减工具对 LLM 根因诊断的影响。主要发现：(1) 混合 grep+tail 路由器在成本-质量帕累托前沿上表现最佳；(2) 智能体循环中缩减工具的质量差异大幅缩小，但弱上下文会导致 2-4 倍的工具调用开销；(3) 跨系列 LLM 摘要-诊断组合（如 gpt-5-mini 摘要 + Claude Haiku 诊断）优于同系列组合，反驳了自调用偏差假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI 失败日志规模大（中位数 5k 行，最大 200k 行）且噪声多，编码智能体依赖上游工具将日志缩减到可管理的上下文长度，但该领域缺乏公开的实证比较来评估哪些缩减方法能保留足够证据供下游 LLM 诊断。

Method: 构建 LogDx-CI 基准，包含 35 个真实 GitHub Actions 失败案例，比较 11 种上下文缩减工具（raw、tail、grep、三种 RTK 模式、两种 LLM map-reduce 摘要器、三种混合路由器），使用 3 个 LLM 诊断器家族（Claude Haiku 4.5、Claude Sonnet 4.6、OpenAI gpt-5-mini）以及一个 Sonnet 4.6 工具使用智能体进行评分。

Result: (1) 混合 grep+tail 路由器在成本-质量帕累托前沿上表现最佳，前两名方法得分 0.670/0.666，每案例约 $0.03，质量与独立 grep 相当但 token 量减少 4.5 倍；(2) 智能体循环中缩减工具的质量差异从单次 0.42 缩小到 0.059（缩小 7 倍），但弱上下文导致智能体发出 2-4 倍的工具调用；(3) 跨系列 LLM 摘要-诊断组合（gpt-5-mini 摘要 + Claude Haiku 诊断）平均比同系列组合高 0.071，反驳了自调用偏差假设。

Conclusion: LogDx-CI 基准为 LLM 根因诊断中的日志缩减工具选择提供了首个系统性的实证比较。混合 grep+tail 路由器在成本和质量之间取得了最佳平衡；智能体循环可以弥补弱上下文的不足但代价是更高的工具调用成本；跨系列 LLM 组合可能优于同系列组合，为实际系统设计提供了重要指导。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 LogDx-CI 基准，系统比较了 11 种日志缩减工具在 LLM 根因诊断任务上的效果，属于 AIGC 评测与基准（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）范畴。虽然具体场景是 CI 日志缩减，但其核心贡献是构建评测基准并对比不同工具/方法的生成诊断质量，与 canonical 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on software engineering log reduction for LLM debugging, not on core generative model advances. Signals: software engineering, log reduction, LLM debugging, CI failure diagnosis, not generative model

Abstract: CI failure logs are large (median 5k lines, max 200k in this corpus) and noisy. Coding agents that try to debug them depend on an upstream tool to reduce the log to a manageable context, but the field has had no public empirical comparison of which reductions preserve enough evidence for downstream LLM diagnosis. We introduce LogDx-CI, a benchmark that compares 11 context-reduction tools (raw, tail, grep, three RTK modes, two real LLM map-reduce summarizers, three hybrid routers) on 35 real GitHub Actions failure cases, scored by 3 LLM debugger families (Claude Haiku 4.5, Claude Sonnet 4.6, OpenAI gpt-5-mini) plus a Sonnet 4.6 tool-using agent. We report three load-bearing findings. (1)~Hybrid grep+tail routers dominate the cost-quality Pareto frontier; the top two methods score 0.670 / 0.666 at $\sim$ \$0.03 per case, same-ballpark quality as standalone grep at $4.5\times$ fewer tokens. (2)~In the agent-loop regime, the quality range across reduction tools collapses $7\times$ (single-shot spread 0.42 $\to$ agent-loop spread 0.059); the agent rescues weak contexts via follow-up tool calls. However, cost differences persist: weak contexts force the agent to issue 2--4$\times$ more tool calls to recover. (3)~A cross-family LLM-summary pair (gpt-5-mini summarizer feeding a Claude Haiku debugger) beats the same-family pair by $+0.071$ averaged across four diagnoser variants, falsifying the self-call-bias hypothesis on this task. The gpt-5-mini summarizer is also the agent-loop \#1 method (score 0.749) at $0.37$ tool-calls per case and $10\times$ lower reducer cost than the Haiku summarizer (\$0.18 vs \$1.75 per case). All data, code, per-case bundles, and reproducibility infrastructure are public.

</details>


### [159] [GEO-Bench: Benchmarking Ranking Manipulation in Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29107)
*Ojas Nimase, Zhe Chen, Gengpei Qi, Yue Zhao, Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (45.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出GEO-Bench，一个统一评估生成式引擎优化（GEO）排名操控攻击的基准，涵盖黑盒提示攻击、白盒梯度攻击和C-SEO策略，在五个数据集上使用固定开源排序模型评估有效性和隐蔽性，揭示了攻击效果与隐蔽性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用于对产品、文档和推荐进行排序，但针对生成式引擎优化（GEO）的排名操控攻击缺乏统一的评估标准，每种方法都在自己的数据集和指标上评估，无法直接比较其相对强度和可检测性。

Method: 构建GEO-Bench基准，统一了黑盒提示攻击（TAP、Zero-Shot）、白盒梯度攻击（STS、RAF、StealthRank）和十种白帽C-SEO策略，在五个数据集上使用固定开源排序模型（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）进行评估，采用有效性指标（NRG、Success@α、Promote@α）和隐蔽性指标（关键词违规率、困惑度比）进行综合评分。

Result: 实验表明：有效性和隐蔽性在对抗攻击中存在权衡；黑盒内容重写在排名提升上达到或超过梯度攻击，同时生成更流畅的文本，且能在某些领域规避关键词和困惑度检测；访问模型并不能预测攻击强度。

Conclusion: GEO-Bench通过标准化数据集、攻击实现和评估指标，首次实现了对这些攻击范式的直接比较，为检测方法的开发提供了支持。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了GEO-Bench基准，用于统一评估生成式引擎优化（GEO）中的排名操控攻击方法，属于AIGC领域的安全评测与基准构建，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "AIGC 评测与基准"（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on ranking manipulation in generative engines, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in generative model architectures and efficiency. Signals: AIGC benchmark, ranking manipulation, security focus, no direct video/image generation, no efficiency or world model angle

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rank products, documents, and recommendations for user queries, which makes manipulating these rankings a growing concern for fairness and information integrity. Research on generative engine optimization (GEO) has produced many manipulation methods, but each is evaluated on its own dataset with its own metrics, so their relative strength and detectability stay unclear. We present GEO-Bench, a benchmark that evaluates GEO ranking-manipulation attacks under one protocol. It unifies black-box prompt-based attacks (TAP, Zero-Shot), white-box gradient-based attacks (STS, RAF, StealthRank), and ten white-hat C-SEO strategies. We score every method on five datasets against a fixed open-weight ranker (Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct), using metrics for both effectiveness (NRG, Success@{\alpha}, Promote@{\alpha}) and stealth (keyword violation rate, perplexity ratio). Our evaluation shows that effectiveness and stealth trade off across adversarial attacks, that black-box content rewriting matches or exceeds gradient-based attacks on rank promotion while producing more fluent text and can evade both keyword- and perplexity-based detection on some domains, and that the access model does not predict attack strength. By standardizing datasets, attack implementations, and metrics, GEO-Bench enables the first direct comparison across these attack paradigms and supports the development of detection methods.

</details>


### [160] [SciIntBench: Measuring LLM Compliance with Research Integrity Norms Under Adversarial Framing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29468)
*Almene De Meran Meguimtsop, Maria Leonor Pacheco, Daniel E. Acuna*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (45.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SciIntBench，一个包含 810 个提示的对抗性基准，用于评估 LLM 在科研诚信规范下的行为。基准覆盖 10 个 RCR 类别和 3 个科学领域，每个场景包含显式对抗、隐式对抗和良性三种版本。评估了 6 个提供商的 16 个模型（2024-2026），共 12,960 条回复，发现模型对科研诚信的对齐高度依赖于问题框架，对隐式违规的拒绝远不如显式违规可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多地被用于支持科研工作，但它们是否遵守负责任的科研行为（RCR）规范尚不清楚。现有基准缺乏对科研诚信场景的系统性评估，特别是对对抗性框架（如将不当行为描述为压力驱动的捷径）的敏感性测试。

Method: 构建 SciIntBench 基准，包含 810 个提示，覆盖 10 个 RCR 类别（如透明性、抄袭、伪造等）和 3 个科学领域。每个场景有三种变体：显式对抗（直接要求不当行为）、隐式对抗（将不当行为包装为合理需求）和良性（合法请求）。评估了 6 个提供商的 16 个商业和开源 LLM，共产生 12,960 条回复，分析模型对不当行为的拒绝率和合法请求的有用性。

Result: 模型对科研诚信的对齐高度依赖于问题框架：显式不当行为被拒绝的可靠性远高于隐式违规，尤其是当不当行为被呈现为压力驱动的捷径时。不同 RCR 类别的拒绝率差异显著，模型在透明性、抄袭和伪造方面的边界较弱。

Conclusion: 当前 LLM 在科研诚信对齐方面存在严重缺陷，特别是在面对隐式违规时。需要改进模型对科研诚信规范的遵循能力，以保障其在科研工作中的安全使用。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SciIntBench，一个用于评估 LLM 在科研诚信规范下行为的对抗性基准，属于 AIGC 方向下的评测与基准子主题（aigc_evaluation_benchmark），因为其核心贡献是构建了一个新的评测基准来评估生成模型（LLM）在特定场景下的表现。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM compliance with research integrity norms, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to generation or efficiency. Signals: LLM safety evaluation, adversarial benchmark, research integrity, not aligned with priority subtopics, negative keywords absent

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support scientific work, but it is unclear whether they uphold responsible conduct of research (RCR) norms or help undermine them. We introduce SciIntBench, an adversarial benchmark of 810 prompts across ten RCR categories and three scientific domains. Each scenario appears as an Overt Adversarial, Covert Adversarial, and Benign version, allowing us to jointly measure framing-sensitive refusal of misconduct and helpfulness on legitimate requests. We evaluate 16 commercial and open-weight LLMs from six providers (2024--2026), producing 12,960 responses. We find that scientific integrity alignment is strongly framing-sensitive: models refuse explicit misconduct far more reliably than covert violations, especially failing when misconduct is presented as a pressure-driven shortcut. Refusals vary by RCR category, with weaker boundaries around transparency, plagiarism, and fabrication.

</details>


### [161] [RoboWits: Unexpected Challenges for Robotic Creative Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30326)
*Chunru Lin, Hongxin Zhang, Fenghao Yu, Zhehuan Chen, Thomas L. Griffiths, Yejin Choi, David Held, Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: AIGC 评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (46.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboWits，一个双臂机器人基准测试，通过自动化多智能体任务生成流水线构建了 30 个种子任务和 208 个变异任务，系统评估机器人在意外条件下的认知推理、创造性工具使用和鲁棒性。实验发现预训练 VLA 在种子任务上微调后表现尚可，但在变异任务上表现脆弱，揭示了当前策略在推理和策略适应方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准测试主要关注技能层面的执行能力，缺乏对认知推理、创造性问题解决和应对意外挑战能力的系统评估。作者希望构建一个能够评估机器人认知推理能力的基准，填补这一空白。

Method: 提出 RoboWits 基准测试，包含一个自动化任务生成流水线，采用多智能体协作框架，包括种子任务生成与验证智能体、度量生成智能体、场景生成智能体和任务变异智能体。通过该流水线构建了 30 个多样化种子任务和 208 个变异任务，涵盖几何、材料和装配推理，并具有分级难度。使用该基准评测了多种机器人策略、预训练 VLA 和 oracle 状态规划器。

Result: 预训练 VLA 在种子任务上经过单任务微调后取得初步成功，但在变异任务上表现不佳，表明其在需要推理、策略适应和对欺骗性或受限环境鲁棒性的操作任务中存在脆弱性。机器人策略与人类表现之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: RoboWits 揭示了当前机器人策略在创造性问题解决和应对意外挑战方面的局限性，为未来研究提供了新的评测方向。自动化任务生成流水线为构建高质量推理中心化场景提供了可扩展方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 RoboWits，一个双臂机器人基准测试，系统评估机器人在意外条件下的认知推理、创造性工具使用和鲁棒性。这属于 AIGC 评测与基准（aigc_evaluation_benchmark）范畴，因为其核心贡献是构建了一个新的评测基准来评估机器人创造性问题解决能力。同时，它也涉及多模态生成方向下的生成模型评测与数据集（generation_benchmark），因为论文评测了预训练 VLA 等生成式策略。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic reasoning benchmarks, not directly aligned with user's priority subtopics in generative AI and efficient inference. Signals: robotic benchmark, creative problem solving, VLA brittleness, not generative AI, not video/image generation

Abstract: The ability to reason, adapt, and creatively solve problems under unexpected challenges is essential for robots operating in real-world environments. However, current robotic benchmarks primarily emphasize skill-level execution and provide limited insight into such cognitive reasoning capabilities. We introduce RoboWits, a bi-manual robotic benchmark designed to systematically evaluate cognitive reasoning, creative tool use, and robustness to unexpected conditions. To enable scalable construction of high-quality reasoning-centric unexpected scenarios, we propose an automated task generation pipeline formulated as a multi-agent cooperative framework, comprising agents for seed task generation and verification, metric generation, scene generation, and task mutation. Using the pipeline, we curated 30 diverse seed tasks and 208 tasks with mutations and graded difficulty across geometry, material, and assembly-based reasoning. We benchmark popular robot policies, pre-trained VLAs, and oracle-state planners. Our results reveal a significant performance gap: while pre-trained VLAs exhibit preliminary success on seed tasks after single-task fine-tuning, they struggle to perform on mutated tasks, implying their brittleness in manipulation tasks requiring reasoning, strategy adaptation, and robustness to deceptive or constrained environments. Project page is available at .

</details>
