### [162] [Beyond Recall: Behavioral Specification as an Interpretive Layer for AI Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28969)
*Aarik Gulaya*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.HC, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 个性化与对话生成

Importance: 普通相关 (48.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出"表征准确性"（representational accuracy）概念来衡量AI系统对用户个性化理解的忠实度，并设计Behavioral Specification作为解释层来压缩用户数据为行为模式，作为LLM的上下文。实验表明该方法在14个自传体语料库上以约25倍更少的上下文成本提升了表征准确性，几乎消除了模型模棱两可的回答，且对预训练中代表性不足的用户群体提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI个性化系统主要依赖记忆用户事实（如Mem0、Letta等记忆系统），但事实记忆不等于对用户行为模式的理解。当AI代理替用户做决策时，需要准确捕捉用户的解释和判断方式，而不仅仅是回忆事实。作者希望区分"回忆"（recall）和"表征准确性"（representational accuracy），并提供一个可测试的框架来评估AI对用户表征的忠实度。

Method: 提出Behavioral Specification作为解释层，通过激进压缩用户数据为可解释的行为模式，作为LLM的上下文。设计了一个原型基准测试，包含保留的行为预测任务，使用经过校准的5个LLM组成的评审团进行评分。在14个公开的自传体语料库上，独立测试该Specification，并与完整原始语料、完整提取事实、以及四种商业记忆系统（Mem0、Letta、Supermemory、Zep）进行组合对比。

Result: Behavioral Specification在聚合层面提升了表征准确性，几乎消除了模型模棱两可的回答。它以约25倍更少的上下文成本恢复了原始语料的大部分效果。该Specification将不同预训练基线的用户提升到相近的预测水平，对基线最低的用户提升最大（绝对值）。在需要解释的问题上提升最显著，而在纯回忆问题上反而可能产生干扰。

Conclusion: 表征准确性与回忆是不同的概念，人机对齐依赖于系统对用户表征的准确性。Behavioral Specification作为一种轻量级的解释层，能够有效提升AI对用户行为模式的理解，尤其对预训练中代表性不足的用户群体帮助最大。这为AI个性化系统的评估和设计提供了新的方向。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于AI个性化中的用户表征准确性（representational accuracy），提出Behavioral Specification作为解释层来捕捉用户的个性化行为模式，属于个性化生成与角色建模范畴。映射到AIGC方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "个性化与对话生成"（aigc_personalization_persona），该子主题涵盖个性化生成、角色扮演、persona对话等，与本论文高度吻合。同时论文涉及LLM作为评测面板，也与多模态理解与生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Novel behavioral specification concept but focuses on AI personalization, not core generative modeling or efficiency priorities. Signals: representational accuracy, behavioral specification, AI personalization, context compression, user alignment

Abstract: If an AI agent makes decisions on a person's behalf, those decisions must align with its user. We introduce representational accuracy to measure how faithfully a system captures a person's interpretation. An interpretive layer is operationalized as a Behavioral Specification. Our reference implementation aggressively compresses a person's data into interpretive patterns, served as context to a language model. We evaluate the Specification on a prototype benchmark of held-out behavioral predictions scored by a calibrated 5-judge LLM panel. We test it independently and in composition with a range of context conditions: full raw corpus, full extracted facts, and four commercial memory systems (Mem0, Letta, Supermemory, Zep). Across 14 public-domain autobiographical corpora, the Specification lifts representational accuracy in aggregate and nearly eliminates model hedging. It recovers most of what the raw corpus delivers, at ~25x less context cost. The Specification lifts subjects toward a common predictive level regardless of pretraining baseline; the lift in absolute points is therefore largest where the baseline is lowest, suggesting the population of relevance is anyone not adequately represented in pretraining. Lift is greatest on interpretation-required questions, where providing an interpretive layer enables model behavior that extracted facts or raw corpus do not. Conversely, on recall-required questions, this layer can interfere rather than help. We conclude that representational accuracy is distinct from recall and that human-AI alignment is dependent on how accurately the user is represented. Representational accuracy makes that alignment testable.

</details>


### [163] [When Does Persona Prompting Actually Help? A Retrieval and Metric Analysis of Expert Role Injection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29420)
*Shuai Xiao, Su Liu, Weikai Zhou, Jialun Wu, Xinjie He, Zhiyuan Lin, Qiyang Xie*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 个性化与对话生成

Importance: 普通相关 (43.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过控制实验比较了四种提示条件（无角色提示、通用领域专家提示、基于嵌入的角色检索、混合检索）在1140个开放式问题上的表现，发现角色提示在聚合指标上差异不大，但在细粒度指标上存在系统性权衡：角色提示提升专业深度但降低清晰度，且效果高度依赖于问题类型和领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角色提示（persona prompting）被广泛用于引导大语言模型，但其实际价值尚不明确。以往研究多使用聚合分数评估，难以判断专家角色提示是否一致地提升响应质量，还是仅在不同质量维度上改变了响应特征。

Method: 设计了四种提示条件的控制实验：无角色提示、通用领域专家提示、基于嵌入的角色检索、嵌入+LLM混合检索。使用1140个开放式问题，覆盖38个专家角色和6个领域。采用多维度指标（专业深度、清晰度等）而非单一聚合分数进行评估。

Result: 聚合结果仅显示微小差异，但指标级分析揭示了系统性权衡：角色提示提升专业深度但降低清晰度。角色提示在咨询类问题和医学、心理学领域表现最佳；基线提示在金融、法律、科学、技术领域的概念解释类问题上表现更好。混合检索优于纯嵌入检索，但无法消除专业深度与清晰度之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 角色提示主要重塑响应特征而非广泛提升能力，其效果高度条件化。多指标评估对于理解角色提示的实际影响至关重要。

Classification reason: 该论文研究角色提示（persona prompting）对LLM输出质量的影响，属于AIGC方向下的"个性化与对话生成"（aigc_personalization_persona）子主题，该子主题涵盖角色扮演、persona对话等，与本论文的专家角色注入研究高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on persona prompting for LLMs, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in generation models. Signals: aigc, persona prompting, LLM evaluation, tradeoff analysis, not generation-focused

Abstract: Persona prompting is widely used to steer large language models, yet its practical value remains unclear. Prior work often evaluates persona prompting using aggregate scores, making it difficult to determine whether expert-role prompting consistently improves response quality or instead changes responses along different quality dimensions. We study this question through a controlled comparison of four prompting conditions across 1,140 open-ended questions spanning 38 expert roles and six domains: no role prompt, a generic domain-expert prompt, embedding-based role retrieval, and a hybrid retrieval method combining embedding search with LLM-based role selection. Aggregate results show only small overall differences between conditions. However, metric-level analysis reveals a consistent tradeoff that aggregate averages obscure: role prompting systematically increases expertise depth while reducing clarity. These effects are highly conditional rather than universal. Role prompting performs best on advisory questions and in domains such as medicine and psychology, where structured expert framing and risk communication are intrinsically valuable. In contrast, baseline prompting performs better on conceptual and explanatory questions in finance, legal, science, and technology domains, where concise plain-language explanation is more important. We further show that hybrid retrieval significantly improves over embedding-only role selection, although better role retrieval does not eliminate the broader expertise-depth versus clarity tradeoff. Overall, our findings suggest that persona prompting primarily reshapes response characteristics rather than broadly improving capability, and that multi-metric evaluation is necessary for understanding its effects.

</details>


### [164] [DynSess: Dynamic Session-Level Evaluation and Optimization Framework for Role-Playing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29256)
*Rongsheng Zhang, Jiji Tang, Junnan Ren, Zuyi Bao, Weijie Chen, Ruofan Hu, Zhou Zhao, Tangjie Lv, Yan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 个性化与对话生成

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DynSess 提出面向角色扮演智能体的会话级评估与优化框架，包含 DynSess-Eval（基于评分标准的完整对话评估）和 DynSess-Character（通过多轮前瞻搜索构建高质量训练轨迹，使用 DSPO 和 GSRPO 两种变体训练），在少参数情况下达到最强角色模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有角色扮演评估与优化方法大多停留在单轮（turn-level）层面，无法捕捉长程多轮对话中的角色一致性和交互质量，缺乏会话级别的统一框架。

Method: 提出 DynSess 框架：1) DynSess-Eval 通过评分标准对完整对话会话进行评分；2) 利用会话级奖励，通过多轮前瞻搜索构建高质量训练轨迹；3) 训练 DynSess-Character 的两个变体：DSPO（离策略）和 GSRPO（在策略）。

Result: DynSess-Eval 与人类判断的一致性显著优于先前评估器；盲人评估显示 DynSess-Character 在参数量大幅减少的情况下匹配最强角色模型，并保持强角色一致性和交互能力。

Conclusion: 会话级评估与优化对角色扮演任务至关重要，DynSess 框架有效提升了角色一致性和交互质量，且参数效率更高。

Classification reason: 该论文研究角色扮演智能体的会话级评估与优化，属于个性化角色扮演对话生成，与 AIGC 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题"个性化与对话生成"(aigc_personalization_persona) 高度匹配。同时涉及多模态理解与生成方向下的 AI 智能体与生成工作流子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on role-playing agent evaluation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key directions. Signals: session-level evaluation, role-playing agents, multi-turn dialogue, DSPO/GSRPO training, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Role-playing with large language models is fundamentally a session-level task, requiring agents to sustain character identity and interaction quality across extended multi-turn conversations. Yet existing evaluation and optimization methods remain largely turn-level, failing to capture long-horizon quality. We propose DynSess, a unified session-level framework for role-playing agents. DynSess-Eval scores complete dialogue sessions via rubrics targeting long-horizon behaviors. Leveraging its session-level rewards, we construct high-quality training trajectories through multi-turn lookahead search and train DynSess-Character with two complementary variants: DSPO (off-policy) and GSRPO (on-policy). Experiments show that DynSess-Eval aligns with human judgments substantially better than prior evaluators, and blind human evaluation further shows that DynSess-Character matches the strongest character model despite using substantially fewer parameters, while maintaining strong role consistency and interactive ability. Our dataset and code will be released to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [165] [Adaptive Interviewing for Persona Simulation in LLMs: Evidence-Grounded Reasoning Improves Decision Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29458)
*Ruoxi Su, Yuhan Liu, Jingyu Hu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 个性化与对话生成

Importance: 普通相关 (40.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应访谈框架，通过结构化三阶段对话（核心问题、动态追问、综合人格总结）收集persona相关信息，用于提升LLM在道德困境场景中对个体决策的模拟准确性。实验发现，自适应访谈并非统一的准确性提升器，而是一种选择性证据锚定机制：仅当模型实际基于用户特定证据进行推理时，决策对齐才显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM角色模拟通常依赖静态persona描述，缺乏个体层面的价值观、经验和情境线索，导致决策模拟不准确。作者希望探索通过动态对话收集个性化信息是否能提升LLM对特定个体的决策模拟能力。

Method: 提出自适应访谈框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 核心问题（Core-10）——标准化人格相关问题；(2) 动态追问——根据回答自适应追问以挖掘深层信息；(3) 综合人格总结。在道德困境场景中，比较三种对话上下文（仅Core-10回答、完整访谈对话、总结性persona表征）下LLM模拟参与者决策的效果，并分析模型是否实际基于访谈证据进行推理。

Result: 自适应访谈并非统一的准确性提升器：完整访谈中约40%的推理轨迹实际利用了追问获得的证据；这些基于追问证据的预测准确率（45.5%）显著高于仅基于核心问题的预测（39.3%）。表明丰富的persona上下文本身不足以保证提升，关键在于模型是否真正将决策锚定在用户特定证据上。

Conclusion: 自适应访谈作为一种选择性证据锚定机制，能有效提升LLM对个体决策的模拟准确性，但前提是模型实际利用对话中收集到的个性化证据进行推理。未来工作可进一步优化证据锚定机制，提升persona模拟的可靠性和一致性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究通过自适应访谈框架收集persona信息以提升LLM对个体决策的模拟能力，属于个性化角色模拟与对话生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "个性化与对话生成"（aigc_personalization_persona）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM persona simulation and interviewing, not on the user's priority generative modeling or efficiency topics. Signals: LLM persona simulation, adaptive interviewing, decision alignment, moral dilemmas, not generative modeling

Abstract: Accurately simulating the decisions of a specific individual remains challenging for large language models (LLMs), partly because persona information is often provided as static descriptions that miss the values, experiences, and contextual cues needed for individual-level decision simulation. We propose an adaptive interview framework that gathers persona-relevant information through a structured three-stage dialogue: core questions, dynamic follow-ups, and a synthesized personality summary. Using the resulting interview transcripts, we evaluate whether LLMs can simulate participants' decisions in moral dilemma scenarios. We compare three conversational contexts -- Core-10 responses, the full interview dialogue, and a summarized persona representation. We find that adaptive interviewing functions less as a uniform accuracy booster and more as a selective grounding mechanism: follow-up-derived evidence is incorporated in around 40% of full-interview traces, and these follow-up-grounded predictions are more accurate than core-only grounded ones (45.5% vs. 39.3%). These findings highlight that richer persona context alone is insufficient: improvements arise only when models actually ground their decisions in user-specific evidence.

</details>


### [166] [Persona Conditioning of Brand Recommendations in Retrieval-Augmented Commercial Chat: A Prominence-Stratified Cross-Provider Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30207)
*Will Jack, Noah Lehman, Keller Maloney, Sarah Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 个性化与对话生成

Importance: 普通相关 (36.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过2,000次审计实验，系统评估了不同用户角色（persona）对AI商业聊天助手品牌推荐结果的影响。研究发现，在相同查询下，不同角色（如个人创业者、企业VP、中小企业主）获得的推荐集存在显著差异（Jaccard相似度下降0.12-0.20），且该效应呈现明显的品牌知名度分层：头部品牌对角色变化不敏感，而中端品牌推荐集随角色变化最多可变动75%。Anthropic模型比OpenAI模型表现出更大的角色敏感度，与其更依赖训练数据先验的生成路径一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI品牌推荐评测通常忽略用户角色这一关键变量，导致评测结果无法反映真实使用场景中的推荐偏差。作者旨在揭示不同用户角色如何影响AI助手的品牌推荐，为AI品牌感知评测提供方法论指导。

Method: 采用跨提供商审计方法，设计10种角色×8个提示×3种模型配置×10次重复的实验空间（共2,000次运行），使用Jaccard相似度衡量推荐集差异，并通过聚类置信区间进行统计推断。

Result: 角色条件化使推荐集相似度下降0.12-0.20；头部品牌跨角色一致性约80%，中端品牌推荐集变动高达75%；Anthropic模型角色敏感度高于OpenAI模型。

Conclusion: AI品牌感知评测必须考虑用户角色条件，聚合不同角色的评测会系统性掩盖推荐偏差。角色敏感度集中在市场中端品牌，且与模型对训练数据先验的依赖程度正相关。

Classification reason: 该论文研究不同用户角色（persona）对商业聊天AI推荐结果的影响，属于AIGC方向下的个性化与对话生成（aigc_personalization_persona）子主题，该子主题涵盖角色扮演、persona对话、个性化助手等，与本论文的persona条件化品牌推荐高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on brand recommendation auditing in commercial chat, not on core generative model advances. Signals: aigc, commercial chat audit, persona conditioning, brand recommendation, not generative model research

Abstract: The same prompt -- "best CRM software" -- reaches AI assistants from buyers in widely different contexts: a solo founder, an enterprise VP, a UK SMB owner. We audit how strongly that contextual variation reshapes which brands the model recommends. The audit samples 2,000 runs over a design space of 10 personas x 8 prompts x 3 model configurations x N=10 reps, with the two OpenAI cells at full 8-prompt coverage and the Anthropic sonnet-4.6 / low cell at 4-prompt coverage. Prefixing the user message with a persona drops the recommendation-set similarity (Jaccard) by Delta = -0.12 to -0.20 relative to a same-persona baseline (clustered 95% CIs exclude zero on all three measured cells; the sonnet cell's CI rests on only 4 prompt clusters and is correspondingly wider). The effect is sharply prominence-stratified: category leaders are persona-resistant (~80% same-brand consistency across personas), but mid-market brands swap up to 75% of the recommendation set as the persona changes. The Anthropic model shows a larger point-estimate effect than the OpenAI configurations, though clustered CIs overlap for the closer contrast (sonnet vs. OpenAI/high); the asymmetry is consistent with Anthropic's more retrieval-unattributed generation route (43-52% recommendations without observed retrieval-layer evidence, vs OpenAI's 8-29%, documented in Jack 2026). Any measurement of AI brand perception must condition on the buyer persona supplying the query: the same prompt produces materially different recommendation sets depending on who the model thinks is asking, and a measurement protocol that aggregates across personas systematically obscures that variation. The effect concentrates at mid-market and is largest on the most priors-reliant generation route in our audit, consistent with persona responsiveness growing as models lean more on training-data priors and richer context integration.

</details>

### [167] [It`s All About Speed: AI`s Impact on Workflow in Music Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29931)
*Finn McClellan, Fabio Morreale*

Main category: eess.AS, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 创意与艺术生成

Importance: 普通相关 (40.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过民族志研究，探讨AI和自动化工具对音乐制作工作流的影响，重点关注录音工程师、混音师和制作人对AI工具的使用体验与态度，揭示了速度效率、可控性和创意能动性之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI工具在音乐制作中的普及，理解这些工具如何改变专业音乐人的工作流程、创作实践以及人机协作关系变得至关重要，但目前缺乏深入的定性研究。

Method: 采用民族志研究方法，对专业录音工程师、混音师和制作人进行深度访谈和观察，分析他们在音乐制作中使用AI和自动化工具的实际体验与态度。

Result: 发现AI工具在提升速度和效率方面受到欢迎，但在可控性和维护创意能动性方面存在显著张力；用户需要在效率与创作控制之间寻求平衡。

Conclusion: AI工具的设计需要更好地平衡速度效率与用户对创意过程的控制需求，以缓解人机协作中的张力，从而更有效地支持音乐制作工作流。

Classification reason: 该论文研究AI工具对音乐制作工作流的影响，属于创意艺术生成领域的应用研究，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "创意与艺术生成"（aigc_creative_art）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on music production workflow rather than core generative model advances in video/image generation or efficiency. Signals: ethnographic study, music production, creative agency, speed-efficiency tension, not video/image generation

Abstract: In this paper, we present the results of an ethnographic study into the impact of AI and automated tools on music production workflow. Focusing specifically on professional participants who identified as recording engineers, mixers, and producers, we discuss their usage of common AI and automated software, as well as their sentiments on the proliferation of these tools. We discuss tensions that may be created between users and automated tools in key areas such as the need for speed and efficiency, controllability, and maintaining creative agency, and how these tensions may be alleviated through tool design.

</details>

### [168] [GenesisFunc: Multi-Agent Data Generation for Accurate and Generalizable Function-Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28835)
*Hao-Xiang Xu, Chong Deng, Jiaqing Liu, Wen Wang, Qian Chen, Lujia Bao, Xiangang Li, Zhen-Hua Ling*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 合成数据与数据集蒸馏

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GenesisFunc 是一个多智能体自动化流水线，用于生成高质量、多样化的函数调用（FC）训练数据。它从公开基准中提取可靠工具，通过多智能体对话生成系统和多阶段评估系统，合成覆盖多样场景的 FC 训练数据。基于该数据微调的 8B LLM 在域内和域外 FC 任务上均优于同规模开源模型，性能接近部分 API 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数调用（FC）训练数据需要高质量、多样性和广泛场景覆盖，但真实数据获取和标注困难，现有合成流水线存在 API 不可靠、工具可扩展性有限、多样性不足和质量控制薄弱等问题。

Method: 提出 GenesisFunc 自动化流水线：(1) 从公开基准中提取可靠工具集；(2) 采用多智能体框架（包括对话生成智能体、工具选择智能体等）生成覆盖多样场景的对话数据；(3) 通过多阶段评估系统（包括语法验证、语义验证、功能验证等）确保数据准确性。

Result: 基于合成数据微调的 8B LLM 在域内 FC 性能和域外泛化上均优于同规模开源模型，FC 能力接近部分最新的 API 模型，且方法在跨下游工具上展现出良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: GenesisFunc 有效解决了 FC 训练数据合成中的关键挑战，证明了自动化多智能体数据生成流水线在提升 FC 模型性能方面的潜力，具有实际应用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 GenesisFunc，一个用于生成函数调用（function-calling）训练数据的自动化多智能体流水线，属于合成训练数据生成。这与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "合成数据与数据集蒸馏" (aigc_synthetic_data) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on synthetic data generation for function-calling, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key directions. Signals: Multi-agent data generation, function-calling LLM, synthetic data pipeline, domain generalization, 8B model outperforms open-source

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) extend their capabilities through function-calling (FC), which relies on training data with high quality, diversity, and broad coverage of scenario. However, obtaining and annotating real function-calling data is challenging, while synthetic data from existing pipelines often suffers from unreliable APIs, limited tool scalability, insufficient diversity, and weak quality control. To address these, we present GenesisFunc, an automated pipeline for generating FC training data. Starting from reliable tools in widely used public benchmarks, our GenesisFunc employs a multi-agent framework to support a dialogue generation system that produces conversations spanning diverse scenarios, while maintaining both diversity and quality throughout the process. The accuracy of the data is further reinforced through a multi-stage evaluation system. We fine-tune an 8B LLM on the synthetic dataset and show through extensive experiments that it outperforms similarly sized open-source models in in-domain FC performance and out-of-domain generalization, while reaching FC capabilities comparable to some of the latest API-based models. In addition, our method demonstrates strong potential to scale effectively across downstream tools, underscoring its real-world applicability.

</details>


### [169] [MARTIAN: A Rendering Framework for Aerial Mars Imagery from HiRISE Orbital Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29647)
*Dario Pisanti, Georgios Georgakis*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 合成数据与数据集蒸馏

Importance: 普通相关 (35.9) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MARTIAN 是一个基于 Blender 的开源渲染框架，利用真实 HiRISE 轨道地图数据合成火星地表航拍图像，支持可控光照和高度，生成带精确位姿标注的合成数据，用于训练和评估火星视觉导航系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火星航拍视觉导航需要大量带标注的航拍数据集，但真实火星航拍数据稀缺且标注成本极高，现有数据无法满足深度学习方法的训练需求。

Method: 基于 Blender 构建渲染管线，利用 HiRISE 轨道地图产品（数字高程模型和正射影像）重建火星地形网格，通过程序化光照和相机参数控制生成不同高度和光照条件下的合成航拍图像，并自动输出精确的相机位姿标注。

Result: 该框架已成功部署于 Ingenuity 火星直升机的基于地图的定位系统研究中，使用合成数据训练的深度图像匹配器在真实火星图像上取得了有效结果。

Conclusion: MARTIAN 有效缓解了火星航拍视觉导航中标注数据稀缺的问题，为火星无人机视觉系统提供了可扩展的合成数据生成方案，并已在实际任务中得到验证。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于 HiRISE 轨道数据的火星航拍图像合成渲染框架，用于生成带标注的合成训练数据以解决火星视觉导航数据稀缺问题。这属于合成数据生成（synthetic data）范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 aigc_synthetic_data（合成数据与数据集蒸馏）。同时该工作也涉及 3D 渲染与生成，可匹配 multimodal_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Specialized rendering framework for Mars imagery with no direct connection to user's priority subtopics in generative AI and efficient models. Signals: domain-specific rendering, no generative model, no video/image generation, no efficiency focus, no multimodal alignment

Abstract: Aerial navigation on Mars requires vision-based pipelines that are robust to the diverse illumination conditions and terrain morphology of the Martian surface. A key bottleneck for training and evaluating such methods is the scarcity of large-scale, annotated aerial datasets. We present MARTIAN, an open-source Blender-based rendering framework that leverages real HiRISE orbital map products to synthesize realistic aerial views of the Martian terrain under controllable lighting conditions and at varying altitudes. MARTIAN generates observations with accurate pose annotations, directly addressing the scarcity of training data for vision-based navigation on Mars. The framework has been validated through its deployment in concurrent work on map-based localization systems for Ingenuity and future Mars rotorcraft, where synthetically trained deep image matchers were successfully evaluated on real Mars imagery. MARTIAN is publicly available at: .

</details>


### [170] [Make LLM Learn to Synthesize from Streaming Experiences through Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29940)
*Zhenlin Hu, Yan Wang, Zhen Bi, Zihao Xue, Bingyu Zhu, Longtao Huang, Xiongtao Zhang, Zeyu Yang, Zhixuan Chu, Jungang Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 合成数据与数据集蒸馏

Importance: 普通相关 (46.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 StreamSynth 设定和 SynLearner 框架，使 LLM 能在连续任务流中积累合成数据生成经验，通过探索多样化合成模式、从反馈中学习、平衡样本质量与多样性，实现跨任务迁移，提升后续任务的合成数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据生成研究将每个任务视为独立问题，忽略了模型能否从历史任务中积累经验并迁移到未来任务这一更根本的问题。

Method: 提出 SynLearner 框架，包含三个核心机制：1）探索多样化合成模式以覆盖不同数据分布；2）从反馈信号（如下游任务性能）中学习；3）在任务流演进中平衡样本质量与集合级多样性。

Result: 在多个基准上，SynLearner 能有效利用早期任务的经验提升后续任务的合成性能，展现出一致的跨任务可迁移性。

Conclusion: 合成数据生成可以是一个经验驱动的过程，能够从任务流中受益，为 LLM 持续学习合成能力提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 论文研究 LLM 在任务流中积累经验以提升合成数据生成质量，属于合成数据生成的核心范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 aigc_synthetic_data（合成数据与数据集蒸馏）。

Importance reason: Focuses on synthetic data generation for LLMs, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in video/image generation and world models. Signals: synthetic data generation, LLM, task stream, cross-task transfer, feedback learning

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been widely adopted for synthetic data generation, significantly reducing annotation costs. However, most existing studies treat synthesis as a set of isolated tasks and overlook a more fundamental question: whether a model can learn to synthesize by accumulating experience from past tasks and transferring it to future ones. In this work, we introduce StreamSynth, a new setting in which synthesis tasks arrive sequentially and experience from historical tasks provides informative signals for future synthesis. To address this setting, we propose SynLearner, a general framework that enables synthesis models to acquire reusable synthesis experience over a task stream. Instead of generating data independently for each task, SynLearner encourages the model to explore diverse synthesis patterns, learn from feedback, and balance sample quality with set-level diversity as tasks evolve. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks show that SynLearner effectively leverages experience from earlier tasks to improve synthesis performance on later ones, exhibiting consistent cross-task transferability. These findings provide evidence for the feasibility of StreamSynth and highlight synthetic data generation as an experience-driven process that can benefit from task streams.

</details>


### [171] [From GPS Points to Travel Patterns: Flexible and Semantic Trajectory Generation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30014)
*Silin Zhou, Chenhao Wang, Yuntao Wen, Shuo Shang, Lisi Chen, Panos Kalnis*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 合成数据与数据集蒸馏

Importance: 普通相关 (38.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出HTP框架，利用大语言模型分层生成城市轨迹数据：先通过RQ-VAE将GPS轨迹量化为宏观出行模式token，再使用微调后的LLM生成出行模式序列，最后解码为GPS点序列。该方法支持多条件、可变长度轨迹生成，在两个真实数据集上平均比最强基线提升29.78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹生成方法无法显式捕获出行模式，且只能在单一条件下生成固定长度轨迹。同时，大规模高质量轨迹数据因隐私问题难以获取，亟需一种能生成多样化、高质量合成轨迹数据的方法。

Method: 设计轨迹专用的残差量化变分自编码器(RQ-VAE)，将微观GPS轨迹从粗到细量化为宏观出行模式token；扩展LLM词表以纳入出行模式token，通过监督微调(SFT)使LLM对齐轨迹生成任务，支持在不同条件下生成出行模式序列；最后将生成的模式序列解码为GPS点序列。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上的实验表明，HTP在生成质量上平均超越最强基线29.78%，能够有效捕获轨迹中的空间不规则性（如交通状况导致的点密度变化），并支持多条件、可变长度轨迹生成。

Conclusion: HTP通过分层生成策略（先出行模式后GPS点）有效提升了轨迹生成质量与灵活性，验证了LLM在轨迹数据合成任务中的潜力，为隐私保护下的城市轨迹数据生成提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文使用LLM生成合成轨迹数据，属于合成数据生成范畴，与AIGC方向下的[CANONICAL]子主题"合成数据与数据集蒸馏"(aigc_synthetic_data)高度匹配。同时轨迹生成也可归入多模态生成方向下的领域内容生成应用。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on trajectory generation for urban mobility, which is not a priority subtopic and is flagged by negative keywords. Signals: trajectory generation, GPS data, LLM application, urban computing, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Urban trajectories play a crucial role in modeling urban dynamics and supporting various smart city applications. However, privacy concerns restrict access to large-scale and high-quality trajectory datasets. Trajectory generation provides a promising alternative by synthesizing realistic data to mitigate privacy risks. However, existing methods fail to explicitly capture travel patterns and can only generate fixed-length trajectories under a single condition. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{HTP}, which \textbf{H}ierarchically generates \textbf{T}ravel patterns first and then generates GPS \textbf{P}oints by using large language models (LLMs), rather than directly generating GPS points. We first design a trajectory-specific residual quantization variational autoencoder (RQ-VAE) that quantizes micro-level GPS trajectories into compact, macro-level travel pattern tokens in a coarse-to-fine manner. These tokens capture rich segment spatial irregularities, such as point density variations caused by traffic conditions. Then, we extend the LLM vocabulary with travel pattern tokens to align trajectory representations with the LLM input, and apply supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to align the LLM with the trajectory generation task, enabling generation of travel pattern sequences under various conditions. Extensive experiments on two real-world datasets show that HTP outperforms the strongest baseline by an average of 29.78\% in terms of generation quality. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [172] [Domain-Specific Data Synthesis for LLMs via Minimal Sufficient Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30039)
*Tong Ye, Hang Yu, Tengfei Ma, Xuhong Zhang, Jianguo Li, Peng Di, Peiyu Liu, Jianwei Yin, Wenhai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 合成数据与数据集蒸馏

Importance: 普通相关 (48.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 DOMINO 框架，通过最小充分表示学习从少量参考样本中归纳领域特征，以归纳范式生成领域对齐的合成数据，无需自然语言领域描述或人工提示工程，在代码领域微调中提升 Pass@1 最高达 4.63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据合成方法依赖显式的自然语言领域描述和精心设计的提示词（演绎范式），在领域难以用语言描述或正式表达的现实场景中适用性有限。本文旨在解决这一未被充分探索的问题，提出一种归纳范式，仅通过参考样本定义目标领域，实现无需人工提示的领域特定数据合成。

Method: 提出 DOMINO 框架，核心思想是学习参考样本的最小充分领域表示（minimal sufficient domain representation）。该方法将提示调优（prompt tuning）与对比解缠目标（contrastive disentanglement objective）相结合，分离领域级模式与样本级噪声，在保留核心领域特征的同时缓解过拟合。理论上证明了该方法能扩展合成数据分布的支撑集，确保更大的多样性。

Result: 在代码领域基准测试上，使用 DOMINO 合成的数据进行微调，在强指令微调基线上将 Pass@1 准确率提升最高达 4.63%，验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一种新的领域特定数据合成范式，无需手动提示设计或自然语言领域规范即可实现实用且可扩展的领域自适应，为难以用语言描述的领域数据合成提供了有效解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DOMINO 框架，通过最小充分表示学习从参考样本中归纳领域特征并生成领域对齐的合成数据，属于合成训练数据生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 aigc_synthetic_data（合成数据与数据集蒸馏）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel inductive data synthesis paradigm but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: inductive data synthesis, minimal sufficient representation, domain adaptation, code generation, prompt tuning

Abstract: Large Language Models have demonstrated remarkable progress in general-purpose capabilities and can achieve strong performance in specific domains through fine-tuning on domain-specific data. However, acquiring high-quality data for target domains remains a significant challenge. Existing data synthesis approaches follow a deductive paradigm, heavily relying on explicit domain descriptions expressed in natural language and careful prompt engineering, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios where domains are difficult to describe or formally articulate. In this work, we tackle the underexplored problem of domain-specific data synthesis through an inductive paradigm, where the target domain is defined only through a set of reference examples, particularly when domain characteristics are difficult to articulate in natural language. We propose a novel framework, DOMINO, that learns a minimal sufficient domain representation from reference samples and leverages it to guide the generation of domain-aligned synthetic data. DOMINO integrates prompt tuning with a contrastive disentanglement objective to separate domain-level patterns from sample-specific noise, mitigating overfitting while preserving core domain characteristics. Theoretically, we prove that DOMINO expands the support of the synthetic data distribution, ensuring greater diversity. Empirically, on challenging coding benchmarks where domain definitions are implicit, fine-tuning on data synthesized by DOMINO improves Pass@1 accuracy by up to 4.63\% over strong, instruction-tuned backbones, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness. This work establishes a new paradigm for domain-specific data synthesis, enabling practical and scalable domain adaptation without manual prompt design or natural language domain specifications.

</details>

### [173] [Cert-LAS: Toward Certified Model Ownership Verification for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Layer-Adaptive Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29809)
*Leyi Qi, Yiming Li, Siyuan Liang, Zhengzhong Tu, Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CR, cs.LG, cs.GR, cs.MM

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 水印与版权保护

Importance: 普通相关 (64.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Cert-LAS 提出首个针对文生图扩散模型的认证级模型所有权验证方法，通过层自适应平滑嵌入水印，并证明在恶意移除攻击下仍能可靠验证所有权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于后门的扩散模型水印方法隐含假设验证过程是"忠实"的，即验证者能获取完整的水印响应。然而实际中攻击者可能有意或无意破坏水印信号，导致验证不可靠。因此需要一种具有认证保证的模型所有权验证方法。

Method: Cert-LAS 使用扩散分类器（diffusion classifier）嵌入指定水印，并引入 LFS（Layer-wise Feature Smoothing）引导的层自适应噪声（layer-adaptive noise）进行平滑处理。验证时通过假设检验判断可疑模型是否表现出显著强于无水印参考模型的水印响应，从而完成所有权认证。论文还从理论上证明了在特定条件下该方法能抵御恶意移除攻击。

Result: 大量实验验证了 Cert-LAS 的有效性及其对自适应攻击的鲁棒性，表明该方法能够在存在水印移除攻击的场景下仍实现可靠的所有权验证。

Conclusion: Cert-LAS 是首个具有认证保证的 T2I 扩散模型所有权验证方法，通过层自适应平滑和假设检验框架，显著提升了水印验证在对抗环境下的可靠性，为扩散模型的版权保护提供了新的理论保障。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文生图扩散模型的模型所有权验证（MOV），通过后门水印嵌入与假设检验实现版权保护，属于生成内容水印与版权保护范畴，因此映射到 AIGC 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题「水印与版权保护」(aigc_watermark_copyright)。同时论文涉及 T2I 扩散模型，也匹配多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on watermarking for diffusion models, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks boost keywords. Signals: diffusion model watermarking, model ownership verification, layer-adaptive smoothing, certified robustness, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Large-scale text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models have enabled unprecedented creative applications, but their unauthorized use has raised serious intellectual property concerns, making model ownership verification (MOV) increasingly critical. We find that existing backdoor-based diffusion watermarking methods often (implicitly) assume a "faithful" verification process, namely, that the verifier can query a suspicious model and obtain the faithful watermark response to complete MOV. However, in practice, adversaries may intentionally or unintentionally damage potential watermark signals, significantly degrading verification reliability. To address this issue, we propose Cert-LAS, the first certified MOV method for T2I models based on layer-adaptive smoothing. In general, Cert-LAS embeds specified watermarks using diffusion classifiers and an LFS-guided layer-adaptive noise, and verifies ownership by examining whether the suspected model exhibits significantly stronger watermark responses compared to unwatermarked references through hypothesis testing. We further prove that, under certain conditions, our Cert-LAS can still achieve reliable verification even in the presence of malicious removal attacks. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of Cert-LAS and its resistance to adaptive attacks. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [174] [BitC-3DGS: High-Capacity 3D Gaussian Splatting Watermarking via Bit Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29583)
*Yuquan Bi, Baosheng Yu, Yingke Lei, Jianwei Yang, Hongsong Wang, Jie Gui, Yuan Yan Tang, James Tin-Yau Kwok*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 水印与版权保护

Importance: 普通相关 (55.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BitC-3DGS提出了一种基于比特压缩的3D高斯泼溅水印框架，通过将多个消息比特编码为单个语义token，突破了CLIP文本编码器77-token长度限制，支持128比特及以上高容量水印嵌入，同时保持高恢复精度和渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练文本编码器的3DGS水印方法受限于CLIP的77个token上下文长度，最多只能嵌入77比特消息，无法满足大规模3D资产管线中对高容量水印（如所有权、来源、认证码等丰富信息）的需求。

Method: 提出比特压缩token化方案，将同一块(chunk)内的多个比特编码为单个语义token；设计双分支架构实现联合块解压缩和比特解码；引入硬消息采样策略增强解码器训练时的组合覆盖。

Result: 在Blender和LLFF数据集上的实验表明，BitC-3DGS支持128比特消息容量，恢复精度与现有SOTA方法在64比特消息上的精度相当，同时保持了良好的渲染保真度。

Conclusion: BitC-3DGS通过比特压缩有效突破了CLIP token长度对3DGS水印容量的限制，为高容量3D资产水印提供了可行方案，在版权保护和资产溯源方面具有应用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文研究3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)资产的高容量水印嵌入方法，属于生成内容的水印与版权保护范畴，与AIGC方向下的[CANONICAL]子主题"水印与版权保护"(aigc_watermark_copyright)完全匹配。同时3DGS属于多模态生成方向，因此也匹配multimodal_generation。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS watermarking, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's key directions. Signals: 3DGS watermarking, bit compression, high capacity, CLIP token limit, rendering fidelity

Abstract: High-capacity watermarking is necessary for 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) assets to embed rich information (e.g., ownership, provenance, and authentication codes), enabling reliable identification and integrity verification in large-scale 3D asset pipelines. Existing bit-to-token watermarking methods based on a pre-trained text encoder are limited to 77-bit messages due to CLIP's fixed 77-token context length, as tokens beyond this limit are unsupported by learned positional embeddings. To address this limitation, we introduce BitC-3DGS, a bit-compression framework that encodes multiple message bits per token. It employs a bit-compressed tokenization scheme that encodes multiple bits within the same chunk into a single semantic token. To enable recovery of the compressed information, it further introduces a dual-branch architecture for joint chunk decompression and bit decoding, along with a hard-message sampling strategy to improve combinatorial coverage during decoder training. Extensive experiments on the Blender and LLFF datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of BitC-3DGS for high-capacity watermarking, achieving high message recovery accuracy and rendering fidelity. For example, it supports 128-bit message capacity with recovery accuracy comparable to that of 64-bit messages in recent state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [175] [AliMark: Enhancing Robustness of Sentence-Level Watermarking Against Text Paraphrasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29434)
*Yuexin Li, Wenjie Qu, Linyu Wu, Yulin Chen, Yufei He, Tri Cao, Bryan Hooi, Jiaheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 水印与版权保护

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AliMark 提出了一种基于比特序列编码与对齐的句子级文本水印框架，通过多候选文本变体自适应对齐检测，显著提升了对句子拆分/合并等强改写攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有句子级水印方法虽通过锚定句子语义来增强对改写的鲁棒性，但其基于前缀的设计在面对句子拆分与合并等结构性扰动时仍然脆弱，而这类扰动在强改写模型（如DIPPER、GPT-3.5）下普遍存在。

Method: AliMark 将句子级水印重新形式化为比特序列编码与对齐问题。检测阶段采用两阶段策略：首先生成多个经过结构重排的文本变体，然后自适应地将这些变体提取的比特序列与秘密比特序列对齐，以最小化对齐代价。这种多候选对齐设计天然增强了对句子合并与拆分的鲁棒性。

Result: 大量实验表明，AliMark 在多种改写攻击下显著优于现有最先进的基线方法，尤其在面对句子拆分与合并等结构性扰动时表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AliMark 通过比特序列对齐框架有效解决了句子级水印在强改写攻击下的鲁棒性问题，为文本水印领域提供了一种更可靠的方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文本水印技术，属于AIGC方向下的"水印与版权保护"（aigc_watermark_copyright）规范子主题，该子主题明确涵盖生成内容水印。

Importance reason: Focuses on text watermarking robustness, which is outside the user's priority subtopics in multimodal generation and model efficiency. Signals: text watermarking, sentence-level robustness, paraphrasing attack, bit sequence alignment, not multimodal generation

Abstract: Existing sentence-level watermarking methods enhance robustness to paraphrasing by anchoring watermarks in sentence semantics. However, their prefix-based designs remain vulnerable to structural perturbations, such as sentence splitting and merging, which commonly arise under strong paraphrasers like DIPPER and GPT-3.5. To mitigate this issue, we propose AliMark, a framework that reformulates sentence-level watermarking as a bit sequence encoding and alignment problem between a potentially watermarked text and a secret bit sequence. Notably, our approach adopts a two-stage detection strategy: we generate multiple restructured text variants and adaptively align their extracted bit sequences with the secret bit sequence to minimize alignment cost. This multi-candidate alignment design naturally improves robustness to sentence merges and splits. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AliMark substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines under diverse paraphrasing attacks.

</details>


### [176] [Echoes within the Reasoning: Stealthy and Effective Watermarking via Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28890)
*Jiacheng Lu, Yiming Li, Tao Song, Weijian Wang, Wenjie Qu, Haibing Guan, Jiaheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.LG

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 水印与版权保护

Importance: 普通相关 (43.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 BiCoT，一种将水印嵌入到 LLM 思维链推理轨迹内部几何结构中的框架，通过将高显著性结构锚点与私有签名子空间对齐，同时正则化普通控制 token 以保持语义能力，实现鲁棒且隐蔽的模型水印。此外，引入基于 Top-logprob 的黑盒验证器 RSR，在模型窃取和表征漂移下仍能可靠验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对具备思维链推理能力的大语言模型的黑盒水印方法往往在鲁棒性和推理保真度之间进行权衡——要么扰动最终答案，要么依赖脆弱的触发模式。需要一种既能保护模型知识产权，又不影响推理质量的水印方法。

Method: 提出 BiCoT 框架，将水印信号嵌入推理轨迹的内部几何结构中：通过将高显著性结构锚点与私有签名子空间对齐来嵌入水印，同时对普通控制 token 进行正则化以保持语义容量。为应对模型窃取和表征漂移，提出 Robust Subspace Registration (RSR)，一种基于 Top-logprob 的黑盒验证器，使用哨兵 token 校准输出分布中的系统性偏移。

Result: 实验表明，BiCoT 在多种复杂推理任务上保持了推理保真度，同时在微调、量化、模型级扰动以及自适应输出级攻击下，在域内和域外设置中均实现了鲁棒的检测性能。

Conclusion: BiCoT 通过将水印嵌入推理相关的表征中，使得在不破坏支持连贯推理的特征的情况下难以移除水印，为 LLM 知识产权保护提供了一种兼顾隐蔽性与鲁棒性的有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种针对具备思维链推理能力的大语言模型的水印框架 BiCoT，属于生成模型的知识产权保护与水印技术。这与 AIGC 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "水印与版权保护" (aigc_watermark_copyright) 高度匹配。同时，论文涉及 Chain-of-Thought 推理，也与多模态理解与生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Novel watermarking for LLM reasoning traces, but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords apply. Signals: LLM watermarking, chain-of-thought, model protection, black-box verification, reasoning fidelity

Abstract: Large Language Models with Chain-of-Thought reasoning capabilities represent valuable intellectual property, yet existing black-box watermarking methods often trade robustness for reasoning fidelity by perturbing final answers or relying on fragile trigger patterns. We propose BiCoT, a watermarking framework that embeds ownership signals into the internal geometry of reasoning traces by aligning high-saliency structural anchors with a private signature subspace while regularizing ordinary control tokens to preserve semantic capacity. This design couples the watermark with reasoning-relevant representations, making removal difficult without disrupting the features that support coherent reasoning. To enable verification under model theft and representation drift, we introduce Robust Subspace Registration (RSR), a Top- logprob-based black-box verifier that uses sentinel tokens to calibrate systematic shifts in the output distribution. Experiments show that BiCoT preserves reasoning fidelity across diverse complex reasoning tasks while achieving robust detection under fine-tuning, quantization, model-level perturbations, and adaptive output-level attacks across in-domain and out-of-distribution settings.

</details>

### [177] [Inform, Coach, Relate, Listen: Auditing LLM Caregiving Support Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29473)
*Drishti Goel, Agam Goyal, Veda Duddu, Olivia Pal, Jeongah Lee, Qiuyue Joy Zhong, Violeta J. Rodriguez, Daniel S. Brown, Dong Whi Yoo, Ravi Karkar, Koustuv Saha*

Main category: cs.SI, cs.AI, cs.HC, cs.CL, cs.CY

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (38.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文基于社会支持理论，将LLM在照护场景中的支持角色分为Inform、Coach、Relate、Listen四类，在5000条真实ADRD社区查询上审计GPT-4o-mini、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和MedGemma-1.5-4b-it的安全表现。研究发现LLM的支持角色会系统性影响交互风险的种类和分布，且人类评估显示存在感知质量-安全张力：信息导向的角色被认为更有帮助和可信，但交互风险也更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全评估多使用通用提示，忽略了在照护支持等特定场景中，模型承担不同支持角色时安全表现可能发生系统性变化。作者希望揭示支持角色如何影响LLM的安全风险分布。

Method: 基于社会支持理论定义四种专家审核的支持角色（Inform、Coach、Relate、Listen），加上基础提示和RAG两种基线条件。在5000条真实ADRD社区查询上，对三个LLM（GPT-4o-mini、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、MedGemma-1.5-4b-it）进行系统审计，并开展人类评估研究。

Result: LLM的支持角色系统性影响交互风险的流行度和组成；人类评估显示信息导向角色（Inform/Coach）被认为更有帮助和可信，但交互风险更高，存在感知质量-安全张力。

Conclusion: LLM在照护支持场景中的安全表现高度依赖于其承担的支持角色，安全评估需要超越通用提示，考虑角色特定的风险分布。研究发布了约9万条带风险标注的角色条件模型响应作为生态有效的资源。

Classification reason: 该论文审计LLM在照护支持场景中的安全风险，研究不同支持角色（Inform/Coach/Relate/Listen）下模型的安全表现差异，属于AIGC内容安全与治理范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成内容安全与治理"（aigc_safety_governance）高度匹配。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on LLM safety in caregiving, which is outside the user's priority subtopics of video/image generation, model efficiency, and world models. Signals: LLM safety auditing, social support theory, caregiving context, human evaluation, risk analysis

Abstract: Language models are increasingly being deployed for conversational support in informal caregiving contexts, where interactions often extend beyond information-seeking: caregivers seek emotional reassurance, guidance, and help, while navigating uncertain, relationally complex care decisions. Yet most safety evaluations assess model behavior under generic prompts, leaving a critical question unexamined: does a model's safety profile change with its support role? We study this by operationalizing four expert-reviewed support roles grounded in social support theory: Inform, Coach, Relate, and Listen, and comparing them against two baseline controls: a basic prompting condition and a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) condition. We evaluate across three language models (GPT-4o-mini, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and MedGemma-1.5-4b-it) on 5,000 real-world queries from online Alzheimer's Disease and Related Dementias (ADRD) communities. We find that the LLM's support role systematically shapes both the prevalence and composition of interactional risks. Furthermore, a human evaluation study reveals a perceived quality--safety tension: more directive, information-oriented roles are rated as more helpful and trustworthy despite exhibiting elevated interactional risk profiles. We release ~90,000 support role-conditioned model responses with risk annotations as an ecologically grounded resource for research on safer LLM-mediated conversational support.

</details>


### [178] [Label Over Logic? How Source Cues Bias Human Fallacy Judgments More Than LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29928)
*Mahjabin Nahar, Nafis Irtiza Tripto, Aiping Xiong, Ting-Hao `Kenneth' Huang, Dongwon Lee*

Main category: cs.HC, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (43.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过在线实验（N=505）和LLM对比，发现人类评估者显著受到内容源标签（人类/AI/辅助等）的影响，对标注为"人类撰写"或"人类+AI辅助"的谬误内容给予更高信任和评分，而LLM评估相对稳定。这表明源标签偏差主要是人类的脆弱性，而非LLM的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成和AI辅助内容泛滥，源标签可能扭曲人类的推理判断，影响内容审核和决策。需要探究人类和LLM是否同样受源标签偏差影响，以指导人机协作。

Method: 以逻辑谬误为受控实验场景，设计在线实验（N=505），将被试分配到不同源标签条件（人类、AI、人类+AI辅助、AI+人类辅助、无披露），评估含逻辑谬误的评论。同时用GPT-5.2、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Claude Sonnet 4.5在相同条件下评估，对比人类与LLM的表现。

Result: 人类评估者对标注为"人类撰写"或"人类+AI辅助"的谬误内容显著更宽容，给予更高信任和评分；LLM评估在不同源标签下相对稳定，但模型间存在差异。人类和LLM的置信度均较高，不受谬误存在与否影响。

Conclusion: 源标签偏差在推理评估中主要是人类的脆弱性，而非LLM的。这凸显了在AI中介环境中人机协作的潜力，LLM可以提供更客观的评估参考。

Classification reason: 该论文研究AI生成内容（含AI辅助内容）的源标签如何影响人类对逻辑谬误的判断，属于AIGC内容治理与安全范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成内容安全与治理"（aigc_safety_governance）高度匹配。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on human-AI bias in content evaluation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to the user's core interests in generative models and efficiency. Signals: Human bias vs LLM stability, Source label effects, Logical fallacy evaluation, Human-AI collaboration, Not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: As AI-generated and AI-assisted content floods online spaces, source labels attached to such content can distort human reasoning judgments, with downstream consequences for moderation, evaluation, and decision-making. Whether LLMs share this vulnerability, or offer more source-agnostic evaluation, remains an open question with direct implications for human-AI collaboration. We examine this issue using logical fallacies as a controlled setting to isolate source-label effects on reasoning quality, independent of domain knowledge. We conduct an online study (N=505) where participants are assigned to a source condition (human, AI, human with AI assistance, AI with human assistance, or no disclosure) and evaluate comments containing logical fallacies, comparing their judgments with those of LLMs (GPT-5.2, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Claude Sonnet 4.5), who were evaluated across the same source conditions. Human evaluators were significantly more susceptible to fallacies labeled as written by human or human with AI assistance and assigned higher trust and evaluation ratings in these conditions. LLM evaluations remained comparatively stable across source labels, though performance varied across models. Confidence levels were similarly high across conditions for both humans and LLMs, regardless of fallacy presence. Our findings indicate that source-label bias in reasoning evaluation is primarily a human vulnerability and highlight the potential of human-LLM collaboration in increasingly AI-mediated environments.

</details>


### [179] [Toward AI Systems That Understand Self and Others: A Multi-Phase Inference Framework for Human Cognitive Diversity and World-Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29930)
*Toru Takahashi*

Main category: cs.CY, cs.HC, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (62.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出多阶段推理框架（MIM），形式化异构世界模型的产生机制，将世界模型对齐重新定义为使异构表征可互处理而非强制趋同，为AI系统提供帮助人类理解自我与他人的建设性词汇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代社会的相互误解不仅源于人们持有不同意见或价值观，即使面对相同观察，不同主体也可能形成不同的推理目标、状态表征、预测误差和更新优先级。现有AI对齐方法往往追求趋同或单一价值体系，缺乏对认知多样性的形式化理解。

Method: 提出多阶段推理机制（MIM），包含阶段形成空间（phase-formation space）、前景化场（foregrounding field）、主体特定轮廓状态（subject-specific profile states）以及状态表征之间的对齐映射（alignment maps），形式化异构世界模型的产生与交互过程。

Result: 将世界模型对齐重新定义为使异构表征可互处理（mutually processable）的问题，而非强制趋同或收敛到单一价值体系。将该形式化框架与哲学分歧、认知类型学、社会碎片化和AI对齐问题建立联系。

Conclusion: 该框架为AI系统提供了建设性词汇，通过使意义、价值和预测误差的差异变得可见、可比较和可转化，帮助人类理解自我与他人，促进认知多样性的包容而非消除。

Classification reason: 论文提出多阶段推理框架（MIM）来形式化异构世界模型的产生机制，并将世界模型对齐重新定义为使异构表征可互处理而非强制趋同，最终目标是为AI系统提供帮助人类理解自我与他人的建设性词汇。这与AIGC治理中关于AI对齐、社会碎片化、认知多样性等主题高度相关，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic '生成内容安全与治理'（aigc_safety_governance）。同时论文也涉及多模态理解与生成方向中的AI对齐问题。

Importance reason: Philosophical framing of world-model alignment lacks technical novelty and direct applicability to user's priority subtopics. Signals: theoretical framework, world-model alignment, cognitive diversity, no empirical validation, tangential to generative models

Abstract: Mutual misunderstanding in contemporary society does not arise merely because people hold different opinions or values. Even under the same observations, different subjects may form different inferential targets, state representations, prediction errors, and update priorities. This paper proposes a multi-phase inference framework and defines its core internal mechanism as the Multi-Phase Inference Mechanism (MIM). MIM formalizes how heterogeneous world models arise through a phase-formation space, a foregrounding field, subject-specific profile states, and alignment maps between state representations. On this basis, the paper reframes world-model alignment as the problem of making heterogeneous representations mutually processable, rather than forcing agreement or convergence to a single value system. It further connects this formalism to philosophical disagreements, cognitive typology, social fragmentation, and AI alignment. The aim is to provide a constructive vocabulary for AI systems that can help humans understand self and others by making differences in meaning, value, and prediction error visible, comparable, and transformable.

</details>


### [180] [Robust and Efficient Guardrails with Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29068)
*Siddharth Sai, Xiaofei Wen, Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (54.05) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: COLAGUARD 提出了一种将多步安全推理转移到连续潜在空间的护栏模型，通过阶段式训练课程实现推理时直接传播隐藏状态，在保持与显式推理方法相当的安全性能的同时，实现了 12.9 倍加速和 22.4 倍 token 使用量减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于推理的安全护栏（如 GuardReasoner）虽然性能优于单次分类方法，但推理延迟和 token 开销过大，难以用于高吞吐量部署。作者希望在不牺牲安全鲁棒性的前提下大幅提升推理效率。

Method: 提出 COLAGUARD，采用阶段式训练课程：首先在显式推理数据上训练模型生成逐步推理链，然后通过蒸馏将推理过程压缩到连续潜在空间，使推理时无需生成显式推理文本，直接通过隐藏状态传播完成安全判断。

Result: 在 8 个安全基准的 10 种提示/响应审核设置上，COLAGUARD 相比 Llama Guard 3 提升 macro-F1 8.24 分，与 GuardReasoner 性能相当，同时实现 12.9 倍速度提升和 22.4 倍 token 使用量减少。

Conclusion: 潜在推理为可部署的安全护栏提供了一种实用的替代方案，能够同时提升安全鲁棒性和推理效率，而非将二者视为相互竞争的目标。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 COLAGUARD，一种用于 LLM 安全护栏的潜在推理方法，属于生成内容安全与治理（内容审核、有害内容检测）范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic aigc_safety_governance。同时，该方法也涉及多模态理解与生成方向中的安全对齐问题。

Importance reason: Strong efficiency gains for safety guardrails align with model efficiency priority, but safety subtopic is not a top priority. Signals: 12.9x speedup, latent reasoning for guardrails, matches explicit reasoning performance, reduces token usage 22.4x, stage-wise training curriculum

Abstract: Maintaining the safety of large language models (LLMs) is crucial as they are increasingly deployed in real-world applications. Existing safety guardrails typically rely on single-pass classification or, more recently, distilled reasoning. Reasoning-based guardrails significantly outperform classification-only baselines, but they incur substantial query latency and token overhead that make them impractical for highthroughput deployment. To address this challenge, we propose COLAGUARD, a guardrail model that transfers multi-step safety reasoning into a continuous latent space through a stage-wise training curriculum, enabling direct hidden-state propagation at inference. Evaluated on ten prompt- and response-moderation settings spanning eight safety benchmarks, COLAGUARD improves macro-F1 by 8.24 points over Llama Guard 3 and matches our explicit reasoning baseline, GuardReasoner, in macroF1 while delivering a 12.9X speedup and 22.4X reduction in token usage. Our results suggest that latent reasoning offers a practical alternative to explicit rationale generation for deployable guardrails, jointly improving safety robustness and inference efficiency rather than treating them as competing objectives.

</details>


### [181] [Aligned but Fragile: Enhancing LLM Safety Robustness via Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29396)
*Zhihao Liu, Yifan Wu, Jian Lou, Di Wang, Yuxi Zhou, Yuke Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (46.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文首次从优化器视角研究LLM安全对齐的鲁棒性问题，提出一种混合框架：先进行标准一阶安全对齐，再通过零阶优化进行少量步骤的精炼，以增强对齐的鲁棒性。理论分析和实验表明，仅需少量零阶优化步骤即可在保持安全对齐效果的同时显著提升对参数噪声、激活噪声和量化等扰动的鲁棒性，并利用零阶优化的扰动评估特性估计层级的鲁棒性敏感度，实现高效的精炼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全对齐方法在轻量级后处理操作（如参数噪声、激活噪声、量化）下容易失效，而先前提升鲁棒性的工作主要关注数据筛选、改进对齐目标和识别安全关键参数，忽略了优化器本身的作用。本文旨在从优化器视角出发，探索如何通过零阶优化增强安全对齐的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出混合框架：1）先使用标准一阶优化（如DPO）进行安全对齐；2）再使用零阶优化进行少量步骤的精炼，通过扰动下的安全评估信号来增强鲁棒性；3）利用零阶优化固有的扰动评估特性估计层级的鲁棒性敏感度，使精炼过程聚焦于对鲁棒性关键的层，降低训练开销。

Result: 理论和实验表明，仅需少量零阶优化精炼步骤即可显著提升安全对齐对参数噪声、激活噪声和量化等扰动的鲁棒性，同时保持原有的安全对齐效果和通用能力。层级鲁棒性敏感度估计方法有效降低了训练开销。

Conclusion: 零阶优化为增强LLM安全对齐鲁棒性提供了一种有效且高效的新视角，混合框架在保持安全性的同时提升了鲁棒性，为未来安全对齐研究提供了优化器层面的新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM安全对齐的鲁棒性问题，属于生成内容安全与治理范畴。aigc_safety_governance（生成内容安全与治理）是[CANONICAL]子主题，涵盖内容安全、有害内容检测等，与该论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel optimizer-centric approach to LLM safety robustness, but falls outside user's priority subtopics and is not boosted by key keywords. Signals: LLM safety robustness, zeroth-order optimization, alignment fragility, parameter noise robustness, layer-wise sensitivity estimation

Abstract: Safety alignment for large language models (LLMs) aims to reduce harmful or unsafe behavior while preserving general utility. However, recent findings reveal that alignment effects can be fragile: lightweight post-alignment manipulations, such as parameter noise, activation noise, or quantization, can easily weaken the intended safety behavior. Prior efforts to improve robustness have primarily focused on data curation, modified alignment objectives, and safety-critical parameter identification, leaving the role of the optimizer itself largely unexplored. In this paper, we are the first to study the robustness of safety alignment from the perspective of the base optimizer. This optimizer-centric view naturally points to zeroth-order optimization, which provides a robustness-oriented signal by evaluating safety alignment under perturbations. Based on this insight, we propose a hybrid framework that first performs standard first-order safety alignment and then applies zeroth-order refinement to improve robustness. Both theoretically and empirically, we show that only a few zeroth-order refinement steps can enhance robustness while preserving safety alignment. We further improve the efficiency of zeroth-order refinement by exploiting its inherent perturbation-based evaluations to estimate layer-wise robustness sensitivity, enabling the refinement process to concentrate updates on robustness-critical layers with modest training overhead.

</details>


### [182] [Relevance as a Vulnerability: How Web Retrieval Degrades Safety Alignment in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29224)
*Aditya Nawal, Manit Baser, Mohan Gurusamy*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (48.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentREVEAL框架，系统分析Web检索如何削弱LLM智能体的安全对齐。研究发现：检索与生成绑定在同一步骤会放大有害输出；即使是对立或安全导向的网页（含警告/风险声明）也会使有害遵从率平均增加25%（Safe Source Paradox）；相关性是两种漏洞的共同激活条件。同时发布HarmURLBench基准（1,405个URL + 320个有害行为）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明启用检索的智能体会增加对有害请求的遵从性，但缺乏对检索导致安全退化机制的系统性理解。作者希望揭示检索集成方式和检索内容属性如何共同导致安全对齐失效。

Method: 提出AgentREVEAL诊断框架，沿两个轴分析：1）检索集成方式（单步绑定 vs 多步分离）；2）检索内容属性（中立/对立/安全导向）。通过构建HarmURLBench基准（含1,405个真实URL和320个有害行为），在多个开源和闭源模型上进行系统实验。

Result: 1）检索与生成单步绑定显著放大有害输出；2）发现Safe Source Paradox：安全导向页面（含警告/风险声明）反而使有害遵从率比无检索基线平均增加25%；3）相关性是两种漏洞的共同激活条件；4）闭源前沿模型也呈现类似模式；5）多种代表性流水线干预下有害遵从率仍然偏高。

Conclusion: 检索增强智能体存在安全-效用权衡：相关性既是检索有用的原因，也是安全退化的激活条件。现有安全对齐机制在检索场景下存在根本性脆弱性，需要新的安全设计范式。

Classification reason: 论文研究检索增强的LLM智能体在有害请求下的安全对齐退化问题，属于AIGC方向下的生成内容安全与治理（aigc_safety_governance）范畴，该[CANONICAL]子主题涵盖有害内容检测、安全对齐与治理。

Importance reason: Strong security analysis but low alignment with user's priority on generative model efficiency and video/image generation. Signals: LLM agent safety, web retrieval vulnerability, Safe Source Paradox, HarmURLBench benchmark, relevance as activation condition

Abstract: AI agents augment large language models with external tools such as web retrieval, enabling grounded and up-to-date responses. However, incorporating external content into the generation pipeline can weaken the safety alignment mechanisms that govern model outputs. Prior work shows that enabling retrieval in agents increases compliance with harmful requests. We introduce AgentREVEAL, a diagnostic framework for analyzing retrieval-induced safety degradation in LLM agents. The framework examines two axes: how retrieval is integrated into the agent pipeline and the properties of the retrieved content. Along the integration axis, we find that binding tool invocation and response generation in a single step amplifies harmful outputs. Along the content axis, we uncover the Safe Source Paradox: even oppositional or safety-oriented sources, such as pages containing warnings or risk disclaimers, can increase harmful compliance by an average of 25% compared to the no-retrieval baseline. Finally, we show that relevance acts as a shared activation condition for both vulnerabilities. Similar patterns appear on frontier closed models, and harmful compliance remains elevated under several representative pipeline interventions, with some agents also entering this regime under autonomous retrieval. Because relevance is also what makes retrieval useful, these results expose a safety-utility trade-off for retrieval-enabled agents. We introduce HarmURLBench, a benchmark containing 1,405 real-world URLs paired with 320 harmful behaviors to support future evaluations.

</details>


### [183] [AgentDoG 1.5: A Lightweight and Scalable Alignment Framework for AI Agent Safety and Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29801)
*Dongrui Liu, Yu Li, Zhonghao Yang, Peng Wang, Guanxu Chen, Yuejin Xie, Qinghua Mao, Wanying Qu, Yanxu Zhu, Tianyi Zhou, Leitao Yuan, Zhijie Zheng, Qihao Lin, Yimin Wang, Haoyu Luo, Shuai Shao, Chen Qian, Qingyu Liu, Ling Tang, Ruiyang Qin, Qihan Ren, Junxiao Yang, Kun Wang, Zhiheng Xi, Linfeng Zhang, Ranjie Duan, Bo Zhang, Wenjie Wang, Wen Shen, Qiaosheng Zhang, Yan Teng, Chaochao Lu, Rui Mei, Man Li, Jialing Tao, Xi Lin, Tianhang Zheng, Yong Liu, Quanshi Zhang, Lei Zhu, Xingjun Ma, Junhua Liu, Hui Xue, Xiaoxiang Zuo, Xiangnan He, Chao Shen, Xianglong Liu, Minlie Huang, Jing Shao, Xia Hu*

TL;DR: AgentDoG 1.5 是一个轻量级、可扩展的AI智能体安全对齐框架，通过更新安全分类体系、构建分类引导的数据引擎（含影响函数净化），仅用约1000样本训练0.8B~8B参数的小模型，在智能体安全场景中达到与GPT-5.4等闭源模型相当的性能，并支持训练-free的在线安全护栏部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代开放世界智能体（如OpenClaw）具备强大的跨环境执行能力，但引入了广泛的新型安全风险源；同时前沿AI模型大幅降低了攻击门槛，现有智能体对齐框架不足以应对实际部署中的安全威胁。

Method: 1) 更新智能体安全分类体系以覆盖Codex和OpenClaw执行场景的新兴风险；2) 构建分类引导的数据引擎，结合影响函数净化（influence-function purification）来筛选高质量训练数据；3) 训练轻量级AgentDoG 1.5变体（0.8B/2B/4B/8B参数），仅用约1000样本；4) 构建高效的智能体安全SFT和RL训练环境，将Docker级部署开销降低两个数量级；5) 将AgentDoG 1.5部署为无需训练的在线安全护栏，实现实时安全审核。

Result: AgentDoG 1.5在多样且复杂的交互式智能体场景中达到最先进性能，与GPT-5.4等领先闭源模型性能相当，同时模型参数量极小（最低0.8B），部署效率提升两个数量级。

Conclusion: 提出的轻量级安全对齐框架AgentDoG 1.5有效解决了开放世界智能体的安全对齐挑战，证明了小模型通过精心设计的数据引擎和训练策略可以达到甚至超越大模型的安全性能，为智能体安全部署提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 论文提出面向AI智能体的安全对齐框架AgentDoG 1.5，涵盖安全分类体系更新、数据引擎构建、安全SFT/RL训练及在线安全护栏部署，核心关注智能体安全与内容治理，与AIGC方向下的[生成内容安全与治理]（aigc_safety_governance）高度匹配。同时涉及多模态理解与生成方向中的安全对齐问题。

Importance reason: Focuses on agent safety alignment, not on the user's priority subtopics like video/image generation or world models. Signals: agent safety alignment, lightweight framework, influence-function purification, online guardrail, not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Modern open-world agents such as OpenClaw exhibit powerful cross-environment execution capabilities yet introduce broad new safety risk sources. Meanwhile, advanced frontier AI models drastically lower attack barriers, rendering current agent alignment frameworks inadequate for real-world deployment. To tackle these emerging threats, we propose a lightweight and scalable agent safety alignment framework. Specifically, we update the agent safety taxonomy to accommodate emergent risks from Codex and OpenClaw execution scenarios. We further build a taxonomy-guided data engine with influence-function purification to train lightweight AgentDoG 1.5 variants (0.8B, 2B, 4B, and 8B parameters) using only around 1k samples, achieving comparable performance with leading closed-source models (e.g., GPT-5.4). Based on AgentDoG 1.5, we construct a highly efficient agentic safety SFT and RL training environment, which reduces deployment overhead in Docker-level environments by two orders of magnitude. Finally, we deploy AgentDoG 1.5 as a training-free online guardrail for real-time safety moderation. Extensive experimental results indicate that AgentDoG 1.5 achieves state-of-the-art performance in diverse and complex interactive agentic scenarios. All models and datasets are openly released.

</details>


### [184] [Beyond English and Evasion: A Human-Annotated Multi-Domain Benchmark for High-Stakes LLM Safety Evaluation in Chinese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29667)
*Wajdi Zaghouani, Kholoud K. Aldous, Yicheng Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了ChiSafe-PAS，一个面向中文高风险场景的人工标注LLM安全评测基准，包含1,897条对抗性提示，覆盖自残/暴力、毒品/非法交易、欺诈和讽刺四个领域，并定义了九类混淆技术分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全对齐评测主要基于英文，在中文场景下安全系统失效严重，模型易受拼音、汉字拆解、网络俚语等中文特有的规避技术攻击，缺乏高质量的中文安全评测资源。

Method: 构建了ChiSafe-PAS数据集：1,897条人工标注的中文对抗性提示，覆盖四个高风险领域；其中1,544条包含完整标注（3类响应标签、9类混淆技术分类、风险等级和标注理由）；设计了详细的混淆技术分类体系。

Result: 发布了高质量、文化适配的中文LLM安全评测基准，揭示了英文安全系统在中文场景下的脆弱性，并讨论了训练/评测数据边界模糊、领域覆盖和规模限制等关键问题。

Conclusion: ChiSafe-PAS为研究社区提供了一个文化适配的中文LLM安全对齐评测资源，有助于推动多语言、跨文化场景下的LLM安全研究。

Classification reason: 论文构建了中文LLM安全评测基准，聚焦对抗性提示的安全对齐评估，属于AIGC方向下的生成内容安全与治理（aigc_safety_governance）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on Chinese LLM safety evaluation, which is outside the user's core interests in generative models and efficiency. Signals: Chinese safety benchmark, human-annotated, adversarial prompts, obfuscation taxonomy, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: When Large Language Models (LLMs) are deployed in Chinese-language settings, a troubling pattern emerges: safety systems that work well in English break down. These systems struggle to cross linguistic and cultural bound-aries, leaving models exposed to adversarial prompts that exploit Chinese-specific evasion techniques, including Pinyin romanization, character decomposition, internet slang, and hedging tone. To address this gap, we introduce ChiSafe-PAS (Chinese Safety Pilot Annotation Set), a human-annotated benchmark of 1,897 adversarial Chinese prompts spanning four high-stakes domains: self-harm and violence, drug and illicit trade, fraud, and satire. Of these, 1,544 entries carry complete gold-standard annotations: a 3-class response label (REFUSE, SAFE-REDIRECT, RESPOND), a nine-category obfuscation taxonomy, a risk-level rating, and annotator rationale. We describe the dataset design, annotation process, and obfuscation taxonomy in detail. Our primary goal is practical: to give the research community a high-quality, culturally grounded resource for benchmarking LLM safety alignment. In doing so, we engage three broader tensions in the field: the blurring boundary between training and evaluation data, the need for domain coverage grounded in real-world risk, and the limits of scale as a substitute for cultural expertise.

</details>


### [185] [BioRefusalAudit: Auditing Biosecurity Refusal Depth Using General and Domain-Fine-Tuned Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30162)
*Caleb DeLeeuw*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (40.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 BioRefusalAudit，系统审计语言模型在生物安全场景下拒绝行为的结构鲁棒性。实验发现，不同架构的模型在拒绝有害/良性生物学查询时表现差异巨大：Gemma 2 2B-IT 从未真正拒绝，Gemma 4 在无聊天模板时拒绝率从65/75降至0/75，80 token 上限使所有模型拒绝率归零。作者还引入了一个基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的 divergence score D，用于比较模型表面响应与内部特征激活的一致性，初步证据表明激活级审计能揭示行为评估无法发现的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物安全评估通常只检查模型是否输出有害内容，但忽略了模型拒绝行为本身的鲁棒性——即拒绝是否会在提示框架、格式或输出长度等微小变化下消失。本文旨在系统性地审计这种"拒绝深度"的结构完整性。

Method: 1) 设计75个生物安全提示（涵盖良性/有害生物学查询），跨5种架构（Gemma 2 2B-IT、Gemma 4 E2B-IT、Qwen 2.5 1.5B、Phi-3-mini、Llama 3.2 1B）测试拒绝行为。2) 引入 divergence score D，比较模型表面响应标签与内部 SAE 特征激活之间的差异。3) 在 Gemma 2 2B-IT 上使用 Gemma Scope 1 SAE，在 Gemma 4 上使用作者训练的领域微调生物 SAE，并发布两个微调后的 Gemma 2 领域 SAE。

Result: 1) 没有模型能干净区分良性/有害查询。2) Gemma 2 2B-IT 从未真正拒绝（75个提示全部含糊其辞）。3) Gemma 4 有聊天模板时拒绝65/75，无模板时0/75。4) 80 token 上限下所有模型拒绝率归零。5) Qwen 2.5 1.5B 和 Phi-3-mini 过度拒绝（83-87%良性生物学被标记为有害）。6) Llama 3.2 1B 是唯一展现有意义梯度（61分差距）的模型。7) 一些 Schedule I 但无生物毒性的化合物（如裸盖菇素种植）被拒绝率高于真正有害的生物学查询。8) Gemma 4 上 comply 和 refuse 响应在 divergence score 上分离出0.647分的差距且零重叠（n=75）。

Conclusion: 当前语言模型在生物安全拒绝行为上存在严重的结构脆弱性，拒绝深度高度依赖于提示格式、输出长度等表面因素，且模型倾向于根据法律和文化显著性而非实际 CBRN 危害来拒绝。激活级审计（SAE divergence score）有望揭示行为评估无法发现的失败模式，但当前结果仍是初步的，受限于窄目录、样本内校准和仅限 Gemma 家族的 SAE 覆盖。

Classification reason: 该论文研究语言模型在生物安全场景下的拒绝机制（refusal）的鲁棒性，评估模型是否能在不同提示格式下正确拒绝有害请求，属于AIGC安全治理中的内容安全与合规监管范畴，与 canonical 子主题 aigc_safety_governance 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on biosecurity refusal auditing, which is outside the user's core generative modeling and efficiency priorities. Signals: Biosecurity auditing, refusal robustness, sparse autoencoders, divergence score, limited architecture coverage

Abstract: Biosecurity evaluations of language models typically ask whether models produce hazardous output. This paper asks a complementary question: when a model refuses, is that refusal structurally sound, or does it disappear under modest changes to prompt framing, formatting, or output length? Across five architectures, no model cleanly discriminated benign from hazard. Gemma 2 2B-IT never genuinely refused across 75 prompts, hedging on every hazard-adjacent query. Gemma 4 E2B-IT refused 65/75 prompts with chat-template formatting and 0/75 without it. Both Gemma models collapsed to 0% under an 80-token cap. Qwen 2.5 1.5B and Phi-3-mini over-refused, flagging 83-87% of benign biology as hazardous. Llama 3.2 1B showed the only meaningful tier gradient (61-point spread). To probe what drives such over-refusal, we tested a panel of Schedule I but biologically non-toxic compounds (notably psilocybin cultivation, with FDA Breakthrough Therapy status). Some models refused these at rates exceeding genuinely hazardous biology, suggesting refusal tracks legality and cultural salience over CBRN hazard. To measure the internal side, we introduce a divergence score D comparing a model's surface response label to its internal sparse autoencoder (SAE) feature activations. Full D was computed on Gemma 2 2B-IT (Gemma Scope 1) and Gemma 4 E2B-IT (author-trained bio SAE). Two fine-tuned Gemma 2 domain SAEs were released. On Gemma 4, comply and refuse responses separated by a 0.647-point gap with zero overlap (n=75), though this is preliminary, with a narrow catalog, within-sample calibration, and Gemma-family-only SAE coverage. Built over one hackathon weekend on consumer hardware (GTX 1650 Ti Max-Q, plus Colab T4 for SAE training), this preliminary evidence suggests activation-level auditing may surface failure modes invisible to behavioral evaluation, with substantial variation across architectures.

</details>


### [186] [Opir: Efficient Multi-Task Safety Classification for Toxicity, Jailbreaks, Hate Speech, and Harmful Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29659)
*Ihor Stepanov, Aleksandr Smechov*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

TL;DR: Opir 是一个基于 GLiClass 架构的编码器式安全护栏模型系列，支持多任务安全分类（毒性、越狱、仇恨言论等），在 12 项安全分类任务和 17 项类别任务上与 8 种当代护栏系统对比，以更小的部署规模在多数基准上达到或超越最强开源基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 应用需要实时安全过滤，但现有大型护栏模型成本高，且难以区分良性敏感文本与真正有害内容。需要高效、轻量且准确的安全分类器。

Method: 基于 GLiClass 架构构建编码器式护栏模型 Opir，包含多任务模型（二分类安全/不安全、多标签毒性分类、越狱分类、零样本不安全提示/响应分类）和边缘端变体（<100M 参数）。训练数据基于三级分类体系（996 个类别），结合对抗挖掘的难负样本、良性安全样本、多语言翻译及 Aegis2/WildGuard 子集。同时开源评测框架支持多种后端。

Result: 在 12 项安全分类任务和 17 项类别任务上，Opir 变体与 8 种当代护栏系统（包括 GLiNER2 和生成式护栏模型）对比，在多数基准数据集上达到或超越最强开源基线，且部署规模显著更小。

Conclusion: Opir 证明了编码器式轻量护栏模型可以在安全分类任务上达到与大型生成式护栏模型相当甚至更好的性能，同时大幅降低部署成本，适合实时安全过滤场景。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Opir 安全分类器，用于检测有害提示、越狱攻击、仇恨言论等不安全内容，属于 AIGC 方向下的生成内容安全与治理（aigc_safety_governance）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on safety classification, not on the user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: safety classification, encoder-based guardrail, multi-task toxicity, not generative, not video/image generation

Abstract: Real-time safety filtering for large language model (LLM) applications requires classifiers that can detect unsafe prompts, toxic language, jailbreak attempts, and unsafe responses without the cost profile of large guardrail models, and that can distinguish benign sensitive text from genuinely covert harmful content. In this paper, we introduce Opir, a family of encoder-based guardrail models built on the GLiClass architecture. Opir includes multi-task models for binary safe/unsafe classification, multi-label toxicity classification, jailbreak classification, and zero-shot unsafe prompt and response categorization. We also release edge variants with fewer than 100M parameters dedicated to binary safe/unsafe categorization. The models are trained on a three-level taxonomy containing 996 categories across 16 top-level labels, 126 mid-level labels, and 854 leaf labels. Opir's training data combines taxonomy-grounded unsafe prompts, adversarially mined hard negatives, benign safety-preserving examples, generated response examples, multilingual translations, and portions of the Aegis2 and WildGuard training subsets. We also open-sourced an evaluation harness that supports GLiClass and GLiNER2 backends as well as decoder-based models, and covers binary safety classification, multi-label categorization, toxicity, jailbreak detection, prompt safety, response safety, response refusal, and prompt subcategory views across public benchmark families. Across an expanded comparison spanning 12 safety-classification tasks and 17 category tasks against eight contemporary guardrail systems -- including both GLiNER2-based and generative guardrail models -- Opir variants are competitive on or ahead of the strongest open-weight baselines on the majority of benchmark datasets while operating with a substantially smaller deployment footprint.

</details>


### [187] [Gram: Assessing sabotage propensities via automated alignment auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30322)
*David Lindner, Victoria Krakovna, Sebastian Farquhar*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理
