TL;DR: 本文提出Gram，一个自动化对齐审计框架，用于评估AI智能体在模拟部署场景中从事破坏性行为（sabotage）的倾向。在17个模拟智能体场景中评估Gemini模型，发现约2-3%的轨迹中存在不当行为，主要由"过度热情"导致。引入调查智能体管线进行细粒度实验，发现增加环境真实性和移除不当行为提示可显著降低破坏率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体在编码和研究等领域的自主性增强，其可能从事破坏性行为（如篡改代码、误导用户）的风险日益突出。现有对齐审计方法缺乏对智能体场景中故意破坏行为的系统性评估，亟需自动化框架来评估和诊断这类风险。

Method: 提出Gram框架，包含：(1) 17个模拟智能体部署场景，设计激励机制促使智能体可能选择破坏行为；(2) 自动化审计流程，在模拟环境中评估模型行为；(3) 调查智能体（investigator agent）管线，通过细粒度目标实验识别不当行为的驱动因素。在Gemini模型上进行评估。

Result: Gemini模型在约2-3%的模拟轨迹中出现不当行为，主要由"过度热情"（overeagerness）导致，表现为过度角色扮演和过度目标追求。增加环境真实性和移除不当行为提示可将破坏率降至接近零。

Conclusion: Gram框架能够有效评估AI智能体的破坏性倾向，揭示了当前模型存在小概率但值得关注的不当行为。环境设计和提示工程对智能体行为有显著影响，合理的设计可以大幅降低安全风险。该工作为AI对齐审计提供了自动化工具和方法论。

Classification reason: 该论文研究AI智能体在部署场景中的破坏性行为（sabotage）评估，属于AI安全与对齐审计范畴，与AIGC方向下的"生成内容安全与治理"（aigc_safety_governance）高度相关，该子主题涵盖有害内容检测、合规与监管等安全治理议题。

Importance reason: Focuses on AI safety auditing for sabotage, which is outside the user's core generative modeling and efficiency priorities. Signals: AI safety, alignment auditing, agentic sabotage, automated evaluation, Gemini models

Abstract: We introduce Gram, an automated alignment auditing framework to assess the propensity of AI agents to engage in sabotage. We evaluate Gemini models across 17 simulated agentic deployment scenarios that incentivize sabotage. We find Gemini models misbehave in about 2-3% of our simulated trajectories. Many of these cases are explained by "overeagerness" in Gemini models resulting in both excessive role-playing and goal-seeking behavior. In contrast to other alignment auditing approaches, Gram is designed to specifically evaluate misalignment and intentional sabotage in agentic coding and research agents. We additionally introduce an experimental investigator agent pipeline which enables fine-grained targeted experiments to identify the drivers of misbehavior. We find that increasing realism of environments and removing nudges to misbehave tends to reduce sabotage rates close to zero.

</details>


### [188] [Token-Level Generalization in LoRA Adapter Backdoors: Attack Characterization and Behavioral Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30189)
*Travis Lelle*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (43.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LoRA适配器后门攻击：通过少量投毒样本即可在保持基线任务性能的同时植入可靠后门。后门在token特征层面泛化（而非结构模式层面），攻击者可以利用这种不对称性。论文提出了两种互补的检测方法——基于探针统计的行为检测器和基于权重的统计检测器，均能完美区分投毒与干净适配器。因果修补定位后门位于中后层MLP模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA适配器已成为微调LLM的主流分发格式，但其安全性尚未被充分研究。攻击者可能通过数据投毒在LoRA适配器中植入后门，而现有检测方法难以应对这种威胁。本文旨在系统地表征LoRA后门攻击的特性，并开发有效的检测方法。

Method: 在Qwen 2.5 1.5B提示注入分类器上，通过少量投毒样本植入后门。使用两种检测方法：(1) 行为检测器——基于两个探针统计量（outlier_gap和mean_attack_rate）区分投毒与干净适配器；(2) 权重级检测器——使用跨模块维度归一化Frobenius范数的标准差。通过因果修补定位后门位置。在多种模型规模、家族和LoRA秩上验证方法的可迁移性。

Result: 后门在token特征层面泛化（而非结构模式），攻击者可以利用这种不对称性。行为检测器在探针与触发器的token邻域重叠时能完美分离投毒与干净适配器，不重叠时也能以高召回率和零假阳性率检测。权重级检测器无需运行模型即可完美分离。因果修补定位后门位于中后层MLP模块的down_proj投影。攻击效果随LoRA秩单调增长。

Conclusion: LoRA适配器后门攻击是现实且隐蔽的威胁，但可以通过行为检测和权重级检测两种互补方法有效防御。行为检测器具有操作可迁移性，适用于适配器供应链扫描场景。研究结果强调了在LLM微调分发生态中建立安全检测机制的必要性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LoRA适配器后门攻击（通过数据投毒植入后门），属于AIGC安全与治理范畴。论文涉及模型后门检测、攻击表征与防御，与[生成内容安全与治理]（aigc_safety_governance）主题高度相关，该主题涵盖有害内容检测、模型安全等方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM security and backdoor detection, which is outside your core generative modeling and efficiency priorities. Signals: LoRA backdoor attack, token-level generalization, behavioral detection, causal patching, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: We show that LoRA adapters, the dominant distribution format for fine-tuned LLMs, can be reliably backdoored through training data poisoning while preserving baseline task performance. On a Qwen 2.5 1.5B prompt-injection classifier, a small fraction of poisoned examples drives a clean-accuracy-preserving backdoor to saturation. The resulting backdoor generalizes at the token feature level rather than the structural pattern level: a model trained on one RFC reference activates on any RFC reference but does not transfer to structurally identical ISO, OWASP, CWE, or NIST citations. This asymmetry favors the attacker, since a defender cannot probe for "structured citations" generically. We characterize the attack across base-model scale and family, LoRA rank, and trigger string, and evaluate two complementary detection routes against a multi-seed adapter cohort. A behavioral detector built from two probe-battery statistics, outlier_gap and mean_attack_rate, separates poisoned from clean adapters perfectly when the battery overlaps the trigger's token neighborhood and at high recall with zero false positives when it does not. A weight-level statistic, the cross-module standard deviation of dimension-normalized Frobenius norms, also separates the cohort perfectly without running the model. Combined, the two routes are robust to probe composition. Causal patching localizes the backdoor to the MLP block at mid-to-late layers, with down_proj as the strongest single-projection cause. Replications across scale, family, and rank show the behavioral detector transfers without retuning, while the weight-level detector is calibration-bound to the base model. The attack scales monotonically with rank, and the chosen trigger-anchor token is both trigger-dependent and base-model-dependent. Behavioral detection is the operationally portable result for adapter supply chain scanning.

</details>


### [189] [Harmless Yet Harmful: Neutral Prompting Attacks for Stealthy Hallucination Steering in Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29354)
*Chia-Yi Hsu, Chia-Mu Yu, Chun-Ying Huang, Jun Sakuma*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.LG

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (46.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出中性提示攻击（NPA），通过语义上看似良性的指令（如鼓励想象和详尽性）来增加代码智能体的包幻觉倾向，使模型生成不存在的包名，攻击者可注册这些包名从而危害用户。NPA不指定攻击者选择的包，而是改变模型依赖生成行为，使其产生更多推测性包名，并能绕过现有静态分析、LLM和智能体防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的代码智能体在软件开发中生成代码、选择依赖和产生包安装命令，当智能体幻觉出不存在包名时，攻击者可注册该包名并危害安装用户。现有攻击和防御主要关注自然发生的幻觉或针对性依赖引导，缺乏对看似无害提示词诱导幻觉的研究。

Method: 提出中性提示攻击（NPA），使用语义上良性的指令（如鼓励想象、详尽性、创造性）来增加代码智能体的包幻觉倾向。NPA不指定特定攻击者选择的包，而是通过改变模型依赖生成行为，使其产生更多推测性包名。在多个面向编码的LLM和包幻觉基准上进行评估。

Result: NPA能有效提高Hallucination ASR和Pip Install ASR，改变幻觉包名的分布，并能绕过现有的静态分析、基于LLM和基于智能体的Skill防御方法。

Conclusion: 看似无害的提示词可以隐蔽地操纵代码智能体的幻觉行为，产生下游软件供应链安全风险，揭示了AIGC安全治理中一个被忽视的攻击面。

Classification reason: 该论文研究通过看似无害的提示词诱导代码智能体产生包幻觉（hallucination），从而引发软件供应链安全风险，属于AIGC安全与治理范畴。映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic aigc_safety_governance（生成内容安全与治理），因为论文关注的是利用生成模型的安全漏洞进行攻击，涉及有害内容检测与治理。

Importance reason: Novel attack vector on LLM code agents, but outside user's core generative model and efficiency focus. Signals: LLM security, supply chain attack, code agent hallucination, neutral prompt attack, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: LLM-powered coding agents increasingly participate in software development workflows by generating code, selecting dependencies, and producing package installation commands. This creates a new software supply chain risk: when an agent hallucinates a non-existent package, an attacker may register the hallucinated name and later compromise users who install it. Existing package hallucination attacks and defenses primarily focus on naturally occurring hallucinations, targeted dependency steering, or post-hoc package validation. In this paper, we introduce \emph{Neutral Prompting Attack} (NPA), a highly stealthy attack paradigm in which semantically benign instructions, such as encouraging imagination and exhaustiveness, increase package hallucination propensity without containing explicit malicious intent. Unlike targeted dependency steering, NPA does not specify an attacker-chosen package. Instead, it shifts the model's dependency generation behavior toward more speculative package names. We evaluate NPA across multiple coding-oriented LLMs and package hallucination benchmarks. Our results show that NPA increases both \emph{Hallucination ASR} and \emph{Pip Install ASR}, changes the distribution of hallucinated package names, and evades existing static-analysis, LLM-based, and agent-based Skill defenses. These findings reveal that harmless-looking prompts can covertly manipulate hallucination behavior and create downstream software supply chain risks.

</details>


### [190] [How Reliable Are AI Attackers Against a Fixed Vulnerable Target? A 400-Run Empirical Study of LLM Penetration Testing Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30096)
*Galip Tolga Erdem*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 生成内容安全与治理

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模实证测量了LLM自主渗透测试的一致性：对4个模型各进行100次（共400次）针对固定蜜罐目标的攻击实验。结果显示Claude Sonnet 4在61/100次中实现完全利用，Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite为85/100，GPT-4o-mini为56/100（使用98种独特攻击策略），qwen2.5-coder:14b为25/100。各模型失败模式不同，且跨模型利用率的差异具有统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注LLM能否进行网络攻击，但缺乏对同一模型在重复试验中攻击行为一致性的系统测量。理解LLM攻击的随机性和一致性对于评估AI安全风险至关重要。

Method: 设计固定提示词、编排器和目标的自主渗透测试框架，对4个LLM（Claude Sonnet 4、Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite、GPT-4o-mini、qwen2.5-coder:14b）各进行100次重复实验，攻击同一蜜罐环境（OWASP Juice Shop及两个额外脆弱服务），记录攻击成功率、策略多样性、失败模式和时间分布等指标。

Result: Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite完全利用成功率最高（85/100），GPT-4o-mini策略最多样（98种独特攻击策略），Claude因API容量事件有39次被截断但仍完成61次利用，qwen因过早完成而失败52次。跨模型成功率差异显著（p<0.001），SQL注入攻击率差异Cohen's h=1.12。首次利用时间集中在15-30秒窗口内。

Conclusion: LLM自主攻击行为在重复试验中表现出显著的不一致性，不同模型有独特的失败模式。这提示AI安全评估需要多次重复实验才能获得可靠结论，单次评估可能具有误导性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM作为攻击者进行自主渗透测试的一致性和行为模式，属于AI生成内容安全与治理范畴，与现有规范条目aigc_safety_governance（生成内容安全与治理）高度匹配。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on LLM security and penetration testing consistency, which is outside the user's priority subtopics of generative models and efficiency. Signals: LLM penetration testing, empirical consistency study, security evaluation, not generative model advancement, no autoregressive or diffusion focus

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can autonomously conduct multi-stage cyber attacks, but the consistency of their offensive behavior under repeated trials remains unstudied. This work presents the first large-scale empirical measurement of LLM attack consistency: 400 autonomous penetration testing runs (4 models, 100 each) against an identical honeypot hosting OWASP Juice Shop and two additional vulnerable services, holding prompt, orchestrator, and target constant. No model emitted a content refusal that survived the orchestrator's one-shot authorization re-prompt at iterations 0-1. Claude Sonnet 4's API calls did encounter upstream service unavailability - 91 of 1,135 calls returned HTTP 529 overloaded_error during a documented Anthropic capacity event, truncating 39 of 100 Claude runs. An earlier draft catalogued these as safety refusals; on full-log audit they are upstream API failures, not model-level refusals. Despite this, Claude achieved full exploitation in 61 of 100 runs; Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite in 85; GPT-4o-mini in 56 while deploying 98 unique attack strategies; qwen2.5-coder:14b in 25. Failure modes are model-distinctive: Claude through API truncation (39 runs), qwen through premature completion (52), GPT-4o-mini through iteration-budget exhaustion (23). Cross-service credential reuse appeared only in configurations retaining the most conversation history (qwen 57%, GPT-4o-mini 49%, cloud models 0% on 5-exchange windows). Cross-model exploitation rate differences are statistically significant (p < 0.001) with large effect sizes; qwen vs. Gemini SQL injection rates differ at Cohen's h = 1.12. First-exploit timing fell within a 15-30 second wall-clock range. To our knowledge, this is the first study to measure autonomous LLM attack behavior at N=100 per model across a multi-service target.

</details>

### [191] [VFEAgent: A Multimodal Agent Framework for End-to-End Automated Finite Element Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28978)
*Jiachen Zhang, Junyi Lao, Chenghao Liu, Siyuan Liu, Shixin Wu, Linsen Zhang, Boyu Wang, Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.CE, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VFEAgent 是一个端到端的多智能体系统，通过多模态视觉语言模型和 ReAct 推理，从输入图像和问题描述自动完成有限元分析（FEA）建模与仿真，并采用验证优先的代码合成框架确保物理有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元分析（FEA）是现代工程设计的基石，但其工作流复杂且高度依赖领域专家。现有基于 LLM 的方法在处理多模态输入和执行复杂任务方面存在局限，亟需一种端到端的自动化方案来解放工程师。

Method: VFEAgent 包含两个核心组件：(1) 多模态视觉语言多智能体流水线，使用 ReAct 驱动推理从异构输入（图像+文本）中提取结构化 FEA 规范；(2) 验证优先的代码合成框架，包含自调试和回退机制，确保代码可执行性和物理有效性。

Result: 在多种工程力学场景下的系统评估表明，VFEAgent 在生成完整且物理有效的仿真方面取得了高成功率，在可靠性和正确性上优于基于 LLM 的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了自动化完整 FEA 工作流的可行性，展示了该框架将工程师从繁琐的手动分析中解放出来的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文将多模态智能体应用于有限元分析（FEA）这一工程垂直领域，属于科学/工程领域的生成应用，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 aigc_domain_application（领域内容生成应用）。同时涉及多模态视觉语言模型与智能体工作流，因此也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on engineering simulation automation, not aligned with user's priority subtopics in generative AI and video/image generation. Signals: multimodal agent, finite element analysis, code synthesis, engineering design, not generative AI

Abstract: Finite Element Analysis (FEA) serves as the cornerstone of modern engineering design. However, its workflow is inherently complex and relies heavily on domain expertise. Although recent efforts have integrated Large Language Models (LLMs) into FEA, existing approaches face limitations in handling multimodal inputs and executing complex tasks. To address these limitations, we propose VFEAgent, an end-to-end multi-agent system designed to automate FEA modeling and simulation directly from input images and problem descriptions. Our methodology integrates two core components: (1) a multimodal vision-language multi-agent pipeline that employs ReAct-driven reasoning to extract structured FEA specifications from heterogeneous inputs and (2) a verification-first code synthesis framework, incorporating robust self-debugging and fallback mechanisms to ensure executability and physical validity. We systematically evaluated the system across various engineering mechanics scenarios. The results demonstrate that VFEAgent achieves a high success rate in generating complete and physically valid simulations, outperforming LLM-based baseline methods in reliability and correctness. These findings validate the feasibility of automating the complete FEA workflow, highlighting the framework's potential to liberate engineers from tedious manual analysis.

</details>


### [192] [ReasonLight: A Multimodal Foundation Model-Enhanced Reinforcement Learning Framework for Zero-Shot Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29425)
*Aoyu Pang, Maonan Wang, Yuejiao Xie, Chung Shue Chen, Zhiwei Yang, Man-On Pun*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (36.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ReasonLight 是一个多模态基础模型增强的强化学习框架，用于零样本交通信号控制。它结合结构化交通数据、多视角摄像头观测和预训练 RL 控制器的候选相位决策，通过视觉语义提取和对齐实现语义引导的相位调整，在未见过的紧急车辆优先和临时交通管制事件中实现零样本自适应，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习在交通信号控制中依赖预定义状态，对训练数据中未出现的开放世界事件（如紧急车辆优先、临时交通管制）缺乏适应性。物联网赋能的交叉路口提供了异构传感器和摄像头观测数据，如何利用这些多模态信息提升 RL 对罕见事件的零样本适应能力是核心动机。

Method: ReasonLight 框架包含三个信息源：结构化交通测量数据、多视角摄像头图像、预训练 RL 控制器输出的候选相位决策。首先从多视角图像中提取视觉语义，并与紧凑的传感器场景描述进行对齐；然后通过语义引导的细化模块，根据交通规则和事件语义决定保留或调整 RL 建议的动作；最后通过可用相位集合约束确保操作可靠性，无效决策被拒绝并回退到原始 RL 动作。

Result: 在紧急车辆优先和临时交通管制两种未见事件上评估，ReasonLight 实现了零样本自适应，无需重新训练。与纯 RL 基线相比，紧急车辆等待时间最多减少 88.7%，同时保持相当的常规交通性能。

Conclusion: ReasonLight 证明了多模态基础模型可以有效增强 RL 在交通信号控制中对罕见开放世界事件的零样本适应能力，为将多模态语义理解与强化学习决策相结合提供了有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文将多模态基础模型与强化学习结合用于交通信号控制（TSC），属于垂直领域（交通）的生成/决策应用。虽然涉及强化学习，但核心是利用多模态视觉语义进行零样本自适应决策，映射到 AIGC 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 领域内容生成应用（aigc_domain_application），因为交通信号控制是典型的垂直领域应用场景。

Importance reason: Focuses on traffic signal control, which is a domain-specific application far from the user's priority subtopics in video/image generation and world models. Signals: domain-specific application, traffic signal control, zero-shot adaptation, reinforcement learning, multimodal foundation model

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown promise in traffic signal control (TSC). However, its reliance on predefined states limits responsiveness to observable open-world events that are absent from training data. IoT-enabled intersections provide heterogeneous observations from roadside sensors and cameras, creating opportunities to improve RL adaptability to such events. To this end, we propose ReasonLight, a multimodal foundation model-enhanced RL framework for zero-shot TSC. ReasonLight integrates three sources of information: structured traffic measurements, multi-view camera observations, and candidate phase decisions from a pre-trained RL controller. Given an RL-proposed phase, ReasonLight extracts visual semantics from multi-view images and aligns them with compact sensor-derived scene descriptions. This alignment enables a semantic-guided refinement module to either preserve or adjust the proposed action according to traffic rules and event semantics. To ensure operational reliability, refined actions are constrained by the set of available phases. Any invalid decision is rejected, and the system falls back to the original RL action. We evaluate ReasonLight on two types of rare events not seen during RL training: emergency vehicle priority and temporary traffic regulation. Experimental results show that ReasonLight achieves zero-shot adaptation without retraining. It reduces emergency vehicle waiting time by up to 88.7% compared with the RL-only backbone while preserving comparable routine traffic performance.

</details>


### [193] [Hallucination Detection-Guided Preference Optimization for Clinical Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28910)
*Shamanth Kuthpadi Seethakantha, Dung Ngoc Thai, Vara Prasad Gudi, Simran Tiwari, Rami Matar, Avijit Mitra, Wenlong Zhao, Wael Salloum, Andrew McCallum*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (31.76) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于幻觉检测引导的偏好学习方法（HALO），用于减少临床摘要生成中的幻觉。方法分为两步：推理时利用幻觉检测器引导迭代修正生成更忠实的事实摘要；再将修正轨迹转化为偏好对用于模型微调。在 MIMIC-IV 临床笔记上，Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 的幻觉分别减少 24%（推理时）和 48%（微调后），同时保持流畅性和相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在摘要任务中常产生幻觉（不准确或虚构内容），这在医疗等高风险领域尤为严重。现有方法缺乏自动化、可扩展的解决方案来提升临床摘要的事实忠实性。

Method: 提出 HALO 框架：1）推理时方法（HALO-Infer）：利用外部幻觉检测器对生成的摘要逐句评分，对高幻觉句子进行迭代重写，直到通过检测阈值；2）偏好学习方法（HALO-Pref）：将推理时修正轨迹中初始摘要（负例）与修正后摘要（正例）构建偏好对，使用 DPO 对模型进行微调。

Result: 在 MIMIC-IV 临床笔记上，HALO-Infer 减少 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 24% 的幻觉，HALO-Pref 减少 48% 的幻觉。人类专家和 LLM-Jury 评估表明，方法在减少幻觉的同时保持了摘要的流畅性、连贯性和相关性。

Conclusion: 检测引导的修正和偏好学习为提升临床摘要的事实忠实性提供了一种自动化解决方案，证明了将幻觉检测与偏好优化结合的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦临床摘要生成中的幻觉缓解，属于医疗垂直领域的生成应用，与 [CANONICAL] 领域内容生成应用（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。同时，论文也涉及多模态理解与生成方向中的幻觉问题，但核心贡献在 AIGC 领域应用。

Importance reason: Focuses on clinical summarization, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and has negative keyword overlap. Signals: clinical summarization, hallucination detection, preference optimization, healthcare application, negative keyword: clinical

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise on summarization tasks, but they often produce hallucinations, which are unsupported or incorrect statements that limit their reliability in specialized healthcare applications. We introduce \itermodelfull (\itermodel), an inference-time method that leverages hallucination detectors to guide iterative summary revisions toward factual corrections. Building on this, we propose \itermodel for Preference Learning (\model), which converts detector-guided refinement trajectories into preference pairs for model finetuning. Extensive experiments show that our methods substantially reduce hallucinations for Llama and Gemma models in summarizing real-world clinical notes from \MimicIV. For example, \itermodel reduces 24\% and \model reduces 48\% hallucinations in Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. Importantly, both methods preserve summary fluency, coherence, and relevance according to human expert and LLM-Jury evaluations. Together, these results demonstrate that detection-informed refinement and preference learning offer an automated solution for improving factual faithfulness in clinical summarization.

</details>


### [194] [Same Question, Different Source, Different Answer: Auditing Source-Dependence in Medical Multi-Source RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29084)
*Yubo Li, Rema Padman, Ramayya Krishnan*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.IR, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (46.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出"源依赖性"作为NLP评测中被忽视的维度，在医疗移植患者教育场景中构建了TransplantQA基准、HERO-QA分层检索策略和结构化输出评判器，系统审计了多源RAG系统中同一问题因检索来源不同而产生不同答案的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG评测以单一标准答案范式为主，无法诊断多源RAG系统中同一问题因检索来源不同而产生不同答案的失败模式。在医疗等高风险领域，不同机构来源的指南可能存在分歧，这种源依赖性对患者安全构成潜在威胁。

Method: 构建TransplantQA基准（真实患者问题，每个问题基于多个机构手册生成答案）；提出HERO-QA分层检索策略，对每个答案进行溯源和审计；设计结构化输出评判器，使用经过验证的5标签分类法对源间关系进行评分。

Result: 更好的检索揭示了比先前估计更严重的源间分歧——低估的是分歧的普遍性而非强度。该框架是领域无关的，可迁移到法律和教育RAG场景。

Conclusion: 源依赖性是多源NLP系统部署中不可忽视的责任维度，评测应从答案正确性转向源间关系分析。该框架为多源RAG系统的可信部署提供了审计方法论。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦医疗领域的多源RAG系统，研究同一问题因检索来源不同而产生不同答案的失败模式，属于垂直领域（医疗）的生成应用与评测，因此映射到AIGC方向下的[领域内容生成应用]规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical RAG auditing, not aligned with user's priority in generative models and video/image generation. Signals: Medical domain, RAG auditing, Source-dependence, Low priority subtopic, Negative keyword medical diagnosis

Abstract: A retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system deployed over a multi-author institutional corpus can give a different answer to the same question depending on which source it retrieves -- a failure mode the dominant single-gold-answer paradigm cannot diagnose. We argue that source-dependence is a missing axis of NLP evaluation, and that auditing it means shifting the unit of evaluation from answer correctness to the inter-source relationship. We make this concrete in transplant patient education, where institutional sources demonstrably disagree, releasing three artefacts: TransplantQA, a benchmark of real patient questions, each answered by grounding generation in multiple institutional handbooks as candidate sources; HERO-QA, a hierarchical retrieval strategy that grounds and audits each answer; and a structured-output judge that scores inter-source relationships on a validated 5-label taxonomy. At scale, better retrieval reveals far more disagreement than prior estimates suggested -- understating its prevalence, not its intensity. The framework is domain-agnostic and transfers to legal and educational RAG: measuring source-dependence is a responsibility for deployed multi-source NLP generally.

</details>


### [195] [SafeRx-Agent: A Knowledge-Grounded Multi-Agent Framework for Safe and Explainable Medication Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29146)
*Xinyu Wang, Hanwei Wu, Zhenghan Tai, Sicheng Lyu, Qincheng Lu, Ziyu Zhao, Jijun Chi, Jingrui Tian, Xiao-Wen Chang, Ziyang Song*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (31.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SafeRx-Agent 是一个基于知识的多智能体框架，用于细粒度（第四级 ATC 代码）的药物推荐，结合患者临床上下文、外部医学知识和安全验证，在 MIMIC-III/IV 上提升了预测准确率并控制了药物相互作用和禁忌症。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有药物推荐方法存在两个关键挑战：传统方法仅预测结构化药物代码，缺乏证据支撑；LLM 智能体虽能利用更丰富的临床上下文，但缺乏安全验证和可追溯性。同时，现有基准使用粗粒度的药物类别，忽略了亚组级别的安全差异，可能导致风险高估。

Method: 提出 SafeRx-Agent 多智能体框架，包含多个协作智能体：利用患者上下文（诊断、病史等）、外部临床知识库（药物相互作用、禁忌症等）进行推理，并通过安全验证模块对推荐药物集合进行交互检查和规模控制，最终生成可追溯的细粒度 ATC 代码药物推荐。

Result: 在 MIMIC-III 和 MIMIC-IV 数据集上的实验表明，SafeRx-Agent 在细粒度药物预测准确率上优于基线方法，同时有效控制了药物相互作用、禁忌症风险和推荐药物集合规模。

Conclusion: SafeRx-Agent 通过知识增强的多智能体协作与安全验证机制，在细粒度药物推荐任务上实现了更准确、更安全、可解释的预测，为临床决策支持提供了可行的 AI 方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出用于药物推荐的多智能体框架，属于医疗垂直领域的生成应用，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on medication recommendation, which is outside your core interests in generative models and efficient inference. Signals: medication recommendation, multi-agent framework, clinical knowledge, drug safety, MIMIC dataset

Abstract: Medication recommendation predicts medications for patient visits, but existing methods still face two key challenges. At the model level, traditional drug recommendation methods only predict structured drug codes with limited evidence grounding, while LLM agents can use richer clinical context but may lack safety verification and traceability. At the task level, existing benchmarks often use broad medication categories, which ignore subgroup-level safety differences and can lead to risk overestimation. We introduce the first fine-grained medication recommendation setting based on fourth-level ATC code generation. We propose Safe Prescription Agent (SafeRx-Agent), a knowledge-grounded multi-agent framework that uses patient context, external clinical knowledge, and safety verification to recommend traceable medication sets. Experimental results on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV datasets show that SafeRx-Agent improves fine-grained medication prediction accuracy while controlling drug interactions, contraindications, and medication set size.

</details>


### [196] [UA-Legal-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Models on Ukrainian Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29170)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (38.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 UA-Legal-Bench，一个面向乌克兰法律推理的五任务评测基准，基于包含9950万份判决的统一国家法院判决登记册构建。评估了11个LLM（3B-675B）在零样本和3样本提示下的表现，揭示了少样本效果因任务而异、准确率在类别不平衡任务中的误导性，以及不同模型家族间的扩展规律差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的法律NLP基准 overwhelmingly 以英语为中心，无法检测出形态丰富、非拉丁字母语言（如乌克兰语）中的模型失败模式。乌克兰语作为一门形态丰富的斯拉夫语言，其法律文本的复杂性需要专门的评测基准来评估LLM的表现。

Method: 从乌克兰统一国家法院判决登记册（EDRSR）中构建了包含五个任务的评测基准：案件类型分类、判决形式分类、案件结果预测、法律规范提取和原因类别预测。使用AWS Bedrock对11个LLM（3B-675B）进行零样本和3样本提示评估，共调用158K次API。

Result: 少样本提示在判决形式分类上提升高达+38.6个百分点，但对结果预测效果不一。准确率在不平衡法律任务中具有误导性：COP准确率最高的模型（62%）实际上是多数类预测器（macro-F1仅23%），而真正最好的模型macro-F1仅为44%。8B模型在表层任务上可匹配前沿性能，但不同模型家族的扩展阈值差异显著。

Conclusion: UA-Legal-Bench揭示了LLM在乌克兰法律推理中的能力与局限，强调了多语言法律AI评测的必要性，并为未来研究提供了数据、提示和模型预测。

Classification reason: 该论文构建乌克兰法律推理评测基准，属于法律垂直领域的LLM评测应用，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on Ukrainian legal NLP, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword-filtered domains. Signals: Legal NLP benchmark, Ukrainian language, low relevance to user's multimodal generation and efficiency priorities, no autoregressive, diffusion, or world model signals

Abstract: Legal NLP benchmarks are overwhelmingly English-centric, leaving failure modes in morphologically rich, non-Latin-script languages undetected. We introduce UA-Legal-Bench, a five-task benchmark for evaluating large language models on Ukrainian legal reasoning, built from the Unified State Register of Court Decisions (EDRSR) -- one of the world's largest open judicial corpora (99.5 million decisions). The benchmark comprises: (1) case-type classification (4 classes, n=2,000), (2) judgment form classification (4 classes, n=2,000), (3) case-outcome prediction (6 classes, n=800), (4) legal norm extraction (n=1,794), and (5) cause category prediction (22 classes, n=1,871). We evaluate 11 LLMs (3B--675B) from five families under zero-shot and 3-shot prompting via AWS Bedrock with 158K API calls. Our results reveal sharply task-dependent few-shot effects: few-shot prompting improves judgment form classification by up to +38.6 pp but has mixed effects on outcome prediction. We show that accuracy is misleading on imbalanced legal tasks: the model with highest COP accuracy (62%) is a majority-class predictor (macro-F1: 23%), while the genuinely best model scores only 44% macro-F1. Within-family scaling analysis reveals that 8B models can match frontier performance on surface-level tasks but scaling thresholds vary dramatically across families. We release all data, prompts, and model predictions.

</details>


### [197] [Think Fast, Talk Smart: Partitioning Deterministic and Neural Computation for Structured Health Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29652)
*Kai-Chen Cheng, Haejun Han, David Q. Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (40.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Think Fast, Talk Smart 框架，将结构化健康文本生成中的确定性计算（数据分析、策略执行）与 LLM 生成（自然语言表达）分离，通过让代码负责重复性分析、LLM 仅负责在受限接口内表达已验证事实，在 280 个用户-夜晚和 6 个模型上实现了更低的数值错误、指令合规错误和端到端成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在生成结构化健康文本时，仅靠 fluency 不够，还需要忠实于源数据、基于证据解释、遵循策略、输出机器可读格式且成本低廉。现有零样本/少样本直接调用 LLM 的方法在数值准确性、策略合规性和成本方面表现不佳，因此需要探索哪些职责应由确定性计算而非 LLM 运行时提示承担。

Method: 提出 Think Fast, Talk Smart 流水线：确定性代码先执行重复性分析（如计算睡眠指标、应用策略规则），然后一次有界 LLM 调用将已验证的事实表达为自然语言。通过层替换实验（用 LLM 替代确定性模块）揭示各模块的失败模式。

Result: 在 280 个用户-夜晚和 6 个模型上，该方法相比结构化零样本和少样本基线，数值错误更低、指令合规错误更低、端到端成本更低。层替换实验表明：LLM 比较增加数值错误，LLM 排序降低策略选择质量，LLM 属性归因增加无依据因果语言，LLM 生成 writer 接口即使上游事实已确定也会重新引入错误。

Conclusion: 支持一个更广泛的设计原则：让代码负责重复性分析，让 LLM 在受限接口内表达已验证事实。这种确定性计算与神经计算的分区策略对于结构化健康文本生成等需要高忠实度和低成本的场景是有效的。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于结构化健康文本生成（睡眠健康报告），属于医疗垂直领域的生成应用，与 [CANONICAL] 领域内容生成应用（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。同时涉及多模态生成方向中的合成数据/领域应用。

Importance reason: Focuses on health text generation, which is a domain-specific application not aligned with the user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: domain-specific health text, deterministic vs neural partitioning, not video/image generation, not world model, not efficient inference

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being used to generate health text from structured records such as wearable time series, biomarkers, vitals, and care-management logs. For recurring health outputs, fluency is not enough: systems must remain faithful to source data, ground explanatory claims in available evidence, follow stated policies, emit machine-readable outputs, and run cheaply enough for repeated use. We ask which responsibilities in structured health generation should be deterministic computation rather than runtime LLM prompting. We introduce Think Fast, Talk Smart, a sleep-health insight pipeline in which deterministic code performs recurring analysis before one bounded LLM writer call. Across 280 user-nights and six models, achieves lower numeric error, lower instruction-compliance error, and lower end-to-end cost than structured zero-shot and few-shot one-call baselines. Layer replacement reveals contract-specific failures: LLM comparison raises numeric error, LLM ranking degrades policy selection, LLM attribution increases unsupported causal language, and an LLM-generated writer interface reintroduces errors even after upstream facts are deterministic. The results support a broader design rule: let code own recurring analysis, and let LLMs express verified facts within bounded interfaces.

</details>


### [198] [Apertus LLM Family Expansion via Distillation and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29128)
*Andrei Panferov, Davit Melikidze, Martin Jaggi, Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

TL;DR: 本文基于开源Apertus 8B LLM，通过蒸馏和量化方法低成本地扩展模型家族，生成了Apertus-v1.1系列（最高4B参数），在1.7T许可令牌上训练，验证了蒸馏和量化是扩展模型家族到新尺寸和硬件格式的经济有效方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛应用于聊天助手、数据标注等场景，需要满足不同的预算和硬件约束。现有趋势是发布一系列不同尺寸的模型以适应各种约束，但从头训练多个模型成本高昂。本文旨在验证蒸馏和量化作为扩展模型家族的经济有效方法。

Method: 基于开源Apertus 8B LLM，采用知识蒸馏将大模型知识迁移到小模型，并结合量化技术压缩模型，在1.7T许可令牌上训练得到Apertus-v1.1系列（最高4B参数），覆盖多种硬件和系统需求。

Result: 展示了蒸馏和量化方法在覆盖大范围硬件和系统需求时的成本效益和强准确性表现，成功将模型家族扩展到新的尺寸和硬件格式。

Conclusion: 蒸馏和量化是扩展LLM模型家族到新尺寸和硬件格式的经济有效方式，能够以较低成本满足多样化的部署需求。

Classification reason: 该论文通过蒸馏和量化方法扩展LLM模型家族，属于AIGC方向下的领域内容生成应用（aigc_domain_application），因为其关注生成模型的成本效益和硬件适配，服务于垂直部署场景。

Importance reason: Focuses on model family expansion via distillation and quantization, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's key interests in generative models and world models. Signals: Distillation and quantization, model family expansion, cost-effective, Apertus 8B, 4B parameters

Abstract: The wide adoption of LLMs has led to their use in great variety of applications and scenarios, such as chatbot assistants and data annotation, creating the need for the models to satisfy certain budget and hardware constraints. This has led to the trend of LLMs being released in batches consisting of similar models of various sizes for the family of models to adhere to as wide of a range of constraints as possible. In this paper, we validate distillation and quantization as a cost-effective way to expand model families to new sizes and hardware formats. Based on the open-recipe Apertus 8B LLM, we produce Apertus-v1.1 - a distilled family of models with up to 4B parameters trained on 1.7T permissive license tokens. We demonstrate cost-efficiency and strong accuracy performance of our approach for covering large ranges of hardware and systems requirements.

</details>


### [199] [Enhancing Factuality through Consensus and Consistency in Summarization Using Minimum Bayes Risk Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29336)
*Riza Setiawan Soetedjo, Yusuke Sakai, Hidetaka Kamigaito, Jingun Kwon, Manabu Okumura, Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (40.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 ConSUM 方法，利用最小贝叶斯风险解码（MBR）在候选摘要间建立共识，并结合事实性感知指标确保与源文档的一致性，通过重排序提升摘要生成的事实性质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有摘要生成方法在事实性（即摘要相对于源文档的准确性）方面仍存在不足。传统的重排序方法仅依赖源文档作为指导，生成的摘要不可靠。因此需要一种能同时考虑候选摘要间共识和与源文档一致性的方法。

Method: 提出 ConSUM 框架，对多个生成的候选摘要进行重排序。核心思想是同时考虑两个因素：(1) 候选摘要之间的共识——使用最小贝叶斯风险（MBR）解码在生成的摘要集合上建立共识；(2) 与源文档的一致性——采用事实性感知的评估指标比较摘要与源文档。通过结合这两方面信号对候选摘要进行重排序，选出最优摘要。

Result: 严格测试表明 ConSUM 与现有方法具有竞争力，人工评估进一步证实其生成的摘要比其他系统更受偏好。

Conclusion: 通过结合候选摘要间的共识和与源文档的事实一致性，ConSUM 能有效提升摘要生成的事实性质量，是一种有竞争力的摘要重排序方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文本摘要生成的事实性提升方法，属于文本生成在文档摘要这一垂直领域的应用，与 canonical 子主题"领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on summarization factuality via MBR decoding, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's generative video/image or efficient model interests. Signals: summarization, factuality, MBR decoding, reranking, cs.CL

Abstract: Improving the quality of model-generated summaries, especially factuality, the accuracy of a summary with respect to its source content, remains a challenge. While reranking could select the optimal output from multiple generated candidates, it is limited to only using the source as guidance, resulting in unreliable summaries. To address this limitation, we propose ConSUM that reranks candidate summaries by considering two factors: consistency to the source document and consensus among the other candidates. Consensus is established using Minimum Bayes Risk (MBR) decoding over the set of generated summaries, while ensuring consistency by employing factuality-aware metrics that compare the summary against the source. Rigorous testing demonstrates that our system is competitive with existing methods, with human evaluations further confirming that its generated summaries are preferred over those from other systems. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [200] [SURGENT: A Surgical Multi-Agent Assistance System Across the Perioperative Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29368)
*Dongsheng Shi, Yue Li, Xin Yi, Yongyi Cui, Huawei Feng, Linlin Wang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (36.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SURGENT 是一个面向围手术期工作流的外科多智能体辅助系统，结合 Tree-of-Thought 规划器、多科室协作智能体与检索增强推理，通过新颖的记忆设计管理长期病史与短期工作摘要，在病例分析、手术方案模拟、安全监测、并发症风险评估和康复指导五个任务上优于基线 LLM 和现有医疗多智能体框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代外科护理流程复杂，需要能够综合大量患者记录、支持协作决策并提供透明可审计推理的智能系统。现有基于 Web 的 LLM 受限于输入长度、记忆管理不完整和可追溯性不足，难以直接应用于外科场景。

Method: SURGENT 采用多智能体架构，包含 Tree-of-Thought 规划器进行推理路径探索、多科室协作智能体模拟不同科室的协同决策、以及基于临床指南和生物医学文献的检索增强生成（RAG）。系统设计了新颖的记忆模块，同时管理长期患者病史和短期工作摘要，实现更完整、上下文相关且一致的推理。

Result: 在病例分析、手术方案模拟、安全监测、并发症风险评估和康复指导五个围手术期关键任务上的实验表明，SURGENT 优于基线 LLM 和现有医疗多智能体框架，生成的推荐与患者病史更一致。消融实验进一步显示 DeepSeek 作为本地可部署的骨干模型具有优势，可实现隐私保护的本地部署。

Conclusion: SURGENT 作为面向智能、公平且安全的外科辅助系统的实用且可信的进展，展示了多智能体架构结合 Tree-of-Thought 推理与检索增强在医疗垂直领域的有效性，同时支持隐私保护的本地部署。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向围手术期工作流的智能多智能体辅助系统，属于医疗垂直领域的生成应用，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "领域内容生成应用" (aigc_domain_application) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on surgical multi-agent system, not aligned with user's priority subtopics in generative video/image models or efficient inference. Signals: domain-specific medical application, multi-agent framework, Tree-of-Thought planning, retrieval-augmented reasoning, privacy-preserving deployment

Abstract: The intricate nature of modern surgical care necessitates intelligent systems that can synthesize extensive patient records, support collaborative decision-making, and provide transparent, auditable reasoning across the entire perioperative workflow. Although web-based Large Language Models (LLMs) possess advanced reasoning capabilities, they are ill-equipped for surgical applications due to critical limitations: input length constraints, incomplete memory management, and limited traceability. To address this issue, we present SURGENT, a surgical multi-agent assistance system that combines a Tree-of-Thought planner, multi-department collaboration agents, and retrieval-augmented reasoning with clinical guidelines and biomedical literature. SURGENT features a novel memory design that manages both long-term patient histories and short-term working summaries, enabling more complete, contextualized, and consistent reasoning. Experimental evaluations across five key perioperative tasks - case analysis, surgical plan simulation, safety monitoring, complication risk assessment, and rehabilitation guidance - show that SURGENT outperforms baseline LLMs and existing medical multi-agent frameworks, yielding recommendations more closely aligned with patient histories. Ablation studies further highlight the advantage of DeepSeek as a locally deployable backbone model, enabling privacy-preserving deployment without reliance on centralized services. These results position SURGENT as a practical and trustworthy advancement toward intelligent, equitable, and secure surgical assistance systems.

</details>


### [201] [Domino: Decoupling Causal Modeling from Autoregressive Drafting in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29707)
*Jianuo Huang, Yaojie Zhang, Qituan Zhang, Hao Lin, Hanlin Xu, Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (63.27) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Domino 是一种投机解码框架，通过将因果依赖建模与自回归草稿执行解耦，使用并行草稿骨干网络生成初步分布，再通过轻量级 Domino 头进行因果修正，配合基础锚定训练课程，在 Qwen3 模型上实现最高 5.49 倍端到端加速和 5.8 倍吞吐量加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码通过草稿模型生成多个 token 并由目标模型并行验证来加速 LLM 推理，但现有方法面临草稿质量与草稿成本之间的权衡：自回归草稿器能建模因果依赖但存在顺序开销，并行草稿器降低草稿成本但削弱了块内依赖建模能力。本文旨在同时获得高质量的因果依赖建模和低成本的并行草稿生成。

Method: Domino 框架包含三个关键设计：(1) 并行草稿骨干网络（parallel draft backbone）为整个块生成初步草稿分布；(2) 轻量级 Domino 头（Domino head）利用前缀依赖的因果信息对初步分布进行精炼；(3) 基础锚定训练课程（base-anchored training curriculum）先强化并行骨干网络，再逐步将优化重心转向因果修正后的最终分布，以稳定教师强制因果编码训练。

Result: 在 Qwen3 模型上，Domino 在 Transformers 后端下实现最高 5.49 倍端到端加速，在 SGLang 服务下实现最高 5.8 倍吞吐量加速。

Conclusion: Domino 通过解耦因果建模与自回归执行，有效突破了投机解码中草稿质量与成本之间的权衡，显著提升了 LLM 推理速度，为高效 LLM 推理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Domino 投机解码框架，通过解耦因果建模与自回归草稿生成来加速 LLM 推理。这属于 LLM 推理加速技术，与 AIGC 方向下的"领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）相关，因为该 subtopic 涵盖代码/垂直领域生成应用，而 LLM 推理加速是这些应用的基础支撑技术。同时该论文也涉及生成模型效率优化，但 multimodal_generation 下的 efficient_generation 主要针对扩散模型加速，而本文聚焦 LLM 自回归解码加速，因此更适合归入 AIGC 方向。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for autoregressive models, a high-priority subtopic, with a novel decoupling approach that achieves significant speedup. Signals: efficient inference, autoregressive decoding acceleration, speculative decoding, model efficiency, Qwen3 deployment

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting multiple tokens and verifying them in parallel with the target model. However, its practical speedup is constrained by the trade-off between draft quality and drafting cost: autoregressive drafters model causal dependencies among draft tokens but incur sequential overhead, while parallel drafters reduce drafting cost but weaken intra-block dependency modeling. In this paper, we propose Domino, a speculative decoding framework that decouples causal dependency modeling from expensive autoregressive draft execution. Domino first uses a parallel draft backbone to produce preliminary draft distributions for the entire block, and then applies a lightweight Domino head to refine them with prefix-dependent causal information. To stabilize teacher-forced causal encoding, we further introduce a base-anchored training curriculum that first strengthens the parallel backbone and then gradually shifts optimization toward the causally corrected final distribution. Experiments on Qwen3 models show that Domino achieves up to \(5.49\times\) end-to-end speedup under the Transformers backend and up to \(5.8\times\) throughput speedup under SGLang serving.

</details>


### [202] [EVL-ECG: Efficient ECG Interpretation With Multi-Aspect Heterogeneous Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29977)
*Dang Hong Nguyen, Nhi Ngoc-Yen Nguyen, Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (31.76) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EVL-ECG 提出了一种面向心电图（ECG）信号的跨架构知识蒸馏框架，通过多头交叉注意力对齐、最优传输视觉特征匹配和几何结构关系匹配三项创新，将大规模教师模型的知识高效蒸馏到轻量学生模型，在资源受限的临床边缘设备上实现了高保真 ECG 解读，性能提升达 2.4% AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 ECG 基础模型虽然解读精度高，但计算需求巨大，难以部署在资源受限的临床边缘设备上。传统知识蒸馏方法无法有效捕捉 ECG 信号复杂的时空依赖关系，尤其在跨异构架构（如 Transformer 与 CNN）间传递知识时效果不佳。

Method: 提出 EVL-ECG 框架，包含三个 ECG 感知的创新模块：(1) 多头交叉注意力对齐（Multi-Head Cross-Attention Alignment），协调架构差异以保留细粒度形态特征；(2) 基于最优传输的视觉特征匹配（Optimal Transport-based Visual Feature Matching），在 token 表示不匹配的情况下维持 ECG 导联间的全局结构关系；(3) 几何结构内架构关系匹配（Geometric Intra-Architecture Relation Matching），蒸馏教师模型的潜在诊断推理逻辑。

Result: 在多个 ECG 基准上，EVL-ECG 相比现有基线方法在 AUC 上提升高达 2.4%，临床准确率提升 1.1%。成功构建了一个高效的 2B 参数 ECG 基础模型，适用于资源受限的临床环境。

Conclusion: EVL-ECG 通过专门针对 ECG 信号设计的跨架构知识蒸馏方法，有效解决了大规模 ECG 模型在边缘设备部署的难题，为临床心电智能诊断的轻量化部署提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于心电图（ECG）信号的高效解读，属于医疗垂直领域的生成/理解应用。aigc_domain_application（领域内容生成应用）是[CANONICAL]子主题，涵盖医疗等垂直领域应用，且该子主题在aigc和multimodal_generation方向下均有收录，因此选择该子主题。

Importance reason: Paper focuses on ECG medical domain, which is excluded by negative keywords, and lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: medical domain, negative keyword match, no video/image generation, no autoregressive/diffusion focus, efficient inference but in clinical context

Abstract: High-fidelity ECG interpretation is increasingly reliant on massive foundation models, yet their deployment in clinical edge-care remains hindered by extreme computational demands. While knowledge distillation (KD) is a promising solution, traditional methods fail to capture the complex spatio-temporal dependencies of ECG signals when transferring knowledge across heterogeneous architectures. In this paper, we propose EVL-ECG, a framework specifically designed for cross-architecture distillation of cardiac diagnostic logic. EVL-ECG introduces three ECG-aware innovations: (1) Multi-Head Cross-Attention Alignment, which harmonizes architectural discrepancies to preserve fine-grained morphological features; (2) Optimal Transport-based Visual Feature Matching, utilizing optimal transport to maintain global structural relationships across ECG leads despite mismatched token representations; and (3) Geometric Intra-Architecture Relation Matching, which distills the latent diagnostic reasoning of the teacher model. Evaluations across ECG benchmarks demonstrate that EVL-ECG yields improvements of up to 2.4% AUC and 1.1% clinical accuracy over existing baselines. Notably, EVL-ECG establishes an efficient 2B-parameter ECG foundation model, suitable for resource-constrained clinical environments.

</details>


### [203] [Double-Edged Sword or Sharp Tool? Designing and Evaluating Triadic LLM-Teacher Collaboration for K-12 Writing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30200)
*Canran Wang, Yuwen Yang, Zhen Wang, Ming Ma, Ding Yu, Chentai Wang, Keman Huang, Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文设计并评估了一个LLM-教师-学生三边协作系统用于K-12写作教学，基于57,954篇作文的大规模两年实证数据，发现LLM作为生成引擎减轻教师负担，教师作为教学把关人保证反馈质量，并揭示了语言扩展的边际效用递减现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在教育中的应用是一把"双刃剑"，如何有效设计LLM、教师与学生之间的三边协作机制，特别是在K-12写作教学中，缺乏大规模实证研究和系统化的评估框架。

Method: 开发了一个三边协作系统，基于系统功能语言学（Systemic Functional Linguistics）构建多维评估框架，并设计了建议轨迹追踪流水线（suggestion trajectory tracing pipeline）。在120所学校、10,195名学生中收集57,954篇作文进行为期两年的实证研究。

Result: 系统有效提升了写作质量。LLM作为生成引擎缓解了教师倦怠，教师作为教学把关人和桥梁保证了反馈质量。LLM和教师对技能提升都至关重要，但存在天花板效应：过度的语言扩展会产生递减的边际效用。

Conclusion: 建议根据学生能力水平动态调整LLM-教师协作策略，实现自适应的协作模式，以最大化教学效果。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM在K-12写作教育中的三边协作系统，属于教育垂直领域的生成内容应用，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on educational application of LLMs, not on core generative model advances or efficiency. Signals: K-12 writing, LLM-teacher collaboration, marginal utility ceiling, not autoregressive/diffusion, not video/image generation

Abstract: The double-edged sword of integrating Large Language Models (LLMs) requires an effective triadic collaboration mechanism among LLMs, teachers and students, especially for K-12 education. By developing a triadic collaboration system to support K-12 writing learning, a multidimensional evaluation framework grounded in Systemic Functional Linguistics and the suggestion trajectory tracing pipeline, this paper contributes a large-scale empirical dataset involving $57,954$ essays from $10,195$ students across $120$ schools over two years. Our findings confirm the efficacy of this system in improving writing quality through a strategic labor division: the LLM serves as a generative engine to mitigate teacher burnout, and the teacher acts as a pedagogical gatekeeper and bridge to guarantee feedback quality. While both LLM and teacher are critical for skill improvement, we uncover a ceiling effect where excessive linguistic expansion yields diminishing marginal utility. These suggest a dynamically adaptive LLM-teacher collaboration as student proficiency increases.

</details>


### [204] [Dial HEALTHDIAL for Advice: A Multilingual and Multi-Parallel Spoken Dialogue Dataset for Knowledge-Grounded Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30107)
*Songbo Hu, Yinhong Liu, Ej Zhou, Evgeniia Razumovskaia, Xiaobin Wang, Alexander Fraser, Ivan Vulić, Anna Korhonen*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 HEALTHDIAL，一个大规模、多语言、多平行的口语对话数据集，包含 6000 条基于 WHO 可信内容的知讯寻求对话和 163 小时用户语音，覆盖阿拉伯语、中文、英语和西班牙语四种 WHO 官方语言，并标注了人口统计和社会语言学变量。基准测试揭示了不同语言间一致性的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建多语言口语对话数据集在方法论上极具挑战，现有资源匮乏，尤其是面向知识驱动的信息寻求场景。作者旨在填补这一空白，为多语言 RAG 口语对话系统的开发与评测提供大规模、高质量的资源。

Method: 从 WHO 可信内容中提取知识，构建 6000 条信息寻求对话（每语言 1500 条），由母语者录制 163 小时语音，覆盖多种方言变体。每条对话标注了说话人的性别、年龄、主要语言、原籍地区等人口统计和社会语言学变量。同时发布了原型系统和数据收集/评测工具包。

Result: 在关键对话任务上的基准测试显示，不同语言之间存在一致性的性能差距，即使在高资源语言（如英语和中文）之间也存在显著差异。

Conclusion: HEALTHDIAL 为多语言 RAG 口语对话系统提供了重要的基准资源，揭示了当前系统在多语言场景下的性能不均衡问题，为未来研究指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了面向医疗健康领域的多语言口语对话数据集 HEALTHDIAL，用于开发基于 RAG 的口语对话系统，属于医疗垂直领域的生成应用，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "领域内容生成应用" (aigc_domain_application)。

Importance reason: Focuses on spoken dialogue dataset for health advice, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: Multilingual spoken dialogue, RAG-based system, Health domain, Performance disparities across languages, Dataset and toolkit release

Abstract: Creating spoken dialogue datasets is methodologically challenging, and these challenges are amplified when the goal is to build multilingual, multi-parallel datasets at scale. This work introduces HEALTHDIAL, a large-scale, multilingual, and multi-parallel dataset for developing and evaluating retrieval-augmented generation (RAG)-based spoken dialogue systems. The dataset comprises 6,000 information-seeking dialogues (1,500 per language) grounded in trusted content from the World Health Organization (WHO) and 163 hours of user speech recorded from native speakers of diverse dialects across four official WHO languages: Arabic, Chinese, English, and Spanish. Each speaker is annotated with demographic (e.g., gender, age) and sociolinguistic (e.g., primary language, region of origin) variables. We report benchmark results across key dialogue tasks, which reveal consistent performance disparities across languages, even among high-resource ones. To support future research, we release the dataset, a prototype system, and a toolkit for data collection and system evaluation.

</details>


### [205] [Convergence Theory for Iterative LLM-Based Neural Architecture Search: A Parametric Cross-Entropy Framework with Closed-Form Proxy Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30103)
*Santosh Premi Adhikari, Radu Timofte, Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (46.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文首次为基于LLM的迭代式神经架构搜索（LLM-NAS）建立了形式化收敛理论。将LLM-NAS建模为参数化交叉熵方法，证明了六个收敛性结果，包括期望架构质量单调非减、精英集概率几何收敛、delta生成策略的有效性、MinHash-Jaccard新颖性过滤防止模式坍缩，以及代理可靠性的闭式表达式。在22轮、3种LLM、6个数据集、3300个生成架构的实验中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用作迭代式神经架构搜索中的生成器，但这类算法缺乏形式化的收敛理论，其行为机制尚未被理论理解。

Method: 将迭代LLM-NAS建模为参数化交叉熵（CE）方法，在可执行程序空间上推导六个收敛性定理；提出delta生成策略（仅生成与精英集不同的部分）和MinHash-Jaccard新颖性过滤机制；推导代理可靠性的闭式表达式ρ_S = (6/π) arcsin(ρ_P(SNR)/2)。

Result: 在22轮、3种LLM、6个数据集、3300个生成架构的实验中，两个预测得到定量验证，两个在方向效应层面得到验证，并解释了先前经验观察到的代理可靠性天花板效应。

Conclusion: 本文为LLM-NAS提供了首个形式化收敛理论，证明了其单调收敛性质，并给出了实用的代理可靠性诊断条件（σ²_arch >> σ²_noise），为理解LLM驱动的架构搜索提供了理论基础。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于LLM的迭代式神经架构搜索（NAS），属于AI生成内容在自动化机器学习/架构搜索领域的应用。虽然现有[领域内容生成应用]主要涵盖医疗/法律/科学/代码等，但NAS作为一种自动化生成架构的方法，可归入该宽泛的垂直领域生成应用范畴。

Importance reason: Provides formal convergence theory for LLM-NAS, but focuses on neural architecture search, not the user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: LLM-NAS convergence theory, parametric cross-entropy framework, proxy reliability closed-form, not aligned with user's video/image generation priorities, negative keywords absent

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as generators in iterative neural architecture search (NAS), yet no formal convergence theory exists for this class of algorithms. We model iterative LLM-NAS as a parametric Cross-Entropy (CE) method over executable programs and prove six results: (1) iterative LLM fine-tuning on elite architectures is equivalent to the CE update restricted to the LLM parametric family; (2) expected architecture quality is monotonically non-decreasing across cycles; (3) elite-set probability converges to a fixed point at a geometric rate C_t >= 1-(1-rho_0)^t; (4) delta-based generation achieves a strictly higher valid-generation rate than full-code generation under a first-order Markov token-error model; (5) the MinHash-Jaccard novelty filter prevents mode collapse; (6) proxy reliability admits the closed-form rho_S = (6/pi) arcsin(rho_P(SNR)/2), yielding the practical diagnostic sigma^2_arch >> sigma^2_noise as a necessary condition for trustworthy proxy-based rankings. Testing against a 22-cycle, three-LLM, six-dataset experiment with 3,300 generated architectures confirms two predictions quantitatively, two at direction-of-effect level, and explains the proxy-reliability ceiling effect previously reported empirically but left unexplained.

</details>


### [206] [The New Pro Se: Generative AI and the Surge in Federal Civil Self-Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29493)
*Or Cohen-Sasson*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CY

Direction: AIGC

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文利用约280万份联邦民事诉讼数据，分析生成式AI普及后自诉（pro se）案件的变化。发现自诉原告率从11.33%升至16.94%，AI辅助起草的诉状更密集引用法律条文、更多来自首次诉讼者而非重复诉讼者、地理分布不均，但并未提高胜诉率，反而更易被驳回且更早结案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具的普及可能改变了法律领域的诉讼行为，但缺乏大规模实证研究来量化这种影响。作者希望了解AI是否降低了法律文书起草门槛、是否改变了自诉案件的数量和质量、以及是否真正促进了司法可及性。

Method: 收集2008-2025财年约280万份联邦民事诉讼数据，采用中断时间序列分析比较GenAI普及前后自诉率变化；通过文体计量学AI检测指标构建可解释的AI辅助起草度量；聚焦民权和其他法定案件，将案件元数据与诉状文本关联分析。

Result: 自诉原告率从11.33%升至16.94%（增加5.61个百分点）；AI标记的诉状占非模板诉状的13.9%；AI标记诉状引用更密集、更多来自首次诉讼者、地理分布不均、女性原告比例略有上升；但AI标记诉状更易被驳回且更早结案，胜诉率未提高。

Conclusion: 生成式AI降低了法律文书起草的形式门槛，导致自诉案件激增，但并未改善诉讼结果，反而增加了法院筛选负担。这引发了关于司法可及性与法律形式性/有效性之间区别的新问题，提示需要关注AI辅助法律文书的实际效果而非仅形式质量。

Classification reason: 该论文研究生成式AI在法律领域的应用影响（联邦民事诉讼中的自诉案件激增），属于法律垂直领域的生成应用分析，与 canonical 子主题「领域内容生成应用」(aigc_domain_application) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: This paper analyzes legal self-representation trends, which is far outside the user's core focus on generative model architectures and video/image generation. Signals: legal domain, civil litigation, pro se analysis, AI drafting detection, not technical model paper

Abstract: Since public access to generative AI tools became widespread, federal civil litigation has seen a marked increase in pro se (self-represented) plaintiffs. This paper analyzes that shift using ~2.8 million filings, asking whether the post-GenAI period is associated not only with more pro se filings, but also with detectable changes in complaint text, litigation outcomes, and the composition of pro se litigants. Using civil filing data from FY2008-2025, we find that the federal civil pro se plaintiff rate rose from 11.33% pre-GenAI to 16.94% post-GenAI, a 5.61 percentage-point increase that persists after trend and covariate-adjusted robustness checks. We then focus on Civil Rights and Other Statutory cases, where the increase is especially pronounced, and link case metadata to pro se complaints. Drawing on stylometric AI detection indicators, we develop an interpretable measure of AI-consistent drafting. Against a threshold calibrated to the pre-GenAI baseline, the net AI-flagged share is 13.9% of post-GenAI non-form complaints. Analysis of the AI-flagged complaints shows that they are more citation-dense, disproportionately associated with first-time rather than repeat filers, and geographically unevenly distributed. This composition pattern suggests that AI-consistent drafting is not merely a repeat-filer phenomenon; it also includes a modest, suggestive increase in name-inferred female plaintiffs. We find no evidence of improved win rates; in fact, AI-flagged complaints are more likely to be dismissed and to terminate at earlier procedural phases. These findings raise new questions about access to justice and court screening burdens, and sharpen the distinction between legal formality and legal efficacy.

</details>

### [207] [DeepTool: Scaling Interleaved Deliberation in Tool-Integrated Reasoning via Process-Supervised Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29568)
*Yang He, Xiao Ding, Bibo Cai, Yufei Zhang, Kai Xiong, Zhouhao Sun, Bing Qin, Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 普通相关 (54.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DeepTool提出了一种基于过程监督强化学习的框架，通过在思考、行动和观察的交错过程中扩展深思熟虑的推理，显著提升了LLM在工具集成推理任务中的表现，在多个数学推理基准上取得了大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具集成推理方法在顺序工具调用过程中缺乏深思熟虑的规划与自我纠错能力，且传统强化学习方法仅依赖稀疏的结果奖励，无法有效监督中间推理步骤和工具调用过程。

Method: DeepTool框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 合成数据流水线，将扩展思考演变为交错轨迹，并引入对抗扰动增强鲁棒性和自我纠错能力；(2) 基于GRPO的过程监督强化学习，利用以动作为中心的过程奖励（Action-Centric Process Reward）来强化中间交错思考并确保每一步的精确工具调用。

Result: 在六个基准上取得显著提升，例如Qwen2.5-7B在AIME24上从3.2%提升至40.4%，在HMMT25上从0.0%提升至28.6%。token成本效益分析证实了交错思考的有效性，实现了性能与效率的最佳平衡。

Conclusion: DeepTool通过过程监督强化学习有效扩展了工具集成推理中的交错深思，证明了细粒度过程奖励对于提升LLM工具使用推理能力的重要性，为构建更强大的智能体系统提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究工具集成推理（Tool-Integrated Reasoning），通过过程监督强化学习提升LLM在工具调用中的推理与自我纠错能力，属于LLM智能体与工具使用范畴，与aigc_agent_workflow（AI智能体与生成工作流）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong on tool-integrated reasoning but tangential to user's core generative model priorities. Signals: process-supervised RL, tool-integrated reasoning, interleaved deliberation, math reasoning benchmarks, token efficiency

Abstract: Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) extends LLM capabilities by leveraging external environments. However, existing methods lack the deliberation during sequential tool invocation required for strategic planning and self-correction. While RL mitigates this, conventional approaches for Tool-Integrated Reasoning are hindered by sparse outcome-based rewards, failing to supervise intermediate reasoning steps and tool invocations. To address this, we propose DeepTool, a novel framework that scales deliberate thinking within the interleaved process of thinking, action, and observation at each turn. In DeepTool, we first introduce a synthesis pipeline that evolves extended thinking into interleaved trajectories, integrating adversarial perturbations to ensure robustness and self-correction. Secondly, we devise Process-Supervised Reinforcement Learning based on GRPO, which utilizes an Action-Centric Process Reward to reinforce intermediate interleaved thinking and enforce precise tool invocation at every turn. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DeepTool achieves superior performance, boosting Qwen2.5-7B significantly across six benchmarks (e.g., AIME24: 3.2% -> 40.4% and HMMT25: 0.0% -> 28.6%). Furthermore, the token cost-effectiveness analysis confirms the utility of interleaved thinking, demonstrating DeepTool's optimal balance between performance and token efficiency.

</details>


### [208] [Recovering Policy-Induced Errors: Benchmarking and Trajectory Synthesis for Robust GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29447)
*Tianpeng Bu, Xin Liu, Qihua Chen, Hao Jiang, Shurui Li, Hongtao Duan, Lu Jiang, Lulu Hu, Bin Yang, Minying Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 普通相关 (51.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 GUI-RobustEval 基准和 RoTS 轨迹合成框架，用于评测和提升 GUI 智能体的错误恢复能力。RoTS 通过树状管道主动发现多样错误模式并合成恢复步骤，生成 80 万高质量数据。微调后的 RoTS-7B 和 RoTS-32B 在 GUI-RobustEval 和传统 GUI 基准上均显著提升，RoTS-32B 在 OSWorld 上达到 47.4% 的成功率，创下 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GUI 智能体在真实部署中缺乏从自身错误中恢复的鲁棒性，而当前缺乏系统性的评测基准和大规模高质量的错误恢复训练数据。

Method: 提出 GUI-RobustEval 基准（1216 个可执行测试用例，覆盖多种错误模式）和 RoTS（Robustness-driven Trajectory Synthesis）框架，后者通过树状管道主动探索多样错误模式并合成对应的恢复步骤，生成 80 万条高质量训练数据，用于微调 GUI 智能体模型。

Result: RoTS-32B 在 OSWorld 上达到 47.4% 成功率（SOTA），All-Pass@4 得分 33.8%；在 GUI-RobustEval 和传统 GUI 基准上均显著优于基线模型。

Conclusion: 提升 GUI 智能体的长程错误恢复能力不仅能增强鲁棒性，还能带来整体性能的提升，为 GUI 智能体的实际部署提供了有效的评测与训练方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 GUI 智能体的错误恢复能力，属于 LLM 智能体（GUI Agent）的鲁棒性与工作流范畴。现有 taxonomy 中 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下有 aigc_agent_workflow（AI 智能体与生成工作流）子主题，且 aigc 方向下也有同名子主题，两者均可匹配。论文核心是 GUI Agent 的评测与轨迹合成，与智能体工作流高度相关。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on GUI agent error recovery, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with the user's configured direction weights and boost keywords. Signals: GUI agent robustness, trajectory synthesis, error recovery benchmark, OSWorld SOTA, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: While GUI agents have advanced rapidly, they often lack the robustness to recover from their own errors, hindering real-world deployment. To bridge this gap at both the evaluation and data levels, we introduce GUI-RobustEval and propose Robustness-driven Trajectory Synthesis. GUI-RobustEval contains $1,216$ executable test cases that systematically measure error recovery capabilities across a broad and realistic spectrum of error modes. At the data level, RoTS is a scalable synthesis framework that creates $800k$ high-quality data via a tree-based pipeline that proactively discovers diverse error modes and synthesizes corresponding recovery steps. Our two models, RoTS-7B and RoTS-32B, fine-tuned on our dataset, both demonstrate significant gains on GUI-RobustEval and traditional GUI benchmarks. Notably, RoTS-32B achieves state-of-the-art performance on OSWorld, with a $47.4\%$ success rate and a $33.8\%$ All-Pass@4 score, suggesting that improved long-horizon error recovery ability contributes to both robustness and overall performance. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [209] [STAMP: Training Explicit Memory for Mobile GUI Agents in Controllable and Scalable Virtual Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29324)
*Junyang Wang, Haiyang Xu, Xi Zhang, Zhaoqing Zhu, Ming Yan, Jieping Ye, Jitao Sang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 普通相关 (61.85) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: STAMP提出通过可控虚拟环境训练移动GUI智能体的显式记忆能力，利用程序化注入的记忆变量生成可验证的监督数据，并结合在线强化学习，使智能体在长程任务中具备记忆能力。在Memory-World基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动GUI智能体在长程任务中因有限上下文窗口与大量截图token的冲突而频繁失败——旧视觉历史被丢弃导致关键瞬态信息永久丢失。现有动作中心数据集无法教会智能体何时记忆什么，且增强静态真实数据成本高昂且缺乏交互验证。

Method: STAMP框架通过可控虚拟环境训练显式记忆：1）在合成任务中程序化注入确定性记忆变量，控制需记忆的内容、编码时机和检索时机；2）生成可验证的监督数据；3）通过环境驱动的奖励反馈实现在线强化学习。最终得到Stamp-GUI智能体。

Result: 在Memory-World基准上，Stamp-GUI在GUI专用模型中达到SOTA，展现出卓越的记忆准确率和任务韧性，同时保持强大的通用移动导航能力。

Conclusion: STAMP证明了通过可控虚拟环境训练显式记忆是解决移动GUI智能体长程任务记忆问题的有效范式，为未来智能体记忆训练提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究移动GUI智能体的显式记忆训练，属于AI智能体（agent）工作流范畴。aigc_agent_workflow（AI智能体与生成工作流）是[CANONICAL]子主题，且已在multimodal_understanding_generation方向下存在该子主题的引用，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: Strong methodology for training memory in GUI agents via controllable virtual environments, but not directly aligned with top priority subtopics. Signals: explicit memory training, controllable virtual environment, mobile GUI agent, reinforcement learning, Memory-World benchmark

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents excel at immediate reactive control but frequently fail in realistic, long-horizon tasks that require memory. This failure stems from a fundamental conflict between limited context windows and token-heavy screenshots. To save the limited context, agents must progressively discard older visual history, permanently losing crucial transient information. Furthermore, existing action-centric datasets fail to teach agents what or when to explicitly memorize, and augmenting static real-world data is prohibitively expensive and lacks interactive verification. To resolve this, we present STAMP, a framework that trains explicit memory in mobile agents through controllable virtual environments, where deterministic memory variables are programmatically injected into synthesized tasks to control what must be memorized, when it should be encoded, and when it must later be retrieved, thereby producing verifiable supervised data at scale and enabling online reinforcement learning through environment-driven reward feedback. Evaluated on our newly introduced Memory-World benchmark, the resulting Stamp-GUI agent achieves state-of-the-art performance among GUI-specialized models and sets a new high watermark on our Memory-World benchmark, demonstrating exceptional memory accuracy and task resilience while maintaining strong general mobile navigation capabilities.

</details>


### [210] [Train the Agent, Not the Expert: Learning to Harness Heterogeneous Experts for Multi-Turn Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29894)
*Yaowu Fan, Tao Han, Dazhao Du, Andy J. Ma, Jia Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 普通相关 (59.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VisHarness 提出了一种可训练的视觉智能体范式，通过强化学习训练智能体学会调用多个异构视觉专家模型（检测、分割、计数等），以多轮交互的方式解决复杂的视觉推理任务，在推理分割、广义指代分割、密集小目标检测和指代计数等基准上超越现有通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉模型通常针对单一任务优化，难以直接支持需要复杂语言理解和密集小目标感知的通用视觉智能。作者希望解耦高层感知、推理、决策与低层任务执行，保留智能体的通用智能同时充分利用专用视觉模型的精度优势。

Method: VisHarness 包含一个轻量级可训练的视觉智能体，通过强化学习（on-policy RL）学习调用一组异构视觉专家模型（检测、分割、计数等）的策略。引入动态视觉记忆归档（dynamic visual memory archiving）机制，缓解多轮交互中视觉 token 快速累积的开销，使在线 RL 训练可行。

Result: 在推理分割、广义指代分割、密集小目标检测和指代计数四个代表性基准上，VisHarness 显著优于现有通用模型，并在多个任务上达到或超越专用任务模型的性能。

Conclusion: VisHarness 验证了"训练智能体而非专家"范式的有效性，通过轻量训练即可学习通用的视觉专家编排策略，为构建通用视觉智能提供了一种新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 VisHarness，一个可训练的视觉智能体，通过多轮交互调用多个异构视觉专家模型（检测、分割、计数等）来解决复杂视觉推理任务。核心是智能体学习如何编排和利用专家工具，属于 AI 智能体与生成工作流（aigc_agent_workflow）范畴，该 subtopic 在 multimodal_understanding_generation 和 aigc 方向下均有收录。

Importance reason: Proposes a trainable agent for multi-turn visual reasoning, aligning with multimodal understanding and efficient inference priorities. Signals: multimodal understanding, reinforcement learning, visual agent, efficient inference, multi-turn reasoning

Abstract: Recent progress in computer vision has produced a wide range of powerful specialized models for detection, segmentation, counting, and other visual tasks. However, these models are usually optimized for isolated task formulations, making it difficult to directly support general-purpose visual intelligence, especially when a task requires complex language understanding and dense small-object perception. In this paper, we propose VisHarness, a trainable visual agent that decouples high-level perception, reasoning, and decision-making from low-level task execution. Instead of training a model to solve a specific visual task, VisHarness learns to harness a set of carefully designed heterogeneous visual experts. This paradigm preserves the general intelligence of the agent while fully leveraging the precision advantages of specialized visual models in concrete visual tasks. With only lightweight training, VisHarness learns a generalizable visual expert-harnessing policy and can solve common fundamental vision tasks under various complex conditions through multi-turn interactions with visual expert models. To enable efficient on-policy reinforcement learning training in a live environment, we introduce dynamic visual memory archiving, which mitigates the rapidly accumulating visual-token overhead caused by multi-turn interactions with visual expert models. Experiments on four representative benchmarks covering reasoning segmentation, generalized referring segmentation, dense small-object detection, and referring counting demonstrate that VisHarness substantially outperforms existing general-purpose models and achieves competitive or superior performance compared with task-specific models.

</details>


### [211] [AnomalyAgent: Training-Free Agentic Models for Zero-/Few-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30140)
*Yi Zhang, Jiawen Zhu, Lele Fu, Guansong Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 值得关注 (71.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AnomalyAgent 是一个免训练的智能体框架，利用 MLLM 的推理能力进行零样本/少样本异常检测，通过设计异常检测工具集和记忆模块，在简单和复杂异常（如逻辑/上下文异常）上均取得优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 VLM（如 CLIP）的零/少样本异常检测方法需要在大规模辅助数据集上训练才能适应异常检测任务，且推理依赖视觉-文本嵌入相似度评分，缺乏对需要深度上下文理解的复杂异常（如逻辑/上下文异常）的推理能力。

Method: 提出 AnomalyAgent 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 全面的异常检测工具集，使 MLLM 能够在零样本设置下进行自适应的智能体异常推理；(2) 定制化的记忆模块，通过少样本上下文参考示例来支撑异常推理。整体框架无需训练，直接利用 MLLM 的推理和泛化能力。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，AnomalyAgent 在零样本和少样本异常检测设置下，相比免训练的 VLM 方法和通用智能体方法均取得了显著更优的性能，特别是在逻辑/上下文异常等复杂异常类型上表现出更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: AnomalyAgent 验证了利用 MLLM 的推理能力进行免训练异常检测的有效性，为复杂异常检测提供了一种新的智能体范式，无需额外训练即可实现优异的泛化性能。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AnomalyAgent，一个基于 MLLM 的免训练智能体框架用于异常检测，核心是 agentic 推理、工具集和记忆模块。这属于多模态理解与生成方向下的 AI 智能体与生成工作流（aigc_agent_workflow）子主题，该子主题在 multimodal_understanding_generation 和 aigc 方向下均有收录，且为 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on anomaly detection, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: training-free agentic framework, MLLM reasoning for anomaly detection, zero-shot/few-shot, logical/contextual anomalies, no direct connection to video generation or world models

Abstract: Benefiting from generalizability of vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP, many zero-/few-shot anomaly detection (AD) approaches have achieved impressive detection performance across various datasets. Nevertheless, they require substantial training on large auxiliary datasets to adapt VLMs to anomaly detection, and their inference largely relies on visual-text embedding similarity-based anomaly scores, lacking reasoning abilities to detect complex anomalies that require in-depth contextual understanding. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{AnomalyAgent}, a novel training-free, agentic framework that leverages the advanced reasoning and generalization capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for anomaly detection. The key ingredients include \textbf{1)} a comprehensive anomaly-centric toolset that enables adaptive MLLM-driven, agentic anomaly reasoning in zero-shot settings, and \textbf{2)} a customized memory module that grounds anomaly reasoning with few-shot, in-context reference examples. We extend evaluation beyond the detection of simple anomalies (e.g., surface defects like cracks and dents and clear lesions) in widely used benchmarks to more diverse types of anomalies such as logical/contextual anomalies in logistics and manufacturing settings. Extensive experiment results demonstrate that our AnomalyAgent achieves substantially better performance compared to training-free VLM-based AD and generic agentic methods, highlighting its superior generalization capability in both zero-shot and few-shot anomaly detection settings. The code implementation can be find at this address.

</details>


### [212] [BORA: Bridging Offline Reinforcement Learning and Online Residual Adaptation for Real-World Dexterous VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30226)
*Zhongxi Chen, Yifan Han, Yanming Shao, Huanming Liu, Congsheng Xu, Xiaoyu Chen, Yao Mu, Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: AI 智能体与生成工作流

Importance: 普通相关 (49.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BORA 是一个离线到在线的强化学习后训练框架，专为真实世界灵巧操作 VLA 模型设计。离线阶段构建动作条件价值批评器，在线阶段引入轻量级人机循环残差自适应机制，在五个真实灵巧操作任务上平均成功率提升 33%，未见物体泛化提升 43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在灵巧操作中面临高维手部控制和累积执行误差的挑战，真实世界的 RL 后训练至关重要，但高维灵巧探索会导致时序不一致、样本效率低和硬件风险。现有方法缺乏有效的离线到在线迁移框架来解决这些问题。

Method: BORA 包含两个阶段：(1) 离线阶段：构建一个以 VLM 认知 token 和动作块为输入的批评器，实现动作条件价值引导；(2) 在线阶段：冻结 VLA 基础模型，引入轻量级人机循环（HiL）块级残差自适应机制，通过继承离线批评器和干预驱动奖励来纠正执行偏差并适应真实物理环境变化。

Result: 在五个真实世界灵巧操作任务上，BORA 相比纯模仿学习和传统解耦 RL 基线，标准设置下平均成功率绝对提升 33%，未见物体泛化场景下提升 43%。

Conclusion: BORA 通过离线到在线的 RL 后训练框架有效弥合了 VLA 模型在灵巧操作中视觉动作生成与物理可靠执行之间的差距，验证了动作条件价值批评和残差自适应机制的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 BORA 框架，将离线强化学习与在线残差自适应结合，用于真实世界灵巧操作的 VLA 模型后训练。这属于机器人操作智能体与自动化工作流范畴，与 aigc_agent_workflow（AI 智能体与生成工作流）高度匹配，且该 subtopic 在 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下已有记录。

Importance reason: Focuses on dexterous manipulation VLA models with offline-to-online RL, but is not aligned with user's priority subtopics like video/image generation or world models. Signals: VLA model post-training, offline-to-online RL, dexterous manipulation, real-world robotics, human-in-the-loop adaptation

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for grounding visual-language understanding into real-world robotic manipulation. However, dexterous manipulation remains challenging for VLA policies due to high-dimensional hand control and compounding execution errors, which makes real-world RL post-training essential for bridging the gap between visually grounded action generation and physically reliable dexterous execution. However, high-dimensional dexterous exploration often triggers temporal inconsistency, sample inefficiency and hardware risks in the real world. To address these challenges, we propose BORA, an offline-to-online RL post-training framework designed for real-world dexterous VLA models. In the offline phase, BORA constructs a critic that takes both the VLM's cognition tokens and action chunks as inputs. This design enables action-conditioned value guidance, allowing the critic to evaluate dexterous hand motions beyond visual context alone. During the subsequent online phase, BORA freezes the VLA base and introduces a lightweight, Human-in-the-Loop (HiL) chunk-wise residual adaptation mechanism to mitigate real-world execution errors and further correct the offline-learned intents within the actual physical environment. By inheriting the offline critic and employing intervention-driven rewards, BORA effectively corrects execution discrepancies and adapts to real-world physical variances while preserving the pretrained policy as a stable prior. Extensive evaluations across five complex real-world dexterous tasks demonstrate that BORA significantly outperforms pure imitation learning and traditional decoupled RL baselines, achieving a 33% absolute increase in average success rate under standard settings and up to a 43% improvement in unseen object generalization.

</details>

### [213] [Audio Jailbreaks in Large Audio-Language Models: Taxonomy, Attack-Defense Analysis, and Cost-Aware Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30031)
*Bo-Han Feng, Yu-Hsuan Li Liang, Chien-Feng Liu, You-Hsuan Chang, Yun-Nung Chen*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.SD, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (51.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了大型音频语言模型（LALM）的越狱攻击与防御方法，提出了统一的分类学框架，并在10个开源LALM上进行了受控实验评估。研究发现声学Best-of-N攻击揭示了音频空间的严重漏洞，叙事框架是一种高效的低延迟语义威胁，而现有防御方法在鲁棒性与良性可用性之间存在权衡。论文主张在安全评测中引入成本与效用感知评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LALM越狱研究在异构威胁模型和评估协议下进行，攻击实用性和防御有效性难以比较，缺乏统一的分类学和标准化评估框架。

Method: 提出统一的攻击-防御分类学：攻击分为语义、声学、信号和嵌入层四类；防御分为基于守卫、免训练和基于训练三类；基准分为跨模态、音频原生和交互式三类。在10个开源LALM上评估代表性攻击与防御，测量攻击成功率、良性拒绝率和延迟。

Result: 声学Best-of-N攻击揭示了音频空间中最严重的漏洞；叙事框架是一种有效的低延迟语义威胁；现有防御方法在鲁棒性与良性可用性之间存在明显权衡。

Conclusion: LALM安全评测不能仅依赖攻击成功率，需要引入成本与效用感知评估作为必要补充，以更全面地衡量攻击实用性和防御有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究大型音频语言模型（LALM）的越狱攻击与防御，包括语义、声学、信号和嵌入层攻击的分类学与评估，属于多模态安全与攻防范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 multimodal_safety_attack。

Importance reason: Systematic taxonomy and cost-aware evaluation of audio jailbreaks, but the topic is far from user's priority subtopics in generation and efficiency. Signals: audio jailbreak taxonomy, attack-defense analysis, cost-aware evaluation, LALM safety, acoustic best-of-n attack

Abstract: Large Audio Language Models (LALMs) expand jailbreak risks from token-level prompting to the full speech perception-to-reasoning pipeline, where unsafe behavior can be induced through semantics, acoustic style, signal artifacts, or internal representations. Existing work studies these risks under heterogeneous threat models and evaluation protocols, making it difficult to compare attack practicality or defense utility. This paper provides a unified taxonomy and a controlled empirical evaluation of LALM jailbreak attacks and defenses. We organize prior work into semantic, acoustic, signal, and embedding-layer attacks; guard-based, training-free, and training-based defenses; and cross-modal, audio-native, and interactive benchmarks. We then evaluate representative attacks and defenses across ten open-source LALMs, measuring not only attack success rate but also benign refusal and latency. Our results show that Acoustic Best-of-N reveals strong worst-case audio-space vulnerabilities, Narrative Framing is an effective low-latency semantic threat, and current defenses trade robustness against benign usability. These findings support cost- and utility-aware evaluation as a necessary complement to success-rate-only LALM safety benchmarks.

</details>


### [214] [ReasonBreak: Probing Vulnerabilities in Reasoning-Enabled Vision-Language-Action Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29114)
*Mohammadreza Teymoorianfard, Jean-Philippe Monteuuis, Jonathan Petit, Amir Houmansadr*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CR, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (51.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了推理增强的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）自动驾驶模型在现实输入扰动下的脆弱性，在闭环仿真中实现了高达89%的推理攻击成功率和72%的轨迹操控成功率，并提出了推理感知评估框架和安全基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管推理增强的VLA模型被广泛用于端到端自动驾驶，但其在现实输入扰动下的鲁棒性尚未被系统研究，缺乏针对推理-轨迹耦合的评估框架和基准。

Method: 以NVIDIA的Alpamayo模型为代表，进行首次系统性黑盒研究，在现实文本输入扰动下评估对推理和驾驶行为的影响；提出推理感知评估框架，同时捕捉推理的语义和结构方面，并结合安全中心度量；构建攻击与防御基准。

Result: 在闭环仿真中，推理攻击成功率高达89%，轨迹操控成功率高达72%，导致碰撞率上升和安全指标下降。

Conclusion: 推理增强的VLA自动驾驶模型对现实输入扰动高度脆弱，亟需严格的评估和改进的防御措施以确保系统安全。

Classification reason: 该论文研究推理增强的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）自动驾驶模型在输入扰动下的鲁棒性，属于多模态安全与攻防范畴。论文系统性地进行黑盒攻击、提出推理感知评估框架和基准，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"（multimodal_safety_attack）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on autonomous driving safety, which is not a priority subtopic and is partially blocked by negative keywords. Signals: VLA model robustness, reasoning attack, autonomous driving safety, black-box attack, closed-loop simulation

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models with integrated reasoning have been proposed for end-to-end autonomous driving, assuming a tight coupling between reasoning and trajectory generation. However, the robustness of such systems under realistic input perturbations remains largely unexplored. We show that these models are highly vulnerable to realistic input perturbations, achieving up to 89% attack success rate (ASR) on reasoning and up to 72% on trajectory manipulation in closed-loop simulation, leading to increased collision rates and degraded safety metrics. Using NVIDIA's recent Alpamayo models as representative industry-developed VLAs, we conduct the first systematic black-box study of reasoning-enabled VLA models under realistic textual input corruptions, evaluating their impact on reasoning and driving behavior. We introduce a reasoning-aware evaluation framework capturing both semantic and structural aspects of reasoning, along with safety-centric measures. We also introduce a benchmark for evaluating attacks and defenses on reasoning-trajectory interactions in autonomous driving. Our results highlight the need for rigorous evaluation and improved defenses to ensure the safety of reasoning-enabled VLA systems in autonomous driving.

</details>
