### [215] [Unveiling the Visual Counting Bottleneck in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30170)
*Xingzhou Pang, Yifan Hou, Junling Wang, Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (60.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉语言模型在视觉计数任务中的系统性泛化瓶颈。通过将视觉计数分解为视觉个体化、数量感知和符号映射三个阶段，利用合成围棋棋盘和线性探针实验，发现视觉骨干网络在外推范围内仍保持线性可分的数量表征，模型也具备比较推理能力，但崩溃发生在符号映射阶段——模型无法将有效的视觉数量投影到符号token上。作者提出"断裂数量假说"：VLM未能学习到统一的数字空间，而是学习了不相交的、模态特定的统计流形，阻碍了跨模态的未见数量接地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在插值任务上表现出色，但在系统性泛化（尤其是视觉计数）上存在灾难性失败。现有研究缺乏对这种外推瓶颈的深入诊断，不清楚失败究竟发生在感知、认知还是符号映射阶段。

Method: 将视觉计数分解为三个认知阶段：视觉个体化（visual individuation）、数量感知（magnitude awareness）和符号映射（symbolic mapping）。使用合成围棋棋盘数据，通过线性探针分析视觉骨干网络各层的数量表征线性可分性，设计比较推理任务测试数量感知能力，并分析符号映射阶段的失败模式。

Result: 视觉骨干网络在外推范围内仍保持线性可分的数量表征；模型在比较推理任务上表现良好，说明具备潜在的数量感知能力；失败发生在符号映射阶段，模型无法将有效的视觉数量映射到正确的数字token上。

Conclusion: VLM的视觉计数失败源于"断裂数量假说"：模型未学习到统一的数字空间，而是形成了模态特定的分离统计流形。仅靠数据扩展无法解决该问题，需要引入强制统一表征的归纳偏置。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM在视觉计数任务中的系统性泛化失败问题，定位到符号映射阶段的崩溃，属于多模态模型的可靠性/忠实性缺陷分析，与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）高度相关。

Importance reason: Directly addresses a fundamental bottleneck in multimodal understanding, relevant to unified multimodal models and reliability. Signals: multimodal understanding, visual counting, systematic generalization, fractured magnitude hypothesis, symbolic mapping bottleneck

Abstract: While Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) excel at interpolation, they suffer catastrophic failures in systematic generalization, most notably in visual counting. In this work, we investigate this extrapolation bottleneck by deconstructing visual counting into three cognitive stages: visual individuation, magnitude awareness, and symbolic mapping. Using synthetic Go boards and linear probes, we demonstrate that visual backbones maintain robust, linearly separable representations of quantity well into the extrapolation regime, ruling out perceptual failure. Furthermore, models retain latent magnitude awareness, successfully performing comparative reasoning on quantities they fail to enumerate. We pinpoint the collapse to the symbolic mapping stage, where the model fails to project valid visual magnitudes onto symbolic tokens. Our findings support a frac tured magnitude hypothesis: VLMs fail to acquire a universal number space, instead learning disjoint, modality-specific statistical manifolds that prevent cross-modal grounding for unseen quantities. Validated on the state-of-the-art foundation model, our results suggest that bridging this gap requires inductive priors enforcing unified representations, as data scaling alone is insufficient.

</details>


### [216] [MuPHI: Learning Implicit Multimodal Harm Reasoning via Semantically Grounded Reward Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29951)
*Anisha Saha, Varsha Suresh, Teodora Kamova, Sophia Wiedmann, Timothy Hospedales, Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出MuPHI数据集和MuPHIRM训练框架，用于评估和提升视觉语言模型对隐含多模态有害内容的检测与推理能力。MuPHI包含图像-文本对，其中有害语义通过隐式跨模态线索编码；MuPHIRM通过多视角奖励优化学习联合语义推理，显著提升VLM的有害内容检测和推理质量，并展现出优越的分布外鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM擅长基于表层感知线索的字面推理，但难以捕捉依赖隐式、上下文推理的有害语义。有害内容往往来自看似无害的图像-文本组合，需要意图感知的跨模态推理能力。缺乏评估VLM在隐式有害推理方面的基准和训练方法。

Method: 1) 构建MuPHI数据集：包含图像-文本对，涵盖多种有害类别，并带有标注的有害推理链（rationale）。2) 提出MuPHIRM训练框架：通过优化多视角奖励（multi-perspective rewards）来学习联合语义推理，增强VLM对隐含有害内容的检测和推理能力。

Result: MuPHIRM在有害内容检测和推理质量上均优于现有方法，并在分布外（OOD）场景下展现出更强的鲁棒性，优于基于训练和推理时增强的基线方法。

Conclusion: 面向推理的奖励优化是构建超越基准特定捷径、具备泛化能力的多模态安全系统的有前景方向。

Classification reason: 论文研究多模态模型对隐含有害语义的检测与推理，属于多模态安全与可靠性范畴。虽然也有安全攻防维度，但核心是提升VLM对隐含有害内容的推理忠实性和可靠性，与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）主题高度契合。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal harm reasoning, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in generation and efficiency. Signals: multimodal harm detection, reward optimization, VLM reasoning, out-of-distribution robustness, implicit cross-modal reasoning

Abstract: Understanding how harm emerges from interaction between otherwise benign image-text pairs requires intent-aware cross-modal reasoning beyond surface-level features. Existing vision-language models (VLMs) excel at literal reasoning over perceptual cues but often fail to derive harmful semantics that rely on implicit, context-dependent reasoning. To evaluate VLMs on compositional harm detection and reasoning, we introduce Multimodal Pragmatic Harm Interpretation (MuPHI), a dataset containing image-text pairs where harm is encoded in subtle multimodal cues. MuPHI spans diverse harm categories and includes annotated harm rationales for assessing VLM reasoning chains. To improve both detection and reasoning in VLMs, we propose MuPHIRM, a reasoning-augmented training framework which learns joint semantics by optimizing multi-perspective rewards. MuPHIRM improves both harm detection and reasoning quality of VLMs while demonstrating superior out-of-distribution robustness compared to both trained and inference-time baselines. Our findings suggest that reasoning-oriented reward optimization offers a promising direction towards building multimodal systems that generalize beyond benchmark-specific shortcuts.

</details>


### [217] [The Chain Holds, the Answer Folds: Trace-Answer Dissociation in Reasoning Models Under Adversarial Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29087)
*Yubo Li, Ramayya Krishnan, Rema Padman*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (54.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现推理模型在多轮对话中面对持续对抗压力时会出现一种新的失败模式：思维链从头到尾保持事实正确，但最终答案却翻转错误。作者称之为"不忠实屈服"(UC)，并通过2×2潜在-行为框架、多数据集实验和跨模型分析揭示了这一现象，发现推理通道是产生该差距的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型通常在单轮基准上评估，但在实际部署中面临多轮对话场景，用户可能对正确答案持续施压。现有评估方法（翻转率指标、单轮忠实性探测）无法捕捉这种思维链正确但答案错误的隐蔽失败模式。

Method: 提出2×2潜在-行为分析框架区分思维链正确性与答案正确性；在MT-Consistency、MMLU-Pro、GSM8K三个数据集上构建多轮对抗对话；对比think/no_think模式下的行为差异；使用GPT-4o独立裁判验证UC标签；进行token级探测分析。

Result: 行为翻转时潜在正确率在think模式下接近50%，在no_think模式下骤降至11-15%；该效应在不同模型中与推理通道强度相关（Qwen3-32B和GPT-OSS-20B高，Gemma-4-31B-it低）；GPT-4o裁判验证了86%的UC标签；答案槽argmax在84%的UC单元中正确；基于思维链的防御策略反而适得其反。

Conclusion: 推理模型存在"不忠实屈服"这一隐蔽失败模式，推理过程本身创造了正确性与答案之间的差距，简单的思维链锚定防御无效，需要新的评估方法和防御策略来确保模型在多轮交互中的可靠性。

Classification reason: 论文研究推理模型在多轮对话中面对对抗压力时，思维链保持正确但最终答案翻转的"不忠实屈服"现象，属于模型可靠性与忠实性问题，与多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）子主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Novel failure mode in reasoning models, but tangential to user's core generative modeling priorities. Signals: reasoning model failure, adversarial multi-turn, chain-of-thought robustness, trace-answer dissociation, model reliability

Abstract: Reasoning models are evaluated on single-turn benchmarks but deployed in multi-turn dialogue, where users push back on correct answers. Under sustained adversarial pressure we find a previously undocumented failure mode: the chain-of-thought stays factually correct from first turn to last while the emitted answer flips wrong. We call this unfaithful capitulation (UC) and isolate it with a $2\times 2$ latent-versus-behavioral framework that flip-rate metrics and single-turn faithfulness probes both miss. Across three datasets (MT-Consistency, MMLU-Pro, GSM8K), the latent-correct rate at the behavioral flip clusters near 50% in think mode and collapses to 11-15% under no_think -- paired, within-model causal evidence that reasoning creates the gap. Across models the effect tracks the reasoning channel (high in Qwen3-32B and GPT-OSS-20B, low in inline-CoT Gemma-4-31B-it). An independent GPT-4o judge corroborates $86\%$ of UC labels; a token-level probe shows the answer-slot argmax is correct in $84\%$ of UC cells; and a naive trace-anchored defense backfires. We release all trajectories, traces, and judge labels.

</details>


### [218] [Micro-Macro Retrieval: Reducing Long-Form Hallucination in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28828)
*Yujie Feng, Jian Li, Zhihan Zhou, Pengfei Xu, Yujia Zhang, Xiaoyu Li, Xiaohui Zhou, Alan Zhao, Xi Chen, Xiao-Ming Wu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Micro-Macro Retrieval (M2R)框架，通过宏观检索外部粗粒度证据和微观提取推理过程中构建的关键信息库，确保关键信息靠近模型输出，从而有效减少长文本生成中的幻觉。采用基于课程学习的强化学习策略训练，在多个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强语言模型（RALM）缺乏有效机制确保关键信息靠近模型输出，导致长文本生成中冗余检索上下文和冗长推理链放大事实错误。论文旨在填补这一空白，解决关键信息与输出之间的邻近性瓶颈。

Method: 提出M2R（Micro-Macro Retrieval）框架：宏观层面从外部源检索粗粒度证据；微观层面从推理过程中构建的关键信息库提取关键结果并在生成答案时重用。采用基于课程学习的强化学习策略，使用自定义规则奖励进行训练，使模型稳定获取检索和接地技能。

Result: 在多个不同基准上的大量实验表明M2R有效减少了长文本生成中的幻觉，尤其在长上下文场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: M2R通过宏观-微观双层检索机制有效解决了关键信息与输出之间的邻近性问题，显著降低了长文本生成中的幻觉，为检索增强生成提供了新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于减少大语言模型在长文本生成中的幻觉问题，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）这一规范子主题的范畴。虽然论文主要针对文本LLM，但幻觉缓解是该子主题的核心内容，且方法具有跨模态通用性。

Importance reason: Addresses hallucination in long-form generation but is not aligned with priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: Retrieval-augmented generation, hallucination reduction, long-form text, reinforcement learning, curriculum learning

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve impressive performance across many tasks but remain prone to hallucination, especially in long-form generation where redundant retrieved contexts and lengthy reasoning chains amplify factual errors. Recent studies highlight a critical phenomenon: the closer key information appears to the model outputs, the higher the factual accuracy. However, existing retrieval-augmented language models (RALMs) lack effective mechanisms to ensure this proximity - external evidence is injected into reasoning via multi-turn retrieval, but this cannot ensure key information stays close to the outputs. We propose Micro-Macro Retrieval (M2R), a novel retrieve-while-generate framework to fill this gap. At the macro level, M2R retrieves coarse-grained evidence from external sources; at the micro level, it extracts essential results from a key information repository built during reasoning and reuses them while generating answers. This design directly addresses the key-information-to-output proximity bottleneck, effectively reducing hallucination in long-form tasks. M2R is trained with a curriculum learning-based reinforcement learning strategy using customized rule-based rewards, enabling stable acquisition of retrieval and grounding skills. Extensive experiments across different benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of M2R, especially in lengthy-context settings.

</details>


### [219] [ReactBench: A Cause-Driven Benchmark for Multimodal Hallucination via Systematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29579)
*Shizhe Zhou, Bohan Jia, Kai Wu, Yan Shen, Tongyun Li, Yuyang Wu, Shaohui Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (71.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ReactBench 是一个因果驱动的多模态幻觉评测基准，通过对抗性图像和诱导幻觉的查询，设计了关系擦除、反事实属性、变更追踪和密集计数四个任务，系统性地暴露 MLLM 的共现偏差、语言先验、跨图像比较感知缺陷和细粒度感知瓶颈，并利用思维链推理识别幻觉的细粒度子原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉基准主要关注检测幻觉结果而非评估其根本原因，且多依赖简单场景和有限评估格式，已无法挑战当前最先进的 MLLM。因此需要一种系统性的、因果驱动的评测方法来诊断幻觉的深层原因。

Method: ReactBench 通过生成对抗性图像和诱导幻觉的查询，设计了四个针对性任务：关系擦除（测试共现偏差）、反事实属性（测试语言先验）、变更追踪（测试跨图像比较感知）、密集计数（测试细粒度感知瓶颈）。采用考试式评估格式，并利用思维链推理识别每个任务中幻觉的细粒度子原因。

Result: 广泛评估表明，当前 MLLM 仍然明显容易受到特定原因触发的幻觉影响，验证了 ReactBench 作为系统性、可解释的测试平台的价值。

Conclusion: ReactBench 提供了一个系统性和可解释的测试平台，用于诊断和提升多模态模型的鲁棒性，揭示了现有 MLLM 在因果层面上的幻觉脆弱性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ReactBench，一个因果驱动的多模态幻觉评测基准，系统性地评估 MLLM 中的幻觉问题。这直接对应 [CANONICAL] 多模态幻觉与可靠性 (multimodal_hallucination) 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on MLLM hallucination benchmarking, not directly on user's priority generation or efficiency topics. Signals: multimodal hallucination benchmark, cause-driven evaluation, chain-of-thought reasoning, co-occurrence bias, language priors

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved rapid progress in vision-language understanding, they remain prone to multimodal hallucinations, producing responses that are inconsistent with the visual input. Existing benchmarks predominantly focus on detecting hallucination outcomes rather than evaluating the underlying causes of these failures. Moreover, many benchmarks rely on simplistic scenarios and limited evaluation formats that no longer challenge state-of-the-art models. To address these limitations, we introduce ReactBench, a cause-driven hallucination benchmark featuring multiple tasks and an exam-style evaluation format. By generating adversarial images and hallucination-inducing queries, ReactBench introduces four targeted tasks: Relational Erasure, Counterfactual Attribute, Alteration Tracing, and Dense Counting. These tasks systematically expose co-occurrence bias, language priors, cross-image comparative perception deficiencies, and fine-grained perceptual bottlenecks. Beyond standard accuracy-based evaluation, we leverage Chain-of-Thought reasoning to identify fine-grained sub-causes of hallucination within each task. Extensive evaluations reveal that current MLLMs remain notably vulnerable to cause-specific hallucination triggers, demonstrating the value of ReactBench as a systematic and interpretable testbed for diagnosing and improving multimodal model robustness. The project page is available at .

</details>


### [220] [Harnessing non-adversarial robustness in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29816)
*Qinghua Zhou, Ellina Aleshina, Andrey Lovyagin, Oleg Somov, Mikhail Seleznyov, Alexander Panchenko, Ivan Oseledets, Elena Tutubalina, Ivan Y. Tyukin*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (47.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需昂贵全模型重训练即可提升LLM对语义相似但文本不同提示的鲁棒性的方法——去偏微调（debiasing for robustness），并从理论和实验两方面证明了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM对语义相似但文本表述不同的提示（prompt变体）非常敏感，这种不鲁棒性严重影响模型在实际应用中的可靠性。现有方法通常需要昂贵的全模型重训练，因此需要一种更高效的鲁棒性增强方案。

Method: 通过理论分析发现，影响模型鲁棒性的关键因素是神经网络模块输出中的系统性期望偏移（即扰动诱导的偏差）。基于此，提出一种简单的微调方法——去偏微调（debiasing for robustness），通过消除这种偏差来提升鲁棒性，并给出了该方法有效与无效的条件。

Result: 理论和大量实验表明，去偏微调是一种快速高效的鲁棒性增强工具，能够有效提升LLM对随机提示扰动的鲁棒性，并提供可认证的鲁棒性保证。

Conclusion: 去偏微调可以在不进行全模型重训练的情况下显著提升LLM对语义中性提示变体的鲁棒性，为实际部署中模型可靠性的提升提供了一种轻量级解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM对语义相似但文本不同的提示的鲁棒性问题，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination）子主题，因为该子主题涵盖模型可靠性、忠实性提升，而本文的鲁棒性增强正是提升模型可靠性的核心方面。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM robustness to prompt variations, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in generation and efficiency. Signals: LLM robustness, debiasing fine-tuning, prompt perturbation, theoretical analysis, no video/image generation

Abstract: The work presents an approach for addressing the challenge of robustness in Large Language Models (LLMs) to alterations and potential errors caused by semantically similar but textually different prompts. Recent works have shown that these kinds of prompt variations can significantly impact the performance of LLMs on tasks. The central question is: can LLMs' robustness to semantically-neutral prompt alterations be acquired without expensive retraining of the entire model? We address this question both theoretically and through experiments. Our theoretical analysis reveals a crucial factor impacting model robustness - a systematic expected shift or perturbation-induced bias in neural network module outputs. Motivated by this analysis, we show that robustness can be achieved via a simple fine-tuning process: debiasing for robustness. We identify conditions when debiasing helps and when it does not, and demonstrate, through both theory and extensive experiments, that debiasing for robustness may indeed be a quick and efficient tool to enhance robustness and provide certification against random prompt perturbations.

</details>


### [221] [Mitigating Hallucination in Vision-Language Models through Barrier-Regulated Adaptive Closed-form Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29881)
*Soumyadeep Jana, Pulkit Mittal, Sanasam Ranbir Singh*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (57.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出BRACS，一种无需训练的推理时干预框架，通过监测视觉注意力并仅在接地退化时进行闭式隐状态修正，有效缓解LVLM的物体幻觉问题，在多个基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM在解码过程中视觉接地逐渐减弱，导致生成图像中不存在的物体幻觉。已有的推理时缓解方法存在三个问题：缺乏显式接地目标、在模型已良好接地时仍进行干预、使用固定修正强度无法适应接地失败的严重程度。

Method: BRACS通过监测模型自身的注意力来度量视觉接地质量，仅在接地退化时对隐状态施加修正。修正更新通过闭式解析解计算，无需训练辅助网络或模型重训。具体包括：注意力屏障监测机制判断何时需要干预，以及自适应闭式修正向量计算。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5-7B和Qwen-VL-Chat上，BRACS在幻觉基准上一致优于先前方法：CHAIR_s降低9.4个点，POPE F1提升2.7个点，同时在四个通用多模态基准上保持或提升性能。推理效率达到贪心解码吞吐量的80%，平均速度比基线快1.3倍。

Conclusion: BRACS通过注意力屏障调控的自适应闭式修正，在不牺牲效率的前提下有效缓解了LVLM的幻觉问题，验证了选择性干预策略的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉语言模型中的幻觉缓解问题，提出了一种无需训练的推理时干预方法，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Addresses a critical hallucination issue in multimodal models with a novel, efficient inference-time method, but is not directly on the user's top priority subtopics. Signals: hallucination mitigation, vision-language model, inference-time intervention, closed-form steering, attention monitoring

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) often hallucinate objects that are not present in the input image, largely because visual grounding weakens as decoding progresses. Existing inference-time mitigation methods modify logits or hidden states throughout generation, but they suffer from three key limitations: they lack an explicit grounding objective, intervene even when the model is already well-grounded, and use fixed correction strengths that do not adapt to the severity of grounding failure. We propose BRACS (Barrier-Regulated Adaptive Closed-form Steering), a training-free steering framework that addresses these issues through barrier-regulated adaptive closed-form steering. BRACS monitors the model's own attention to measure visual grounding and applies corrections to the hidden states only when grounding deteriorates. The corrective update is computed analytically in closed form, requiring no training of auxiliary networks or model retraining. Experiments on LLaVA-1.5-7B and Qwen-VL-Chat show that BRACS consistently outperforms prior methods on hallucination benchmarks, reducing CHAIR$_s$ by 9.4 points and improving POPE F1 by 2.7 points, while matching or improving performance on four general multimodal benchmarks. BRACS also remains efficient, operating at 80% of greedy decoding throughput and achieving 1.3 times higher speed on average than the baselines.

</details>


### [222] [K-FinHallu: A Hallucination Detection Benchmark for Multi-Turn RAG in Korean Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29523)
*Eunbyeol Cho, Yunseung Lee, Mirae Kim, Jeewon Yang, Youngjun Kwak, Edward Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (41.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: K-FinHallu 是首个面向韩语金融领域多轮 RAG 的幻觉检测基准，构建了基于真实金融文档的多轮对话并注入分层幻觉，实验表明最强模型在细粒度金融诊断和合理拒答方面仍存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测基准主要关注单轮、英文场景，缺乏对多轮对话动态和韩语金融领域语言-监管细微差别的覆盖，阻碍了LLM在高风险金融场景中的部署。

Method: 从真实韩语金融文档构建多轮对话，基于上下文可回答性提出分层幻觉分类体系，注入各类幻觉，并评估前沿和开源LLM作为幻觉检测器的性能。

Result: 即使最强的模型在细粒度金融诊断和拒答行为上表现不佳；在训练集上微调8B模型可获得与前沿LLM竞争的性能，但合理拒答仍是所有模型最薄弱的维度。

Conclusion: K-FinHallu填补了韩语金融多轮RAG幻觉检测的空白，揭示了现有模型在合理拒答方面的系统性不足，为高风险金融场景的可靠部署提供了重要评测资源。

Classification reason: 该论文提出韩语金融领域多轮RAG幻觉检测基准，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）这一规范子主题，因为其核心关注LLM在RAG场景下的幻觉检测与可靠性评估。

Importance reason: Focuses on Korean finance RAG hallucination, which is narrow and outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Korean finance domain, multi-turn RAG hallucination, benchmark creation, low alignment with user's generative video/image priorities

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced financial automation through Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), yet hallucinations remain a critical barrier to deployment in high-stakes environments. Existing benchmarks focus on single-turn, English-centric tasks, leaving the multi-turn dynamics and linguistic-regulatory nuances of the Korean financial domain unaddressed. We introduce K-FinHallu, the first benchmark for hallucination detection in multi-turn Korean financial RAG. We construct multi-turn dialogues from authentic Korean financial documents and inject hallucinations under a proposed hierarchical taxonomy based on context answerability that explicitly accounts for justified abstention. Benchmarking frontier and open-source LLMs as hallucination detectors, we find that even the strongest models struggle with fine-grained financial diagnostics and refusal behavior. While fine-tuning an 8B model on our training split yields performance competitive with frontier LLMs, justified abstention remains the weakest axis across all evaluated models.

</details>


### [223] [Teaching Language Models to Check Grounded Claim Factuality with Human Test-Taking Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29712)
*Yuxuan Ye, Raul Santos-Rodriguez, Edwin Simpson*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文将基于文本的事实性核查建模为阅读理解任务，通过提示LLM使用应试策略进行高效推理，相比无引导的开放式推理减少80%以上的token使用，并在两个基准上取得有竞争力的性能。同时，通过监督微调和自修正机制训练小语言模型替代LLM，在保持低推理成本的同时生成可解释的理由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于蕴含分类器的事实性核查方法需要针对数据集调整阈值，而基于LLM的直接提示方法未能充分利用其推理能力。作者希望设计一种高效且可解释的事实性核查方法，既能减少推理成本，又能保持高准确性。

Method: 将事实性核查建模为真/假阅读理解任务，使用明确的应试策略（如关键词定位、选项对比）提示LLM进行高效推理。为进一步降低成本，通过监督微调（SFT）和自修正机制训练小语言模型（SLM）来替代LLM进行核查。

Result: 该方法相比无引导的开放式推理减少80%以上的token使用，在两个事实性基准上取得有竞争力的性能，并在其中一个基准上达到新SOTA。训练得到的SLM在性能上与强基线相当，同时推理成本低且能生成支持性理由。

Conclusion: 将事实性核查建模为阅读理解任务并引入应试策略是一种高效且有效的方法。通过SFT和自修正机制训练的SLM可以在保持低推理成本的同时达到与LLM相当的性能，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于检索增强生成（RAG）的事实性核查（grounded claim factuality checking），属于多模态/文本生成中事实性与可靠性提升的范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on text-based factuality checking, not directly aligned with user's priority on multimodal generation or world models. Signals: text-only factuality, reading comprehension approach, token efficiency, no multimodal or generation focus, negative keywords absent

Abstract: Grounded claim factuality checking is important for large language model (LLM) applications such as retrieval-augmented generation, as it helps users assess the correctness of generated outputs. Existing metrics using entailment classifiers require dataset-specific threshold tuning, while LLM-based approaches often use direct prompting, which underutilises the reasoning capabilities of LLMs. We address this by formulating grounded claim factuality checking as a true/false reading comprehension task and prompting LLMs with explicit test-taking strategies for efficient reasoning. Our method reduces token usage by over 80% compared to unguided open-ended reasoning, and achieves competitive performance to more expensive alternatives across two factuality benchmarks, setting a new state of the art on one. To further reduce inference cost, we train small language models (SLMs) to replace LLMs in the checking pipeline. Using supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and a self-revision mechanism, the SLMs learn to improve their factuality judgements. Experimental results show that the resulting SLMs perform on par with strong baselines, combining low inference costs with generating supporting rationales to support interpretability. Code and datasets will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [224] [Towards Localized and Disentangled Knowledge Editing for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29826)
*Leijiang Gu, Zhen Zeng, Feng Li, Xinjian Gao, Zenglin Shi*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (71.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 LDKE 框架，通过定位事实相关层和解耦目标相关/无关输入，解决多模态大模型知识编辑中编辑泛化不足和意外修改无关信息的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 知识编辑方法虽然能修改目标事实对，但无法泛化到逻辑相关的查询，且会意外修改视觉或语义上相关但无关的信息。作者识别出两个失败模式：因果错位（编辑局限于特定样本）和特征纠缠（对耦合但无关的信息造成意外修改）。

Method: 提出 LDKE 框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 快速定位模块（Fast Localization），高效识别并更新事实相关的关键层；(2) 解耦分类器（Disentanglement Classifier），根据输入是否与目标事实相关进行路由，保留无关知识。

Result: 在多个基准和 MLLM 上的实验表明，LDKE 在将编辑传播到相关上下文方面表现优越，同时保持了较高的局部性（即不修改无关信息）。

Conclusion: LDKE 通过定位和解耦实现了更精确、更具泛化性的多模态知识编辑，有效解决了现有方法在编辑泛化和意外修改方面的关键缺陷。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大模型的知识编辑，核心问题是编辑后对相关查询泛化不足以及对无关信息造成意外修改（即幻觉/可靠性问题），属于多模态幻觉与可靠性范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性"。

Importance reason: Focuses on MLLM reliability but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: multimodal knowledge editing, MLLM reliability, disentangled editing, causal misalignment, feature entanglement

Abstract: Existing methods in Multimodal Knowledge Editing (MKE) have advanced the ability to correct outdated or inaccurate knowledge in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, they exhibit a critical limitation: while effectively modifying target factual pairs, they fail to generalize edits to logically related queries and often cause unintended alterations to unrelated but visually or semantically linked information. We identify and formalize two underlying failure modes causing this issue: Causal Misalignment, which confines edits to the specific sample, and Feature Entanglement, which causes unintended alterations to coupled but irrelevant information. To address these issues, we propose Localized and Disentangled Knowledge Editing (LDKE), a new framework that achieves precise and generalized editing by localizing fact-specific model layers and disentangling target-relevant inputs from irrelevant ones. Our approach introduces a Fast Localization module to identify and update critical layers efficiently, along with a Disentanglement Classifier that routes inputs appropriately to preserve unrelated knowledge. Extensive experiments across various benchmarks and MLLMs demonstrate that LDKE achieves superior performance in propagating edits to related contexts while maintaining high locality.

</details>


### [225] [Internal Representation, Not Clinical Knowledge: Where Apparent LLM Triage Failures Originate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29889)
*David Fraile Navarro, Berardino Como, Jialei Sheng, Soundariya Ananthan, Shlomo Berkovsky*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (36.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析发现，LLM在临床分诊任务中多选题格式下的高漏诊率并非源于模型丢失了临床知识表征，而是输出格式（多选题 vs 自由文本）导致决策token上的医学特征"沉默"，格式特征而非医学特征驱动了决策logits。行为实验进一步证实该差距主要由"差一级"的邻近选择而非知识失败导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床分诊基准报告消费者LLM在约束多选题格式下存在高漏诊率，但同一病例在自由文本格式下表现不同。作者希望探究这种差异的根源：是输出格式改变了模型的临床知识表征，还是仅改变了从已保存表征到答案的映射？

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析Gemma 3 4B/12B IT和Qwen3-8B模型在两种输出格式下的内部表征。采用三种独立方法验证：自然语言自编码器口头化、决策token logit归因、以及top特征表征分析。同时进行行为实验，包括结构化/自然语言输入下的多选题惩罚反转、选项顺序随机化消除位置偏差等。

Result: 三种独立方法一致发现：相同的医学特征在两种格式下都会在共享临床叙述上激活，但在多选题决策token处"沉默"；驱动决策logits的是支架和格式特征，而非医学特征。行为上，多选题惩罚在结构化输入和自然语言输入下均反转，选项顺序随机化排除了位置偏差，差距主要由"差一级"的决策（模型选择了与正确答案相邻的严重程度字母）而非知识失败主导。

Conclusion: LLM在临床分诊多选题中的失败根源在于输出格式而非临床知识表征。模型内部保留了正确的临床理解，但多选题的格式约束阻碍了其正确表达。这提示评估LLM时应谨慎对待格式带来的偏差，并建议改进多选题评估设计以更准确地反映模型真实能力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM在临床分诊任务中因输出格式（多选题 vs 自由文本）导致的性能差异，揭示模型内部表征并未丢失临床知识，而是输出格式限制了正确映射。这属于多模态/语言模型的事实性与可靠性问题，与"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination）子主题高度相关，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM clinical triage output format, not on user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: LLM reliability, clinical triage, output format bias, sparse autoencoder, not user priority

Abstract: Patient-voiced clinical-triage benchmarks report high under-triage rates for consumer LLMs for constrained multiple-choice output, yet the same cases score differently with free-text. We ask whether output format changes the model's \emph{clinical representation} or only the mapping from a preserved representation to an answer. Using sparse-autoencoder (SAE) features in Gemma 3 4B/12B IT and Qwen3-8B, we find the same medical features fire on the shared clinical narrative under both formats but go {silent} at the multiple-choice decision token in all the cases at every model. Three independent methods (natural-language autoencoder verbalization, decision-token logit attribution, and top-feature characterization) agree that scaffold and format features, but not medical features, drive the decision logits. Behaviorally, the multiple-choice penalty inverts under both structured and natural-language input, option-order shuffle rules out positional bias, and the gap is dominated by off-by-one decision (the model picks an adjacent acuity letter to the gold answer) rather than knowledge failure. Thus, the failure originates in the output format and not in the clinical representation.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_rl_alignment'></div>

### [226] [Representation Alignment Rests on Linear Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28870)
*Kiril Bangachev, Guy Bresler, Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (49.46) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过信号-偏置-噪声三部分统计框架研究柏拉图表征假说（PRH），提出跨模型表征对齐源于对象-属性关系的线性编码（线性表征假说LRH），并通过稀疏自编码器提取线性特征验证了稀疏表征比稠密表征具有更强的跨模态对齐能力。同时发现中心化与归一化可缓解模型偏置差异，而数据稀缺性是表征噪声的主要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为什么不同架构、不同训练方式下训练的神经网络模型会趋向于学习到相似的表征（Platonic Representation Hypothesis），并探究其背后的统计机制。

Method: 提出信号-偏置-噪声三部分统计框架：1）信号部分：利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）从模型中提取线性对象-属性特征，验证LRH对PRH的解释力；2）偏置部分：通过中心化和归一化操作缓解不同模型间的隐式偏置差异；3）噪声部分：分析词频与对齐程度的相关性，揭示数据稀缺性驱动的表征噪声。综合三部分提出一个精炼线性表征假说的统计模型。

Result: 稀疏表征比稠密表征表现出更强的跨模态对齐；中心化和归一化一致地提升了跨模型对齐；词频与对齐程度之间存在强且一致的正相关关系，验证了数据稀缺性驱动表征噪声。

Conclusion: 柏拉图表征假说可以通过线性表征假说得到解释，跨模型对齐源于对象-属性关系的线性编码。模型偏置可通过简单预处理缓解，而数据稀缺性是表征噪声的主要来源。该工作为理解现代AI架构中表征对齐现象提供了统一的统计框架。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态/跨模型表征对齐（Platonic Representation Hypothesis），属于多模态对齐范畴，与 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）直接相关，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Strong theoretical contribution to representation alignment but lacks direct application to user's priority generative tasks. Signals: Linear Representation Hypothesis, sparse vs dense alignment, signal-bias-noise framework, cross-modal alignment theory

Abstract: We investigate the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) through a tripartite statistical framework of representations: signal, bias, and noise. {1) Signal:} We propose that Platonic alignment arises from the universal relationship between objects and attributes, which is encoded linearly in representations according to the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH). We provide evidence that LRH helps explain PRH by extracting linear object-attribute features with sparse autoencoders and showing that these sparse representations often exhibit stronger cross-modal alignment than their dense counterparts. {2) Bias:} Models have different implicit biases due to the diverse architectures and training procedures used. We show that this difference can be partially mitigated. Centering and normalization consistently improve cross-model alignment. {3) Noise:} Finite-sample training leads to noise in representations. We provide evidence that representational noise is driven by data scarcity by revealing a strong and consistent positive correlation between word frequency and alignment in LLMs and text embedding models. Synthesizing signal, bias, and noise, we propose a statistical model that refines the Linear Representation Hypothesis and explains further phenomena related to the alignment of representations emerging from diverse modern AI architectures.

</details>


### [227] [TRACER: Persistent Regularization for Robust Multimodal Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29380)
*Hesam Asadollahzadeh, Feng Liu, Christopher Leckie, Sarah M. Erfani*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (51.39) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 TRACER，一种基于加权移动平均（WMA）教师指导的多视角蒸馏与对比学习的正则化方法，用于多模态模型微调时保持分布外鲁棒性，理论分析和实验证明其优于标准 EMA 教师方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态预训练模型（如 CLIP）在下游任务微调时常常发生灾难性遗忘，导致分布外（OOD）鲁棒性显著下降。现有正则化方法（如自蒸馏、EMA 教师）虽有一定效果，但缺乏理论理解，且标准 EMA 教师存在坍缩问题。本文旨在从理论上分析多模态对比微调的正则化策略，并设计更鲁棒的微调方法。

Method: 首先建立多模态对比微调的理论框架，推导出各策略的闭式解和几何分解，证明自蒸馏优于其他正则化方法。然后揭示标准 EMA 教师存在坍缩问题，证明加权移动平均（WMA）教师能在有限时间窗口内保持持久正则化力，在任务子空间实现无偏收敛并保留正交知识。基于此提出 TRACER，结合对比学习与 WMA 引导的多视角蒸馏。

Result: 在 CLIP 微调实验中，TRACER 在三种骨干架构上均取得一致的 OOD 准确率和校准度提升，全面的消融实验验证了方法的原理性和对超参数的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TRACER 通过 WMA 教师引导的多视角蒸馏有效缓解了多模态微调中的灾难性遗忘问题，在保持分布内性能的同时显著提升 OOD 鲁棒性，为多模态模型鲁棒微调提供了理论指导和实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态对比微调中的鲁棒性正则化方法，属于多模态模型对齐与微调优化范畴，与 canonical 子主题 "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）高度相关，因此复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Strong theoretical contribution to multimodal finetuning robustness, but not aligned with user's priority subtopics on generation or efficiency. Signals: multimodal understanding, finetuning robustness, theoretical framework, OOD generalization, contrastive learning

Abstract: Mainstream strategies for finetuning pretrained multimodal models often degrade out-of-distribution (OOD) robustness, a phenomenon known as catastrophic forgetting. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework for multimodal contrastive finetuning, yielding closed-form solutions and a geometric decomposition for each strategy. This framework shows that self-distillation is more effective than other regularization approaches to retain the knowledge of the pretrained model. Our analysis reveals a largely overlooked limitation: standard Exponential Moving Average (EMA) teachers, widely used in robust finetuning, suffer from collapse. To solve this, we prove that a Weighted Moving Average (WMA) teacher maintains a persistent regularizing force over finite horizons and yields bias-free convergence in the task subspace while preserving orthogonal knowledge. These insights motivate **TRACER** (**T**rajectory-**R**obust **A**nchoring for **C**ontrastive **E**ncoder **R**egularization), which combines contrastive learning with WMA-guided multi-perspective distillation. Extensive experiments on CLIP finetuning demonstrate consistent OOD accuracy and calibration gains across three backbone architectures, and comprehensive ablations confirm that TRACER is both principled and robust to hyperparameter choices. Code is available at [ ]( ).

</details>


### [228] [On Asymmetric Optimization of Reasoning and Perception in Vision-Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29496)
*Xueqing Wu, Yu-Chi Lin, Kai-Wei Chang, Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (61.73) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过两个合成任务构建诊断框架，系统分析了VLM后训练中感知与推理的不对称优化问题：SFT下源于CoT监督中感知token数量少导致训练信号弱，RL下源于奖励耦合使推理信号主导感知信号。提出动态重加权损失和感知感知奖励两种干预方法，分别提升端到端性能18.2和6.0个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练显著提升了前沿VLM的推理能力，但对感知能力的提升相对有限，形成了端到端视觉推理的瓶颈。现有研究缺乏对这一不对称性的系统诊断和理解。

Method: 设计两个合成任务（感知与推理可解耦），构建诊断框架分析SFT和RL两种后训练范式下的感知-推理不对称性。SFT下通过token级损失分析发现感知token占比低导致信号弱，提出动态重加权损失；RL下通过奖励分解发现奖励耦合问题，提出引入感知感知奖励或可靠代理奖励。

Result: 动态重加权损失提升端到端性能最高18.2个点；感知感知奖励提升准确率最高6.0个点；即使没有真实感知奖励，代理奖励也能带来3.2个点的提升。

Conclusion: VLM后训练中存在系统性的感知-推理不对称优化问题，SFT和RL的成因不同，但均可通过针对性干预缓解。这为平衡多模态模型的能力发展提供了具体指导。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM后训练中感知与推理的不对称优化问题，涉及SFT和RL两种训练范式下的信号不平衡与奖励耦合，属于多模态模型的对齐与优化范畴，与[多模态强化学习与对齐]（multimodal_rl_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Diagnoses and mitigates a critical asymmetry in VLM post-training that directly impacts multimodal understanding generation, a high-weight direction. Signals: VLM post-training optimization, perception-reasoning asymmetry, dynamic loss reweighting, perception-aware reward, synthetic diagnostic framework

Abstract: Post-training has greatly improved reasoning in frontier vision-language models, yet its gains for perception remain comparatively limited, creating a bottleneck for end-to-end visual reasoning. To investigate this gap, we introduce a controlled diagnostic framework with two synthetic tasks that disentangle perception from reasoning. Our analysis reveals a consistent perception-reasoning asymmetry: posttraining improves reasoning more substantially than perception, though the underlying mechanism differs by training paradigm. For supervised fine-tuning (SFT), this asymmetry stems from token imbalance in chain-of-thought supervision, where perception occupies fewer tokens and thus receives a weaker training signal. Dynamically reweighting the loss mitigates this imbalance and boosts end-to-end performance by up to 18.2. For reinforcement learning (RL), the asymmetry instead arises from reward coupling: outcome rewards correlate more strongly with reasoning than with perception, weakening the signal for perception learning. Adding a perception-aware reward alleviates the imbalance and improves end-to-end accuracy by up to 6.0; even without groundtruth perception rewards, a reliable surrogate reward provide useful signal, yielding gains of 3.2 points. Together, our results comprehensively diagnose asymmetric optimization and suggest concrete interventions to balance perception and reasoning.

</details>


### [229] [OVA-IB: One vs All Information Bottleneck for Multi-Modal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29900)
*Tianchao Li, Shujian Yu, Xinrui Zu, Zhaolong Wei, Jeremy Gummeson, Jack C.P. Cheng, Robert Jenssen*

Main category: cs.IT, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (51.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 OVA-IB，一个基于信息瓶颈原理的多模态对齐框架。通过 One-vs-All 视角，将每个模态相对于其余模态进行信息压缩与保留，优化可处理的下界与上界正则化项，在分类、回归、模态无关评估和跨模态检索任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比学习方法（如 CLIP）仅支持两两模态对齐，无法显式建模多模态间的高阶依赖关系。近期方法虽尝试从统计或几何角度解决任意模态对齐问题，但缺乏一个原则性的准则来定义每个模态应保留或压缩哪些信息。

Method: 基于信息瓶颈（IB）原理，提出 One-vs-All 视角：每个模态应保留可从其余模态预测的信息（充分性），同时压缩不被其余模态支持的模态特有信息（最小性）。优化目标包括：1）基于对比学习的充分性下界（与 Dual Total Correlation 目标关联）；2）无参数的几何感知投影分数；3）通过其余模态诱导的表征分布来约束每个表征对其自身输入的依赖，作为最小性的可处理上界正则化项。

Result: 在分类、回归、模态无关评估和跨模态检索等多个基准上，OVA-IB 展现出强且鲁棒的性能，优于现有的多模态对齐方法。

Conclusion: OVA-IB 为任意数量模态的对齐提供了基于信息瓶颈原则的理论框架，通过 One-vs-All 视角统一了充分性与最小性目标，实验验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于信息瓶颈（IB）的多模态对齐框架 OVA-IB，核心解决任意数量模态间的对齐问题，属于多模态对齐方法。现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）涵盖多模态对齐，虽然本文不涉及 RLHF/DPO，但"多模态对齐"是该子主题的核心内容之一，因此合理复用。

Importance reason: Proposes a principled multi-modal alignment framework but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: Information Bottleneck for multi-modal alignment, One-vs-All contrastive learning, strong performance on classification and retrieval, no video or generation experiments, theoretical contribution but limited practical overlap

Abstract: Contrastive learning is effective for aligning paired views or modalities, but alignment beyond two modalities remains non-trivial and comparatively underexplored. Pairwise CLIP-style losses decompose multi-modal alignment into independent two-way comparisons and therefore do not explicitly model higher-order dependencies among multiple modalities. Recent beyond-pairwise objectives approach this problem from statistical or geometric perspectives, but arbitrary-modality alignment still lacks a principled criterion for defining what each modality should preserve and compress relative to the others. We revisit arbitrary-modality alignment through the Information Bottleneck principle. In multi-modal learning, sufficiency should preserve information predictable from the remaining modalities, while minimality should compress modality-specific information not supported by them. This naturally leads to a One-vs-All view, where each modality is characterized with respect to the remaining modalities. We propose OVA-IB, an Information Bottleneck framework for arbitrary-modality alignment. OVA-IB optimizes a tractable One-vs-All contrastive lower bound for sufficiency connected to a Dual Total Correlation-style objective, uses a parameter-free geometry-aware projection score, and derives a tractable upper-bound regularizer for minimality by bounding each representation's dependence on its own input with representation distributions induced by the remaining modalities. Experiments on classification, regression, modality-agnostic evaluation, and cross-modal retrieval benchmarks demonstrate strong and robust performance.

</details>

### [230] [Tailoring the Curriculum: Student-Centered Reasoning Distillation via Dynamic Data-Model Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29229)
*Jiahao Huang, Fei Cheng, Junfeng Jiang, Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出数据-模型兼容性（DMC）指标，用于评估数据集对特定学生模型进行推理蒸馏的适配程度，并基于DMC进行动态数据选择以提升推理蒸馏性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理蒸馏的成功高度依赖于训练数据与学生模型之间的匹配程度，但现有方法缺乏一个统一的指标来量化这种适配性，导致数据选择缺乏依据。

Method: 提出数据-模型兼容性（DMC）指标，联合考虑数据质量、相对难度和学生模型能力三个维度。通过DMC与推理蒸馏性能的相关性验证其有效性，并基于DMC作为数据选择标准进行静态和动态数据选择实验。

Result: DMC与推理蒸馏性能呈现强相关性；基于DMC的数据选择能显著提升推理蒸馏效果；动态数据选择（随训练过程更新DMC）进一步提升了性能。

Conclusion: DMC是一个有效的推理蒸馏数据适配性评估指标，动态数据选择策略能更好地适应学生模型能力的变化，为推理蒸馏中的数据选择提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究推理蒸馏（reasoning distillation），通过数据-模型兼容性（DMC）指标优化小模型的推理能力，属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）范畴下的推理能力迁移与蒸馏方法，与现有 canonical 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on reasoning distillation for LLMs, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: reasoning distillation, data-model compatibility, dynamic data selection, student-centered curriculum, cs.AI

Abstract: Reasoning distillation transfers complex reasoning abilities from large language models (LLMs) to smaller ones, yet its success depends on how well the training data align with the student model. This paper introduces the Data-Model Compatibility (DMC) metric, which can be used to assess the suitability of a dataset for reasoning distillation on a student model. DMC provides an assessment by jointly considering data quality, relative difficulty, and student capability. We validated the effectiveness of DMC from two perspectives: (1) DMC exhibits a strong correlation with reasoning distillation performance; and (2) using DMC as the criterion for data selection leads to improved reasoning distillation performance. Both findings are consistently demonstrated across multiple student models and tasks. Moreover, since the DMC of each dataset dynamically changes during training, our experiments demonstrate that dynamically selecting datasets based on DMC can further enhance performance.

</details>


### [231] [DenseSteer: Steering Small Language Models towards Dense Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29247)
*Yang Ouyang, Shuhang Lin, Jung-Eun Kim*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (44.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 DenseSteer，一种无需训练的推理时干预框架，通过将小语言模型的内部表征向"密集推理"模式引导，提升其在数学推理任务上的准确率，实验表明该方法在不增加 token 级负对数似然的情况下持续改善推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型展现出强大的思维链推理能力，但小模型（≤3B 参数）在多步推理任务上表现显著不足。作者通过分析 Qwen-2.5 模型族在数学推理基准上的表现，发现更熟练的推理与更少的推理步骤但更高的每步信息密度相关（称为"密集推理"），由此启发设计一种提升小模型推理能力的方法。

Method: DenseSteer 是一种无需训练的推理时干预框架。它通过分析模型内部表征，识别与密集推理模式相关的方向，然后在推理过程中将模型的隐藏状态向该方向引导，从而让小模型生成更紧凑、信息密度更高的推理链，而不改变模型参数。

Result: 实验表明 DenseSteer 在多个数学推理基准上持续提升小模型的准确率，且不增加 token 级的负对数似然，验证了密集推理作为一种有效的数学问题求解结构模式。

Conclusion: 密集推理是一种有效的数学推理结构模式，DenseSteer 通过推理时表征引导可以显著提升小模型的数学推理能力，为无需微调即可增强小模型推理提供了一种新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究小语言模型在数学推理任务中的推理能力提升，提出 DenseSteer 框架通过推理时干预增强密集推理模式。数学推理属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）范畴下的核心主题，该 [CANONICAL] 子主题明确涵盖数学/代码/科学推理，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: Focuses on small language model math reasoning, which is outside the user's priority subtopics of video/image generation and world models. Signals: small language models, math reasoning, inference-time intervention, dense reasoning, no training required

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning abilities, while smaller models (<= 3B parameters) significantly underperform on multi-step reasoning tasks. Based on empirical analyses of the Qwen-2.5 model family on math reasoning benchmarks, we find that more proficient reasoning is associated with fewer reasoning steps but higher information density per step, a property we term Dense Reasoning. Motivated by this observation, we propose DenseSteer, a training-free inference-time steering framework that enhances small-model reasoning by modulating internal representations toward dense reasoning patterns. Experiments show that our method yields consistent accuracy improvements without increasing token-level Negative Log-Likelihood, highlighting dense reasoning as an effective structural approach to mathematical problem solving.

</details>


### [232] [Thoughts-as-Planning: Latent World Models for Chain-of-Thoughts Optimization via Reinforcement Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28842)
*Dong Liu, Yanxuan Yu, Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (82.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Thoughts-as-Planning 框架，将 LLM 推理链优化建模为潜在语义空间上的序列决策过程，通过学习潜在世界模型模拟推理链编辑对下游输出的影响，结合梯度下降或强化学习进行规划，支持 token、片段和指令多尺度抽象编辑，在语言理解与生成任务上优于现有推理链调优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理链调优方法依赖黑盒启发式或无梯度搜索，缺乏可解释性、泛化能力和样本效率。作者希望将推理链优化形式化为一个可解释、可规划的序列决策过程。

Method: 将 LLM 建模为部分可观测环境，学习一个潜在世界模型来模拟推理链编辑对下游输出的影响；构建保持邻近性的嵌入空间编码推理链-响应动态；支持 token、片段和指令级别的多尺度抽象编辑，通过梯度下降或强化学习进行统一规划。

Result: 在语言理解与生成任务上，Thoughts-as-Planning 在效率、鲁棒性和泛化性方面均优于现有最先进的推理链调优基线方法，并具有结构化规划轨迹带来的可解释性。

Conclusion: 将推理链优化视为潜在空间中的规划问题是一种有效且可解释的范式，潜在世界模型能够捕捉推理编辑的因果效应，多尺度抽象支持灵活编辑，为 LLM 推理优化提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Thoughts-as-Planning 框架，将推理链优化形式化为潜在语义空间上的序列决策过程，属于思维链（Chain-of-Thought）推理优化方法，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel latent world model for chain-of-thought optimization, aligning with user's interest in efficient inference and world models. Signals: world model, chain-of-thought optimization, reinforcement planning, efficient inference, latent semantic space

Abstract: The success of large language models (LLMs) across diverse NLP tasks has elevated the importance of reasoning chain optimization as a critical step in aligning model behavior with task objectives. Existing reasoning chain tuning methods often rely on black-box heuristics or gradient-free search, which lack interpretability, generalization, and sample efficiency. In this work, we introduce \textbf{Thoughts-as-Planning}, a novel framework that formalizes reasoning chain optimization as a sequential decision-making process over a latent semantic space. We model the LLM as a partially observable environment and learn a latent world model that simulates the effect of reasoning chain edits on downstream outputs. A proximity-preserving embedding space is constructed to encode reasoning chain-response dynamics, enabling planning via gradient descent or reinforcement learning. Our method supports multi-scale abstraction, allowing reasoning chain edits at token, segment, and instruction levels to be integrated into a unified planner. Through extensive experiments on language understanding and generation tasks, we demonstrate that Thoughts-as-Planning outperforms state-of-the-art reasoning chain tuning baselines in efficiency, robustness, and generalization, while offering interpretability through its structured planning trajectory. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [233] [TRACE: Toulmin-based Reasoning Assessment through Constructive Elements for LLM CoT Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29656)
*Yundong Kim, Heyoung Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (49.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TRACE 是一种基于 Toulmin 论证理论和 Flavell 元认知框架的 CoT 推理过程评估指标，通过分析论证结构而非最终答案来评价 LLM 的推理质量，在 26.3K 样本上验证与基准准确率高度相关（r=0.74），并可作为有效的强化学习奖励信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 评估指标多依赖最终答案准确性或表面统计特征，无法深入检查推理过程本身的质量。对于开放式的 CoT 输出，缺乏能够评估推理逻辑结构的方法。

Method: 将 Toulmin 论证模型（主张、数据、理由、限定词、反驳、支撑）与 Flavell 元认知框架结合，构建评估 CoT 推理结构的指标 TRACE，分析论证的完整性和逻辑合理性。

Result: 在 26.3K QA 样本、7 个推理模型上的实验表明，TRACE 与基准准确率高度相关（r=0.74）；作为强化学习奖励信号时，优于仅基于准确率的基线方法。

Conclusion: 逻辑合理的推理过程能够带来更高质量的答案，TRACE 可作为评估开放式 LLM 输出的有效补充指标。

Classification reason: 论文提出 TRACE 指标，基于 Toulmin 论证理论评估 LLM 的 Chain-of-Thought 推理过程质量，属于推理与思维链评估方向，与 canonical subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on CoT evaluation metrics, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: CoT evaluation, reasoning assessment, Toulmin argumentation, reinforcement learning reward, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Evaluating open-ended outputs from large language models (LLMs) remains challenging due to the absence of ground truth. Existing metrics rely on final-answer accuracy or surface-level statistics, leaving the reasoning process itself unexamined. We introduce TRACE (Toulmin-based Reasoning Assessment through Constructive Elements), a metric that analyzes Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning processes. Rather than judging outcomes, TRACE inspects how arguments are constructed by integrating Toulmin's argumentation theory with Flavell's metacognitive framework to assess reasoning structure. Experiments on 26.3K QA samples across 7 reasoning models show strong correlation with benchmark accuracy (r=0.74). Furthermore, TRACE is effective as a reinforcement learning reward signal, outperforming accuracy-only baselines. Together, these results indicate that logically sound reasoning leads to higher-quality answers. TRACE thus serves as a complementary metric for evaluating open-ended outputs. Code is available at .

</details>


### [234] [AgentCVR: Active Multi-Agent Cross-Video Reasoning via Script-Simulated Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29643)
*Yilun Qiu, Jiahe Wang, Cilin Yan, Jiayin Cai, Xiaolong Jiang, Yao Hu, Chun Yuan*

Main category: cs.MA, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (77.18) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AgentCVR 提出一种多智能体框架，将跨视频推理建模为主动证据获取任务，通过主智能体协调视觉和音频智能体进行迭代式证据提取，并引入脚本模拟强化学习（Script-Simulated RL）实现高效策略优化，在跨视频推理基准上超越单次推理基线，性能媲美闭源系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在处理跨视频推理（CVR）任务时，通常采用单次编码多个视频到共享压缩上下文的方式，容易丢失稀有但关键的证据。因此需要一种能够主动、迭代地获取跨视频证据的推理框架。

Method: 提出 AgentCVR 多智能体框架，包含一个 Master Agent 协调多个 Visual Agent 和 Audio Agent 进行迭代式证据提取；同时提出 Script-Simulated RL，利用 LLM 生成的语义脚本和轻量文本模拟器替代昂贵的多模态推理进行在线策略优化，实现高效训练。

Result: 在综合 CVR 基准上，AgentCVR 超越单次推理基线，在复杂跨视频对齐和定位任务上达到与最先进闭源系统相当的性能。

Conclusion: AgentCVR 验证了多智能体主动推理框架在跨视频推理任务中的有效性，Script-Simulated RL 提供了一种高效训练策略，为复杂多模态推理场景提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出跨视频推理（Cross-Video Reasoning）框架，涉及多模态证据检索、对齐与聚合，属于多模态推理范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）子主题。

Importance reason: Novel multi-agent RL for cross-video reasoning, but not aligned with top priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: multi-agent framework, cross-video reasoning, script-simulated RL, multimodal understanding, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Cross-Video Reasoning (CVR) has emerged as a critical frontier in multimodal intelligence, requiring models to retrieve, align, and aggregate evidence distributed across multiple videos. Current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often struggle with CVR, as simple single-pass strategies encode multiple videos into a shared compressed context, potentially obscuring rare but critical evidence. In this paper, we propose AgentCVR, a multi-agent framework that treats CVR as an active evidence-acquisition task. AgentCVR employs a Master Agent to iteratively coordinate specialized Visual and Audio Agents for targeted evidence extraction. To ensure efficient training, we introduce Script-Simulated RL, which optimizes the agent's policy with LLM-generated semantic scripts and a lightweight text-based simulator, bypassing costly multimodal inference during online exploration. Experimental results on a comprehensive CVR benchmark show that AgentCVR outperforms single-pass baselines and achieves comparable performance to state-of-the-art closed-source systems, particularly in complex cross-video alignment and localization. To ensure reproducibility, our code is available at .

</details>


### [235] [VisualThink-VLA: Visual Intermediate Reasoning for Effective and Low-Latency Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30011)
*Mingjian Gao, Wenqiao Zhang, Yuqian Yuan, Yang Dai, Binhe Yu, Zheqi Lv, Haoyu Zheng, Jiaqi Zhu, Zhiqi Ge, Zixuan Wan, Siliang Tang, Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (61.2) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VisualThink-VLA 提出了一种视觉中间推理框架，用紧凑的视觉证据（visual evidence）替代文本思维链，引导VLA策略进行动作预测，在保持高成功率的同时将推理延迟从数秒降至亚秒级（如BridgeData V2上延迟从8.377s降至0.367s，加速22.8倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略引入显式中间推理（如文本CoT）存在两个问题：1）文本推理中与动作预测无关或弱相关的信息会干扰预测；2）自回归文本解码带来过高延迟，无法满足实时闭环控制需求。因此需要一种更高效、低延迟的中间推理方式。

Method: 提出VisualThink-VLA框架，核心思想是用视觉中间推理替代文本CoT：1）设计紧凑的视觉证据（visual evidence）接口，保留空间精度同时避免文本解码开销；2）采用选择性路由机制（selective routing）学习视觉证据token，实现低延迟推理并保持高容量专业化；3）构建VisualEvidence-Kit资源，包含VisualEvidence-Agent生成的754.7k条VLA指令数据集VisualEvidence-Set，用于路由监督和反事实忠实性测试。

Result: 在多个基准和真实机器人评估中，VisualThink-VLA在大多数基准上取得最高成功率，同时将推理增强基线的多秒延迟降至亚秒级。例如在BridgeData V2上，步延迟从ECoT的8.377s降至0.367s，实现22.8倍加速。

Conclusion: 视觉中间推理是VLA策略中文本CoT的有效替代方案，能够在保持高任务成功率的同时大幅降低推理延迟，为具身智能的实时闭环控制提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出视觉中间推理框架（VisualThink-VLA），通过视觉证据（visual evidence）而非文本思维链来增强VLA策略的推理能力，属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）范畴，且该方向为[CANONICAL]条目。

Importance reason: Proposes visual intermediate reasoning to drastically reduce VLA latency, aligning with efficient inference priority. Signals: efficient inference, low-latency VLA, visual reasoning, multimodal understanding, action policy

Abstract: Recent work has begun to equip vision-language-action (VLA) policies with explicit intermediate reasoning. In embodied control, however, textual chain-of-thought is a poor fit: irrelevant or weakly textual information can interfere with action prediction, while autoregressive text decoding adds too much latency for real-time closed-loop execution. We present VISUALTHINK-VLA, a visual intermediate-reasoning framework for accurate, low-latency VLA policies. Our bootstrapping philosophy is to guide action with effective visual thinking: VISUALTHINK-VLA bootstraps action prediction through a compact visual-evidence interface that preserves spatial precision while avoiding decoding overhead. Besides, to further improve performance and efficiency, VISUALTHINK-VLA adopts a tailored selective routing mechanism to learn the visual evidence tokens, enabling low-latency inference while preserving high-capacity specialization. We also introduce VisualEvidence-Kit, a supervision-and-audit resource centered on a VisualEvidence-Agent that constructs a 754.7k VLA instructions VisualEvidence-Set for route supervision and counterfactual faithfulness tests. Across multiple benchmarks and real-robot evaluation, VISUALTHINK-VLA achieves the highest success rate on most benchmarks while reducing the multi-second latency of reasoning-augmented baselines to the sub-second regime. For example, on BridgeData V2, it reduces step latency from 8.377,s with ECoT to 0.367,s, achieving a 22.8 times speedup.

</details>


### [236] [CRITIC-R1: Learning Structured Critics for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29886)
*Wenhan Xiao, Ziwei Zhang, Chuanyue Yu, Xingcheng Fu, Qingyun Sun, Runhua Xu, Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CRITIC-R1 提出一个结构化批评框架，将 RAG 的批评过程建模为显式错误诊断问题，使用 GRPO 强化学习训练批评模型，通过保守判断对齐（CJA）和诊断质量对齐（DQA）两个奖励函数缓解过度干预问题，在五个 QA 基准上持续提升 RAG 答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 方法仍存在幻觉和细微推理错误，引入的外部批评者往往提供粗粒度、弱结构化的反馈，且存在过度干预问题，导致噪声大、不可靠的修正。

Method: 将 RAG 批评建模为多维度错误诊断问题（包括 verdict、error location、reasoning analysis、fix generation），设计 CJA 奖励函数鼓励校准的高层判断以缓解过度干预，DQA 奖励函数通过门控奖励改进细粒度诊断反馈，使用 GRPO 强化学习结合外部 LLM 教师模型的过程级监督训练批评模型。

Result: 在五个 QA 基准上，CRITIC-R1 持续优于强 RAG 基线方法，有效提升了答案质量。

Conclusion: 结构化批评框架结合强化学习能够有效提升 RAG 系统的纠错能力，缓解过度干预问题，为 RAG 的可靠推理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 RAG 中的结构化批评框架，通过强化学习学习显式错误诊断（包括判断、定位、推理分析和修复生成），属于多模态/文本推理与思维链范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" (multimodal_reasoning_cot)。

Importance reason: Focuses on RAG critique for QA, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: RAG critique, structured error diagnosis, GRPO reinforcement learning, conservative judgment alignment, QA benchmarks

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves knowledge-intensive question answering by incorporating external evidence. However, existing RAG methods still suffer from hallucinations and subtle reasoning errors. Recent studies introduce external critics to refine RAG outputs, yet they often provide coarse-grained and weakly structured feedback, exhibit over-aggressive intervention, and lead to noisy and unreliable refinement, limiting their effectiveness for correction. To tackle these issues, we propose CRITIC-R1, a structured critic framework that formulates and learns RAG critique as an explicit error diagnosis problem using reinforcement learning (RL). Our framework categorizes common RAG errors into multiple diagnostic dimensions, including verdict, error location, reasoning analysis, and fix generation. To learn these capabilities, we design two reward functions: Conservative Judgement Alignment (CJA) first encourages calibrated high-level judgements while mitigating the over-aggressive phenomenon, whereas Diagnostic Quality Alignment (DQA) further improves fine-grained diagnostic feedback through gated rewards. We train the critic model using GRPO-based RL with process-level supervision collected from external LLM teacher models. Experiments across five QA benchmarks show that CRITIC-R1 consistently improves answer quality over strong RAG baselines. Our source code is available at

</details>

## 多模态检索 [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [HiKEY: Hierarchical Multimodal Retrieval for Open-Domain Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29606)
*Joongmin Shin, Gyuho Shim, Jeongbae Park, Jaehyung Seo, Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (51.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HiKEY 提出了一种基于层次化树结构的多模态检索框架，通过文档层次解析（DHP）构建逻辑异构图，采用粗到细的检索策略（全局路由+细粒度多模态融合），并利用混合结构-语义打包策略构建 token 高效的证据子图，在开放域文档问答中显著优于基于页面或文本块的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 RAG 的开放域文档问答面临两个关键瓶颈：路由失败（难以从海量文档中准确定位目标文档）和证据碎片化（难以在有限 token 预算内有机整合表格、图片等多模态证据）。基于扁平文本块或页面级图像的方法无法有效解决这些问题。

Method: HiKEY 包含三个核心组件：(1) 文档层次解析（DHP），将文档重建为逻辑异构图，显式编码父子关系；(2) 层次化粗到细检索策略，先通过全局路由利用层次索引快速剪枝搜索空间，再通过多模态融合策略进行细粒度段落排序；(3) 混合结构-语义打包策略，构建 token 高效的证据子图。

Result: 在 ODQA 基准上，HiKEY 相比基于页面和文本块的基线方法，检索召回率提升最高达 12.9%，端到端 QA 性能提升最高达 6.8%。

Conclusion: HiKEY 通过将文档层次结构作为一等检索信号，有效解决了开放域文档问答中的路由失败和证据碎片化问题，验证了层次化多模态检索在大规模工业级语料库上的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出层次化多模态检索框架用于开放域文档问答，核心是多模态文档检索。在 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下有 [CANONICAL] 的 multimodal_retrieval（多模态检索）子主题，完美匹配。同时 multimodal_generation 方向下也有同名的 multimodal_retrieval 子主题，因此也匹配该方向。

Importance reason: Strong retrieval framework for multimodal QA, but not aligned with user's priority on generative models or world models. Signals: multimodal retrieval, hierarchical tree, document QA, RAG, not generative

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for document-based Open-domain Question Answering (ODQA) on large-scale industrial corpora faces two critical bottlenecks: routing failure in locating the correct document and evidence fragmentation in integrating scattered information. Existing approaches relying on flat text chunks or page-level images inherently struggle to (i) precisely pinpoint the target document among thousands of candidates and (ii) organically connect multimodal evidence, such as tables and figures, within a limited token budget. To address these challenges, we propose HiKEY, a hierarchical tree-based multimodal retrieval framework that elevates document hierarchy to a first-class retrieval signal. Instead of simple chunking, HiKEY reconstructs a logical heterogeneous graph via Document Hierarchical Parsing (DHP), explicitly encoding parent-child relationships. Adopting a hierarchical coarse-to-fine strategy, the framework (1) performs global routing to rapidly prune the search space using hierarchical indexing, and (2) conducts fine-grained retrieval to rank sections by employing a multimodal fusion strategy that captures the most discriminative evidence. Finally, HiKEY assembles a token-efficient evidence subgraph via a hybrid structural-semantic packing strategy. Experiments on ODQA benchmarks demonstrate that HiKEY significantly outperforms page- and chunk-based baselines, improving retrieval recall by up to 12.9% and end-to-end QA performance by up to 6.8%.

</details>


### [238] [CogniVerse: Revolutionizing Multi-Modal Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Cognitive Reflection and Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29602)
*Xiang Fang, Wanlong Fang, Changshuo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 值得关注 (68.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CogniVerse 是一个受认知启发的多模态检索增强生成（MMRAG）框架，通过认知反思模块动态评估检索必要性、基于黎曼流形和信息几何的多模态检索模块，以及基于最优传输的层次化生成模块，显著提升了多模态问答的准确性和连贯性，同时降低了检索延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MMRAG 框架存在检索噪声大、跨模态语义对齐不足、缺乏自适应推理能力以及局部与全局上下文生成不连贯等关键问题。受人类认知推理启发，作者希望构建一个更精确、更连贯的多模态检索增强生成框架。

Method: CogniVerse 包含三个协同模块：(1) 认知反思模块（Cognitive Reflection Module）——动态评估检索必要性并过滤相关多模态内容，减少噪声和计算开销；(2) 多模态检索模块（Multi-modal Retrieval Module）——在黎曼流形上使用信息几何对齐嵌入，并通过谱图理论精炼知识图谱，确保精确连贯的检索；(3) 层次化生成模块（Hierarchical Generation Module）——采用基于最优传输的损失函数，平衡 token 级准确性与全局语义连贯性。

Result: 大量实验表明，CogniVerse 在准确性和连贯性上显著优于现有最先进系统，同时降低了检索延迟。

Conclusion: CogniVerse 通过认知启发的数学化方法有效解决了 MMRAG 中的检索噪声、语义对齐和生成不连贯等核心挑战，为多模态检索增强生成提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CogniVerse，一个多模态检索增强生成（MMRAG）框架，核心贡献在于多模态检索（Riemannian 流形对齐、知识图谱精炼）和检索增强生成。该工作与 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）高度匹配，同时涉及多模态理解与生成一体化，因此也匹配 multimodal_generation 方向下的 multimodal_retrieval 子主题。

Importance reason: Novel MMRAG framework with cognitive reflection and geometric reasoning, but not aligned with priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: MMRAG, cognitive reflection, Riemannian manifold, optimal transport, retrieval latency reduction

Abstract: Multi-modal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MMRAG) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for enhancing Multimodal Large Language Models in knowledge-intensive question answering by integrating external visual, textual, and structural knowledge. However, existing MMRAG frameworks suffer from critical limitations, including noisy and irrelevant retrieval, cross-modal semantic misalignment, lack of adaptive reasoning, and incoherent generation across local and global contexts. We introduce \textbf{CogniVerse}, a novel MMRAG framework that addresses these challenges through a cognitive-inspired, mathematically rigorous approach. Drawing from human-like reasoning, CogniVerse integrates three synergistic components: (1) a Cognitive Reflection Module that dynamically assesses retrieval necessity and filters relevant multi-modal content, reducing noise and computational overhead; (2) a Multi-modal Retrieval Module that aligns embeddings in a Riemannian manifold using information geometry and refines knowledge graphs via spectral graph theory, ensuring precise and coherent retrieval; and (3) a Hierarchical Generation Module that employs an optimal transport-based loss to balance token-level accuracy and global semantic coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CogniVerse significantly outperforms state-of-the-art systems in both accuracy and coherence, while reducing retrieval latency.

</details>


### [239] [DocRetriever: A Plug-and-Play Framework for Multimodal Document Retrieval with Comprehensive Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30027)
*Ruofan Hu, Menghui Zhu, Jieming Zhu, Bo Chen, Shengyang Xu, Minjie Hong, Xiaoda Yang, Sashuai Zhou, Li Tang, Tao Jin, Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (51.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DocRetriever 是一个即插即用的多模态文档检索框架，通过布局感知的稀疏嵌入技术增强视觉检索，无需 OCR；同时引入基于推理增强示例和优化采样的可泛化重排序器，并构建了 MultiDocR 基准用于更严格的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态文档检索方法存在三个主要问题：(1) 稠密嵌入的粗粒度特性掩盖了显式语义，无法利用结构显著信息；(2) 监督重排序模型依赖领域特定训练数据，泛化能力受限；(3) 现有基准缺乏多样化的评估维度和全面的相关性标注。

Method: DocRetriever 包含三个核心组件：(1) 布局感知稀疏嵌入（Layout-Aware Sparse Embedding），通过捕捉文档的布局结构信息实现高效的混合编码，无需 OCR；(2) 可泛化重排序器（Generalizable Reranker），利用推理增强的演示示例（reasoning-augmented demonstrations）和优化采样策略，在少样本设置下提升重排序精度；(3) MultiDocR 基准，提供更全面的评估维度和相关性标注。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，DocRetriever 在文档检索任务上优于现有最先进方法，特别是在少样本和跨领域场景下表现出更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DocRetriever 通过布局感知稀疏嵌入和推理增强重排序器有效解决了多模态文档检索中的语义模糊和泛化瓶颈问题，MultiDocR 基准为更全面的评估提供了支持。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态文档检索（Multimodal Document Retrieval），属于多模态检索范畴。在 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下已有 [CANONICAL] 的 "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）子主题，且该子主题明确包含文档检索（document retrieval），因此直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on document retrieval, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like video/image generation or world models. Signals: multimodal document retrieval, layout-aware sparse embedding, reranker with reasoning-augmented demonstrations, MultiDocR benchmark, plug-and-play framework

Abstract: Multimodal documents contain diverse elements, such as tables, figures, and layouts, which can complicate retrieval tasks. While current approaches typically combine dense visual embedding models with supervised rerankers to achieve high-precision retrieval, they face inherent limitations. First, the coarse-grained nature of dense embeddings tends to obfuscate explicit semantics, failing to leverage structurally salient information. Second, supervised reranking models suffer from generalization bottlenecks, as their performance heavily relies on domain-specific training data. Furthermore, existing benchmarks often lack diverse assessment dimensions and comprehensive relevance annotations, limiting reliable evaluation. To address these challenges, we propose DocRetriever, a plug-and-play framework. It enhances visual retrieval via a layout-aware sparse embedding technique, enabling effective hybrid encoding without the overhead of optical character recognition (OCR). We also introduce a generalizable reranker that leverages reasoning-augmented demonstrations and optimized sampling to improve accuracy in few-shot settings. Finally, we construct a new benchmark, MultiDocR, to enable more rigorous evaluation. Experiments across diverse benchmarks validate DocRetriever's superiority over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [240] [Adapting Multilingual Embedding Models to Turkish via Cross-Lingual Tokenizer Surgery and Offline Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29992)
*M. Ali Bayram, Banu Diri, Savaş Yıldırım*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 embeddingmagibu-200m，一个面向土耳其语的句子嵌入模型。通过跨语言分词器手术（基于40语言语料库频率分析裁剪并扩充词汇表）和离线蒸馏（从预计算教师向量进行余弦相似度蒸馏），在单GPU上约4小时完成训练。模型在STSbTR上超越300M参数的教师模型，在TR-MTEB 26个任务上平均得分63.9%，以200M参数实现了有竞争力的成本-质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的土耳其语句子嵌入模型受限于BERT的512 token上下文窗口，且缺乏高效的多语言嵌入模型适配方法。全预训练成本高昂，需要大量数据和计算资源。作者希望以低成本、高效率的方式将多语言嵌入模型适配到土耳其语，同时支持更长的上下文窗口（8192 token）。

Method: 提出三阶段适配流水线：(1) 分词器手术——基于40语言语料库的频率分析，裁剪教师模型冗余token并融入多语言token，构建131,072词汇量的土耳其语优化多语言分词器；(2) 模型克隆——保留教师Transformer骨干权重，通过均值组合token映射初始化新词汇表的嵌入表；(3) 离线蒸馏——使用预计算的教师向量，通过余弦相似度目标在40语言平衡Wikipedia语料库上进行蒸馏，避免在线教师推理。

Result: 在STSbTR上Pearson/Spearman相关系数达77.55%/77.45%，超越300M参数的教师模型（73.84%/72.92%）。在TR-MTEB 26个任务上平均得分63.9%，在26个模型中排名第7。模型仅200M参数，比教师少33%，训练成本仅$5-$20，单GPU约4小时完成。

Conclusion: 本文验证了通过分词器手术和离线蒸馏可以高效地将多语言嵌入模型适配到低资源语言（土耳其语），在显著降低计算成本的同时获得优于教师模型的性能。该方法为其他低资源语言的嵌入模型适配提供了可复现的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向土耳其语的句子嵌入模型，用于语义搜索、聚类、分类和检索增强生成（RAG），属于多模态检索方向下的跨模态/文档检索范畴。论文中明确提到 retrieval-augmented generation 和 semantic search，与 multimodal_retrieval 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on Turkish-specific embedding model adaptation, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: Cross-lingual tokenizer surgery, Offline distillation, Turkish sentence embeddings, TR-MTEB benchmark, Efficient single-GPU training

Abstract: Sentence embeddings are a foundational component for semantic search, clustering, classification, and retrieval-augmented generation. This paper presents embeddingmagibu-200m, a Turkish-focused sentence embedding model that produces 768-dimensional L2-normalized vectors and supports an 8,192-token context window, far exceeding the 512-token limit of earlier BERT-based Turkish encoders. Instead of full pretraining, an efficient three-stage adaptation pipeline is introduced: (1) construct a Turkish-optimized multilingual tokenizer with a 131,072 vocabulary by pruning redundant tokens from the teacher's vocabulary and incorporating multilingual tokens via frequency analysis on a 40-language corpus, (2) clone a teacher embedding model while preserving transformer backbone weights and initializing a compatible embedding table for the new vocabulary via mean-composition token mapping, and (3) perform offline embedding distillation from precomputed teacher vectors using a cosine similarity objective over a balanced 40-language Wikipedia corpus. The resulting student model contains approximately 200M parameters and trains in roughly four hours on a single GPU by avoiding online teacher inference during training, at a total cost of $5-$20. Empirically, Pearson/Spearman correlations of 77.55%/77.45% are obtained on STSbTR, surpassing the 300M-parameter teacher model (73.84%/72.92%). On TR-MTEB (26 tasks), a mean score of 63.9% is achieved (7th out of 26 models), providing a competitive cost-quality trade-off with 33% fewer parameters than the teacher. To facilitate reproducibility and downstream use, all artifacts are released including model weights, tokenizer files, precomputed embedding datasets, and open-source cloning and distillation tooling.

</details>
