## 多模态评测与基准 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态评测、新基准、评测协议、自动评估。

### [241] [VideoFDB: Evaluating Full-Duplex Vision-Speech Capabilities in Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30256)
*Amrita Mazumdar, Seonwook Park, Rajarshi Roy, Nikhil Srihari, Shengze Wang, Yuhao Zhou, Julia Wang, Koki Nagano, Shalini De Mello*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.HC, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VideoFDB 是首个评估全双工视听对话智能体的基准，包含 237 个真实视频通话片段、11 种非语言对话动态，以及基于 LM-as-Judge 的评估框架。实验发现现有系统存在"字幕坍缩"和"视觉流忽略"等系统性失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然对话是全双工且视听融合的，但现有全双工基准仅评估语音，缺乏对视听联合交互能力的系统评测，阻碍了下一代多模态对话智能体的发展。

Method: 构建了包含 237 个双人对话片段的基准数据集，覆盖 11 种非语言对话动态（如点头、微笑、手势等）；提出感知-生成行为分离的分类体系；设计基于 LM-as-Judge 的评估框架，包含可解释的评估轴。

Result: 现有开放和闭源视觉-语音智能体存在系统性失败模式：字幕坍缩（将视觉信息压缩为文本描述后丢失细节）和视觉流忽略（未能持续利用视觉流进行联合视听理解）。级联式语音到虚拟人系统因架构限制无法产生全双工非语言线索。

Conclusion: VideoFDB 作为首个全双工 AV2AV 交互基准，为系统评估奠定了基础，有望加速下一代多模态对话智能体的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 VideoFDB，首个评估全双工视听对话智能体的基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。同时涉及 AIGC 方向下的对话智能体与生成工作流（aigc_agent_workflow），但主要贡献是评测基准。

Importance reason: Novel benchmark for full-duplex audiovisual conversation, but not aligned with user's priority subtopics on generation or efficiency. Signals: First full-duplex AV2AV benchmark, systematic failure modes identified, not autoregressive or diffusion-based generation, no efficiency focus, negative keywords absent

Abstract: Natural human conversation is full-duplex and audio-visual: people simultaneously speak and listen while continuously interpreting and producing nonverbal cues, such as nods, smiles, and gestures. To support successful human-agent interaction, agents must model full-duplex audiovisual conversation; however, existing full-duplex benchmarks evaluate only speech. In this work, we present VideoFDB, the first benchmark to evaluate full-duplex audio-visual-to-audio-visual (AV2AV) conversational agents. VideoFDB contributes (i) 237 dyadic clips spanning 11 nonverbal conversational dynamics from real-world video calls, (ii) a taxonomy separating perception from generation behaviors, and (iii) a rubric-based LM-as-judge evaluation framework with interpretable axes for assessing conversational quality with respect to nonverbal conversational dynamics. Across open- and closed-source vision-speech agents, we find systematic failure modes: captioning collapse and visual-stream ignorance, and we show that current systems exploit vision for explicit visual question answering but not for the streaming joint audiovisual grounding required in natural conversation. We further evaluate cascaded speech-to-avatar systems and find that their architecture fundamentally precludes the production of full-duplex nonverbal cues. As the first benchmark for full-duplex AV2AV interaction, VideoFDB establishes a foundation for systematic evaluation and, we hope, will accelerate the advancement and development of next-generation multimodal conversational agents.

</details>


### [242] [Embodied3DBench: Benchmarking Low-Level Embodied Spatial Intelligence of Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29074)
*Jiyao Zhang, Mingxu Zhang, Yitong Peng, Haoxuan Liu, Chenshuo Wang, Yuxing Long, Haoyang Huang, Dongjiang Li, Nan Duan, Hui Shen, Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (60.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Embodied3DBench 是一个面向具身 3D 环境的 VLM 低层空间智能评测基准，包含 6 个任务类别、12 个子类别和超过 21k 个 QA 对。评测 13 个 SOTA 模型发现，当前模型在高层空间推理上表现较好，但在交互导向感知（如抓取点预测、轨迹预测）上能力薄弱。为弥补这一差距，作者进一步合成了 130 万 QA 对的训练数据集，微调后显著提升了低层空间智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 评测基准多关注高层语义理解（如物体识别、场景描述），缺乏对具身 3D 环境中低层空间智能（如空间定位、抓取点预测、轨迹预测）的系统评估。作者希望填补这一空白，为具身智能体所需的交互感知能力提供标准化评测框架。

Method: 构建 Embodied3DBench 基准，包含 6 个任务类别：空间结构理解（Grounding、空间关系预测、多视图对应）和交互导向感知（Affordance 预测、抓取点预测、轨迹预测），共 12 个子类别、21k+ QA 对。基于 3D 资产和仿真环境自动生成数据。同时合成 130 万 QA 对的训练数据集用于微调。

Result: 评测 13 个 SOTA VLM 模型（包括 GPT-4o、Gemini、Qwen2-VL 等），结果显示模型在高层空间推理（如物体间位置关系）上表现较强，但在交互导向感知任务（如抓取点预测、轨迹预测）上表现脆弱。使用合成的 130 万 QA 对数据进行微调后，模型在低层空间智能任务上取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 当前 VLM 在具身 3D 环境的低层空间智能方面存在显著不足，尤其是交互导向感知能力。Embodied3DBench 提供了系统化的评测框架和大规模数据解决方案，为开发交互感知的多模态系统设定了明确目标。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 Embodied3DBench，一个面向具身 3D 环境的 VLM 基准，包含空间结构理解和交互导向感知两大类共 6 个任务类别。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题，因此直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for embodied spatial intelligence in VLMs, but not directly aligned with your priority subtopics. Signals: Embodied 3D benchmark, VLM spatial intelligence, interaction-oriented perception, large-scale synthetic training data, fills evaluation gap

Abstract: Are current Vision Language Models (VLMs) ready to comprehend and reason about complex embodied interactions in 3D environments? We introduce Embodied3DBench, a robot-centric benchmark targeting low-level spatial intelligence in embodied 3D environments. To systematically evaluate these foundational perceptual capabilities, the benchmark includes 6 task categories divided into two core groups: Spatial Structural Understanding (Grounding, Spatial Relation Prediction, and Multi-view Correspondence) and Interaction-Oriented Perception (Affordance Prediction, Grasp Point Prediction, and Trajectory Prediction). The benchmark spans 12 subcategories and contains over 21k high-quality question-answer pairs. We evaluate 13 state-of-the-art models, and the results show that while current models exhibit relatively strong high-level spatial reasoning, such as understanding object-to-object positional relations, they remain fragile in interaction-oriented perception, highlighting a significant lack of robust 3D-aware interaction priors. To actively bridge this capability gap revealed by our benchmark, we further synthesize a large-scale training dataset comprising 1.3M QA pairs. Notably, fine-tuning on this dataset yields significant improvements in low-level spatial intelligence. Ultimately, Embodied3DBench fills a critical gap by providing both a systematic evaluation framework and a scalable data solution, setting a clear target for the development of interaction-aware multimodal systems.

</details>


### [243] [CrystalXRD-Bench: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for XRD Peak Indexing Across Diverse Crystalline Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29446)
*Chengliang Xu, Xiaogang Li, Peiyao Xiao, Beng Wang, Hu Wei, Bing Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (46.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CrystalXRD-Bench，一个包含 250 个样本的基准，用于评估视觉语言模型从 XRD 图谱中识别 Miller 指数（HKL）的能力。实验表明，最佳模型（GPT-5.4）的 Jaccard 分数仅为 0.5888，精确匹配率 37.6%，六成模型低于 0.50，该任务远未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态基准缺乏对科学图表中定量峰值定位与多步晶体学推理能力的测试，作者希望填补这一空白，系统评估 VLM 在科学图表理解上的局限性。

Method: 从 10 个公开晶体学数据库中构建 250 个 XRD 图谱样本，每个样本包含渲染的 XRD 图像、源 CIF 文本和化学式，任务是从图谱中恢复最高强度峰对应的完整 HKL 集合。评估了 7 个视觉语言模型，使用 Jaccard 分数和精确匹配率作为指标。

Result: GPT-5.4 取得最佳 Jaccard 分数 0.5888 和精确匹配率 37.6%，但 6/7 的模型 Jaccard 低于 0.50。双峰情况尤其脆弱，高召回模型通过过度预测 HKL 来提升覆盖率，CIF 文本的提供并未弥合晶体学计算上的差距。

Conclusion: 当前 VLM 在定量科学图表理解任务上表现不足，CrystalXRD-Bench 揭示了模型在科学推理中的系统性错误模式，为未来改进提供了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 CrystalXRD-Bench，一个用于评估视觉语言模型在 XRD 图谱峰索引任务上表现的新基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Specialized materials science benchmark with no direct connection to user's priority subtopics in generative AI and efficient models. Signals: XRD peak indexing, vision-language benchmark, crystallographic reasoning, low model performance, no autoregressive or diffusion generation

Abstract: Miller-index identification from powder XRD patterns requires capabilities untested by existing multimodal benchmarks: the model must read a narrow peak location from a rendered scientific curve and then connect that observation to multi-step crystallographic reasoning. We introduce CrystalXRD-Bench, a 250-sample benchmark built from 10 public crystallographic databases for a single task: recover the full set of HKLs contributing to the highest-intensity peak in an XRD pattern. Each sample pairs the rendered XRD image with the source CIF text and chemical formula, so visual extraction errors and reasoning errors can be examined side by side. We evaluate seven vision-language models. The best Jaccard score is 0.5888 (GPT-5.4) with an exact-match rate of 37.6%, yet six of seven models remain below Jaccard 0.50; the task is far from solved. Error patterns vary systematically: double-peak cases are especially brittle, recall-heavy models gain coverage by over-predicting HKLs, and access to CIF text does not close the gap in crystallographic calculation. Alongside model rankings, the benchmark identifies the conditions under which current VLMs fail on quantitative scientific figures. All data and evaluation code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [244] [FinVerBench: Benchmark Validity and Calibration in Large Language Model Financial Statement Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29586)
*Silu Panda*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (39.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FinVerBench 是一个面向大语言模型财务报表验证的基准与有效性研究，基于 SEC 10-K XBRL 文件构建，包含四类错误扰动。实验发现多数 LLM 在干净报表上产生 95-100% 的假阳性，而校准模型在真实场景下可实现 0% 假阳性率，表明财务报表验证本质上是校准判断而非简单的算术检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 评测多关注通用能力，缺乏针对财务报表验证这一高价值、高严谨性任务的专门基准。财务报表验证需要模型判断数值一致性，涉及算术、跨表关联、同比变化和幅度扰动等多种错误类型，且面临不完整可观测性和数值渲染等现实挑战，亟需一个系统性的评测框架。

Method: 基于 43 家 S&P 500 公司的 SEC 10-K XBRL 文件构建 FinVerBench，定义四类错误分类体系（算术错误、跨表关联错误、同比错误、幅度扰动）。对 15 个当代 LLM 进行评测，使用引导式清单提示，在 105 个可观测诊断子集（43 个干净、62 个注入错误）上评估二分类指标，并对比未取整与取整两种数值渲染变体。

Result: 14 个完整 LLM 运行中，9 个在干净报表上产生 95-100% 的假阳性；仅一个运行实现 0% 观测假阳性。在取整变体上，校准模型的召回率为 79.0%（0% FPR），而在未取整诊断变体上召回率为 100.0%。Gemini 2.5 Pro 因 40/108 次网关调用失败被排除。

Conclusion: 财务报表验证不是简单的算术检测任务，而是在不完整可观测性、提示诱导假设和真实数值渲染条件下的校准判断。基准的渲染选择会显著影响评测结果，研究支持构建有效性结论而非最终排行榜。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 FinVerBench，一个用于大语言模型财务报表验证的基准与有效性研究，属于多模态/文档理解领域的评测基准工作，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on financial verification, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: financial statement verification, benchmark, LLM calibration, false positive analysis, domain-specific evaluation

Abstract: We introduce FinVerBench, a benchmark and validity study for financial statement verification: determining whether a set of corporate financial statements is numerically consistent from the information shown to the model. FinVerBench is built from SEC 10-K XBRL filings for 43 S&P 500 companies and defines a four-category error taxonomy covering arithmetic, cross-statement linkage, year-over-year, and magnitude perturbations. We attempt fifteen contemporary LLM evaluations and report fourteen complete runs; a Gemini 2.5 Pro run is excluded from the main comparison because 40/108 gateway calls failed. All binary metrics exclude underdetermined positive instances whose perturbed line item is not rendered, leaving a 105-instance observable diagnostic subset (43 clean, 62 error-injected). Under the original guided-checklist prompt on the unrounded diagnostic subset, nine of fourteen complete LLM runs produce 95-100% false positives on clean statements, while one run achieves 0% observed false positives. Benchmark rendering choices materially affect measured recall: on a realistic rounded variant of the same observable subset, the calibrated model's recall is 79.0% with 0% observed FPR, compared with 100.0% recall on the unrounded diagnostic variant. These results support a construct-validity conclusion rather than a final leaderboard: financial statement verification is not merely arithmetic detection, but calibrated judgment under incomplete observability, prompt-induced assumptions, and realistic numerical rendering. FinVerBench and all code are publicly available.

</details>


### [245] [DMC-CF: Dynamic Multimodal CounterFactual QA benchmark for Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29339)
*Junzhe Zhang, Huixuan Zhang, Guirong Wang, Xingyao Zhang, Pei Liu, Lin Qu, Hu Wei, Xiaojun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (71.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 DMC-CF，一个基于真实世界视频的大规模多模态因果反事实推理基准，包含静态（DMC-CF-Static）和动态（DMC-CF-Dynamic）两部分，用于评估多模态大模型的因果推理能力。实验表明当前 MLLM 在真实场景下的因果推理能力仍有较大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果推理数据集规模有限，且多基于合成图像/视频、卡通内容等非真实来源，缺乏基于真实世界视频的大规模基准。同时，传统静态评测存在数据污染问题。

Method: 收集真实世界视频构建 DMC-CF-Static 大规模基准；提出动态图干预（DGI）框架，利用因果图表示因果事件，从静态基准中动态生成评测样本 DMC-CF-Dynamic，以缓解数据污染。

Result: 在 DMC-CF 基准上评测了多个主流 MLLM，结果表明当前模型在真实场景下的多模态因果反事实推理能力仍显著不足。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大模型在真实世界因果推理方面仍有很大提升空间，DMC-CF 可作为评估和推动该能力发展的重要基准。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个多模态因果反事实推理基准 DMC-CF，包含静态和动态评测，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on causal reasoning benchmark, not directly aligned with user's priority subtopics like video generation or model efficiency. Signals: multimodal causal reasoning, counterfactual benchmark, real-world video, MLLM evaluation, dynamic evaluation framework

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), models have demonstrated increasingly powerful multimodal capabilities. However, whether MLLMs trained through statistical learning can truly understand the causal relationships underlying the real world remains a key research question. In recent years, numerous multimodal causal reasoning datasets have been proposed. Nevertheless, these datasets are either limited in scale or constructed from synthetic images and videos, cartoon-based content, or other non-realistic multimodal sources. To address these limitations, we collect real-world videos and construct DMC-CF-Static, a large-scale benchmark for multimodal causal counterfactual reasoning. Furthermore, to mitigate issues such as data contamination in traditional static evaluation, we represent causal events using causal graphs and propose the Dynamic Graph Intervention (DGI) framework to build the dynamic evaluation benchmark DMC-CF-Dynamic from DMC-CF-Static. Experimental results on the overall DMC-CF, which includes both static and dynamic evaluation benchmarks, demonstrate that the multimodal causal reasoning capabilities of current multimodal large language models in real-world scenarios still require substantial improvement.

</details>


### [246] [WorldMemArena: Evaluating Multimodal Agent Memory Through Action-World Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29341)
*Chengzhi Liu, Yuzhe Yang, Sophia Xiao Pu, Yepeng Liu, Lin Long, Yichen Guo, Nuo Chen, Zhaotian Weng, Elena Kochkina, Simerjot Kaur, Charese Smiley, Xiaomo Liu, James Zou, Sheng Liu, Yuheng Bu, Songyou Peng, Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (70.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldMemArena，一个包含400个多会话多模态任务的基准，用于系统评估多模态智能体的记忆能力。该基准基于"动作-世界交互循环"的四阶段记忆生命周期（写入、维护、检索、使用），支持细粒度阶段级诊断，并首次实现了长上下文、手工设计（RAG/外部记忆系统）和基于框架的智能体记忆方法的头对头比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅测量静态对话中的记忆召回，将记忆简化为单一任务准确率，并将视觉观察降级为文本描述，无法定位记忆失败的具体阶段（写入、维护、检索或使用）。随着智能体框架开始自主管理记忆，缺乏比较手工设计管道与自管理方案的原则性方法。

Method: 将多模态智能体记忆形式化为"动作-世界交互循环"的四阶段生命周期（写入→维护→检索→使用），并构建WorldMemArena基准：400个多会话多模态任务，涵盖终身演化（个人/任务状态演化）和智能体执行（基于真实观察、动作和反馈的记忆）两大类别，标注了黄金记忆点、更新、干扰项和证据链，支持阶段级诊断。

Result: (1) 更好的记忆写入和存储并不保证更好的性能；(2) 多模态记忆仍难以充分利用视觉证据；(3) 系统在不同领域间不稳定，在真实智能体轨迹上性能下降；(4) 框架自管理记忆更灵活但成本高且可靠性较低。

Conclusion: WorldMemArena揭示了当前多模态智能体记忆系统的关键局限性，为未来研究提供了细粒度的诊断工具和评估框架，强调了记忆系统各阶段均衡发展的重要性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了WorldMemArena，一个用于评估多模态智能体记忆能力的基准，包含400个多会话多模态任务和细粒度阶段级诊断。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，同时涉及AIGC方向的AI智能体与生成工作流（aigc_agent_workflow）相关概念，但核心贡献是评测基准。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for multimodal agent memory, but only tangentially touches your priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: multimodal agent memory benchmark, action-world interaction loop, four-stage memory lifecycle, head-to-head memory method comparison, multimodal understanding generation

Abstract: Multimodal large language models are increasingly deployed as long-horizon agents, where memory must do more than recall: it must track an evolving world, revise what has gone stale, and surface the right evidence at decision time. Existing benchmarks measure recall over static dialogue, collapse memory into a single end-of-task accuracy, and reduce visual observations to captions, leaving us unable to localize failures to writing, maintenance, retrieval, or use. The rise of agent harnesses that author their own memory sharpens this gap, since we have no principled way to compare hand-designed pipelines with self-managing alternatives. To close these gaps, we formulate multimodal agent memory as an Action-World Interaction Loop with an observable four-stage lifecycle, and instantiate it in WorldMemArena: 400 multi-session multimodal tasks spanning Lifelong Evolution (evolving personal and task states) and Agentic Execution (memory from real observations, actions, and feedback), annotated with gold memory points, updates, distractors, and evidence chains for stage-level diagnosis. This enables the first head-to-head comparison of long-context, manually designed (RAG and external memory systems), and harness-based memory agents. Results show that: (1) better memory writing and storage do not guarantee better performance; (2) multimodal memory still struggles to fully use visual evidence; (3) systems are unstable across domains and degrade on realistic agentic trajectories; and (4) harness memory is more flexible but remains costly and less reliable.

</details>


### [247] [FormInv: A Measurement Protocol for Semantic Invariance in Mathematical Reasoning Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29001)
*Nishal Thomas, Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (51.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 FormInv，一个用于检测数学推理基准中语义不变性缺陷的审计协议。通过对 MathCheck 基准的 paraphrase 质量审计，发现 3.1% 的语义错误 paraphrase 足以改变模型排名（GPT-4o 从第2降至第4）。进一步发现，9个前沿模型在聚合准确率仅差 86-96% 的情况下，语义一致性率（SCR）却横跨 50-82%，存在 32 个百分点的隐藏差距。论文还证明了"无免费基准"推论：基准设计者选择的 paraphrase 族权重隐式决定了哪个模型获胜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理基准假设问题表述的语义等价改写不会影响模型表现，但这一假设未被充分验证。标准评测仅关注聚合准确率，忽略了模型对语义等价变体的不一致响应，导致排名可能被有缺陷的 paraphrase 扭曲。

Method: 提出 FormInv 审计协议：1) 使用多模型一致性投票自动检测语义不正确的 paraphrase；2) 引入语义一致性率（SCR）和每定理 Cochran's Q 作为不变性度量；3) 在 Lean4 验证的定理上对 9 个模型进行评测（366-811 项）；4) 提出 FormInvSelector 用于基于场景的模型选择。

Result: MathCheck 中检测到 4 个语义错误 paraphrase（3.1%），移除后 GPT-4o 从第2降至第4；Claude Haiku 4.5 准确率 86% 但 SCR 仅 50%；9 个模型准确率范围 86-96% 但 SCR 范围 50-82%（32 点差距）；跨模型一致性检测成本低于 $10。

Conclusion: 数学推理基准存在严重的语义不变性缺陷，聚合准确率掩盖了模型对语义等价变体的不一致性。基准设计者选择的 paraphrase 族隐式决定了排名结果。FormInv 提供了一种低成本、可复现的审计方法来揭示这些问题，并指导更可靠的模型选择。

Classification reason: 该论文提出数学推理基准的语义不变性测量协议（FormInv），属于对基准评测质量的审计与改进，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on benchmark auditing for math reasoning, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: Benchmark auditing, semantic invariance, ranking instability, no-free-benchmark corollary, low cost cross-model detection

Abstract: A paraphrase-quality audit of MathCheck (ICLR 2025) detected 4 semantically incorrect paraphrases in 129 groups (3.1%); removing them drops GPT-4o from rank 2 to rank 4 and elevates Claude Haiku and DeepSeek V3 above it; these ranking changes are invisible to any single-model evaluation. Cross-model unanimity found these errors automatically (>= 3/4 models for MathCheck; >= 6/9 for our primary evaluation) for under $10; in our own dataset the same protocol found that 47% of auto-generated connective-variation paraphrases were semantically incorrect. That flaw compounds a deeper measurement gap: Claude Haiku 4.5 achieves 86% accuracy yet SCR=50%, meaning half its theorems are answered differently under semantically equivalent restatements, while aggregate accuracy across 9 models spans only 86-96% yet Semantic Consistency Rates (SCR) span 50-82% -- a 32-point gap invisible to standard benchmarks. Formally, for any target ranking over 9 frontier models there exists a weighting over paraphrase families that realizes it (No-Free-Benchmark corollary), because no model Pareto-dominates all families -- so benchmark designers who select families are implicitly choosing which model wins. FormInv supplies the audit protocol (replicated on external benchmarks at 100% recall), SCR and per-theorem Cochran's Q as primary invariance measures evaluated on 9 models across 366-811 items (on Lean4-verified theorems), and FormInvSelector for regime-aware model selection.

</details>


### [248] [NICE: A Theory-Grounded Diagnostic Benchmark for Social Intelligence of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29685)
*Yunjin Qi, Zhaojun Jiang, Xuan Wu, Hanxi Pan, Yixuan Wang, Yanfang Liu, Xiang Ji, Churu Yu, Chunyuan Zheng, Yingze Chen, Jie He, Liuqing Chen, Zaifeng Gao*

TL;DR: NICE是一个基于社会心理学理论构建的LLM社交智能诊断评测基准，包含4个类别、11个维度和137个测试项，通过中国语境下的代表性场景评估LLM的社交智能。实验发现前沿LLM在整体准确率上表现尚可，但在"沟通"维度存在一致性的薄弱环节，具体体现在多轮沟通、非语言沟通和同步性三个能力面上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM社交智能评测缺乏统一的理论框架，无法对模型社交能力进行细粒度的诊断性评估。随着LLM在情感陪伴、客户服务等社交场景中的广泛应用，衡量其社交智能对人机交互质量和安全性至关重要。

Method: 通过文献综述和多轮专家验证，基于心理测量学原则构建了一个包含4个类别（规范、互动、认知、体验）和11个维度的社交智能框架。在此基础上，设计了137个测试项，通过代表性中国语境进行操作化，构建了NICE诊断基准。对5个前沿LLM和人类对照组进行了评估。

Result: LLM在整体准确率上得分较高，但在"沟通"维度存在一致的薄弱环节，框架将其定位到3个具体能力面：多轮沟通、非语言沟通和同步性。

Conclusion: NICE将社交智能评估重新定义为基于理论指导的、对LLM社交关键弱点的诊断性评估，为未来LLM社交能力的改进提供了细粒度的方向指引。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个基于社会心理学理论构建的LLM社交智能诊断评测基准NICE，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。虽然该基准聚焦于社交智能而非传统视觉-语言多模态，但评测基准类工作属于该子主题的覆盖范围，且该子主题为[CANONICAL]条目，优先复用。

Importance reason: Strong theoretical grounding and diagnostic granularity, but focuses on social intelligence evaluation, not the user's priority generative or efficiency topics. Signals: theory-grounded diagnostic benchmark, social intelligence evaluation, LLM communication weakness, Chinese context, multimodal understanding generation

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied in social contexts such as emotional companionship and customer service, measuring their social intelligence has become critical to the quality and safety of human-AI interaction. However, existing social intelligence benchmarks lack a unified framework that organizes social abilities into a unified structure, and therefore cannot enable fine-grained diagnosis. To build the first holistic diagnostic evaluation grounded in social theory, we first construct a social intelligence framework through a literature review and multi-stage expert validation guided by psychometric principles. The resulting framework includes 4 categories and 11 dimensions, each further specified by fine-grained capability facets. Building on this framework, we introduce NICE (Norm, Interaction, Cognition, Experience), a diagnostic benchmark of 137 items operationalized through representative Chinese contexts. Across 5 frontier LLMs and a human reference group, models score higher in aggregate accuracy yet show a consistent weakness in Communication, which the framework localizes to 3 specific capability facets: multi-turn communication, nonverbal communication, and synchrony. NICE thus reframes social intelligence evaluation toward theory-grounded diagnosis of socially consequential weaknesses in LLMs.

</details>

<details>

Motivation: 现有 LVLM 评测基准多面向通用场景，缺乏针对中文金融领域全业务流程的多模态评测数据集，无法系统评估模型在金融场景下的感知、理解、推理和认知能力。

Result: 最优模型在问答任务上准确率为 66.11%，在检测、识别和信息抽取任务上平均得分为 77.18，表明当前 LVLM 在金融多模态任务上仍有显著提升空间。

Conclusion: CFMME 填补了中文金融领域 LVLM 多模态评测的空白，揭示了现有模型在金融场景下的不足，为未来提升 LVLM 在垂直领域的多模态能力提供了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个中文金融多模态评测基准 CFMME，用于评估 LVLM 在金融场景下的感知、理解、推理和认知能力，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Financial domain benchmark is too narrow and not aligned with your priority subtopics on generation and world models. Signals: Financial domain benchmark, Multimodal evaluation, Chinese context, Low accuracy ceiling, Not generation-focused

Abstract: The emergence of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) has substantially expanded model capabilities beyond text-only understanding, enabling unified inference across both visual and textual modalities and supporting a broader range of real-world applications. To comprehensively evaluate the perception, understanding, reasoning, and cognition capabilities of LVLMs throughout the entire financial business workflow in Chinese contexts, we introduce CFMME, a novel Chinese financial multimodal evaluation benchmark. CFMME comprises 6,052 instances spanning from fundamental academic knowledge to complex real-world applications, covering eight primary financial image modalities and four core multimodal tasks. On CFMME, we conduct a thorough evaluation of representative LVLMs. The results show that the state-of-the-art model attains an overall accuracy of 66.11\% on the question answering task and an average score of 77.18 on the detection, recognition, and information extraction tasks, indicating substantial room for improvement in current LVLMs. In addition, we conduct detailed analyses of error causes, cross-modal capabilities, and multi-orientation settings, yielding valuable insights for future research. We hope that CFMME will spur further progress in LVLMs, especially by improving their performance on multiple multimodal tasks in the financial domain.

</details>


### [250] [MusTBENCH: Benchmarking and Advancing Temporal Grounding in Music LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29300)
*Daeyong Kwon, Qiyu Wu, Shinobu Kuriya, Junghyun Koo, Shuyang Cui, Zhi Zhong, Wei-Hsiang Liao, Hiromi Wakaki, Yuki Mitsufuji*

TL;DR: 本文提出 MusTBENCH，一个由音乐专家验证的基准，用于评估大音频语言模型（LALMs）在音乐理解中的时间定位能力，包含五项时间定位问答任务。同时提出 MusT，一种四阶段时间优化策略（音乐编码器适配、LLM适配、LLM监督微调、RL优化），显著提升了模型的时间定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大音频语言模型在音乐理解方面展现出一定能力，但其回答是否准确定位到音频中正确的时间区域尚未被充分探索。音乐中的关键信息（如乐器进入、节奏转换）往往是时间局部化的事件，因此评估和提升模型的时间定位能力至关重要。

Method: 1) 构建 MusTBENCH 基准：由音乐专家验证，包含五项时间定位问答任务。2) 提出 MusT 四阶段时间优化方案：音乐编码器适配 → LLM适配 → LLM监督微调 → 基于强化学习的优化。

Result: 实验表明现有 LALMs 在精确时间定位上表现不佳，而 MusT 在强基线上带来了显著改进。

Conclusion: 时间定位是当前 LALMs 缺失的关键能力，MusTBENCH 可作为未来时间定位音乐理解研究的挑战性基准。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 MusTBENCH，一个用于评估音乐大语言模型（LALMs）时间定位能力的基准，属于多模态（音频-语言）评测任务，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on music temporal grounding, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: music temporal grounding benchmark, LALM evaluation, four-stage optimization, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Recent Large Audio-Language Models (LALMs) have demonstrated promising abilities in understanding musical content. However, whether their responses are grounded in the correct temporal regions of the audio remains underexplored. This limitation is particularly critical for music understanding, where key information often occurs as temporally localized events, such as instrument entries and rhythmic transitions. To address this gap, we introduce MusTBENCH, a music-expert-validated benchmark designed to evaluate temporal grounding in LALMs through five temporally grounded question-answering tasks. To further improve temporal grounding in existing models, we propose MusT, a novel four-stage temporal optimization recipe spanning music encoder adaptation, LLM adaptation, LLM supervised fine-tuning, and RL-based optimization. Experiments on MusTBENCH show that existing LALMs struggle with precise temporal grounding, while MusT brings significant improvements over strong baselines. These results establish temporal grounding as a key missing capability in current LALMs and position MusTBENCH as a challenging benchmark for future research in temporally grounded music understanding.

</details>


### [251] [DiffSpot: Can VLMs Spot Fine-Grained Visual Differences in Web Interfaces?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29615)
*Linhao Zhang, Aiwei Liu, Yuan Liu, Xiao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 DiffSpot，一个基于代码驱动的网页界面找不同基准，通过 CSS 属性突变构建受控图像对，用于评估 VLM 的细粒度视觉差异感知能力。实验表明，即使最好的模型也只能识别 40.7% 的真实变化，Hard 难度召回率低于 23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在高层次图文对齐方面取得了显著进展，但其感知细微视觉差异的能力仍然有限。网页界面中的局部视觉变化既是细粒度感知的诊断测试，也是 GUI 智能体和设计工具的实际需求，但目前缺乏系统性的评测基准。

Method: DiffSpot 通过突变自包含 HTML 中目标元素的单个 CSS 属性，重新渲染页面，并记录变化的属性、元素和突变幅度，构建受控图像对。通过 grounding gate 确保像素差异仅局限于目标元素。基准包含 4400 对图像，涵盖 13 种 CSS 属性操作符和三个难度等级，以及 500 对无差异图像用于幻觉控制。

Result: 在 13 个前沿 VLM 的零样本评估中，最佳模型仅识别出 40.7% 的真实变化，所有模型在 Hard 难度下的召回率均低于 23%。难度高度依赖于 CSS 属性类型，像素幅度和 CLIP 距离均不能可靠预测召回率。

Conclusion: 当前 VLM 在细粒度视觉差异感知方面存在显著不足，DiffSpot 揭示了这一能力差距，并为未来研究提供了系统性的评测工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 DiffSpot，一个用于评估 VLM 在网页界面中细粒度视觉差异感知能力的基准。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题，因为其核心贡献是构建了一个新的多模态评测基准来测试 VLM 的细粒度视觉感知能力。

Importance reason: Focuses on GUI-specific fine-grained VLM evaluation, not directly aligned with your priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: VLM fine-grained perception, web interface benchmark, CSS mutation, low model recall, hallucination control

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have made strong progress on high-level image-text alignment, yet their ability to perceive subtle visual differences remains limited. We study this problem in rendered web interfaces, where localized visual changes are both a diagnostic test of fine-grained perception and a practical requirement for GUI agents and design tools. We introduce \textbf{DiffSpot}, a code-driven benchmark for open-ended spot-the-difference on web interfaces. DiffSpot constructs controlled image pairs by mutating a single CSS property of a target element in self-contained HTML, re-rendering the page, and recording the changed property, element, and mutation magnitude. A grounding gate retains only pairs whose rendered pixel difference is confined to the target element. The benchmark contains 4{,}400 pairs, including 3{,}900 has-diff pairs balanced across 13 CSS-property operators and three difficulty tiers, plus 500 no-diff pairs for hallucination control. Evaluating 13 frontier VLMs zero-shot, we find that even the best model identifies only $40.7\%$ of true changes, with Hard-tier Recall below $23\%$ for every model. DiffSpot further shows that difficulty is strongly property-dependent: across CSS operators, neither pixel magnitude nor CLIP distance reliably predicts Recall.

</details>


### [252] [OmniMatBench: A Human-Calibrated Multimodal Reasoning Benchmark Across 19 Materials Science Subfields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29833)
*Wanhao Liu, Jiaqing Xie, Qian Tan, Weida Wang, Jue Wang, Ran Sun, Zhuo Yang, Wanli Ouyang, Lei Bai, Tianfan Fu, Lu Chen, Xin Chen, Yuqiang Li*
