TL;DR: OmniMatBench是一个人工校准的材料科学多模态推理基准，包含3171个专家标注问题，覆盖19个材料学子领域。评测13个MLLM发现最佳模型仅得0.372分，揭示了当前模型在材料科学推理上的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有材料科学基准主要关注属性预测、知识问答或表征理解，缺乏从材料知识到应用的完整推理过程评测。因此需要构建一个覆盖多子领域、多模态、端到端推理的基准来评估MLLM在材料科学中的实际能力。

Method: 构建了OmniMatBench基准，包含3171个专家标注的QA和计算问题，覆盖19个材料学子领域（分为四大类：基础材料知识、结构/工程材料、材料加工与制造、功能/应用材料）。对13个开源和闭源MLLM进行系统评测，并分析了公式辅助、检索辅助、代码辅助等设置下的模型表现。

Result: 最佳模型（GPT-4o）仅取得0.372总体得分，远低于人类水平。模型在不同子领域表现差异显著，存在固定推理启发式、材料知识不均衡、高级知识应用能力有限等问题。公式、检索、代码辅助设置下模型表现有所提升但仍有限。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM在材料科学多模态推理上存在显著不足，OmniMatBench为评估和推动AI在材料科学研究中的应用提供了重要基准和洞察。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了OmniMatBench，一个面向材料科学的多模态推理基准，包含3171个专家标注的QA和计算问题，覆盖19个子领域，并对13个MLLM进行了系统评测。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，因为其核心贡献是构建了一个新的多模态评测基准。

Importance reason: Specialized materials science benchmark with low cross-domain relevance to your priority topics. Signals: materials science benchmark, multimodal reasoning, MLLM evaluation, domain-specific, low model scores

Abstract: As multimodal language models play an increasingly important role in scientific research, materials science offers a critical testbed due to its interdisciplinary, multimodal, and application-driven nature. However, existing materials benchmarks mainly focus on property prediction, knowledge QA, or characterization understanding, leaving the broader reasoning process from materials knowledge to application underexplored. To fill this gap, we present OmniMatBench, a human-calibrated multimodal reasoning benchmark for materials science. OmniMatBench contains 3,171 expert-curated QA and calculation problems across 19 materials-science subfields, spanning fundamental materials knowledge, structural and engineering materials, materials processing and manufacturing, and functional and applied materials. We evaluate 13 open-source and closed-source MLLMs and find that the best model achieves only a 0.372 overall score, revealing a substantial gap in current materials-science reasoning. Further analysis shows strong variation across subfields, fixed reasoning heuristics, uneven materials knowledge, and limited high-level knowledge application under formula-, retrieval-, and code-assisted settings. OmniMatBench provides crucial insights into the capabilities and limitations of current MLLMs and establishes a foundation for reliable AI assistants in materials-science research.

</details>


### [253] [World Models in Words: Auditing Physical State-Transition Commitments in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29585)
*Emmanuelle Bourigault*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (79.15) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 WMW 评估框架，用于审计视觉语言模型（VLM）在物理场景问答中的语言表达物理承诺（physical commitments）。该框架要求模型输出结构化追踪（初始状态、状态变迁、结果状态、答案），并通过混合验证器检查模式有效性、状态接地、变迁一致性和答案-追踪兼容性，揭示仅靠最终答案评估无法发现的物理推理失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 评估仅关注最终答案的正确性，无法判断模型是否感知到正确的物体、表达了正确的物理状态、预测了合理的状态变迁，还是仅仅碰巧选对了答案。作者希望构建一个可审计 VLM 物理推理过程的评估协议，而不仅仅是另一个最终答案的物理基准。

Method: 提出 WMW 框架，要求 VLM 输出结构化追踪 (s0, Δs, s1, a)，包含初始状态、状态变迁、结果状态和答案。设计混合验证器检查：模式有效性、状态接地（物体/关系/属性）、变迁一致性（力/时间/单位/尺度）、答案-追踪兼容性。构建 TraceBank 数据集，包含多个物理系列的合成场景和最小扰动对比偏好对，并提供验证器代码和审计指南。

Result: 在多个 VLM 上的实验表明：中等模型 35% 的正确答案背后是物理无效的追踪；验证器引导的重排序可在不牺牲答案准确率的情况下提升最多 7 个百分点的追踪有效性；追踪级别的偏好微调可将隐藏不一致性相对降低 41%。

Conclusion: WMW 揭示了仅靠最终答案评估无法发现的 VLM 物理推理失败模式，提供了一种可复用的审计协议来测量 VLM 的物理世界陈述是否与其答案同时成立，为更可靠的 VLM 物理推理评估提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个评估框架 WMW，用于审计 VLM 在物理场景理解中的状态-变迁承诺（state-transition commitments），属于多模态评测范畴。它并非提出新的最终答案基准，而是提供一种可复用的评测协议来揭示 VLM 的物理推理失败模式。这与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel auditing framework for VLM physical reasoning, but is a benchmark/evaluation paper, not a generative method. Signals: VLM evaluation, physical reasoning audit, trace-based verification, not a generative model, no direct video/image generation

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to answer questions about physical scenes, yet most evaluations reduce performance to a final answer. This hides whether the model perceived the right objects, represented the right physical state, predicted a plausible transition, or merely selected the right option for the wrong reasons. We introduce \wmw, an evaluation framework for auditing the \emph{language-expressed physical commitments} of VLMs. Instead of scoring only $I,q\mapsto a$, we ask models to produce a typed trace $I,q\mapsto(s_0,\Delta s,s_1,a)$: an initial state, a state transition, a resulting state, and an answer. A hybrid verifier then checks schema validity, state grounding, transition consistency, and answer-trace compatibility, yielding typed error labels such as object, relation, force, transition, temporal, unit/scale, and faithfulness errors. We release \tracebank, a controlled trace resource with \nSeed schema- and recomputation-validated synthetic scenarios across \nFamilies physics families, \nPairs minimally perturbed contrastive preference pairs, verifier code, audit guidelines, and model outputs. We evaluate \nModels VLMs on both controlled and external physical-reasoning examples. \wmw reveals failures that answer-only evaluation misses: 35\% of correct answers from mid-tier models are backed by physically invalid traces. Verifier-guided reranking recovers up to 7 percentage points of trace validity without sacrificing answer accuracy, and trace-level preference tuning reduces hidden inconsistency by 41\% relative. The contribution is not another final-answer physics benchmark, but a reusable protocol for measuring whether a VLM's stated physical world can be true at the same time as its answer.

</details>


### [254] [Multi-Legal-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Legal Reasoning Across Jurisdictions, Languages, and Legal Traditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29738)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (49.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Multi-Legal-Bench 是首个跨司法管辖区、跨语言的法律推理评测基准，覆盖6个国家、4个语系、1.34亿份法院判决，定义了5个法律推理任务。在7个前沿LLM和4个小模型上的实验揭示了任务相关的少样本效果、无单一模型主导、跨语言迁移不遵循语言亲缘性、以及分词器效率不能显著预测跨语言准确率等发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律NLP基准大多评估单一语言或聚合不同司法管辖区中本质不同的任务，导致跨语言比较无法进行。缺乏一个在相同任务定义下跨多个司法管辖区和语言进行公平评估的基准。

Method: 构建了一个5×6的任务-司法管辖区矩阵（20/30个单元格有数据），包含法院类型分类、判决形式分类、案件结果预测、法律规范提取和原因类别预测五个任务，覆盖乌克兰、法国、荷兰、波兰、捷克、立陶宛六个国家。使用AWS Bedrock对7个前沿LLM进行零样本和3样本提示评估，并加入4个3-12B的小模型进行规模分析。

Result: (1) 任务相关的少样本效果在乌克兰发现后，在所有司法管辖区得到复现；(2) 没有单一模型在所有语言上占优，排名随任务和司法管辖区变化；(3) 跨语言少样本迁移不遵循语言亲缘性（UA→FR 罗曼语族 -2.1pp 优于 UA→PL 斯拉夫语族 -13.7pp），标签集对齐比语系更能预测迁移质量；(4) 分词器生育率（2.3倍差异）不能显著预测跨语言准确率（r=-0.27, p=0.14）。

Conclusion: Multi-Legal-Bench 为跨司法管辖区法律推理评估提供了首个标准化框架，揭示了跨语言迁移的复杂性和模型架构/预训练数据相比分词器效率的主导作用。该基准有助于推动法律AI的跨语言公平评估。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个跨司法管辖区、跨语言的法律推理评测基准 Multi-Legal-Bench，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。虽然基准聚焦于法律文本（语言模态），但其核心贡献是构建跨语言、跨任务的评测框架，与多模态评测基准的范畴一致。

Importance reason: Focuses on legal NLP benchmarks, which is outside the user's core interests in generative video/image models and efficient inference. Signals: Cross-jurisdictional legal benchmark, Multilingual LLM evaluation, No direct connection to video/image generation or world models, Negative keywords (legal) present, Low priority subtopic match

Abstract: Legal NLP benchmarks overwhelmingly evaluate a single language or aggregate tasks that differ fundamentally across jurisdictions, making cross-lingual comparison impossible. We introduce Multi-Legal-Bench, the first cross-jurisdictional legal benchmark that evaluates identical tasks across six countries (Ukraine, France, Netherlands, Poland, Czech Republic, Lithuania), four language families, and 134 million court decisions. The benchmark defines five tasks court-type classification, judgment form classification, case-outcome prediction, legal norm extraction, and cause category prediction mapped to structured metadata from national court registries, forming a deliberately sparse 5x6 task-jurisdiction matrix (20 of 30 cells filled). We evaluate 7 frontier LLMs under zero-shot and 3-shot prompting via AWS Bedrock, with 4 additional small/medium models (3-12B) for scaling analysis. Our results reveal that: (1) task-dependent few-shot effects discovered in Ukrainian replicate across all jurisdictions; (2) no single model dominates any language rankings shift with both task and jurisdiction; (3) cross-lingual few-shot transfer does not follow language proximity: UA->FR (Romance, -2.1 pp) transfers better than UA->PL (Slavic, -13.7 pp), with label-set alignment predicting transfer quality better than language family; and (4) tokenizer fertility, despite a 2.3x spread, does not significantly predict cross-lingual accuracy (r=-0.27, p=0.14), suggesting that model architecture and pretraining data dominate tokenizer efficiency. We release all data, prompts, and model predictions.

</details>


### [255] [ExCAM: Explainable Cultural Awareness Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29897)
*Christoph Leiter, Haiyue Song, Hour Kaing, Jin Tei, Hideki Tanaka, Masao Utiyama, Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (49.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ExCAM 是首个专门用于评估大语言模型文化意识的可解释评估指标，能够识别、评分并解释生成文本中的文化错误。论文同时构建了 ExCAM40k 数据集，在平衡测试集上达到 80% 的错误检测准确率，优于包括 GPT-5 在内的多个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估 LLM 文化意识的基准依赖耗时昂贵的人工标注，且缺乏对自由文本中文化错误的细粒度、可解释评估。需要一种自动化的、可解释的评估指标来识别和解释文化错误。

Method: 提出 ExCAM（Explainable Cultural Awareness Metrics），一种可解释的文化意识评估指标。构建 ExCAM40k 数据集，整合 9 个现有基准并加入合成错误进行训练和评估。ExCAM 能够对指令-输出对中的文化错误进行识别、评分并提供解释。

Result: 在平衡测试集上，ExCAM 达到 80% 的错误检测准确率，优于包括 GPT-5 在内的多个基线方法。

Conclusion: ExCAM 为自由文本的细粒度、可解释文化评估开辟了新路径，有助于确保 LLM 生成文本的公平性和跨文化泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出首个可解释的文化意识评估指标 ExCAM，用于检测和解释 LLM 生成文本中的文化错误，属于多模态/文本生成内容的评测与基准。映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark），因为该 subtopic 涵盖评测指标、基准和自动评估方法，ExCAM 正是一种新的评估指标。

Importance reason: Focuses on cultural awareness evaluation in NLP, not aligned with user's priority on generative video/image models and efficiency. Signals: cultural awareness metric, explainable evaluation, NLP benchmark, no generative model focus, low priority subtopic match

Abstract: Evaluating the cultural awareness of large language models is crucial to ensure the fairness of generated text and the generalizability of applications across the world. Recent benchmarks explore cultural goods like food or values like behavior in stressful situations through the lens of question answering or text generation tasks. However, creating these benchmarks requires time-intensive and costly human annotations. Also, benchmarks that evaluate cultural awareness in free text are scarce and often rely on dated evaluation mechanisms. To address this gap, we introduce ExCAM, an Explainable Cultural Awareness Metric, which is, to our knowledge, the first dedicated evaluation metric that identifies, rates and explains cultural errors in instruction-output pairs. To train and evaluate ExCAM, we introduce ExCAM40k, a dataset comprised of nine existing benchmarks that we reformat and enhance with synthetic errors. Compared to several baselines, including GPT-5, ExCAM achieves the highest error detection rate with up to 80% accuracy on a balanced test set. Therefore, ExCAM opens the pathway towards fine-grained and explainable cultural evaluation of free text.

</details>


### [256] [SEAL: Can Saturated Benchmarks Be Revived by LLM-as-a-Meta-Judge?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30104)
*Jiamin Chen, Yidi Wu, Qiexiang Wang, Qianben Chen, Yuchen Li, Yansen Zhang, Xiaokun Zhang, Wangchunshu Zhou, Chen Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (49.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SEAL（Seeded Elimination with Adaptive LLM-as-a-Meta-Judge），一种自改进的评估协议，通过种子淘汰赛制结合任务级原则和自改进检查清单，利用LLM作为元评判者从已饱和的基准测试中提取有意义的排名信号。在代码生成、数学推理、知识问答和工具使用等多个饱和基准上，SEAL在排名准确性与延迟之间取得了更优的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型基准测试日益饱和，前沿系统得分接近，标准指标无法有效区分模型能力。构建更难的基准成本高昂且可能很快再次饱和，因此作者提出是否可以通过改进评估方法（而非构建新任务）来使现有基准重新具有区分力。

Method: SEAL采用种子淘汰赛制：将候选输出种子化后进入单败淘汰赛，每场比赛使用任务级原则加上自改进的检查清单标准进行评估。LLM作为元评判者，根据任务原则和动态生成的检查清单对每对候选输出进行比较，逐步淘汰较差的输出，最终得到完整的排名。

Result: 在多个饱和基准上，SEAL与全对全评判相比达到了0.83-1.00的Spearman排名一致性，以及4/4的top-1一致性，同时每个任务仅需11.89次调用，而全对全评判需要28.00次调用。

Conclusion: SEAL通过改进评估协议而非构建新基准，成功从已饱和的基准中提取出有意义的排名信号，在排名准确性和效率之间取得了良好的平衡，为基准测试的持续利用提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种利用LLM作为元评判者（meta-judge）来从已饱和的基准测试中提取排名信号的评估协议SEAL，核心贡献在于改进评测方法而非构建新基准。这与[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）高度相关，该子主题涵盖评测协议、自动评估等方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM evaluation methodology rather than core generative model advances in user's priority subtopics. Signals: LLM-as-a-judge, saturated benchmarks, ranking accuracy, evaluation protocol, code generation

Abstract: Widely used language-model benchmarks are increasingly saturated, with frontier systems often receiving near-tied scores that standard metrics cannot resolve. Rather than constructing harder alternatives, we ask whether existing tasks can be made informative again through improved evaluation over the same candidate outputs. Therefore, we present Seeded Elimination with Adaptive LLM-as-a-Meta-Judge, a self-improving evaluation protocol for extracting latent ranking signal from saturated benchmarks. SEAL seeds candidate outputs into a single elimination and evaluates each match with task-level principles plus self-improving checklist criteria. We evaluate SEAL on multiple saturated benchmarks covering code generation, mathematical reasoning, knowledge-intensive question answering, and tool-use agent task completion. Across these settings, SEAL improves the ranking-accuracy--latency trade-off over competing protocols, attaining 0.83--1.00 Spearman agreement with full pairwise judging and 4/4 top-1 agreement, while requiring only 11.89 calls per task compared with 28.00 for full pairwise evaluation.

</details>


### [257] [GRUFF: LLM Pronoun Fidelity, Reasoning, and Biases in German](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30214)
*Fabian Mewes, Anne Lauscher, Vagrant Gautam*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (44.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文构建了 GRUFF 数据集，用于评测 LLM 在德语中的代词忠实度（pronoun fidelity），涵盖四种性别一致系统和四组代词。实验发现：无显式上下文时模型对阳性和阴性名词的语法一致性强，但对新代词 xier 和 en 表现不佳；模型普遍对干扰项不鲁棒；职业刻板偏见在不同语法格之间相关性弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代词忠实度研究主要聚焦英语，而英语语法性别有限且几乎没有性别一致现象。德语具有丰富的语法性别系统，能更全面地评估 LLM 的指代推理能力与刻板偏见，因此需要构建德语代词忠实度评测数据集。

Method: 构建大规模德语代词忠实度数据集 GRUFF，覆盖四种名词性别一致系统（阳性、阴性、中性、新代词 xier/en）和四组代词。设计实验评估 LLM 在不同上下文条件下的代词选择能力，并分析职业刻板偏见的影响。

Result: 无显式上下文时，模型对阳性和阴性名词的语法一致性强，但对新代词 xier 和 en 表现不佳；模型普遍对干扰项不鲁棒；编码器模型在德语中比英语更鲁棒；职业刻板偏见在不同语法格之间相关性弱，仅在架构相近的模型间相关。

Conclusion: 德语语法性别对 LLM 的代词忠实度有重要影响，现有模型在处理新代词和干扰项时仍存在不足，职业刻板偏见在不同语境下表现不一致，需要进一步研究性别包容性语言和指代推理。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了德语代词忠实度评测数据集 GRUFF，研究 LLM 在德语中的代词指代推理与性别刻板偏见，属于多模态/语言模型评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on German pronoun fidelity, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: German LLM evaluation, pronoun fidelity, grammatical gender, not video/image generation, not efficient inference

Abstract: Third-person singular pronouns have long been used to study stereotypical biases in language models and to test their abilities to reason about reference. More recently, the interplay between reasoning and bias has been investigated with the task of pronoun fidelity, which assesses models' abilities to correctly reuse a previously-specified pronoun for a discourse entity, independent of other potentially distracting discourse entities mentioned in between. However, such research focuses on English, which is a language with limited grammatical gender and almost no gender agreement. In this paper we contribute a novel, large-scale dataset, GRUFF, to measure pronoun fidelity in German, covering four different gender agreement systems in nouns, and four sets of pronouns. With this dataset, we show that LLMs show strong grammatical agreement for masculine and feminine entities in the absence of explicit context, but not for neopronouns xier and en. Models are generally not robust to distractors, but encoder-only models are more robust in German than in English, reflecting the importance of grammatical gender. Finally, we show that occupational stereotypes in this context are poorly correlated across grammatical cases, and across most models, except ones with closely related architectures. We release all code and data to encourage further work on gender-inclusive language and referential reasoning in German.

</details>


### [258] [CommunityFact: A Dynamic, Multilingual, Multi-domain Benchmark for Misinformation Detection in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30241)
*Sahajpreet Singh, Insyirah Mujtahid, Min-Yen Kan, Kokil Jaidka*

Main category: cs.SI, cs.CL, cs.CY

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 CommunityFact，一个动态、多语言、多领域的虚假信息检测基准，包含 15,992 条跨五语言两领域的声明。评估了十种 LLM 在不同推理能力下的表现，发现封闭输入验证仍具挑战性，联网搜索带来最大提升，且 LLM 的源选择策略与人类标注者存在系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的虚假信息检测基准多为静态数据集，无法反映快速变化的多语言在线环境，且缺乏对模型在真实场景中推理能力的全面评估。作者希望构建一个可刷新的、覆盖多语言多领域的基准，以更准确地衡量 LLM 在野外的虚假信息检测能力。

Method: 基于 Community Notes 平台构建了包含 15,992 条声明的基准数据集，覆盖英语、印地语、印尼语、中文和西班牙语五种语言，以及健康和政治两个领域。评估了十种 LLM（包括 GPT-4o、Claude、Gemini 等）在封闭输入、思维链推理和联网搜索三种推理条件下的表现，并分析了模型源选择策略与人类标注者的一致性。

Result: 封闭输入验证对所有模型仍具挑战性；联网搜索带来最大的性能提升；LLM 的源选择策略与人类 Community Notes 评分者存在系统性偏差；不同语言-领域切片上的表现差异显著；通过检索扩展或剪枝的模型特定机制可以缩小与人类源选择之间的差距。

Conclusion: CommunityFact 作为一个动态、可刷新的基准，揭示了当前 LLM 在真实场景虚假信息检测中的局限性，特别是联网搜索能力虽能提升性能，但模型的源选择策略仍需与人类偏好对齐。Community Notes 可作为训练信号用于改进声明条件源推荐系统。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个多语言、多领域的虚假信息检测基准 CommunityFact，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题，因为其核心贡献是构建了一个可刷新的评测基准，用于评估 LLM 在真实场景中的虚假信息检测能力。

Importance reason: Misinformation detection benchmark is relevant to multimodal understanding but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: Multilingual misinformation, dynamic benchmark, LLM evaluation, web-search gains, source-selection misalignment

Abstract: Misinformation verification increasingly occurs in public, fast-moving, and multilingual online settings, where static benchmarks provide an incomplete measure of model reliability. We introduce CommunityFact, a refreshable benchmark for misinformation detection in the wild, with three major goals: coverage, granularity, and redistributability. This release contains 15,992 standalone claims across five languages and two domains. We evaluate ten LLMs under varying inference-time capabilities, including thinking and web-search. Our results show that closed-input verification remains challenging, web access yields the largest gains, and web-enabled LLMs' source-selection policies are systematically misaligned with the sources human Community Notes raters converge on -- a gap that closes through model-specific mechanisms of retrieval expansion or pruning. We further find substantial variation across language-domain slices and across the evidence ecosystems used by web-enabled systems. Beyond evaluation, CommunityFact positions Community Notes as a training signal for claim-conditioned source suggesters that could improve factual verification on novel claims.

</details>


### [259] [Resolution Diagnostics for Paired LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30315)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (44.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分辨率诊断方法，用于评估LLM排行榜中成对比较的统计功效。通过将成对LLM评估形式化为假设检验问题，计算每对比较的分辨率比率q=N/N*，发现Open LLM Leaderboard v1中11/40的成对比较和MMLU-Pro中4/9的相邻排名对在常规检验标准下未达到分辨率要求。研究还揭示了广泛使用的非配对Cohen-h-plus-(1-rho)捷径在接近比较场景下存在约两倍的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM排行榜广泛使用成对比较来排名模型，但许多比较缺乏足够的统计功效来可靠地检测差异，导致排名可能不可靠。需要一种系统的方法来诊断排行榜中成对比较的分辨率。

Method: 将成对LLM评估形式化为假设检验问题，反转水平α、功效(1-β)的检验，计算每对的分辨率比率q=N/N*。推导了小效应量下的二阶展开式，分析了非配对Cohen-h-plus-(1-rho)捷径的偏差，并在多重校正和任意有效序贯检验下验证了未解决对模式。

Result: Open LLM Leaderboard v1中11/40的成对比较和MMLU-Pro中4/9的相邻排名对在(α, 1-β)=(0.05, 0.8)下未达到分辨率要求。在真实主题级聚类下，MMLU-Pro未解决数升至6/9。非配对Cohen-h-plus-(1-rho)捷径在接近比较场景下偏差约两倍。

Conclusion: 许多LLM排行榜中的成对比较缺乏足够的统计功效，现有非配对捷径方法存在显著偏差，需要更严谨的统计诊断方法来确保排行榜排名的可靠性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM排行榜中成对比较的分辨率诊断问题，属于评测方法论，映射到[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM evaluation methodology, not on the user's priority generative or world model topics. Signals: LLM evaluation, statistical power, resolution diagnostics, not generative, not video/image/world model

Abstract: Across two public LLM leaderboards, many displayed pairwise rankings do not meet a conventional paired-test resolution target under the actual paired evaluation design: 11 of 40 Open LLM Leaderboard v1 pairwise comparisons and 4 of 9 MMLU-Pro top-10 adjacent-rank pairs are unresolved at (alpha, 1-beta) = (0.05, 0.8). The MMLU-Pro count rises to 6/9 under real subject-level clustering and stays at 5-6 out of 9 in 99.9% of category-bootstrap resamples. We frame paired LLM evaluation as a hypothesis-testing problem, invert level-alpha, power-(1-beta) tests, and report a per-pair resolution ratio q = N/N* as the primary diagnostic. A sharp small-effect expansion with an explicit second-order constant shows that the widely-used unpaired Cohen-h-plus-(1-rho) shortcut deviates from the correct N* by approximately a factor of two in the close-comparison regime, a deficit that three of five off-the-shelf calculators(Cohen 1988, G*Power, R pwr) silently inherit when the user post-multiplies their per-arm output by (1-rho). The unresolved-pair pattern remains under multiplicity correction and anytime-valid sequential testing.

</details>


### [260] [Code-QA-Bench: Separating Code Reasoning from Documentation Memorization in Repository-Level QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29277)
*Jun Zhang, JianYing Qu, Hanwen Du, Zhongkai Sun, Yehua Yang, Qiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Code-QA-Bench 是一个全自动框架，用于合成仓库级代码理解基准，通过"答案优先"生成流水线和三条件实验设计（闭卷、仅代码、完整文档），将真正的代码推理与文档记忆和预训练记忆分离。在10个Python仓库上生成528个代码可推导和100个文档依赖任务，实验表明代码访问是主要因素，文档提供有限增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级代码理解基准无法区分模型是真正理解代码结构，还是仅仅依赖文档记忆或预训练记忆，导致评测结果无法反映真实的代码推理能力。

Method: 提出全自动框架 Code-QA-Bench：(1) 答案优先生成流水线——工具增强的智能体先探索源码生成验证过的黄金答案，再推导问题；(2) 三条件实验设计——闭卷（无仓库）、仅代码（移除文档）、完整文档，通过差值量化文档效用和记忆影响。

Result: 在10个Python仓库上生成528个代码可推导和100个文档依赖任务。四个前沿模型实验显示：代码访问是主导因素（比闭卷平均提升+0.23），文档在文档依赖任务上提供适度增益（+0.071），代码可推导任务上仅代码≈完整文档。

Conclusion: Code-QA-Bench 有效分离了代码推理与文档记忆，验证了代码访问是仓库级代码理解的关键因素，框架开源且可应用于任何文档完善的Python仓库。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个用于仓库级代码理解的评测基准 Code-QA-Bench，属于多模态/代码理解评测范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准 (multimodal_benchmark) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on code QA benchmarks, not aligned with user's priority on generative models and efficient inference. Signals: Code reasoning benchmark, Repository-level QA, Documentation vs code access, Not generative model, No video/image generation

Abstract: We present Code-QA-Bench, a fully automated framework for synthesizing repository-level code understanding benchmarks that separates genuine code comprehension from documentation recall and pretraining memorization. The framework makes two methodological contributions: (1) an answer-first generation pipeline where a tool-equipped agent explores source code to produce verified gold answers before deriving questions, ensuring every task is grounded in real code structure; and (2) a three-condition experimental design evaluating agents under closed-book (no repository), code-only (documentation removed), and documented (full repository) conditions, with deltas directly quantifying documentation utility and memorization. We generate 528 code-derivable and 100 doc-dependent tasks across 10 Python repositories from SWE-Bench, scored by an LLM judge on accuracy, completeness, and specificity. Experiments on four frontier models reveal that code access is the dominant factor (+0.23 mean gain over closed-book), documentation provides modest additional benefit (+0.071 on doc-dependent tasks), and code-only $\approx$ documented on code-derivable tasks, validating the design. The framework is open-source and applicable to any well-documented Python repository.

</details>


### [261] [GUITestScape: Towards Open-set Evaluation on Exploratory GUI Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29532)
*Xiaoyi Chen, Yifei Gao, Yang Xu, Xingxing Song, Yi Zhang, Jitao Sang*

Main category: cs.SE, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (71.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GUITestScape是一个覆盖61个真实Android应用和508个预设缺陷的交互式基准，用于评估MLLM智能体在探索性GUI测试中的能力；同时提出GUIJudge开放集评估器，将测试轨迹分解为独立可诊断的能力维度，实现超越预定义标注的过程感知评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM智能体在探索性GUI测试中的评估存在两个不足：一是基准仅关注交互缺陷而忽略显示缺陷；二是评估协议依赖预定义缺陷标注，将测试过程简化为单一终态判断，混淆了不同性质的失败模式。

Method: 构建GUITestScape基准（61个Android应用、508个缺陷，涵盖交互和显示两类）；提出GUIJudge开放集评估器，将智能体的测试轨迹分解为多个独立可诊断的能力维度（如导航、检测、报告等），实现过程感知评估。

Result: GUIJudge在开放集评估上显著优于所有基线方法；基准测试表明检测能力是现有模型在两类缺陷上的关键瓶颈；将GUIJudge的验证器集成到现有智能体中无需重新训练即可显著提升检测性能。

Conclusion: GUITestScape和GUIJudge为MLLM智能体在探索性GUI测试中的评估提供了更全面、更细粒度的框架，揭示了检测瓶颈并展示了无需训练的改进路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向MLLM智能体在探索性GUI测试中的交互式基准GUITestScape和开放集评估器GUIJudge，属于多模态智能体评测范畴，与[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for GUI testing but outside core generative modeling priorities. Signals: MLLM agent evaluation, open-set GUI testing, process-aware evaluation, Android benchmark, interaction and display defects

Abstract: Exploratory GUI testing is a particularly demanding setting for MLLM agents: without predefined test scripts, an agent must autonomously navigate an application and discover defects through its own interaction. However, current evaluation falls short on two fronts. First, existing benchmarks focus almost exclusively on interaction defects, leaving display defects outside the evaluation frame. Second, evaluation protocols are bound to predefined defect annotations, collapsing the testing process into a single end-state judgment that conflates qualitatively distinct failure modes. To address these challenges, we present GUITestScape, an interactive benchmark covering 61 real-world Android applications and 508 preset defects spanning interaction and display types, and introduce GUIJudge, an open-set evaluator that decomposes an agent's testing trajectory into independently diagnosable capabilities. Experimental results demonstrate that GUIJudge achieves reliable process-aware evaluation beyond predefined annotations, substantially outperforming all baselines. Benchmarking on GUITestScape further reveals that detection remains the critical bottleneck for existing models across both defect types, and that integrating GUIJudge's verifiers into existing agents significantly boosts their detection performance without retraining.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-efficient_multimodal'></div>

## 多模态高效化与轻量化 [[Back]](#toc)

高效 MLLM、PEFT、蒸馏、量化、token 压缩、稀疏推理。

### [262] [Knowledge Offloading: Decomposing LLMs into Sparse Backbones and Memory Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29075)
*Karim Galliamov, Rochelle Choenni, Ivan Titov*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 普通相关 (61.98) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出知识卸载（KOFF）框架，将预训练LLM分解为稀疏共享主干和领域特定记忆模块。通过联合学习结构化剪枝掩码和轻量恢复模块（LoRA适配器+学习型KV缓存），在Llama和Qwen模型（3B-8B）上实现约12%全局稀疏度下保持大部分性能，且语言特定神经元优先被移除，语言通用神经元保留在主干中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM将通用能力和领域知识编码在同一组参数中，缺乏模块化组织。作者希望探索能否将知识重新组织：将广泛有用的计算保留在共享主干中，而将专门知识迁移到外部记忆模块中，从而实现更高效的知识管理和模型部署。

Method: 提出KOFF框架，从冻结的基础模型出发，联合学习结构化剪枝掩码和轻量恢复模块。恢复模块包括LoRA适配器和学习型KV缓存。通过剪枝移除冗余/领域特定参数，LoRA和学习型KV缓存补偿剪枝带来的性能损失，实现知识在共享主干和可交换外部记忆之间的重新分配。

Result: 在Llama和Qwen模型（3B-8B）上，约12%全局稀疏度下KOFF保留了未剪枝模型的大部分性能，而相同剪枝率下无记忆模块的模型性能急剧下降。消融实验表明LoRA和学习型KV缓存互补，专门化分析表明语言特定神经元优先被移除，语言通用神经元主要保留在主干中。

Conclusion: 知识可以在共享核心和可交换外部记忆之间重新分配，这为LLM的模块化分解和高效部署提供了新思路。KOFF框架证明了结构化剪枝结合轻量恢复模块是一种有效的知识卸载策略。

Classification reason: 该论文提出知识卸载（KOFF）框架，将预训练LLM分解为稀疏共享主干和外部记忆模块，通过结构化剪枝和LoRA适配器实现模型轻量化与高效化。这与多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）子主题高度相关，该子主题涵盖PEFT、蒸馏、量化、稀疏推理等效率优化方法。

Importance reason: Directly addresses model efficiency and modular decomposition, aligning with high-priority subtopics and boost keywords. Signals: model efficiency, efficient inference, compression, modular LLM, structured pruning

Abstract: LLMs encode both general capabilities and domain-specific knowledge in a single set of parameters. We ask whether this capacity can be reorganized: keeping broadly useful computation in a shared backbone, while moving specialized knowledge into external memory modules. We propose \emph{knowledge offloading} (KOFF), a framework for decomposing a pretrained LLM into a sparse shared backbone and domain-specific memories. Starting from a frozen base model, we jointly learn a structured pruning mask and lightweight recovery modules, implemented as LoRA adapters and learned key-value caches. Across Llama and Qwen models from 3B to 8B, we find that non-trivial capacity can be moved out of the shared backbone without a large loss in model ability. At around 12\% global sparsity, KOFF preserves much of the unpruned model's performance, while pruning the same frozen model without memories degrades sharply. Ablations show that LoRA and learned KV memories are complementary, and specialization analyses suggest that the learned decomposition is meaningful: language-specific neurons are preferentially removed while language-general neurons largely remain in the backbone. These results suggest that knowledge can be reallocated between a shared core and swappable external memories.

</details>


### [263] [Reasoning-preserved Efficient Distillation of Large Language Models via Activation-aware Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29327)
*Junlin He, Yihong Tang, Tong Nie, Guilong Li, Binyu Yang, Jinxiao Du, Lijun Sun, Wei Ma*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (65.22) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 RED（Reasoning-preserved Efficient Distillation），通过激活感知初始化（Activation-aware Initialization）解决现有高效蒸馏方法（EDistill）中存在的推理能力崩溃（reasoning collapse）问题。RED 将投影矩阵初始化为通道选择矩阵，从理论上缓解了有效秩坍塌（eRank collapse），在 Llama 和 Qwen 系列模型上显著恢复了多步推理能力，同时保持高效训练和通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效蒸馏方法（EDistill）通过结构化剪枝和轻量模块微调压缩 LLM，在通用能力基准上表现优异，但存在严重的多步推理能力退化（称为推理崩溃）。作者希望揭示这一问题的几何根源并提出解决方案。

Method: RED 方法提出激活感知初始化（Activation-aware Initialization），将投影矩阵初始化为通道选择矩阵，从而避免随机初始化导致的奇异值分布不均和有效秩坍塌（eRank collapse），从理论上保证隐藏表示的可区分性，保留多步推理能力。

Result: 在 Llama 和 Qwen 系列模型上的实验表明，RED 在保持高效训练和 SOTA 通用能力的同时，显著恢复了多步推理能力，有效缓解了推理崩溃问题。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了高效蒸馏中推理崩溃的几何根源——有效秩坍塌，并提出 RED 方法通过激活感知初始化有效缓解该问题，为 LLM 高效压缩中保留推理能力提供了理论指导和实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究大语言模型的高效蒸馏方法，属于模型轻量化与高效化范畴，与多模态理解与生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题"多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配，该子主题涵盖蒸馏、PEFT、量化等效率优化方法。

Importance reason: Directly addresses model efficiency and reasoning preservation in LLMs, aligning with high-priority subtopics and boosting efficient inference. Signals: model efficiency, efficient inference, reasoning collapse, activation-aware initialization, LLM compression

Abstract: Efficient Distillation (EDistill) compresses large language models (LLMs) by structured pruning parameters and tuning lightweight modules with high training efficiency. Although these EDistilled LLMs achieve state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on general ability benchmarks relative to similarly sized LLMs, we identify a severe degradation in their multi-step reasoning ability, which we term reasoning collapse. We systematically analyze the geometric origins of reasoning collapse and show that the SOTA EDistill method based on width-reducing projection matrices suffers from eRank collapse, in which the effective rank (eRank) of hidden representations drops. We theoretically explain how singular values of randomly initialized projection matrices become unevenly distributed, leading to eRank collapse and thus token indistinguishability. To address this issue, we propose RED (Reasoning-preserved Efficient Distillation) for LLMs, which introduces activation-aware initialization to initialize projection matrices as channel-selection matrices, thus theoretically mitigating eRank collapse. Experiments on Llama and Qwen series demonstrate that RED substantially recovers reasoning while maintaining high training efficiency and SOTA general ability.

</details>


### [264] [OccamToken: Efficient VLM Inference with Training-Free and Budget-Adaptive Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29657)
*Geng Li, Guohao Chen, Ting Chen, Shilin Shan, Kuangji Zuo, Bofan Lyu, Tuo An, Gen Li, Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (75.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OccamToken 提出一种无需训练的视觉 token 剪枝框架，通过寄存器锚定的相对证据测试替代传统的绝对排名剪枝范式，在 LLaVA-NeXT 等 VLM 上可将 2880 个视觉 token 压缩至约 40 个（保留 93%+ 准确率），显著提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 依赖长视觉 token 序列进行视觉理解，导致预填充阶段计算和内存开销巨大。已有的绝对排名剪枝范式存在根本缺陷：注意力汇聚会扭曲 token 重要性排名，且固定 token 预算无法适应不同输入。因此需要一种更鲁棒、自适应且无需额外训练的 token 剪枝方法。

Method: OccamToken 提出寄存器锚定的相对证据测试框架。核心洞察是寄存器 token 天然吸收低信息注意力模式，可作为稳定参考。方法包含两个阶段：(1) 图像自适应冗余剪枝——通过寄存器注意力动态阈值去除冗余视觉 token；(2) 查询自适应相关剪枝——根据查询与视觉 token 的交互进一步筛选与当前查询相关的 token。整个过程无需训练，阈值动态计算。

Result: 在 LLaVA-NeXT、LLaVA-v1.5 和 Qwen3-VL 上，OccamToken 一致地提升了准确率-效率权衡。在 LLaVA-NeXT 上，将 2880 个视觉 token 压缩至约 40 个（1.4% 保留率），仍保持 93% 以上的原始准确率。

Conclusion: OccamToken 通过寄存器锚定的相对证据测试，有效解决了绝对排名剪枝范式的缺陷，实现了训练无关、预算自适应的视觉 token 剪枝，为 VLM 高效推理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出训练无关的视觉 token 剪枝方法以加速 VLM 推理，属于多模态模型效率优化，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态高效化与轻量化" (efficient_multimodal) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Training-free token pruning for VLMs directly boosts efficient inference, a top priority subtopic, with strong results. Signals: efficient inference, training-free token pruning, VLM acceleration, budget-adaptive, register-anchored pruning

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) rely on long visual token sequences for visual understanding, making the prefill stage expensive in both computation and memory. Most existing pruning methods follow an absolute-ranking paradigm, assigning importance scores to visual tokens and retaining a fixed top-K subset. In this work, we argue that this paradigm is fundamentally brittle: attention sinks distort token importance rankings, while image redundancy and query-dependent visual evidence make fixed token budgets unreliable across inputs. We propose OccamToken, a training-free framework that replaces absolute token ranking with register-anchored relative evidence testing. Instead of asking which tokens are globally important, OccamToken evaluates whether a visual token provides information beyond a register-based reference. Our key insight is that register tokens naturally absorb low-information attention patterns, making them a stable reference for identifying genuinely informative visual evidence. Based on this principle, OccamToken performs both image-adaptive redundancy pruning and query-adaptive relevance pruning through dynamic thresholds derived from register attention. Across LLaVA-NeXT, LLaVA-v1.5, and Qwen3-VL, OccamToken consistently improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off without additional training. Notably, on LLaVA-NeXT, it reduces 2,880 visual tokens to approximately 40 while preserving over 93% of the original accuracy, enabling stable visual token compression even in the extreme 1.4% retention regime.

</details>


### [265] [SAFE-Pruner: Semantic Attention-Guided Future-Aware Token Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29662)
*Shilin Ma, Chubin Zhang, Changyuan Wang, Yuji Wang, Yue Wu, Zixuan Wang, Jingqi Tian, Zheng Zhu, Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (72.6) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SAFE-Pruner提出一种语义注意力引导的未来感知token剪枝框架，利用VLA模型在跨执行步骤中注意力集中在相同语义实体上的特性，通过前向预测深层token重要性并引入自适应子任务划分策略，实现高效且稳定的视觉token剪枝，在仿真和真实场景中达到1.89倍加速且成功率下降不到1.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人控制中需要实时推理，但现有视觉token剪枝方法大多基于浅层线索做剪枝决策，容易丢弃深层所需的关键视觉信息，导致加速不稳定或性能下降。

Method: 提出SAFE-Pruner框架：1）发现VLA模型存在"语义注意力一致性"现象，即模型在多个执行步骤中注意力概率质量集中在相同语义实体上；2）基于此设计前向预测策略，利用浅层注意力预测深层token重要性，避免过早移除关键token；3）引入自适应子任务划分策略，检测注意力突变点以提升预测准确性和剪枝可靠性。

Result: 在仿真和真实场景实验中，SAFE-Pruner实现最高1.89倍加速，成功率下降不到1.7%，优于现有SOTA方法最多1.9%。

Conclusion: SAFE-Pruner通过语义注意力一致性和前向预测策略，有效解决了VLA模型视觉token剪枝中浅层决策导致关键信息丢失的问题，实现了高效稳定的推理加速。

Classification reason: 该论文提出视觉token剪枝方法加速VLA模型推理，属于多模态模型高效化与轻量化方向，与[multimodal_understanding_generation]下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel token pruning for VLA models with strong efficiency gains, but outside core priority subtopics. Signals: token pruning, VLA acceleration, semantic attention consistency, 1.89x speedup, robotic manipulation

Abstract: Real-time inference of vision-language-action (VLA) models is essential for robotic control. While visual token pruning has shown strong potential for accelerating inference, most existing methods mainly base pruning decisions on shallow-layer cues and risk discarding visual information required by deep layers. To address this issue, we propose SAFE-Pruner, a plug-and-play pruning framework that incorporates attention cues of future layers into pruning decisions. Specifically, we identify semantic attention consistency, the tendency that VLA models concentrate their attention probability mass on the same semantic entity across execution steps. Based on this observation, we design a forward-looking strategy to forecast the token saliency in deep layers, which prevents the premature removal of critical tokens and leads to more stable acceleration. We further introduce an adaptive subtask division strategy to detect abrupt attention shifts, thereby improving forecasting accuracy and pruning reliability. Extensive experiments in simulation and real-world settings demonstrate that our method achieves up to 1.89x speedup with a minimal degradation in success rate of less than 1.7%, while outperforming state-of-the-art methods by up to 1.9%.

</details>


### [266] [Improving CLIP Adaptation by Breaking Tail Alignment for Source-Free Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29776)
*Shuai Yi, Yixiong Zou, Yuhua Li, Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 普通相关 (60.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现 CLIP 在跨域少样本场景下，对低相似度的"尾部"图像 token 强行对齐会导致过拟合，而主动推远这些 token 反而提升性能。基于此提出 ATHA 自适应头尾对齐策略，在四个基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP 等视觉语言模型在零样本场景表现良好，但在跨域少样本学习（CDFSL）中性能显著下降。现有微调方法对所有图像 patch token 统一进行文本对齐，但本文发现这种均匀对齐策略在域偏移大、训练数据少的情况下会导致过拟合，需要更精细的对齐策略。

Method: 提出 Adaptive Tail-Head Alignment (ATHA) 微调策略：将图像 patch token 分为"头部"（高相似度）和"尾部"（低相似度）两类，对头部 token 加强对齐以提取语义信息，对尾部 token 主动推远（打破对齐）以避免过拟合，实现自适应对齐范式。

Result: 在四个具有挑战性的跨域少样本学习基准上，ATHA 取得了 SOTA 性能，显著优于现有 CLIP 微调方法。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了 CLIP 在跨域少样本场景下"尾部 token 对齐有害"的反直觉现象，并证明自适应头尾对齐策略能有效缓解过拟合，为 CLIP 在数据稀缺的跨域场景下的微调提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 CLIP 在跨域少样本学习场景下的高效微调策略（ATHA），属于视觉语言模型的高效微调与适配方法，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）中的 PEFT 和高效适配方向高度吻合。

Importance reason: Novel insight on CLIP adaptation for cross-domain few-shot learning, but not directly aligned with top priority subtopics. Signals: CLIP adaptation, cross-domain few-shot learning, tail token alignment, overfitting mitigation, SOTA on benchmarks

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as CLIP demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization, but their performance significantly degrades in cross-domain scenarios with scarce target-domain training data (Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning, CDFSL). In this paper, we focus on the target-domain few-shot finetuning in the CLIP-based CDFSL task. Prevailing finetuning paradigms uniformly align all image patch tokens with their corresponding textual embeddings. However, we find a counterintuitive phenomenon: actively pushing away certain low-similarity image tokens, termed "tail tokens", from their textual embeddings consistently improves target-domain performance. We delve into this phenomenon and provide a novel interpretation: under great domain shifts and scarce training data, the model can hardly extract semantic information from visual inputs; therefore, the common belief of alignment is valid only for tokens already containing sufficient semantic information; for tail tokens, forcing the alignment would lead to excessive overfitting to the scarce training, while breaking the alignment is more useful. Motivated by this, we propose Adaptive Tail-Head Alignment (ATHA), a novel fine-tuning strategy for CLIP that transforms the conventional uniform alignment paradigm to an adaptive alignment paradigm, with both alignment strengthening and weakening. Extensive experiments on four challenging CDFSL benchmarks validate our state-of-the-art performance. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [267] [EarlyTom: Early Token Compression Completes Fast Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30010)
*Hesong Wang, Xin Jin, Lu Lu, Chenhaowen Li, Jian Chen, Qiang Liu, Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EarlyTom 是一种训练无关的早期 token 压缩框架，通过在视觉编码器内部进行早期视觉 token 压缩，显著降低视频大语言模型的首 token 延迟（TTFT）和计算量，同时保持与全 token 基线相当的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Video-LLM 在处理大量视觉 token 时效率低下，虽然已有方法在预填充后期进行 token 压缩，但视觉编码阶段本身占据了 TTFT 的很大一部分，这一阶段的效率优化尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出 EarlyTom 框架，在视觉编码器内部进行早期 token 压缩，包含两个关键设计：(1) 在视觉编码器的中间层插入压缩操作，提前减少 token 数量；(2) 引入解耦的空间 token 选择策略，提升压缩效果。该方法无需额外训练，可直接应用于现有模型。

Result: 在 LLaVA-OneVision-7B 模型上，EarlyTom 在单张 NVIDIA A100 GPU 上实现了高达 2.65 倍的 TTFT 降低和 61% 的 FLOPs 减少，同时保持了与全 token 基线相当的准确率。

Conclusion: EarlyTom 通过早期 token 压缩有效提升了 Video-LLM 的推理效率，使其在实际生产场景中的部署更加可行，为视频理解模型的高效推理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 EarlyTom，一种在视觉编码器内部进行早期 token 压缩的训练无关框架，旨在加速视频理解模型的推理效率（降低 TTFT 和 FLOPs）。这属于多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题，因为其核心贡献是 token 压缩以提升 MLLM 效率。

Importance reason: Directly addresses model efficiency for video understanding, a high-priority subtopic with strong practical deployment impact. Signals: training-free token compression, early encoder compression reduces TTFT, decoupled spatial token selection, strong efficiency gains with accuracy maintained, aligns with efficient inference priority

Abstract: Video large language models (Video-LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in video understanding tasks. However, their practical deployment is still hindered by the inefficiency introduced by processing massive amounts of visual tokens. Although recent approaches achieve extremely low token retention ratios while maintaining accuracy comparable to full-token baselines, most of them perform compression only at the late stage of prefilling, leaving the efficiency of the vision encoder unoptimized. In this paper, we first show that vision encoding contributes a large portion to the time-to-first-token (TTFT). Therefore, instead of compressing visual tokens only after the vision encoder, performing compression inside the encoder still leaves substantial room for exploration. Based on this insight, we propose EarlyTom, a training-free token compression framework that performs early-stage visual token compression inside the vision encoder, enabling significantly better TTFT reduction and higher throughput. In addition, we introduce a decoupled spatial token selection strategy that improves the overall compression effectiveness. EarlyTom reduces TTFT by up to 2.65x and FLOPs by up to 61% on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU for the LLaVA-OneVision-7B model, while maintaining accuracy comparable to the full-token baseline. These improvements substantially enhance the practicality of deploying Video-LLMs in real-world production scenarios.

</details>


### [268] [AsymVLM: Asymmetric Token Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29535)
*Yilin Feng, Ahmed Burak Gulhan, Mahmut Taylan Kandemir*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (76.35) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AsymVLM 提出一种非对称 token 剪枝方法，针对 VLM 中视觉 token 的空间冗余和文本 token 的因果依赖特性分别采用不同的压缩策略：视觉 token 在预填充阶段通过可学习重要性评分器进行自适应剪枝，文本 token 在解码阶段通过阈值驱逐控制缓存大小，在文档/图表理解任务上优于现有方法，FLOPs 节省最高达 54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM token 压缩方法对视觉和文本 token 采用统一处理，忽略了二者在冗余特性上的本质差异：视觉 token 空间冗余大且主要在预填充阶段消耗计算，文本 token 具有因果依赖且随解码累积。这种不对称性未被充分利用，导致压缩效率受限。

Method: AsymVLM 包含两个非对称模块：(1) 视觉 token 剪枝——使用可学习重要性评分器对每个视觉 token 打分，结合每样本自适应预算（根据图像复杂度动态决定保留数量），在预填充前进行激进剪枝；(2) 文本 token 驱逐——基于时间阈值的缓存驱逐策略，仅在文本 token 超过固定预算时丢弃早期 token，适应 VLM 短上下文特性。

Result: AsymVLM 在 FLOPs 节省上最高达 54%，优于现有 SOTA 方法；在文档和图表理解任务上比现有方法提升 2-3%；在整体性基准上保持竞争性精度；在文本主导场景中，其驱逐策略显著优于标准 LLM 缓存压缩方法。

Conclusion: 利用视觉与文本 token 的不对称特性设计差异化压缩策略，可以在大幅降低计算开销的同时保持甚至提升下游任务性能，为高效 VLM 推理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出视觉语言模型中的非对称 token 剪枝方法，属于多模态模型推理效率优化，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses VLM inference efficiency via asymmetric token pruning, aligning with model efficiency and unified multimodal priorities. Signals: efficient inference, token pruning, vision-language model, asymmetric compression, FLOPs reduction

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) process thousands of visual tokens per image alongside comparatively few text tokens, yet existing compression methods treat both modalities uniformly. We observe that the two modalities have fundamentally different properties: vision tokens are spatially redundant and dominate prefill, while text tokens are causally dependent and accumulate during decoding. Based on this asymmetry, we propose and empirically evaluate AsymVLM, which applies aggressive pruning to vision tokens before prefill using a learned importance scorer with per-sample adaptive budgeting, and temporal threshold-based eviction to text tokens only when they exceed a fixed budget. Our experiments indicate that AsymVLM achieves the highest FLOPs savings (up to 54%) among state-of-the-art methods while outperforming existing approaches by 2--3% on document and chart understanding tasks where visual information is spatially localized and query-specific, and maintaining competitive accuracy on holistic benchmarks. In text-dominated scenarios, our eviction strategy substantially outperforms standard LLM cache compression methods by adapting to the short-context nature of VLM.

</details>


### [269] [PARCEL: Pool-Anchored Resampling with Conditioned Elastic Queries for Efficient Vision-Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30126)
*Selim Kuzucu, Alessio Tonioni, Vasile Lup, Bernt Schiele, Federico Tombari, Muhammad Ferjad Naeem*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (73.1) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PARCEL提出了一种池化锚点重采样与条件弹性查询的视觉token化架构，通过将空间池化token作为低频布局锚点，并让弹性查询token基于这些锚点进行条件重采样，从而在视觉token压缩中兼顾空间细节与语义表达，在27个基准上优于现有弹性压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）将视觉输入映射为密集token序列，导致推理时二次复杂度瓶颈。现有弹性视觉token压缩方法在激进压缩下表现不佳：空间压缩（如嵌套池化）导致频谱混叠丢失细节，查询压缩（如嵌套查询重采样）则破坏空间定位能力。需要一种能同时保留空间细节和语义表达的高效视觉token化方案。

Method: PARCEL架构包含两个关键组件：(1) 空间池化token作为低频布局锚点，保留粗略的空间结构信息；(2) 池条件查询重采样（Pool-Conditioned Query Resampling），让弹性查询token以这些池化锚点为条件，学习互补的视觉特征而非冗余的空间映射。通过这种分工，池化token负责空间布局，查询token负责细节语义，实现"一次训练，任意部署"的弹性压缩。

Result: 在27个基准上的广泛评估表明，PARCEL在不同视觉token预算下均优于现有弹性压缩基线（matryoshka baselines），显著提升了性能-效率帕累托前沿，在激进压缩场景下尤其突出。

Conclusion: PARCEL通过池化锚点与条件弹性查询的分工设计，有效解决了弹性视觉token压缩中空间细节与语义表达之间的冲突，为高效视觉语言理解提供了一种更优的token化方案，保持了"一次训练，任意部署"的灵活性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出视觉token压缩方法PARCEL，通过池化锚点重采样与弹性查询实现高效视觉语言理解，属于多模态模型效率优化，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference in multimodal models, a priority subtopic, with a novel token compression method that improves the performance-efficiency trade-off. Signals: efficient inference, multimodal understanding, visual token compression, elastic queries, performance-efficiency Pareto frontier

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) map visual inputs into dense token sequences, imposing a quadratic computational bottleneck for inference. Elastic visual-token compression addresses this by training a single model that can run at multiple visual-token budgets. However, existing approaches struggle under aggressive compression. Spatial-only compression, as in nested pooling, behaves as an imperfect low-pass filter and induces spectral aliasing that obscures fine-grained detail. Query-only compression, as in nested query resampling, replaces explicit grid-aligned tokens with non-local summaries and substantially degrades spatial grounding. To resolve this representational conflict, we introduce PARCEL (Pool-Anchored Resampling with Conditioned Elastic Queries for Efficient Vision-Language Understanding), a visual tokenization architecture that dynamically partitions the labor of feature extraction. PARCEL establishes spatial pool tokens as low-frequency layout anchors and conditions elastic query tokens on these anchors through Pool-Conditioned Query Resampling. This encourages query tokens to focus on complementary visual features rather than redundant spatial mapping. Extensive evaluations across 27 benchmarks show that PARCEL improves the performance-efficiency Pareto frontier, consistently outperforming existing matryoshka baselines across visual-token budgets while preserving the "train once, deploy anywhere" paradigm.

</details>

### [270] [Reinforcement Learning with Robust Rubric Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30244)
*Ya-Qi Yu, Hao Wang, Fangyu Hong, Xiangyang Qu, Gaojie Wu, Qiaoyu Luo, Nuo Xu, Huixin Wang, Wuheng Xu, Yongxin Liao, Zihao Chen, Haonan Li, Ziming Li, Dezhi Peng, Minghui Liao, Jihao Wu, Haoyu Ren, Dandan Tu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (51.39) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出RLR^3，将可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）从任务级验证扩展到准则级验证，通过双路径执行rubric奖励（LLM提取器+确定性验证器与LLM裁判）、最小暴露策略和层次化聚合，在15个视觉语言基准上显著优于RLVR，提升4.7分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法仅适用于可确定性检查的任务（如数学、代码），但许多视觉语言任务只能部分验证，需要多准则监督（如感知细节、推理步骤、约束条件）。Rubric（评分准则）提供了细粒度监督的自然接口，但其在线RL中的执行准确性尚未被充分探索。

Method: RLR^3将rubric奖励通过两条路径执行：(1) LLM作为提取器+确定性验证器，用于可验证准则；(2) LLM作为裁判，用于不可验证准则。引入最小暴露策略：对提取器屏蔽真实答案，对裁判屏蔽图像，防止作弊。采用层次化聚合优先考虑必要准则而非附加准则，并在rollout组内缓解分数饱和问题。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B上评估15个基准，RLR^3一致优于RLVR，相比基础模型提升4.7分，超过官方instruct-to-thinking模型差距。受控审计证实确定性验证和最小暴露策略显著减少了可利用的假阳性。

Conclusion: RLR^3有效扩展了RLVR到部分可验证的视觉语言任务，通过鲁棒的rubric奖励设计实现了更可靠的细粒度监督，为多模态模型的强化学习对齐提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出RLR^3，将RLVR从任务级验证扩展到准则级验证，使用rubric奖励进行多准则监督，核心贡献在于奖励建模与对齐方法。该主题与multimodal_understanding_generation下的"生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配，该subtopic已涵盖RLHF、奖励建模、偏好优化等方向。同时该论文也涉及多模态生成（multimodal_generation）下的generation_alignment。

Importance reason: Focuses on RL reward design for vision-language tasks, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like autoregressive video generation or world models. Signals: RLVR extension to multi-criteria, rubric rewards for vision-language, Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B evaluation, 4.7-point improvement, minimal exposure strategy

Abstract: While Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is effective for deterministically checkable tasks, many vision-language tasks are partially verifiable, demanding multi-criteria supervision (e.g., perceptual details, reasoning steps, and constraints). Rubrics provide a natural interface for this fine-grained supervision, but their effectiveness depends on the execution accuracy during online RL. We propose Reinforcement Learning with Robust Rubric Rewards ($\text{RLR}^3$), extending RLVR from task-level verification to criterion-level verification. $\text{RLR}^3$ routes instance-specific rubrics through two execution paths: an LLM-as-an-extractor paired with a deterministic verifier, or an LLM-as-a-Judge for non-verifiable criteria. To ensure faithful scoring, $\text{RLR}^3$ introduce a minimal exposure strategy that masks ground truths from extractors and images from judges. Furthermore, $\text{RLR}^3$ employs hierarchical aggregation to prioritize essential criteria over additional criteria, and mitigates score saturation within rollout groups. Evaluated on Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B across 15 benchmarks, $\text{RLR}^3$ consistently outperforms RLVR, yielding a 4.7-point improvement over the base model and exceeding the official instruct-to-thinking model gap. Controlled audits confirm our deterministic verification and minimal exposure significantly reduce exploitable false positives.

</details>
