### [271] [Planning with the Views via Scene Self-Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29563)
*Kangrui Wang, Linjie Li, Zhengyuan Yang, Shiqi Chen, Zihan Wang, Li Fei-Fei, Jiajun Wu, Leonidas Guibas, Lijuan Wang, Manling Li*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (49.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出ViewSuite基准和自探索框架，发现VLM在3D场景中具备基础视角变换知识但缺乏多步规划能力，通过自探索与视图图蒸馏将Qwen2.5-VL-7B的交互式视角规划准确率从2.5%提升至47.8%，超越GPT-4.5 Pro和Gemini 2.5 Pro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在静态视觉理解任务上表现优异，但在需要主动推理和多步规划的3D空间任务（如视角规划）中能力不足。作者希望系统性地评估VLM的视角规划能力，并探索如何通过自探索来弥补这一规划鸿沟。

Method: 提出ViewSuite基准（基于ScanNet真实场景的3D点云环境），评估13个前沿VLM的视角规划能力。提出迭代式自探索框架：VLM在3D场景中自由探索，所有探索轨迹（无论成功与否）构成视图图（view graph），通过蒸馏该图生成多样化的监督训练任务，重塑策略分布，克服纯RL的稀疏奖励问题。

Result: Qwen2.5-VL-7B从2.5%提升至47.8%，超越GPT-4.5 Pro（18.5%）和Gemini 2.5 Pro（21.4%）。消融实验验证了视图图蒸馏和自探索迭代的关键作用。

Conclusion: 自探索是提升VLM在3D空间中主动推理与规划能力的有效路径，视图图蒸馏能够将探索经验转化为可学习的监督信号，显著缩小VLM在视角规划上的能力鸿沟。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM在3D场景中的视角规划能力，涉及空间推理与多步规划，属于[空间与3D多模态推理]（spatial_3d_reasoning）这一CANONICAL子主题。同时论文涉及3D场景理解与生成式探索，也匹配多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Strong on spatial reasoning and VLM planning but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: VLM view planning, 3D spatial reasoning, self-exploration framework, planning gap in VLMs, ScanNet benchmark

Abstract: Can VLMs predict how each camera move changes the view, and plan many such moves ahead? We call this capability view planning, requiring (1)understanding how a single action transforms the view, and (2)composing many such transformations across multi-turn plans to identify a target view. We probe both abilities in our proposed ViewSuite, a 3D point-cloud environment on real ScanNet scenes. Across 13 frontier VLMs, a critical planning gap emerges: they possess basic view-action knowledge but fail to compose it across multi-turn plans, with the gap widening as viewpoint distance grows. To close this gap, we propose an iterative framework that alternates self-exploration with view graph distillation. The key insight is that all exploration trajectories, regardless of their outcome, collectively form a view graph that compactly captures how viewpoints connect across a scene. Distilling this graph into diverse supervised tasks reshapes the policy distribution and overcomes the sparse rewards that stall pure RL. This improves Qwen2.5-VL-7B from 2.5% to 47.8% on interactive view planning, surpassing GPT-5.4 Pro (18.5%) and Gemini 3.1 Pro (21.4%). Self-exploration emerges as a promising path toward VLMs that can actively reason and plan in 3D space.

</details>


### [272] [Learning Context-Conditioned Predicate Semantics via Prototype Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29610)
*NamGyu Jung, Chang Choi*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 AlignG，通过原型反馈学习上下文条件化的谓词语义，解决场景图生成中谓词多义性问题。该方法从图像中的关系候选中推断上下文条件化的谓词语义，并反馈以重新校准关系表示，同时通过全局语义中心锚定防止语义漂移。在 VG-150 和 GQA-200 上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景图生成中，谓词（如"on"、"next to"）的含义会随上下文变化（多义性），现有方法将谓词分解为多个静态原型或检索相似样本，但无法根据图像具体证据动态重组语义，导致歧义场景下的系统性混淆。

Method: AlignG 包含两个核心模块：(1) 上下文条件化语义推断模块，从图像的关系候选中学习动态的谓词语义表示；(2) 原型反馈机制，将推断出的上下文语义反馈回关系表示进行重新校准。训练时通过全局语义中心锚定损失防止语义漂移，同时允许在场景提供一致关系线索时选择性重组语义。

Result: 在 VG-150 和 GQA-200 数据集上，SGDet 任务中 F@100 分别提升 +1.4 和 +2.7，超越现有 SOTA 方法。可视化分析显示原型相似度发生上下文相关的重组，谓词原型根据场景证据选择性合并或分离。

Conclusion: AlignG 通过上下文条件化的动态谓词语义学习有效解决了场景图生成中的谓词多义性问题，验证了原型反馈机制在语义重组中的有效性，为场景理解中的语义歧义问题提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究场景图生成（Scene Graph Generation），属于视觉场景理解与结构化推理任务，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "空间与 3D 多模态推理"（spatial_3d_reasoning）高度相关，因为场景图生成涉及对图像中物体间空间关系和语义关系的推理。

Importance reason: Scene graph generation is a niche subtask with limited direct impact on the user's priority topics like video generation or world models. Signals: scene graph generation, predicate polysemy, prototype feedback, VG-150, GQA-200

Abstract: In scene graph generation, a central challenge is modeling polysemous predicates whose meanings shift across contexts. Prior approaches address this issue by decomposing predicates into multiple static prototypes or retrieving semantically similar exemplars. However, these strategies keep predicate representations static and cannot reorganize semantics to reflect image-specific evidence, leading to systematic confusions in ambiguous contexts. We propose AlignG, which learns context-conditioned predicate semantics via prototype feedback. AlignG infers context-conditioned predicate semantics from the relation candidates within each image and feeds the adapted semantics back to recalibrate relation representations. The learning objective anchors this adaptation to global semantic centers, preventing semantic drift while still allowing selective reorganization when the scene provides consistent relational cues. Experiments on VG-150 and GQA-200 show consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines, with F@100 improvements of +1.4 on VG-150 and +2.7 on GQA-200 under SGDet. We further visualize per-image prototype similarity shifts and observe coherent context-dependent reorganization where prototypes selectively merge or separate predicates according to scene evidence. The code is available at .

</details>


### [273] [Why Far Looks Up: Probing Spatial Representation in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30161)
*Cheolhong Min, Jaeyun Jung, Daeun Lee, Hyeonseong Jeon, Yu Su, Jonathan Tremblay, Chan Hee Song, Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (57.05) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种表征级分析框架，通过构建最小对比对来测量VLM嵌入中空间轴的组织与解缠程度。发现模型普遍存在垂直-距离纠缠偏差（将图像垂直位置与距离混淆），该偏差随数据规模扩大而加剧，且即使基准分数相似的模型其内部表征也可能不同。作者还引入合成基准SpatialTunnel以隔离该偏差，证明良好分离的空间轴能带来更鲁棒的空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLM在空间推理基准上表现良好，但尚不清楚这种能力是否源于结构化的3D理解，还是仅仅利用了自然图像中的统计捷径。现有评估方法无法区分模型是否真正理解了空间关系，因此需要从表征层面深入分析VLM内部空间轴的编码方式。

Method: 提出表征级分析框架，通过构建最小对比对（minimal contrastive pairs）来测量VLM嵌入中空间轴（如垂直、水平、深度）的组织与解缠程度。引入SpatialTunnel合成基准，通过去除自然图像中的常见相关性来暴露空间捷径偏差。在多个模型家族上进行系统分析。

Result: 发现所有模型均存在一致的垂直-距离纠缠偏差：模型将图像中的垂直位置与距离混淆，反映了自然照片的透视偏差。该偏差导致透视一致样本与反启发式样本之间存在显著的准确率差距，且随数据规模扩大而加剧。基准分数相似的模型可能具有不同的内部表征，而这些差异能预测模型在不同空间推理基准上的准确率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VLM中的空间表征存在固有的垂直-距离纠缠偏差，该偏差是模型内在的而非评估集偏差所致。具有良好分离空间轴的模型展现出更强的鲁棒性，表明结构化的空间表征有助于更可靠的空间推理。这项工作为理解VLM的空间推理机制提供了新视角，并为改进模型的空间理解能力指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM中的空间表征，分析垂直位置与距离的混淆偏差，属于空间推理范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "空间与3D多模态推理" (spatial_3d_reasoning) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Probing spatial representation in VLMs is methodologically sound but not aligned with user's priority on generative models and world models. Signals: spatial reasoning probing, vertical-distance entanglement, representation analysis, synthetic benchmark, not generative or world model

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong performance on spatial reasoning benchmarks, yet it remains unclear whether this reflects structured 3D understanding or reliance on statistical shortcuts in natural images. We introduce a representation-level analysis framework that constructs minimal contrastive pairs to measure how spatial axes are organized and disentangled within VLM embeddings. Our analysis across multiple model families reveals a consistent vertical-distance entanglement: models conflate vertical image position with distance, mirroring the perspective bias of natural photographs. This bias produces a significant accuracy gap between perspective-consistent and counter-heuristic examples, and intensifies under data scaling even as overall benchmark accuracy improves. We further show that models with similar benchmark scores can exhibit different internal representations, and that these differences predict accuracy and robustness across diverse spatial reasoning benchmarks. To isolate this bias from evaluation-set skew, we introduce SpatialTunnel, a synthetic benchmark designed to expose spatial shortcut biases by removing common correlations present in natural images. Experiments confirm that the entanglement is model-intrinsic, and that models with well-separated spatial axes exhibit greater robustness, suggesting that well-structured spatial representations lead to more reliable spatial reasoning across diverse benchmarks. Code and benchmark are available on the project page: .

</details>


### [274] [Beyond 3D VQAs: Injecting 3D Spatial Priors into Vision-Language Models for Enhanced Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30231)
*Chun-Hsiao Yeh, Shengyi Qian, Manchen Wang, Yi Ma, Joseph Tighe, Fanyi Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (64.4) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出GASP框架，通过向VLM的Transformer层注入3D几何先验（点对应和深度一致性监督），在不使用任何3D VQA数据的情况下，显著提升了VLM的3D空间推理能力，在多个基准上取得大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在3D空间推理上表现不佳，主流方法要么依赖3D VQA数据集微调导致过拟合数据集偏差，要么集成专用3D视觉编码器导致灵活性差。作者认为真正的空间理解应来自学习基础几何先验而非高层VQA监督。

Method: 提出GASP框架：1）在LLM的所有Transformer层上附加一个小型对应头（correspondence head）作为深度监督信号；2）利用大规模视频场景的真实几何信息设计双目标训练——对比损失强制2D视角不变性（点对应匹配），深度一致性损失解决3D几何歧义；3）训练后移除对应头，模型可直接用于下游任务。

Result: 标准VLM内部对应匹配准确率低于5%，GASP将峰值层对应提升至70%以上，时间鲁棒性保持85%以上（基线低于5%）。在All-Angles Bench上提升+18.2%，在VSI-Bench上提升+29.0%，且未使用任何3D VQA训练数据。

Conclusion: 从基础几何先验中学习是提升VLM 3D空间推理能力的一种有前景且可泛化的路径，证明了注入几何先验比依赖高层VQA监督更有效。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于提升视觉语言模型（VLM）的3D空间几何推理能力，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的空间与3D多模态推理（spatial_3d_reasoning）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，直接匹配其定义和关键词。

Importance reason: Boosts VLM 3D reasoning via geometric priors, aligning with unified multimodal and efficient inference priorities. Signals: 3D spatial reasoning, vision-language model, geometric prior, point correspondence, depth consistency

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often struggle with robust 3D spatial reasoning. Prevailing methods that rely on fine-tuning with 3D visual question-answering (VQA) datasets may overfit dataset-specific biases, while integrating specialized 3D visual encoders is often inflexible and cumbersome. In this paper, we argue that genuine spatial understanding should emerge from learning fundamental geometric priors, not only from high-level VQA supervision. We propose GASP (Geometric-Aware Spatial Priors), a framework that injects these priors directly into the LLM's transformer layers. GASP employs a small correspondence head, applied as a deep supervision signal across all layers, and is trained with a dual objective leveraging ground-truth geometry from large-scale video scenes: a contrastive loss on ground-truth point correspondences enforces 2D view-invariance, while a depth consistency supervision resolves 3D geometric ambiguities. Our analysis first provides a diagnostic showing that standard VLMs' internal correspondence matching accuracy is very low (often below 5%). We then demonstrate that our training substantially improves this behavior, boosting peak layer-wise correspondence to over 70% and maintaining over 85% temporal robustness while baselines remain below 5%. These internal improvements translate to significant gains on downstream spatial benchmarks including +18.2% on All-Angles Bench and +29.0% on VSI-Bench, all without training on any 3D VQA data. Our findings indicate that learning from fundamental geometric priors is a promising and generalizable pathway towards VLMs with more reliable 3D spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [275] [Grounded 3D-Aware Spatial Vision-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30307)
*An-Chieh Cheng, Yang Fu, Yatai Ji, Ligeng Zhu, Guanqi Zhan, Zhuoyang Zhang, Zhaojing Yang, Song Han, Yao Lu, Pavlo Molchanov, Vidya Nariyambut Murali, Jan Kautz, Xiaolong Wang, Hongxu Yin, Sifei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (62.45) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GR3D 是一个空间视觉语言模型，在同一框架内集成了显式 2D 定位、隐式 2D 定位和单目 3D 定位三种互补的定位能力。隐式定位机制在生成空间思维链响应时动态插入区域 token 引用视觉证据；区域提示的单目 3D 定位设计从定位区域查询预测相机视角下的 3D 边界框。GR3D 在定位和非定位空间基准上均取得一致改进，证明定位可作为增强 VLM 空间理解的有效归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在空间理解方面存在不足，尤其是缺乏将 2D 视觉感知与 3D 空间推理有效结合的能力。作者希望利用定位（grounding）作为归纳偏置，增强 VLM 的空间理解能力。

Method: GR3D 提出三种互补定位机制：(1) 显式 2D 定位——标准区域级定位；(2) 隐式 2D 定位——在生成空间思维链响应时自动识别实体提及并插入对应区域 token，使模型能动态引用视觉证据；(3) 单目 3D 定位——从定位区域查询出发，结合内参归一化和密集几何监督，预测相机视角下的 3D 边界框。三者统一在单一框架中，将复杂空间理解问题分解为定位的 2D 感知加 3D 推理。

Result: GR3D 在多个定位和非定位空间基准上均取得一致改进，证明了定位作为增强 VLM 空间理解归纳偏置的有效性。这些定位能力共同提升了超越定位任务本身的通用空间理解能力。

Conclusion: 定位（grounding）是一种有效的归纳偏置，能够显著增强 VLM 的空间理解能力。将 2D 定位与 3D 推理结合的统一框架为空间 VLM 提供了新的发展方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 GR3D，一个具备显式 2D 定位、隐式 2D 定位和单目 3D 定位能力的空间视觉语言模型，专注于空间理解与 3D 推理，属于 [CANONICAL] 空间与 3D 多模态推理 (spatial_3d_reasoning) 子主题。

Importance reason: 该工作将显式与隐式2D定位及单目3D定位统一于VLM框架，显著增强空间理解，与用户对统一多模态模型和高效推理的高优先级高度契合。 Signals: 统一多模态模型, 空间推理, 3D定位, 隐式定位, 视觉语言模型

Abstract: We present GR3D, a spatial vision language model equipped with three complementary grounding capabilities--explicit 2D grounding, implicit 2D grounding, and monocular 3D grounding--within a single framework. GR3D introduces an implicit grounding mechanism that identifies entity mentions during generation and inserts the corresponding region tokens into the text stream, allowing the model to reference visual evidence on the fly when producing spatial chain-of-thought responses. In parallel, a region-prompted monocular 3D grounding design predicts 3D bounding boxes in the camera view from grounded region queries, supported by intrinsic-aware normalization and dense geometric supervision. Together, these grounding capabilities enable GR3D to decompose complex spatial understanding problems into grounded 2D perception followed by 3D inference. GR3D achieves consistent improvements across grounded and non-grounded spatial benchmarks, demonstrating grounding as an effective inductive bias for strengthening spatial understanding in VLMs. These grounding capabilities collectively enhance general spatial understanding beyond the grounding task itself.

</details>


### [276] [3DVLA: Enhancing Vision-Language-Action Models via 3D Spatial and Instance Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29416)
*Zhongyu Xia, Yousen Tang, Bingqing Wei, Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (51.39) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 3DVLA是一个即插即用的框架，通过3D空间特征编码（多视图一致性约束+空间条件几何聚合）、实例估计模块和掩码自监督3D编码分支，为预训练视觉-语言-动作模型注入3D推理能力，在LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0上显著提升机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏3D场景理解能力，表现为三个相互关联的挑战：缺乏多视图一致性的3D空间位置提取能力不足、3D实例理解不充分、遮挡下推理脆弱。虽然已有成熟的3D感知方法，但直接集成到VLA流程中面临架构不兼容和对昂贵实例级标注的依赖问题。

Method: 3DVLA包含三个核心模块：(1) 全模态3D特征编码，通过显式的多视图一致性约束和空间条件几何聚合方法增强3D空间理解；(2) 实例估计模块，利用高层级实例token实现3D实例感知；(3) 掩码自监督3D编码分支，保留预测器进行视觉token补全以处理遮挡问题。该框架即插即用，无需额外人工标注或丢弃VLM先验知识。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0数据集上，3DVLA集成到多个VLA基线模型中均取得一致且显著的操作性能提升，验证了其有效性和即插即用兼容性。

Conclusion: 3DVLA通过注入3D空间和实例理解能力有效增强了VLA模型的3D推理能力，且无需额外标注，是一种通用且有效的即插即用框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出3DVLA框架，通过3D空间特征编码、实例理解和遮挡推理增强VLA模型的3D场景理解能力，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的空间与3D多模态推理（spatial_3d_reasoning）子主题。

Importance reason: Strong 3D spatial reasoning for VLA, but outside core generative video/image priorities. Signals: 3D spatial understanding, vision-language-action, robotic manipulation, plug-and-play framework, multi-view consistency

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models have achieved remarkable progress in robotic manipulation, yet they suffer from a critical limitation: a lack of 3D scene understanding. This deficiency manifests as three intertwined challenges: weak extraction of 3D spatial positions without enforcing multi-view consistency, inadequate 3D instance understanding, and fragile reasoning under occlusion. Although mature 3D perception methods exist, their direct integration into VLA pipelines is hindered by architectural incompatibility and by heavy reliance on costly instance-level annotations. To address the above challenges, we propose 3DVLA, a plug-and-play framework that injects robust 3D reasoning into pretrained VLAs without requiring extra manual labels or discarding VLM priors. Specifically, 3DVLA tackles the three challenges through: (1) pervasive 3D feature encoding with explicit multi-view consistency constraints across all modalities and a Spatially-Conditioned Geometry Aggregation method, (2) an instance estimation module with high-level instance tokens for 3D instance awareness, and (3) a masked self-supervised 3D encoding branch that retains its predictor for visual token completion to handle occlusions. We integrate 3DVLA with multiple VLA baselines and evaluate on LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0. Results show consistent and significant gains in manipulation performance, validating both the effectiveness and plug-and-play compatibility of our approach.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-unified_multimodal_model'></div>

## 统一多模态模型 [[Back]](#toc)

理解与生成一体化、统一 token 表征、Any-to-Any 多模态模型。

### [277] [Qwen-VLA: Unifying Vision-Language-Action Modeling across Tasks, Environments, and Robot Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30280)
*Qiuyue Wang, Mingsheng Li, Jian Guan, Jinhui Ye, Sicheng Xie, Yitao Liu, Junhao Chen, Zhixuan Liang, Jie Zhang, Xintong Hu, Xuhong Huang, Pei Lin, Junyang Lin, Dayiheng Liu, Shuai Bai, Jingren Zhou, Jiazhao Zhang, Haoqi Yuan, Gengze Zhou, Hang Yin, Ye Wang, Yiyang Huang, Zixing Lei, Wujian Peng, Delin Chen, Yingming Zheng, Jingyang Fan, Xianwei Zhuang, Xin Zhou, Haoyang Li, Anzhe Chen, Tong Zhang, Xuejing Liu, Yuchong Sun, Ruizhe Chen, Zhaohai Li, Chenxu Lü, Zhibo Yang, Tao Yu, Xionghui Chen*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (83.75) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Qwen-VLA 是一个统一的具身基础模型，将视觉-语言理解与连续动作/轨迹生成整合到单一框架中，通过 DiT 动作解码器和具身感知提示，在操作、导航和轨迹预测等多个任务上实现跨机器人形态、环境和场景的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能研究通常针对操作、导航等单一任务开发专用模型，导致能力碎片化，缺乏跨任务、跨环境和跨机器人形态的泛化能力。本文探索是否可以将异构的具身决策问题统一到一个视觉-语言-动作模型中。

Method: Qwen-VLA 基于 Qwen 的视觉-语言模型堆栈，扩展了一个 DiT（Diffusion Transformer）动作解码器用于连续动作和轨迹生成。采用大规模联合预训练策略，融合机器人操作轨迹、人类第一人称演示、合成仿真数据、视觉-语言导航数据、轨迹监督数据和辅助视觉-语言数据。引入具身感知提示（embodiment-aware prompt conditioning），通过机器人特定的文本描述指定当前形态和控制约定，将操作、导航和轨迹预测统一为动作-轨迹预测框架。

Result: Qwen-VLA-Instruct 在多个基准上取得优异结果：LIBERO 97.9%、Simpler-WidowX 73.7%、RoboTwin-Easy/Hard 86.1%/87.2%、R2R OSR 69.0%、RxR SR 59.6%、真实世界 ALOHA 实验平均 OOD 成功率 76.9%、DOMINO 动态操作零样本成功率 26.6%。

Conclusion: Qwen-VLA 证明了异构具身决策问题可以统一到单个视觉-语言-动作模型中，在操作、导航和轨迹预测任务上展现出跨场景布局、背景、光照、物体配置和机器人形态的分布外泛化能力，为具身智能的统一建模提供了有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Qwen-VLA，将视觉-语言理解与连续动作/轨迹生成统一到一个模型中，属于理解与生成一体化的统一多模态模型。该方向在 multimodal_understanding_generation 和 multimodal_generation 下均有 unified_multimodal_model 子主题，且为 [CANONICAL] 子主题，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: Unifies vision-language-action into a single model with strong cross-embodiment generalization, directly aligning with the priority on unified multimodal models and boosting keywords. Signals: unified multimodal model, vision-language-action, cross-embodiment generalization, DiT action decoder, strong benchmark results

Abstract: Embodied intelligence is often studied through specialized models for individual tasks such as manipulation or navigation, resulting in fragmented capabilities and limited generalization across tasks, environments, and robot embodiments. In this work, we study whether heterogeneous embodied decision-making problems can be unified within a single vision-language-action model. We present Qwen-VLA, a unified embodied foundation model that extends Qwen's vision-language modeling stack from perception, understanding, and reasoning to continuous action and trajectory generation through a DiT-based action decoder. Qwen-VLA is trained with a large-scale joint pretraining recipe over diverse data sources, including robotics manipulation trajectories, human egocentric demonstrations, synthetic simulation data, vision-and-language navigation data, trajectory-centric supervision, and auxiliary vision-language data. To support multiple robot platforms, we introduce embodiment-aware prompt conditioning, where robot-specific textual descriptions specify the current embodiment and control convention. We further cast manipulation, navigation, and trajectory prediction into a unified action-and-trajectory prediction framework, enabling transferable visual grounding, spatial reasoning, and continuous action generation across robot morphologies, task families, and environments. Experiments on manipulation, navigation, and trajectory-centric benchmarks show consistent multi-task performance and out-of-distribution generalization under variations in scene layout, background, lighting, object configuration, and robot embodiment. Qwen-VLA-Instruct achieves 97.9% on LIBERO, 73.7% on Simpler-WidowX, 86.1%/87.2% on RoboTwin-Easy/Hard, 69.0% OSR on R2R, 59.6% SR on RxR, 76.9% average OOD success in real-world ALOHA experiments, and 26.6% zero-shot success on DOMINO dynamic manipulation.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-vlm_mllm_general'></div>

## 视觉语言模型与 MLLM [[Back]](#toc)

通用视觉语言模型、MLLM 架构、多模态指令微调与对话。

### [278] [Analyzing Persona Effects in Generated Explanations from Multimodal LLM Agents in Urban Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29064)
*Neemias da Silva, Myriam Delgado, Rodrigo Minetto, Daniel Silver, Thiago H Silva*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.MA, cs.HC, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 普通相关 (62.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多模态大语言模型在城市感知任务中，不同 persona（角色）提示对生成文本（标题、理由、感知标签）的影响。实验使用 1200 个 persona 条件智能体和两种无 persona 设置，分析了 59,808 条标注。结果表明：不同 persona 生成的标题高度趋同，但理由文本呈现与 socio-economic 和政治属性相关的系统性差异，感知标签则无统计显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型被越来越多地用于生成解释性文本，但 persona（角色）提示如何影响生成内容的风格、内容和偏见尚不清楚。理解这种影响对于确保 AI 生成内容的公平性和可靠性至关重要。

Method: 使用 1,200 个不同 persona 条件（结合社会经济、政治等属性）的 MLLM 智能体，以及两种无 persona 基线设置，对城市场景图像生成标题、理由和感知标签。通过文本相似度分析、统计检验和主题分析等方法，量化不同 persona 对生成文本的影响。

Result: 标题在不同 persona 间高度趋同；理由文本呈现与 socio-economic 和政治属性相关的系统性差异；感知标签无统计显著差异但存在趋势性效应；主题分析显示不同 persona 在解释同一场景时强调不同的评价主题。

Conclusion: Persona 提示对 MLLM 生成解释的影响因文本类型而异：简短描述性文本（标题）受 persona 影响小，而较长解释性文本（理由）受 persona 影响显著。这提示在需要公平性和一致性的应用场景中，应谨慎使用 persona 提示。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中 persona prompting 对生成解释文本的影响，属于通用 MLLM 的行为分析与 prompt 研究，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on persona effects in MLLM explanations for urban perception, which is niche and not aligned with user's priority subtopics on generation or efficiency. Signals: multimodal LLM, persona prompting, urban perception, text analysis, not autoregressive or diffusion generation

Abstract: We study how persona prompting shapes language generated by multimodal large language models in an urban perception setting. Using 59,808 annotations from 1,200 persona-conditioned agents and two no-persona settings, we analyze captions, justifications, and perception tags across personas. Results indicate strong convergence in captions for different personas, whereas justifications display systematic variation associated with socioeconomic and political attributes, while perception tags show no statistically significant persona-related differences, though effect trends are observed. Topic analysis further reveals that personas emphasize different evaluative themes when interpreting the same scenes.

</details>


### [279] [FlowSeg: Dynamic Semantic Guidance for LLM-Conditioned Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29461)
*Zekang Zhang, Guangyu Gao, Youyun Tang, ChengJing Wu, Xiaochao Qu, Chi Harold Liu, Jianbo Jiao, Yunchao Wei, Luoqi Liu, Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (79.1) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FlowSeg提出了一种动态语义引导机制，通过双向语义流让LLM条件嵌入与掩码解码过程在每个阶段相互更新，解决了现有"先提议后选择"分割管线中语言条件与生成掩码语义错位的问题，在指代表达分割和推理分割任务上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM条件化分割方法采用"先提议后选择"（propose-then-select）管线，虽然能生成高质量掩码候选，但最终预测常与给定的语言条件不匹配。作者通过系统分析发现，许多错误源于语义错位而非掩码质量差——语言语义通常仅作为静态提示或事后匹配信号，未参与迭代掩码生成过程。

Method: FlowSeg引入双向语义流机制：(1) 语言条件在掩码生成的每个阶段主动引导掩码细化；(2) 条件嵌入通过不断涌现的视觉证据逐步更新。此外，还引入轻量级边界感知细化模块，选择性增强不确定区域而不扰动置信区域内部。

Result: 在指代表达分割和推理分割任务上的大量实验表明，FlowSeg持续改善了语言-掩码对齐，并取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过让语言条件动态参与掩码生成过程而非仅作为静态提示，FlowSeg有效解决了语义错位问题，验证了双向语义流在LLM条件化分割中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM条件化的图像分割（指代表达分割与推理分割），属于视觉语言模型（VLM/MLLM）的典型应用场景。论文提出的FlowSeg通过双向语义流将LLM条件嵌入与掩码解码过程动态交互，属于MLLM架构与多模态指令理解范畴，因此归入[CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general"（视觉语言模型与MLLM）。

Importance reason: Advances LLM-conditioned segmentation with dynamic semantic guidance, relevant to multimodal understanding but not a top priority subtopic. Signals: multimodal_understanding_generation, visual language model, semantic alignment, state-of-the-art segmentation, no autoregressive or video generation focus

Abstract: LLM-conditioned segmentation has recently advanced rapidly by coupling large language models with iterative mask generation frameworks. However, we identify a persistent failure mode in current propose-then-select pipelines. Although high-quality mask candidates are often generated, the final prediction may fail to match the given linguistic condition. This failure arises because language semantics are typically used as static prompts or post-hoc matching signals, rather than participating in the iterative mask generation process. Through systematic analysis, we show that many errors stem from semantic misalignment rather than poor mask quality. To address this issue, we propose FlowSeg, which introduces dynamic semantic guidance via a bidirectional semantic flow between intermediate decoding states and LLM-derived condition embeddings throughout the generation process. Language conditions actively guide mask refinement at each stage, while condition embeddings are progressively updated by emerging visual evidence. This design yields semantically grounded mask representations and visually aligned language conditions, enabling more reliable matching. We further incorporate a lightweight boundary-aware refinement to selectively enhance uncertain regions without perturbing confident interiors. Extensive experiments on referring expression segmentation and reasoning segmentation tasks demonstrate that FlowSeg consistently improves language-mask alignment and achieves state-of-the-art performance. Project page:

</details>


### [280] [Mitigating State Aliasing in Vision-Language-Action Models via Inverse Dynamics Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29577)
*Kyujin Lee, Injae Kim, Jihwan Park, Yejun Ju, Minseok Joo, Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (68.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出在 VLA 模型中引入逆动力学学习作为辅助目标，通过预测当前与未来观测之间的动作来直接监督视觉编码器，从而缓解 VLM 表征对细微视觉差异不敏感导致的状态混叠问题，在 CALVIN ABC-D 和 SimplerEnv 上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型将预训练 VLM 适配到机器人操作任务，但 VLM 衍生的视觉表征对低层控制所需的细微视觉差异不敏感，导致视觉相似但动作不同的状态之间出现"状态混叠"（state aliasing），影响控制精度。

Method: 在 VLA 视觉编码器上引入逆动力学学习作为辅助目标：给定当前观测和未来观测，预测它们之间的动作。同时提出伪反向监督（pseudo-reversed supervision），通过交换观测顺序并反转动作标签，让编码器暴露于更广泛的动作方向，提升泛化能力。该方法仅使用标准观测-动作对，无需额外标注，测试时保持原始推理流程不变。

Result: 在 CALVIN ABC-D 和 SimplerEnv 基准上，该方法在多种 VLA 基线（如 RT-2、Octo 等）上均取得一致性能提升。冻结编码器探针实验和状态-特征对齐分析表明，该方法学习到了更具状态判别性的视觉表征，有效减少了状态混叠，并更好地与机器人状态变化对齐。

Conclusion: 逆动力学学习作为一种简单有效的辅助目标，能够直接监督 VLA 视觉编码器学习细粒度视觉差异，缓解状态混叠问题，且无需额外标注或修改推理流程，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 VLA（Vision-Language-Action）模型，通过逆动力学学习改进 VLM 衍生的视觉表征以缓解状态混叠问题。这属于视觉语言模型（VLM/MLLM）的架构与训练改进，与 canonical 子主题"视觉语言模型与 MLLM"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses state aliasing in VLA models via inverse dynamics, but focuses on robot manipulation, not the user's priority subtopics. Signals: VLA model improvement, inverse dynamics learning, state aliasing mitigation, robot manipulation, CALVIN benchmark

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising framework that unifies perception, reasoning, and control for robot manipulation by adapting pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) to action prediction. However, VLM-derived representations are often insensitive to subtle visual distinctions required for low-level control, causing state aliasing between visually similar states that require substantially different actions. Prior VLA studies improve visual understanding by generating visual or reasoning outputs, such as future frames, 2D grounding points or traces, or intermediate spatial reasoning steps, but these objectives typically shape the vision encoder only indirectly through end-to-end prediction and do not explicitly analyze state aliasing in the learned visual feature space. To mitigate state aliasing, we introduce inverse dynamics learning as an auxiliary objective that directly supervises the VLA vision encoder. By predicting the action between current and future observations, our objective encourages the encoder to capture fine-grained visual distinctions that determine low-level actions. We further use pseudo-reversed supervision to expose the encoder to a broader range of action directions and improve generalization under limited robot demonstrations. Our method applies to diverse VLA baselines, uses only standard observation-action pairs without additional annotations, and preserves the original inference pipeline at test time. Experiments on CALVIN ABC-D and SimplerEnv show consistent gains across diverse VLA baselines. Frozen-encoder probing and state-feature alignment analyses further show that our method learns state-discriminative visual representations that reduce state aliasing and better align with robot state changes.

</details>


### [281] [VLA-Trace: Diagnosing Vision-Language-Action Models through Representation and Behavior Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30117)
*Haoyuan Shi, Xiancong Ren, Yingji Zhang, Qinfan Zhang, Jiayu Hu, Haozhe Shan, Han Dong, Jinpeng Lu, Yinda Chen, Yi Zhang, Yong Dai, Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (72.84) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VLA-Trace 是一个渐进式诊断框架，通过表征动力学、因果控制归因和行为表现三个层次分析 VLA 模型如何将多模态知识转化为具身控制。在 π0.5 和 OpenVLA 上的实验揭示了两种模型在微调适应、多模态路由策略和语义跟随能力上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在多模态理解和具身控制方面取得了进展，但其内部工作机制——如何将多模态知识转化为具体动作控制——仍然是一个未解的黑箱问题。缺乏系统的诊断工具来理解 VLA 模型的行为。

Method: 提出 VLA-Trace 框架，包含三个渐进层次：(1) 表征动力学分析——使用跨模态和检查点漂移中心核对齐（CKA）追踪表征演化；(2) 因果控制归因——通过注意力剔除（attention knockout）干预识别模态特定的控制通路；(3) 行为表现探测——通过 rollout 级行为探针检测接地性、捷径依赖和语义跟随能力。

Result: 在 π0.5 和 OpenVLA 上的实验发现：(1) 两种模型在 VLA 微调过程中表现出不同的模态特定适应动态；(2) 它们在动作解码时依赖不同的多模态路由策略和层级依赖关系；(3) VLA 策略在视觉接地轨迹生成方面表现出色，但在细粒度语义跟随方面仍然有限。

Conclusion: VLA-Trace 为理解 VLA 模型提供了系统性的诊断工具，揭示了不同 VLA 模型在表征演化、因果通路和行为表现上的差异，为表征保持的适应、因果 VLA 电路和组合语义控制等未来方向提供了启示。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 VLA（Vision-Language-Action）模型的可解释性诊断框架，属于视觉语言模型（VLM/MLLM）的分析与理解范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Provides diagnostic insights into VLA models but is not directly aligned with user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: VLA model diagnosis, representation tracing, causal control attribution, behavioral probing, multimodal routing strategies

Abstract: Understanding how Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models transform multimodal knowledge into embodied control remains an open challenge. We present VLA-Trace, a progressive diagnostic framework that analyzes VLA models through a unified evidence chain from representation dynamics to causal control attribution and behavioral manifestation. It specifically combines cross-modal and checkpoint-drift centered kernel alignment (CKA) to trace representation evolution, attention knockout interventions to identify modality-specific control pathways, and rollout-level behavioral probes to examine grounding, shortcut dependence, and semantic following. Experiments on $\pi_{0.5}$ and OpenVLA reveal three key findings. First, the two models exhibit distinct modality-specific adaptation dynamics during VLA finetuning. Second, they rely on different multimodal routing strategies and layer-wise dependencies during action decoding. Third, although VLA policies excel at visually grounded trajectory generation, they remain limited in fine-grained semantic following. These findings highlight future directions for representation-preserving adaptation, causal VLA circuits, and compositional semantic control.

</details>


### [282] [LoMo: Local Modality Substitution for Deeper Vision-Language Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30265)
*Feng Han, Zhixiong Zhang, Zheming Liang, Yibin Wang, Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.85) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现 VLM 对文本和图像载体存在"载体敏感性"问题——将文本问题渲染为图像后模型性能大幅下降，原因是训练数据中文本和图像的角色不对称。为此提出 LoMo，一种轻量级数据策展范式，通过将单模态提示重构为"文本-图像-文本"交错多模态序列，为语义等价的文本和图像载体提供跨模态表征不变性监督。在 13 个多模态基准上，LoMo 在 LLaVA-OneVision-1.5-8B 和 Qwen3.5-9B 上分别提升 2.67 和 2.82 个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 VLM 训练数据中文本和图像的角色不对称（文本作为语言查询，图像作为视觉参考），导致模型对不同模态的信息获取存在偏好差异，无法对齐语义等价的文本和图像载体表征，使得模型在模态替换场景下推理脆弱。

Method: 提出 Local Modality Substitution (LoMo)，一种架构无关的轻量级数据策展范式。核心思想是将单模态提示中的目标文本片段动态替换为渲染后的图像，构造"文本-图像-文本"交错多模态序列，从而为语义等价的文本和图像载体提供跨模态表征不变性的监督信号。

Result: 在 13 个多模态基准上，LoMo 在 LLaVA-OneVision-1.5-8B 上比标准 SFT 提升 2.67 分，在 Qwen3.5-9B 上提升 2.82 分，显著增强了多模态推理能力和跨模态融合深度。

Conclusion: LoMo 通过简单的数据策展策略有效缓解了 VLM 的载体敏感性问题，验证了提供跨模态表征不变性监督对提升多模态融合质量的重要性，且方法轻量、架构无关，具有良好的通用性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）中文本与图像模态之间的表征对齐问题，提出了一种轻量级数据策展方法 LoMo 来增强跨模态融合。这属于通用视觉语言模型与 MLLM 架构优化的范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses a fundamental VLM carrier sensitivity issue with a lightweight data curation method, boosting multimodal reasoning across benchmarks. Signals: addresses carrier sensitivity in VLMs, lightweight data curation paradigm, cross-modal representation invariance, consistent gains on LLaVA and Qwen models, relevant to multimodal understanding generation direction

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved substantial progress across a wide range of understanding and reasoning tasks, driven by large-scale image-text training aimed at multimodal fusion. Ideally, replacing a textual question with its rendered-image counterpart should leave model performance essentially unaffected. In practice, however, such modality substitution induces dramatic performance degradation. We attribute this "carrier sensitivity" issue to an inherent bias in current training corpora. Across prevalent datasets such as image captioning, VQA, OCR, and web-sourced interleaved data, text and images are typically organized into distinct and asymmetric roles, with text serving as linguistic queries and images as visual references. Such data bias leads VLMs to exhibit distinct preferences for information acquisition across different modalities. Consequently, VLMs fail to align representations of semantically equivalent content across textual and visual carriers, making model reasoning fragile under modality substitution. To address this, we propose Local Modality Substitution (LoMo), a lightweight, architecture-agnostic data curation paradigm designed to provide supervision for cross-modal representational invariance between semantically equivalent text and image carriers. LoMo achieves this by reformulating single-modality prompts into seamlessly interleaved multimodal sequences. It dynamically selects target text spans and recasts them as rendered images, thereby preserving the same semantics across "text, visual, text" carriers. Extensive experiments across 13 diverse multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that LoMo significantly improves overall multimodal reasoning and yields deeper cross-modal fusion. Specifically, it delivers consistent gains across foundational models, improving over standard SFT by 2.67 points on LLaVA-OneVision-1.5-8B and 2.82 points on Qwen3.5-9B.

</details>


### [283] [Same Evidence, Different Answers: Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Turn Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30251)
*Zizhuo Lin, Quanling Liu, Jinsheng Quan, Chao Zhang, Yifan Zhu, Xing Shi, Jingtao Xu, Zhihui Li, Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (68.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 论文发现LLM在单轮完整提示下能正确解题，但在多轮对话中逐步接收相同信息时却容易失败，原因是"自我锚定漂移"——模型在部分信息下产生的假设会污染后续推理。为此提出CCOPD方法，用完整提示下的教师模型蒸馏多轮对话中的学生模型，在数学对话上训练后，多轮对话性能平均提升32%，且零样本泛化到5个域外任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在单轮完整提示中能正确完成任务，但当相同信息在多轮对话中逐步呈现时，模型表现显著下降。作者认为这一差距的关键原因是"自我锚定漂移"（self-anchored drift）：模型在部分信息下生成的回复会引入未经支持的假设，这些假设在后续轮次中扭曲最终答案。现有方法缺乏对这种多轮推理漂移的系统性缓解。

Method: 提出Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation (CCOPD)。使用同一基础模型的两个副本：冻结的教师模型以完整FULL提示为条件，可训练的学生模型通过多轮对话逐步接收相同证据。CCOPD在学生模型自身的推理轨迹上，将其行为与教师的完整上下文行为进行对齐（KL散度蒸馏）。训练仅在数学问题对话数据上进行。

Result: 在数学对话上训练后，CCOPD在RAW-SHARDED（多轮分片）设置下相比原始基础模型平均提升32%的相对性能，同时基本保持FULL提示下的完整性能。在5个零样本域外任务（包括MMLU、HotpotQA、GSM8K等）上也观察到一致改进。分析表明CCOPD增强了模型对用户证据的依赖，降低了对早期助手回复的敏感性。

Conclusion: CCOPD通过将多轮对话中的模型行为对齐到完整上下文下的教师行为，有效缓解了多轮推理中的自我锚定漂移问题。该方法仅需数学对话训练即可泛化到多种域外任务，为提升LLM在多轮交互中的推理忠实性提供了有效途径。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多轮对话中语言模型的推理漂移问题，提出CCOPD蒸馏方法。虽然论文主要聚焦于纯文本（数学推理）场景，但其核心问题——多轮对话中模型对用户证据的忠实推理——与多模态理解与生成方向中的MLLM多轮对话能力密切相关。论文方法可推广至多模态对话场景，因此归入vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与MLLM）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on multi-turn LLM robustness, not on the user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: multi-turn reasoning, distillation, self-anchored drift, math dialogue, zero-shot generalization

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often solve a task when all instructions are given in a single prompt, but fail when the same information is revealed gradually across turns. When a clean FULL prompt and a RAW-SHARDED conversation contain the same complete user evidence, the model should still arrive at the same answer. We argue that a key reason for this gap is self-anchored drift: responses produced under partial information introduce unsupported assumptions, and those assumptions later distort the final answer. To reduce this effect, we propose Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation (CCOPD). During training, the same base model is used in two roles: a frozen teacher conditioned on the clean FULL prompt and a trainable student that receives the same evidence incrementally through a multi-turn conversation; CCOPD aligns the student's behavior on its own trajectories with the teacher's canonical full-context behavior. Trained only on math problem conversations, CCOPD yields a 32\% average relative improvement in RAW-SHARDED performance over the original base model across math and five zero-shot out-of-domain task families, while largely preserving full-context performance. Further analyses suggest that CCOPD strengthens grounding in user evidence and reduces sensitivity to contamination from earlier assistant turns.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_vqa_doc'></div>

## 视觉问答与文档理解 [[Back]](#toc)

VQA、文档/图表/表格理解、OCR-free 文档智能。

### [284] [State-Anchored Complete-View Distillation for Robust Conversational Multimodal Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29590)
*Zhaoyan Pan, Xiangdong Li, Wenke Wu, Mengting Ma, Ye Lou, Ji Zhou, Jiatong Pan, Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.MM

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (44.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoRe-KD，一种基于状态锚定和冲突正则化的完整视图知识蒸馏框架，用于鲁棒的对话多模态情感识别。通过完整视图教师提供结构化参考，利用状态锚定对齐不完整视图学生预测，并引入非言语冲突暴露训练来减少标签偏差，在 IEMOCAP 和 MELD 数据集上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话多模态情感识别中，语言、声学或视觉模态可能缺失或不可靠。现有缺失模态方法通常重建缺失输入，但对话上下文中重建可能不唯一，且非言语线索可能与目标话语冲突。因此需要一种更鲁棒的框架来处理模态缺失问题。

Method: 提出 CoRe-KD 框架，包含：(1) 完整视图教师模型提供结构化参考（预测级参考、融合状态、模态特定状态）；(2) 完整视图状态锚定（CSA）将不完整视图学生的预测和状态与参考对齐；(3) 非言语冲突暴露（NCE）在保留目标标签的非言语冲突视图上训练，减少捐赠者标签偏差。

Result: 在 IEMOCAP 和 MELD 数据集上，在固定缺失和随机缺失协议下均取得一致性能提升。CMU-MOSEI 作为补充的 utterance 级验证也支持方法的有效性。消融实验验证了 CSA 的作用和 NCE 的互补效果。

Conclusion: CoRe-KD 通过状态锚定和冲突正则化的知识蒸馏，有效提升了对话多模态情感识别在模态缺失场景下的鲁棒性，CSA 和 NCE 两个组件相互补充，共同发挥作用。

Classification reason: 该论文研究对话多模态情感识别（MER），属于多模态理解任务，与视觉问答（VQA）和多模态理解方向高度相关。虽然情感识别不是传统VQA，但属于多模态理解与问答的范畴，且现有[CANONICAL] subtopic 'multimodal_vqa_doc' 是最接近的匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on conversational multimodal emotion recognition, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key boost keywords. Signals: multimodal emotion recognition, knowledge distillation, missing modality, state anchoring, conflict regularization

Abstract: Conversational multimodal emotion recognition (MER) requires reliable prediction when language, acoustic, or visual observations are missing or unreliable. Many missing-modality methods reconstruct absent inputs, yet such recovery can be non-unique in dialogue context, and nonverbal cues may conflict with the target utterance. To this end, we propose CoRe-KD (Complete-view Reference-guided Knowledge Distillation), a state-anchored, conflict-regularized complete-view distillation framework for robust conversational MER. A complete-view teacher provides structured references, including prediction-level references, fused states, and modality-specific states. Complete-view State Anchoring (CSA) aligns incomplete-view student predictions and states with these references, while Nonverbal Conflict Exposure (NCE) trains on target-preserving nonverbal conflict views to reduce donor-label bias. Experiments on IEMOCAP and MELD, with CMU-MOSEI as a supplementary utterance-level check, show consistent gains under fixed- and random-missing protocols. Comprehensive ablation studies and further analyses support the role of CSA and the complementary effect of NCE.

</details>


### [285] [Architecture-Sensitive Supervised Fine-Tuning for Screen-Conditioned Action Prediction: A PiSAR Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29400)
*Rahul Bissa, Abhishek Vyas, Yash Jain*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.HC, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 PiSAR 基准，包含 12,929 条屏幕锚定行为推理数据，评测了监督微调模型与前沿零样本模型在屏幕条件动作预测任务上的表现。实验发现，微调后的 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 在语义相似度上达到 0.783，远超 Claude Opus 4.7（0.459）和 GPT-5.5（0.482），而 Gemma-4-26B 微调后仅 0.441，表明存在配方-模型不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在屏幕条件动作预测（根据屏幕内容预测用户意图和下一步动作）方面缺乏系统性的基准评测，尤其是监督微调与零样本前沿模型之间的对比尚不充分。

Method: 构建 PiSAR 数据集（12,929 条屏幕锚定行为推理元组），包含 Persona、Intent、Screen、Action、Rationale 五个维度。在 661 行保留测试集上，使用语义相似度（sem_sim）作为评测指标，对比了监督微调模型（Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct、Gemma-4-26B-A4B-IT）与前沿零样本模型（Claude Opus 4.7、GPT-5.5）的性能。

Result: 微调后的 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 达到 sem_sim 0.783，79% 的行 sem_sim >= 0.7；Claude Opus 4.7 为 0.459（仅 1-2% 行 >= 0.7），GPT-5.5 为 0.482。Gemma-4-26B 微调后仅 0.441，与零样本基线相当。

Conclusion: 监督微调在屏幕条件动作预测任务上显著优于零样本前沿模型，但效果高度依赖模型与微调配方的匹配度；推理调优的大参数模型（如 Gemma-4-26B）可能因参数空间固化而难以通过标准微调有效迁移。

Classification reason: 论文研究屏幕条件动作预测（screen-conditioned action prediction），涉及屏幕内容理解与行为推理，属于多模态文档/屏幕理解范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）。

Importance reason: Focuses on screen-conditioned action prediction, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key directions like video generation or world models. Signals: screen-conditioned action prediction, supervised fine-tuning benchmark, recipe-model mismatch, no autoregressive or diffusion focus, negative keywords not triggered

Abstract: We benchmark three supervised fine-tuned models against frontier zero-shot baselines on a 661-row held-out slice of PiSAR (Persona, intent, Screen, Action, Rationale), a 12,929-tuple corpus of screen-anchored behavioural rationales curated from public app-store reviews, Pew American Trends Panel demographics, and the OPeRA shopper traces. Every model, frontier or fine-tuned, is evaluated on the same 661-row slice with the same scoring pipeline. Two findings. First, frontier zero-shot baselines (Claude Opus 4.7 and GPT-5.5) reach sem_sim 0.459 and 0.482 respectively; a fine-tuned Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct reaches 0.783 and clears sem_sim >= 0.7 on 79% of rows, against 1-2% for either frontier baseline, a gap of 0.30 absolute on the same test set. Second, the same training data and recipe on Gemma-4-26B-A4B-IT scores only 0.441, in the same band as the frontier zero-shot baselines rather than the fine-tuned Qwen. We read this as a recipe-vs-model mismatch: the reasoning-tuned high-parameter model resists displacement and would likely need either more data or a stronger fine-tuning method.

</details>


### [286] [Pocket-Dentist: On-Device Dental Image Understanding via Efficient Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29299)
*Kai Bian, Xucheng Guo, Bin Chen, Lingyan Ruan, Yiran Shen, Ting Dang, Hong Jia*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (57.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Pocket-Dentist 提出了一个面向牙科图像理解的效率感知多模态问答基准，涵盖3个数据集、5种任务类型和7项指标，并发现紧凑型VLM（2B参数）在准确率上优于大模型且计算成本更低。在iPhone 17 Pro上部署的Pocket-Dentist-2B每样本仅需4.31秒，延迟降低4.9倍，内存减少2.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有牙科视觉语言模型的评估分散在不同的数据集、任务定义和指标上，且忽略了计算成本，限制了其在专科中心之外的牙科筛查场景中的实际部署。实际场景需要及时推理、有限硬件和本地化处理患者图像以保护隐私。

Method: 构建了一个效率感知的牙科多模态问答基准Pocket-Dentist，整合了3个数据集（约1159名患者）、5种任务类型和7项指标。在14个VLM上进行系统评估，发现紧凑型VLM（如2B参数）在准确率上优于大模型。进一步在iPhone 17 Pro上本地部署微调后的紧凑型VLM Pocket-Dentist-2B进行实际性能测试。

Result: 紧凑型VLM（2B参数）在牙科图像理解中准确率超过更大模型，同时计算成本显著降低。Pocket-Dentist-2B在iPhone 17 Pro上每样本处理时间为4.31秒，相比7B基线延迟降低4.9倍，内存使用减少2.3倍。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在牙科图像理解这一特定领域，紧凑型VLM可以在保持甚至提升准确率的同时大幅降低计算开销，适合在移动设备上实现隐私保护的临床预筛查。Pocket-Dentist基准为牙科多模态模型的标准化评估提供了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦牙科领域的多模态视觉问答（VQA）基准与模型，属于视觉问答与文档理解（multimodal_vqa_doc）这一规范子主题。同时涉及领域内容生成应用（aigc_domain_application）的医疗垂直方向，但主要贡献是牙科VQA基准与轻量模型评估，因此主方向为多模态理解与生成。

Importance reason: Specialized dental application with limited generalizability to core generative video/image priorities. Signals: domain-specific dental benchmark, compact VLM efficiency on mobile, negative keyword "medical diagnosis" matches, no autoregressive/diffusion/world model signal

Abstract: Evaluations of dental vision-language models remain fragmented across datasets, task definitions and metrics, and often ignore their computational cost. This limits their widespread deployment for dental screening outside specialist centres, where timely inference, limited hardware, and local handling of patient images are vital for practical, privacy-preserving clinical prescreening. Here we present Pocket-Dentist, an efficiency-aware benchmark for dental multimodal question answering that brings together three datasets spanning approximately 1,159 patients, five task types and seven metrics. Across typical 14 VLMs, our results reveals an interesting observation: compact VLMs (e.g., 2B-parameter models) outperform larger VLMs in accuracy while requiring substantially lower computational costs in dental image understanding. Deployed locally on an iPhone 17 Pro, our finetuned compact VLM Pocket-Dentist-2B processed each sample in 4.31 s, reducing latency by 4.9-fold and memory use by 2.3-fold compared with a 7B baseline.

</details>


### [287] [Multi-Stage VLM Pipeline for Zero-Shot Traffic Accident Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29325)
*Fumiya Tatematsu, Fumihiko Takahashi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (44.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了CVPR 2026 AUTOPILOT Workshop ACCIDENT挑战赛的第一名解决方案。基于冻结的Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct模型，构建了一个三阶段流水线（全视频联合预测→时间精炼→单帧撞击中心点定位），并在235B MoE模型上重复运行，以9:1比例融合输出，最终将预测点对齐到最近的车辆检测框。该方法在Public LB上达到0.55469，Private LB上达到0.57080，比最强基线Molmo-7B（0.358）高出约0.21分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通事故理解对于自动驾驶和交通安全至关重要，但现有方法通常需要大量标注数据。本文旨在探索在零样本条件下，利用大规模视觉语言模型（VLM）从CCTV监控视频中理解交通事故（预测事故时间、撞击中心点和碰撞类型），以降低对标注数据的依赖。

Method: 基于冻结的Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct模型，构建三阶段流水线：(1) 全视频联合预测阶段，将视频帧拼接后输入VLM，同时预测事故时间、撞击中心点和碰撞类型；(2) 时间精炼阶段，对第一阶段预测的时间窗口进行细化；(3) 单帧撞击中心点定位阶段，在精炼后的关键帧上定位撞击中心点。随后在235B MoE模型上重复相同流水线，以9:1比例融合两个模型的输出，最后将每个预测点对齐到最近的车辆检测框。

Result: 最终系统在Public LB上达到0.55469，Private LB上达到0.57080，比最强基线Molmo-7B（0.358）高出约0.21分，赢得挑战赛第一名。论文还报告了各组件消融实验和负结果。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过精心设计的流水线，冻结的大规模VLM可以在零样本条件下有效完成交通事故理解任务，显著超越专用基线模型。多模型融合和车辆检测对齐是提升性能的关键组件。

Classification reason: 该论文使用冻结的VLM（Qwen3-VL-32B）构建多阶段流水线，从CCTV视频中零样本预测事故时间、撞击中心点和碰撞类型，本质上是一个视频问答/视觉理解任务。这与[多模态评测与基准]下的视觉问答与文档理解（multimodal_vqa_doc）子主题高度相关，因为论文的核心是使用VLM进行视觉场景理解与问答式预测。

Importance reason: Pipeline engineering for a specific traffic accident benchmark lacks novelty in multimodal understanding or generation. Signals: Workshop challenge solution, zero-shot VLM pipeline, no new model or method, negative keywords: traffic prediction, low generalizability

Abstract: We present the 1st-place solution to the ACCIDENT challenge at the CVPR 2026 AUTOPILOT Workshop, which asks for zero-shot prediction of accident timing, impact centroid, and collision type from CCTV footage. On a frozen Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct checkpoint we build a three-stage pipeline (full-video joint prediction, time refinement, and single-frame grounding of the impact centroid), run the same pipeline a second time on a 235B Mixture-of-Experts sibling, blend the two outputs 9:1, and finally snap each predicted point onto the nearest vehicle detection. The final system reaches Public LB 0.55469 / Private LB 0.57080, roughly +0.21 over the strongest host baseline (Molmo-7B, 0.358) and wins the challenge. We ablate each component, report the negative results that shaped the final design, and release the code at .

</details>


### [288] [From Data to Insights: Exploring Program-of-Thoughts Prompting for Chart Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28874)
*Yutong Qu, Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (46.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种零样本的图表摘要方法，利用 Program-of-Thought (PoT) 策略，通过 Python 程序作为中介进行数值推理，并引入图表到字典（chart-to-dictionary）的辅助任务，使轻量级 VLM 在图表摘要任务上达到与现有方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图表摘要需要语义视觉理解和数值推理，现有 VLM 方法缺乏统计事实正确性的验证机制且计算开销大。本文旨在探索一种轻量级、零样本的策略来提升图表摘要的准确性和效率。

Method: 提出零样本的 Program-of-Thought (PoT) 策略，使用 Python 程序作为中间推理步骤来推导图表中的统计摘要；同时引入 chart-to-dictionary 辅助任务，将图表信息转换为字典形式的灵活表示，替代传统的 chart-to-table 方法，便于与 PoT 集成。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提策略在语义指标和事实性指标上与现有图表摘要方法表现相当。

Conclusion: 零样本 PoT 策略结合 chart-to-dictionary 辅助任务能够有效提升轻量级 VLM 在图表摘要任务上的表现，为图表理解提供了一种计算高效且事实性更强的方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究图表摘要（chart summarization），属于文档/图表理解范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on chart summarization with PoT, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key directions. Signals: chart summarization, program-of-thought, zero-shot, lightweight VLM, no video/image generation

Abstract: Charts play a critical role in conveying numerical data insights through structured visual representations. However, semantic visual understanding and numerical reasoning requirements hinder the accurate description of charts, interpreting a challenging task in chart summarization. Despite recent advancements in visual language models (VLMs), approaches lack robust mechanisms for verifying statistical fact correctness and are computationally heavy. To address this gap, this paper explores a strategy of using zero-shot learning to motivate the lightweight VLMs to perform computational reasoning, via Python programs as intermediaries to derive valid summary statistics for chart understanding. Specifically, we introduce a novel chart-to-dictionary auxiliary task, offering a more flexible representation compared to traditional chart-to-table methods, making it particularly well-suited for integration with the Program-of-Thought (PoT) strategy. Experimental results demonstrate our strategy performs on par with existing chart summarization methods across semantic and factual metrics. Code is available on .

</details>


### [289] [Brain-IT-VQA: From Brain Signals to Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29588)
*Roman Beliy, Matias Cosarinsky, Oliver Heinimann, Navve Wasserman, Michal Irani*

Main category: q-bio.NC, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (41.95) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Brain-IT-VQA框架，从fMRI脑信号解码语言token并与语言模型结合进行视觉问答，同时构建了NSD-VQA基准数据集（20个问题类别，每图约20个QA对），显著优于此前方法，并用于分析不同脑区在视觉理解中的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从fMRI信号解码视觉内容并进行视觉问答是一个长期挑战，现有方法性能有限，且缺乏细粒度、可控的评测基准来系统分析不同视觉信息在脑中的表征结构。

Method: 基于Brain Interaction Transformer (Brain-IT)架构，从fMRI脑活动解码语言token，再与语言模型（LM）集成以回答视觉问题。同时构建NSD-VQA数据集，包含20个受控问题类别，每张自然图像平均20个QA对，覆盖多层次的视觉理解。

Result: Brain-IT-VQA在fMRI-based captioning和VQA任务上显著优于此前方法。NSD-VQA提供了更可靠、可解释的评测，并量化了不同视觉和语义信息从fMRI信号中解码的可靠性。

Conclusion: Brain-IT-VQA和NSD-VQA共同提供了一个强大的预测框架和脑表征研究工具，可用于分析不同脑区在不同类型视觉问题中的贡献，推动脑信号解码与视觉理解交叉研究。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从fMRI脑信号进行视觉问答（VQA），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉问答范畴。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）涵盖VQA任务，虽然输入模态特殊（fMRI），但核心任务是VQA，因此复用该subtopic。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on fMRI-based VQA, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and is flagged by negative keywords like clinical. Signals: fMRI decoding, visual question answering, brain signals, NSD-VQA benchmark, low relevance to user's generative AI focus

Abstract: Decoding visual content from fMRI signals recorded while a person views images, and specifically answering questions about the seen images, is a long-standing challenge. While significant progress has been made in recent years in visual question answering (VQA) from fMRI, performance remains limited. Moreover, although recent models can make increasingly accurate predictions, they have rarely been used as tools for understanding the structure of visual representations in the brain. We present Brain-IT-VQA, a framework for visual question answering from fMRI. Building on the Brain Interaction Transformer (Brain-IT), our method decodes language tokens from brain activity and integrates them with a language model to answer visual questions. Our model substantially outperforms previous fMRI-based captioning and VQA approaches. We further introduce NSD-VQA, a new dataset and benchmark for visual question answering from fMRI. Unlike existing image-fMRI VQA datasets, which typically provide only a few broad and weakly controlled questions per image, NSD-VQA provides on average 20 question-answer pairs per image across 20 controlled question categories that disentangle multiple levels of visual understanding. This enables more reliable and interpretable evaluation despite limited fMRI test data. Together, Brain-IT-VQA and NSD-VQA provide both a strong predictive framework and a tool for studying brain representations. Using this benchmark, we quantify which forms of visual and semantic information can be reliably decoded from fMRI responses to natural images. We further analyze the contributions of different brain regions across question types.

</details>


### [290] [Comparative Evaluation of Machine Translation Systems on Images with Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29476)
*Blai Puchol, Sergio Gómez González, Miguel Domingo, Francisco Casacuberta*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (66.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种图像文本翻译范式：模块化流水线（OCR+LLM）、多模态大语言模型（MLLM）和端到端模型Translatotron-V。实验表明MLLM表现最佳，模块化流水线次之，端到端方法最弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像中的文本翻译（如路标、菜单、文档截图）是计算机视觉与自然语言处理的交叉任务，现有研究缺乏对模块化流水线、MLLM和端到端模型三种范式的系统性比较评估。

Method: 构建了包含多种语言对的并行多模态数据集，对比三种范式：(1) 模块化流水线：docTR OCR + Llama/EuroLLM翻译；(2) MLLM：Gemini 2.5不同配置直接处理图像并翻译；(3) 端到端模型：Translatotron-V直接生成翻译后的图像。使用BLEU、chrF、TER指标评估。

Result: MLLM（Gemini 2.5）在所有语言对上取得最佳翻译质量，模块化流水线次之但表现稳健，Translatotron-V端到端方法效果最差。MLLM展现出更强的上下文理解和灵活性。

Conclusion: 多模态推理在图像文本翻译任务中效果显著，MLLM凭借联合视觉-语言理解能力优于传统流水线方法，为未来融合视觉理解与多语言生成的研究提供了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文研究图像中文本的机器翻译，涉及OCR、MLLM和端到端翻译系统的对比评估，属于多模态文档理解与翻译任务，与[multimodal_vqa_doc]（视觉问答与文档理解）高度相关，同时涉及多模态生成（端到端翻译图像生成）和AIGC（内容生成应用）。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on image text translation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's boosted keywords. Signals: MLLM evaluation, image translation, modular pipeline, not autoregressive generation, not video/world model

Abstract: This work presents a comparative evaluation of machine translation systems applied to images containing textual information, a task that lies at the intersection of computer vision and natural language processing. The study compares three main paradigms: modular pipelines that separate text detection, recognition, and translation; multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) capable of processing both image and text jointly; and an end-to-end model, Translatotron-V, which directly generates translated images. The modular systems employ state-of-the-art OCR (docTR) combined with multilingual LLMs such as Llama and EuroLLM, while the evaluated MLLMs include different configurations of Gemini 2.5. Experiments were conducted on parallel multilingual datasets covering multiple language pairs, with evaluation based on BLEU, chrF, and TER metrics. The results show that modular pipelines outperform the end-to-end approach, while MLLMs achieve the best overall performance, demonstrating superior flexibility and contextual understanding. These findings underscore the effectiveness of multi-modal reasoning for image-to-text translation and provide a solid foundation for future research on integrating visual understanding and language generation in multilingual settings.

</details>


### [291] [Not All Inputs Are Valid: Towards Open-Set Video Moment Retrieval Using Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29812)
*Xiang Fang, Wanlong Fang, Daizong Liu, Xiaoye Qu, Jianfeng Dong, Pan Zhou, Renfu Li, Zichuan Xu, Lixing Chen, Panpan Zheng, Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (46.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文首次提出开放集视频时刻检索（OS-VMR）任务，要求模型不仅能根据相关查询（ID）检索精确时刻，还需拒绝无关查询（OOD）。提出OpenVMR模型，利用归一化流学习ID分布、不确定性分数划分边界、正-无标签学习进行时刻检索，在三个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频时刻检索（VMR）方法均基于封闭集假设，即所有查询都与视频相关。但在开放场景中，用户可能输入与视频无关的查询（OOD），现有方法仍会强行检索，在高风险场景（如犯罪检测）中可能导致不可挽回的损失。因此需要一种能同时处理ID查询检索和OOD查询拒绝的新范式。

Method: 提出OpenVMR模型，包含三个核心模块：(1) 基于归一化流的ID分布学习，假设ID查询特征服从多元高斯分布，通过可逆变换学习分布；(2) ID-OOD边界搜索，引入不确定性分数来划分决策边界，并通过拉近ID查询特征来细化边界；(3) 时刻检索模块，设计视频-查询粗粒度匹配和帧-查询细粒度匹配进行跨模态交互，并采用正-无标签（PU）学习进行时刻定位。

Result: 在三个VMR数据集（Charades-STA、ActivityNet Captions、TACoS）上进行了实验，OpenVMR在OOD检测（AUROC、FPR95等指标）和ID时刻检索（R@K, mIoU等指标）两方面均优于现有基线方法，验证了模型在开放集场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文首次探索了开放集视频时刻检索任务，提出的OpenVMR模型通过归一化流区分ID/OOD查询并基于ID查询进行时刻检索，在多个数据集上取得了优异效果。该工作为视频理解中的开放集问题提供了新思路，未来可进一步探索更复杂的开放场景。

Classification reason: 该论文研究开放集视频时刻检索（Open-Set Video Moment Retrieval），属于视频问答与检索范畴。虽然任务涉及OOD检测，但其核心是视频-文本跨模态检索，与多模态VQA/检索方向高度相关。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）涵盖VQA及视频理解相关检索任务，因此复用该subtopic。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on a niche video retrieval task with no direct connection to the user's priority subtopics like generation or world models. Signals: open-set video moment retrieval, OOD rejection, normalizing flow, positive-unlabeled learning, not generation

Abstract: Video Moment Retrieval (VMR) targets to retrieve the specific moment corresponding to a sentence query from an untrimmed video. Although recent works have made remarkable progress in this task, they implicitly are rooted in the closed-set assumption that all the given queries as video-relevant\footnote{In this paper, we treat ``video-relevant query'' as ``in-distribution (ID) query'' and ``video-irrelevant query'' as ``out-of-distribution (OOD) query''.}. Given an OOD query in open-set scenarios, they still utilize it for wrong retrieval, which might lead to irrecoverable losses in high-risk scenarios, \textit{e.g.}, criminal activity detection. To this end, we creatively explore a brand-new VMR setting termed Open-Set Video Moment Retrieval (OS-VMR), where we should not only retrieve the precise moments based on ID query, but also reject OOD queries. In this paper, we make the first attempt to step toward OS-VMR and propose a novel model \textbf{OpenVMR}, which first distinguishes ID and OOD queries based on the normalizing flow technology, and then conducts moment retrieval based on ID queries. Specifically, we first learn the ID distribution by constructing a normalizing flow, and assume the ID query distribution obeys the multi-variate Gaussian distribution. Then, we introduce an uncertainty score to search the ID-OOD separating boundary. After that, we refine the ID-OOD boundary by pulling together ID query features. Besides, video-query matching and frame-query matching are designed for coarse-grained and fine-grained cross-modal interaction, respectively. Finally, a positive-unlabeled learning module is introduced for moment retrieval. Experimental results on three VMR datasets show the effectiveness of our OpenVMR.

</details>


### [292] [Tiny but Trusted: Efficient Vision-Language Reasoning for Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30344)
*Xiaona Zhou, Muntasir Wahed, Tianjiao Yu, Constantin Brif, Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (46.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出VisAnomBench——一个基于公开时间序列数据集并利用大VLM生成高质量异常解释的基准，并在此基础上微调得到参数高效的VisAnomReasoner模型，在时间序列异常检测任务上显著优于所有基线方法，并在跨基准泛化中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在时间序列异常检测任务上表现不佳，且公开异常检测基准仅提供区间标注而缺乏自然语言解释，难以微调VLM生成可解释的异常检测结果。

Method: 构建VisAnomBench基准：从公开时间序列数据集中选取数据，利用多个大VLM生成异常解释，并通过细粒度任务特定奖励筛选高质量解释。在此基础上，对参数高效的VLM进行微调，得到VisAnomReasoner模型。

Result: 在VisAnomBench上，VisAnomReasoner在精确率和F1上分别比所有基线提升至少21.23和23.87个百分点；在TSB-AD-U基准上跨基准泛化实验显示精确率和F1分别提升9.57和13.39个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过构建高质量解释基准并微调参数高效VLM，可以显著提升时间序列异常检测的准确性和可解释性，且具有良好的跨基准泛化能力。

Classification reason: 论文构建了时间序列异常检测的VLM基准VisAnomBench并微调VLM进行异常定位与解释，属于视觉问答与文档理解（VQA/图表理解）范畴下的时间序列分析任务，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）合理匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on time-series anomaly detection, not aligned with user's priority subtopics in generation or multimodal understanding. Signals: time-series anomaly detection, VLM fine-tuning, parameter-efficient model, cross-benchmark generalization, not generative or video/image focused

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved impressive performance across many tasks, yet prior studies report unsatisfactory performance when applying large language or multimodal models to finding abnormal patterns in sequential data. Public anomaly detection benchmarks typically provide interval annotations but not natural-language rationales, making it difficult to fine-tune VLMs to produce grounded, interpretable decisions. To address this gap, we construct VisAnomBench, a curated benchmark built from public time-series datasets and augmented with high-quality anomaly explanations selected from multiple large VLMs using fine-grained, task-specific rewards. Through fine-tuning on this benchmark, we develop VisAnomReasoner, a parameter-efficient VLM for time-series anomaly detection. Experimental results on VisAnomBench show that VisAnomReasoner achieves more accurate anomaly localization and consistently outperforms all baselines, with improvements of at least 21.23 and 23.87 percentage points in precision and F1, respectively. Additional experiments on the TSB-AD-U benchmark demonstrate strong cross-benchmark generalization, with VisAnomReasoner improving precision and F1 by 9.57 and 13.39 percentage points, respectively.

</details>

### [293] [Semantic and Visual Evidence for Efficient Long-Video Reasoning: A Solution for the HD-EPIC VQA Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29402)
*Yinsong Xu, Wei Jing, Liuxin Zhang, Wanjun Lv, Hui Li*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 值得关注 (71.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将长视频推理解耦为语义证据（全局过程结构）和视觉证据（细粒度目标级定位）两种互补形式，通过查询条件检索与动态整合实现高效长视频问答，在 HD-EPIC VQA 挑战中取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在处理长视频理解时受限于上下文长度和对细粒度视觉细节的定位能力不足，HD-EPIC 基准测试揭示了这一局限。作者希望设计一种显式解耦语义与视觉证据的框架，以提升长视频推理效果。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，将长视频推理解耦为两种互补证据：1）语义证据：通过粗到细的提取管道捕获全局过程结构；2）目标级视觉证据：通过边界框和视觉嵌入保留细粒度定位信息。推理时，将推理过程建模为查询条件驱动的证据检索与整合，动态从两种来源选择相关信息。

Result: 在 HD-EPIC VQA 挑战的多个任务类别上取得了有竞争力的性能，验证了显式结构化、检索和整合语义与视觉证据对长视频理解的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式地将长视频推理解耦为语义证据和视觉证据，并通过检索与整合机制动态利用两者，是提升 MLLM 长视频理解能力的关键策略。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于长视频理解与视频问答（HD-EPIC VQA），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题"视频理解与时序推理"（video_understanding_temporal），该子主题涵盖视频 LLM、视频问答、长视频理解等。

Importance reason: Focuses on long-video QA with MLLMs, not directly on user's priority subtopics like autoregressive/diffusion video generation or world models. Signals: Long-video reasoning, semantic and visual evidence, HD-EPIC VQA, MLLM context limitation, query-conditioned retrieval

Abstract: Understanding long-form egocentric videos remains challenging for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) due to limited context length and insufficient grounding of fine-grained visual details. The recently proposed HD-EPIC benchmark highlights these limitations: even strong long-context models achieve relatively low performance across diverse video question answering tasks. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that decouples long-video reasoning into two complementary forms of evidence: semantic evidence and visual evidence. Semantic evidence captures global procedural structure through a coarse-to-fine extraction pipeline, while object-centric visual evidence preserves fine-grained grounding through bounding boxes and visual embeddings. During inference, we formulate reasoning as a query-conditioned evidence retrieval and integration process, dynamically selecting relevant information from both sources. Our approach achieves competitive performance in the HD-EPIC-VQA Challenge across multiple task categories. More broadly, our results demonstrate that explicitly structuring, retrieving, and integrating semantic and visual evidence is critical for effective long-video understanding with MLLMs.

</details>


### [294] [Masked Diffusion Vision-Language Models for Temporal Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29858)
*Fengshun Wang, Zhengbo Zhang, Zhigang Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (52.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MDVLM-TAL，将掩码扩散视觉语言模型（MDVLM）适配到时序动作定位（TAL）任务，通过双向注意力迭代去噪实现语义与时间边界的联合精炼，并引入边界感知掩码、逐步加权重建和步级 IoU 奖励来解决标准扩散训练与 TAL 任务之间的不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自回归视觉语言模型的时序动作定位方法从左到右逐 token 生成，后续语义证据无法修正早期的时间戳预测。本文希望利用掩码扩散模型的双向注意力与迭代去噪特性，使语义 token 和边界 token 在生成过程中可被持续编辑和联合优化。

Method: 提出 MDVLM-TAL 框架，包含三个关键设计：(1) Planned Training Objective，采用边界感知掩码策略（优先掩码时间 token，延迟恢复）和逐步加权重建损失；(2) Step-Level IoU Reward，在去噪过程中提供重叠感知的奖励信号；(3) 结合标准序列级交叉熵作为基础重建信号，在 ActivityNet-RTL、ActivityNet-1.3 和 THUMOS-14 三个数据集上评估。

Result: 在 ActivityNet-RTL、ActivityNet-1.3 和 THUMOS-14 上，MDVLM-TAL 在时序推理和边界定位方面均优于自回归视觉语言基线方法，尤其在更严格的时序 IoU 标准下提升显著。

Conclusion: 掩码扩散视觉语言模型能够有效改进时序动作定位任务，通过双向注意力和迭代去噪实现语义与时间边界的联合优化，为视频时序理解提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 论文研究时序动作定位（TAL），属于视频理解中的时序定位任务，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视频理解与时序推理" (video_understanding_temporal) 高度匹配。同时该方法使用掩码扩散视觉语言模型，也涉及多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on temporal action localization, a niche video understanding task, not on the user's priority subtopics like generative video/image models or world models. Signals: masked diffusion for TAL, boundary-aware masking, step-level IoU reward, improves temporal reasoning, not generative or world model

Abstract: Temporal action localization (TAL) requires recognizing the target event and localizing its start and end times precisely in untrimmed videos. Recent vision-language formulations improve semantic reasoning and support language-conditioned outputs, but their autoregressive decoders still generate tokens from left to right, preventing later semantic evidence from revising earlier timestamp predictions. We adapt masked diffusion vision-language models (MDVLMs) to TAL so that semantic tokens and boundary tokens remain editable throughout iterative denoising with bidirectional attention, allowing temporal boundaries and semantic content to be refined jointly. Direct adaptation, however, creates two TAL-specific mismatches: standard masked diffusion training corrupts all positions uniformly at random, but the time tokens are more reliable when enough semantic context is available; and token-level cross-entropy does not reflect temporal IoU. To address these mismatches, we introduce a Planned Training Objective that uses boundary-aware masking and step-weighted reconstruction to rehearse the late recovery of time tokens, together with a Step-Level IoU Reward that provides overlap-aware supervision during denoising. A standard sequence-level cross-entropy term provides the base reconstruction signal. Experiments on ActivityNet-RTL, ActivityNet-1.3, and THUMOS-14 show that MDVLM-TAL improves both temporal reasoning and boundary localization over autoregressive vision-language baselines, with especially strong gains under stricter temporal IoU criteria.

</details>

### [295] [Usability Analysis of Configurator User Interfaces with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29456)
*Sebastian Lubos, Alexander Felfernig, Damian Garber, Adnan Kraljić, Tarik Kraljić, Viet-Man Le, Thi Ngoc Trang Tran, Gerhard Leitner, Julian Schwazer, Doris Suppan, Reinhard Willfort, Ivan Dukic, Jeremias Fuchs, Manuel Henrich*

Main category: cs.SE, cs.HC

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (58.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文探索使用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）对配置器用户界面进行可扩展的半自动化可用性分析。作者从文献中综合了18个配置器特定的可用性标准，并基于MLLM对16个真实配置器进行了分析，为每个标准生成严重性评级和可操作的改进建议。结果表明MLLM能够可靠地识别配置器特定的可用性问题并提供领域感知的改进建议，尽管人工验证仍然必要，但该方法可显著减少分析配置器可用性所需的工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配置器是定制复杂软件系统、服务和产品的关键技术，其成功应用不仅取决于技术正确性、性能和领域建模，还取决于可用性。然而，现有的通用可用性启发式方法缺乏配置器特定的标准，且缺乏系统化的UI分析工具支持。因此，需要一种可扩展的、半自动化的方法来分析配置器用户界面的可用性。

Method: 作者首先从文献中综合了18个配置器特定的可用性标准。然后，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）对16个真实世界配置器的用户界面进行分析，对每个标准单独评估，生成可用性问题的严重性评级和可操作的改进建议。

Result: 实验结果表明，MLLM能够可靠地识别配置器特定的可用性问题，并提供领域感知的改进建议。该方法虽然仍需要人工验证，但可以显著减少分析配置器可用性所需的工作量。

Conclusion: MLLM在配置器UI可用性分析方面具有潜力，能够提供可扩展的半自动化分析方案。尽管人工验证仍然必要，但该方法可显著降低分析成本，为配置器可用性评估提供了新的工具支持。

Classification reason: 该论文探索使用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）对配置器用户界面进行可用性分析，属于将MLLM应用于软件工程/人机交互领域的垂直应用。这与现有子主题"领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）匹配，该子主题涵盖医疗/法律/科学/代码等垂直领域应用，软件工程领域的UI可用性分析可归入此类。

Importance reason: Focuses on configurator UI usability, not aligned with priority subtopics like video/image generation or world models. Signals: MLLM application, usability analysis, configurator-specific criteria, domain-specific, low priority direction

Abstract: Configuration is a key technology for tailoring complex software systems, services, and products. A successful application of configurators not only depends on technical correctness, performance, and domain modeling but also on their usability. While general usability heuristics are widely used, configurator-specific criteria and tool support for systematic user interface (UI) analysis are limited. This paper explores the use of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for scalable and semi-automated usability analysis of configurator UIs. We synthesize 18 configurator-specific usability criteria from the literature and apply these criteria in an MLLM-based analysis of 16 real-world configurators. Each criterion is assessed individually to generate severity ratings for usability issues and actionable improvement suggestions. A review of the results confirms that MLLMs can reliably identify configurator-specific usability issues and provide domain-aware improvement recommendations. Although human validation remains necessary, this approach has the potential to significantly reduce the required effort to analyze configurator usability.

</details>


### [296] [CCS: Clinical Consensus Selection for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30131)
*Xi Zhang, Yingshu Li, Zaiqiao Meng, Jake Lever, Edmond S. L. Ho*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (56.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Clinical Consensus Selection (CCS)，一种解码器无关的推理时选择框架，通过采样多个候选报告并选择临床共识最高的报告来提升放射学报告生成质量，在多个数据集和模型上一致优于单路径解码和通用 Best-of-N 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学报告生成（RRG）通常采用单路径解码生成单一报告作为最终输出，但研究发现固定模型在候选池中往往能生成临床质量更高的报告，只是默认解码策略未能选出它们。推理时的决策机制是当前被忽视的性能瓶颈。

Method: CCS 框架包含三个步骤：(1) 对固定放射学 MLLM 进行多次采样生成候选报告池；(2) 使用文本效用（如 RadGraph F1）和基于图像-报告多模态嵌入器的放射学适配效用计算候选报告之间的临床共识；(3) 选择临床共识得分最高的报告作为最终输出。该方法与具体解码器无关，可即插即用。

Result: 在三个数据集和多个放射学 MLLM 上，CCS 一致优于单路径解码和通用 Best-of-N 基线，在临床指标上提升尤为显著。分析表明，基于图像的效用与文本共识形成了不同的选择维度，且推理时仍有较大改进空间。

Conclusion: CCS 证明了推理时选择策略在放射学报告生成中的有效性，揭示了当前 RRG 方法在推理阶段存在未被充分利用的性能潜力，为提升临床报告质量提供了新的方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究放射学报告生成（RRG），属于医疗垂直领域的生成应用，与 [CANONICAL] 领域内容生成应用（aigc_domain_application）高度匹配。该 subtopic 同时存在于 multimodal_understanding_generation 和 aigc 方向下，因此选择复用。

Importance reason: Focuses on radiology report generation, a narrow medical domain, with limited transfer to the user's priority topics in video/image generation and world models. Signals: clinical domain, inference-time selection, radiology-specific, decoder-agnostic, multimodal embedder

Abstract: Radiology report generation (RRG) is commonly formulated as a single-path generation task, where a multimodal large language model (MLLM) produces one decoded report as the final output. While recent progress has largely been driven by scaling training data, model capacity, and retrieval mechanisms, improving report quality at inference time remains underexplored. In this work, we observe that fixed radiology MLLMs often generate clinically stronger reports elsewhere in their candidate pool than the one selected by default decoding, suggesting that inference-time decision making remains an overlooked bottleneck. To address this, we propose Clinical Consensus Selection (CCS), a decoder-agnostic inference-time selection framework that samples multiple candidate reports and selects the one with the highest clinical consensus across the rollout pool. CCS unifies text-based utilities with a radiology-adapted utility computed by an image--report-trained multimodal embedder, which measures candidate agreement beyond surface-level textual similarity. Across three datasets and multiple radiology MLLMs, CCS consistently improves inference-time performance over single-path decoding and generic Best-of-N baselines, with particularly clear gains on clinical metrics. Further analysis shows that image-grounded utility forms a selection axis distinct from textual consensus and that substantial headroom remains for improving RRG at inference time.

</details>


### [297] [MedCase-Structured: A Text-to-FHIR Dataset for Benchmarking Diagnostic Reasoning in Clinically Realistic EHR Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30295)
*Valentina Bui Muti, Eugénie Dulout, Ziquan Fu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 领域内容生成应用

Importance: 普通相关 (36.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出MedCase-Structured，一个从非结构化临床文本生成结构化HL7 FHIR R4数据包的pipeline，并构建了用于评测LLM诊断推理能力的合成数据集。该pipeline结合分阶段LLM生成与术语验证修复机制，在82.5%的病例上生成了有效的FHIR数据。评测发现LLM在结构化FHIR输入上的诊断准确率显著低于纯文本输入，凸显了部署对齐评测的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM临床推理评测多基于静态数据集或非结构化输入，无法反映真实电子健康记录（EHR）系统中使用的结构化、可互操作的数据格式，导致评测结果与临床实际部署存在差距。

Method: 提出一个pipeline，包含分阶段LLM生成（从非结构化文本逐步生成FHIR资源）与术语验证修复（基于标准医学术语库进行编码验证和修正），以减少幻觉编码并保证结构语义一致性。将该pipeline应用于MedCaseReasoning数据集，构建了MedCase-Structured合成数据集。

Result: 在82.5%的病例上成功生成有效FHIR数据。评测表明，LLM在结构化FHIR输入上的诊断准确率一致低于纯文本输入，揭示了结构化数据格式对LLM推理能力的挑战。

Conclusion: 结构化EHR数据格式对LLM临床推理构成额外挑战，现有评测范式需要向部署对齐的方向改进；所提出的生成pipeline可支持可控的临床决策支持系统评测。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦医疗领域，构建从非结构化文本生成结构化FHIR医疗数据（电子健康记录格式）的pipeline，属于医疗垂直领域的生成应用，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "领域内容生成应用"（aigc_domain_application）。同时该工作涉及LLM临床推理评测，也匹配多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on clinical EHR data generation, which is outside your priority subtopics and flagged by negative keywords. Signals: clinical NLP, FHIR data generation, diagnostic reasoning, negative keyword match, low direction weight

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show promise for clinical reasoning and decision support, but evaluation in realistic, electronic health record-congruent settings remains limited. Existing benchmarks often rely on static datasets or unstructured inputs that do not reflect the structured, interoperable data formats used in clinical systems. We introduce a pipeline for generating clinically realistic HL7 FHIR R4 bundles from unstructured text, enabling controllable evaluation of clinical decision support systems. The pipeline combines staged LLM generation with terminology-grounded validation and repair to reduce hallucinated codes and enforce structural and semantic consistency. Applying this approach to MedCaseReasoning, we construct MedCase-Structured, a synthetic dataset aligned with clinician-authored diagnostic cases, achieving valid FHIR generation for 82.5% of cases. Evaluation on MedCase-Structured reveals consistently lower diagnostic accuracy for LLMs on structured FHIR inputs than with plain text, highlighting the importance of deployment-aligned benchmarking.

</details>
